# Brahmavaivarta-purāṇa 1

## Chapter 0

## \|\| 0.1 \|\|

*gaṇeśabrahmaśasureśaśeṣāḥ surāśca sarve manavo munīndrāḥ \|*  
*sarasvatīśrīgirijādikāśca yaṃ namanti devyaḥ praṇamāmi taṃ vibhuma \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. I bow in reverence to the all-pervading lord, who is adored by Ganesha, Brahma, Shiva, Indra, serpent Shesha, the gods, all the Manus, the sages, Sarasvati, Lakshmi besides Paravti and others.

## \|\| 0.2 \|\|

*sthūlāstanūrvidadhataṃ triguṇaṃ virājaṃ viśvāni lomavivareṣu mahāntamādyam \|*  
*sṛṣṭyunmukhaḥ svakalayā'pi sasarja sūkṣmaṃ nityaṃ sametya hṛdi yastamajaṃ bhajāmi \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The one who has the solid body of three phases with universal form, in whose body innumerable worlds reside, the one who is the primeval person; the one who is engaged in the creation of the universe and the one who resides in the heart of everyone in a tiny form, the same Brahman is adored by me.

## \|\| 0.3 \|\|

*dhyāyante dhyānaniṣṭhāḥ suranaramanavo yogino yogarūḍhāḥ santaḥ svapne'pi santaṃ katikatijanibhiryaṃ na paśyanti taptvā \|*  
*dhyāye svecchāmayaṃ taṃ triguṇaparamaho nirvikāraṃ nirīhaṃ bhaktyā dhyānaikahetornirupamaruciraśyāmarūpaṃ dadhānam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. All the gods, humans, animals, Manus and the ascetics or yogis, adore him attentively and several of the truth-seekers, adore him performing hard tapas for several births, but are unable to have a trace of him, the same lord is adored by me,who has a beautiful dark complexion.

## \|\| 0.4 \|\|

*vande kṛṣṇaṃ guṇātītaṃ paraṃ brahmācyutaṃ yataḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūvuḥ prakṛtibrahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. I bow in reverence to lord Krishna who represents the three gunas (elements), out of whose body, Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva have emerged.

## Chapter 1

## \|\| 1.1 \|\|

*oṃ namo bhagavate vāsu devāya \|*  
*nārāyaṇaṃ namaskṛtya naraṃ caiva narottamam \|*  
*devīṃ sarasvatīṃ vyāsaṃ tato jayamudīrayet \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Having been saluted to lord Narayana and Nara, the most exalted being, and goddess Sarasvati, one should recite the Jaya (Mahabharata, Puranas etc.).

## \|\| 1.2 \|\|

*amṛtaparamapūrvaṃ bhāratīkāmadhenuṃ śrutigaṇakṛtavatso vyāsadevo dudoha \|*  
*atirucirapurāṇaṃ brahmavaivarttametatpibata pibata mugdhā dugdhamakṣayyamiṣṭam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The sage Vyasadeva conceiving Sarasvati as the wish fulfilling cow and the Vedas as calf, milked the nectar of the theme of the Brahmavaivarta Purana. Therefore, О gentlemen, consume this invisible milk.

## \|\| 1.3-4 \|\|

*bhārate naimiṣāraṇye ṛṣayaḥ śaunakādayaḥ \|*  
*nityāṃ naimittikīṃ kṛtvā kriyāmūṣuḥ kuśāsane \|\| 3 \|\|*

*etasminnantare sautimāgacchantaṃ yadṛcchayā \|*  
*praṇataṃ suvinītaṃ taṃ vilokya dadurāsanam \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. In the holy place of Naimisaranya, the sacred sages headed by the great sage Shaunaka had been performing their daily prayers seated over the kitsdsana. At that point of time Ugrashrava, the son of Suta, suddenly arrived there. He bowed in reverence to all the sages, who offered him a seat of the kusha-grass.

## \|\| 1.5 \|\|

*taṃ sampūjyātithiṃ bhaktyā śaunako munipuṅgavaḥ \|*  
*papraccha kuśalaṃ śāṃtaṃ śāntaḥ paurāṇikaṃ mudā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Then welcoming the guest, the best of the sages, Shaunaka enquired about his welfare, adoring him with devotion and delight.

## \|\| 1.6-10 \|\|

*vartmāyāsavinirmuktaṃ vasantaṃ susthirāsane \|*  
*sasmitaṃ sarvatattvajñaṃ purāṇānāṃ purāṇavit \|\| 6 \|\|*

*paraṃ kṛṣṇakathopetaṃ purāṇaṃ śruti saṃmatam \|*  
*maṅgalaṃ maṅgalārhaṃ ca maṅgalyaṃ maṅgalālayam \|\| 7 \|\|*

*sarvamaṅgalabījaṃ ca sarvadā maṅgalapradam \|*  
*sarvāmaṅgalanighnaṃ ca sarvasaṃpatkaraṃ param \|\| 8 \|\|*

*haribhaktipradaṃ śaśvatsukhadaṃ mokṣadaṃ bhavāt \|*  
*tattvajñānapradaṃ dāraputrapautravivarddhanam \|\| 9 \|\|*

*papraccha suvinītaṃ ca suprīto munisaṃsadi \|*  
*yathā''kāśe tārakāṇāṃ dvijarājo virājate \|\| 10 \|\|*

6-10. Getting free from the fatigue of the journey, the sage was seated over Jmsasana. and smilingly put a question to the sage Suta, who was well-read in the Puranas and looked like the moon among the stars. “The best of the stories, which relates to Lord Krishna, are pleasant to listen to, О bestower of welfare, you are the abode of welfare, the seed of all the welfare, the one who showers grace always and removes all the misfortunes, bestows all the riches, showers the devotion of the lord, which always bestows pleasure and molcsa (salvation). He is the one who imparts divine knowledge and the one who ensures the increase of the family, sons and grand-sons.

## \|\| 1.11-16 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*prasthānaṃ bhavataḥ kutra kuta āyāsi te śivam \|*  
*kimasmākaṃ puṇyadinamadya tvaddarśanena ca \|\| 11 \|\|*

*vayameva kalau bhītā viśiṣṭajñānavarjitāḥ \|*  
*mumukṣavo bhave magnāstaddhetustvamihāgataḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*bhavānsādhurmahābhāgaḥ purāṇeṣu purāṇavit \|*  
*sarveṣu ca purāṇeṣu niṣṇāto'tikṛpānidhiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*śrīkṛṣṇe niścalā bhaktiryato bhavati śāśvatī \|*  
*tatkathyatāṃ mahābhāga purāṇaṃ jñānavarddhanam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*garīyasī yā sākṣācca karmamūlanikṛntanī \|*  
*saṃsārasannibaddhānāṃ nigaḍacchedakartarī \|\| 15 \|\|*

*bhavadāvāgnidagdhānāṃ pīyūṣa vṛṣṭivarṣiṇī \|*  
*sukhadā''nandadā saute śaśvaccetasi jīvinām \|\| 16 \|\|*

11-16. Shaunaka asked: “Where are you going? Where from have you arrived? We feel grateful by your visit today. All of us are terrified because of the Age of Kali and possess no special knowledge. We are engrossed in the worldly affairs and are desirous of redemption. This (is possibly) the cause of your arrival here. You are noble, immensely virtuous, well-versed in the Puranas, have well-read all the Puranas and are full of compassion. You better tell us about some Purana, by which we might be able to achieve some devotion to Lord Krishna, because, О Son of Suta, the devotion of Lord Krishna is better than even the salvation and it removes the worldly fetters of the human being. It showers nectar over the people who suffer from the conflagration of the worldly ills and instils pleasures in the minds of the people”.

## \|\| 1.17-19 \|\|

*yatrādau sarvabījaṃ ca paraṃ brahmanirūpaṇam \|*  
*tasya sṛṣṭyunmukhasyāpi sṛṣṭerutkīrttanaṃ param \|\| 17 \|\|*

*sākāraṃ vā nirākāraṃ paramātmasvarūpakam \|*  
*kimākārañca tadbrahma taddhyānaṃ kiñca bhāvanam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*dhyāyante vaiṣṇavāḥ kiṃvā śāntāśca yoginastathā \|*  
*mataṃ pradhānaṃ keṣāṃ vā gūḍhaṃ vede nirūpitam \|\| 19 \|\|*

17-19. You recite the stories from such a Purana, in the beginning of which the seeds of the entire universe are enshrined and is also infested with Parabrahman. It should also deal with elements of Parabrahman in the creation of the universe. We intend to enquire from you as to whether Paramatma (the great soul) is having the visible or invisible form; what is the true form of the Brahman? How to meditate on or adore the lord? Whom do the ascetics or the yogis adore? What is the deep tattva propounded in the Vedas?

## \|\| 1.20-24 \|\|

*prakṛteśca ya ākāro yatra vatsanirūpitaḥ \|*  
*guṇānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ yatra mahadādeśca niścayaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*golokavarṇanaṃ yatra yatra vaikuṇṭhavarṇanam \|*  
*varṇanaṃ śivalokasya yatrānyat svargavarṇanam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*aṃśānāṃ ca kalānāṃ ca yatra saute nirūpaṇam \|*  
*ke prākṛtāḥ kā prakṛtiḥ ka ātmā prakṛteḥ paraḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*nigūḍhaṃ janma yeṣāṃ vā devānāṃ devayoṣitām \|*  
*samutpattiḥ samudrāṇāṃ śailānāṃ saritāmapi \|\| 23 \|\|*

*ke vāṃ'śāḥ prakṛteścāpi kalāḥ kā vā kalākalāḥ \|*  
*tāsāñca caritaṃ dhyānaṃ pūjāstotrādikaṃ śubham \|\| 24 \|\|*

20-24. “O Son, you tell me about the Purana in which the form of nature has been described, besides the symptoms of the virtues, the Mahat, the Goloka, Vaikuntha Shivaloka and the heavens; the arts and the arhshas. О Son of Suta, what are the natural elements? What is nature and what is the soul beyond nature, the gods and the goddesses who are born on earth secretly, you tell us about them besides the oceans, the mountains, the rivers, their genesis and evolution. Which is integral part of the nature? What are the arts? Tell us about their character, the meditation, adoration and the sacred recitations and the Puranas in which all these details are enshrined.

## \|\| 1.25-28 \|\|

*durgā sarasvatī lakṣmī sāvitrīṇāñca varṇanam \|*  
*yatraiva rādhikākhyānamatyapūrvaṃ sudhopamam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*jīvakarmavipākaśca narakāṇāñca varṇanam \|*  
*karmaṇāṃ khaṇḍanaṃ yatra yatra tebhyo vimokṣaṇam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*yeṣāñca jīvināṃ yadyatsthānaṃ yatra śubhāśubham \|*  
*jīvināṃ karmaṇo yasmādyāsu yāsu ca yoniṣu \|\| 27 \|\|*

*jīvināṃ karmaṇo yasmādyoyo rogo bhavediha \|*  
*mokṣaṇaṃ karmaṇo yasmātteṣāñca tannirūpaya \|\| 28 \|\|*

25-28. You tell us the name of the Purana, which enshrines the character of Durga, Sarasvati, Lakshmi and Savitri, besides the one which contains the stories of Radhika. You also tell us details about the hells achieved by the people and the deeds by which one attains these hells and the remedies to escape these hells. The places which are sacred for the people and those which are unholy may be told to us besides the deeds by performing of which, one achieves a place in the heaven or the hell; please tell us also the deeds, which attract the particular type of ailments and those lead one to salvation.

## \|\| 1.29 \|\|

*manasā tulasī kālī gaṅgā pṛdhvī vasundharā \|*  
*āsāṃ yatra śubhākhyānamanyāsāmapi yatra vai \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. “O Son of Suta, you tell us the name of the Purana in which details about Tulasi, Manasa, Kali, Ganga, Vasundhara, PrthivI and the one containing the stories about the goddesses besides the details about the humans having different births. Which are the deeds, attracting the ailments and the deeds which lead one to achieve redemption, you please tell us.”

## \|\| 1.30-36 \|\|

*śāligrāmaśilānāñca dānānāñca nirūpaṇam \|*  
*apūrvaṃ yatra vā saute dharmādharmanirūpaṇam \|\| 30 \|\|*

*gaṇeśvarasya caritaṃ yatra tajjanma karma ca \|*  
*kavacastotramantrāṇāṃ gūḍhānāṃ yatra varṇanam \|\| 31 \|\|*

*yadapūrvamupākhyānamaśrutaṃ paramādbhutam \|*  
*kṛtvā manasi tatsarvaṃ sāmprataṃ vaktumarhasi \|\| 32 \|\|*

*yatra janma bhramo viśve puṇyakṣetre ca bhārate \|*  
*paripūrṇatamasyāpi kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

*janma kasya gṛhe labdhaṃ puṇye puṇyavato mune \|*  
*sutaṃ prasūtā kā dhanyā mānyā puṇyavatī satī \|\| 34 \|\|*

*āvirbhūya ca tadgehātkvāgataḥ kena hetunā \|*  
*gatvā kiṃ kṛtavāṃstatra kathaṃ vā punarāgataḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*bhārāvataraṇaṃ kena prārthito goścakāra saḥ \|*  
*vidhāya kiṃvā setuṃ ca golokaṃ gatavānpunaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

30-36. “О Son of Stita, you tell us about the scripture containing the stories of Saligrama, the Dhanna and the Sin. You also tell us about Ganapati, the deep secrets connected with the god, details about his life, his recitation mantrast secret kavachas, the mantras and the surprising stories connected with him. You tell us all about them in detail. You tell us about the Purana enshrining the details about the life of Krishna in (Bharata) India. О sage, in which sacred place was he bom? Who was the chaste and virtuous lady who gave birth to a son like Krishna? Where did he go after his birth? Why did he leave the place of his birth and what for? What did he do after leaving that place? Then how did he return to his abode? At whose request he relieved the burden of the earth? What for did he establish the norms? Why did he return again to the Goloka?

## \|\| 1.37-39 \|\|

*itīdamanyadākhyānaṃ purāṇaṃ śrutidurlabham \|*  
*durvijñeyaṃ munīnāṃ ca manonirmalakāraṇam \|\| 37 \|\|*

*svajñānādyanmayā pṛṣṭamapṛṣṭaṃ vā śubhāśubham \|*  
*sadyo vairāgyajananaṃ tanme vyākhyātumarhasi \|\| 38 \|\|*

*śiṣyapṛṣṭamapṛṣṭaṃ vā vyākhyānaṃ kurute ca yaḥ \|*  
*sa sadguruḥ satāṃ śreṣṭho yogyāyogye ca yaḥ samaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

37-39. The sages are unaware of the Purana in which the entire details about Krishna are contained and it is beyond their reach. This is the means to cleanse the mind. Whatever good or bad question have been asked by me according to my own knowledge, you enlighten us over the issues raised which would inculcate the sense of vairagya. A good teacher explains the points on which clarifications are asked for as well as those over which no questions are asked.”

## \|\| 1.40-49 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*sarvaṃ kuśalamasmākaṃ tvatpādapadmadarśanāt \|*  
*siddhakṣetrādāgato'haṃ yāmi nārāyaṇāśramam \|\| 40 \|\|*

*dṛṣṭvā viprasamūhaṃ ca namaskartumihāgataḥ \|*  
*draṣṭuṃ ca naimiṣāraṇyaṃ puṇyadaṃ cāpi bhārate \|\| 41 \|\|*

*devaṃ vipraṃ guruṃ dṛṣṭvā na namedyastu saṃbhramāt \|*  
*sa kālasūtraṃ vrajati yāvaccandradivākarau \|\| 42 \|\|*

*harirbrāhmaṇarūpekṣaṇa śaśvad bhramati bhūtale \|*  
*sukṛtī praṇametpuṇyādbrāhmaṇaṃ harirūpiṇam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*bhagavanyattvayā pṛṣṭaṃ jñātaṃ sarvamabhīpsitam \|*  
*sārabhūtaṃ purāṇeṣu brahmavaivarttamuttamam \|\| 44 \|\|*

*purāṇopapurāṇānāṃ vedānāṃ bhramabhañjanam \|*  
*haribhaktipradaṃ sarvatattvajñānavivarddhanam \|\| 45 \|\|*

*kāmināṃ kāmadaṃ cedaṃ mumukṣūṇāṃ ca mokṣadam \|*  
*bhaktipradaṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ kalpavṛkṣasvarūpakam \|\| 46 \|\|*

*brahmakhaṇḍaṃ sarvabījaṃ parabrahmanirūpaṇam \|*  
*dhyāyante yoginaḥ santo vaiṣṇavā yatparātparam \|\| 47 \|\|*

*vaiṣṇavā yoginaḥ santo na ca bhinnāśca śaunaka \|*  
*svājñānaparipākena bhavanti jīvinaḥ kramāt \|\| 48 \|\|*

*santo bhavanti satsaṅgādyogisaṃgena yoginaḥ \|*  
*vaiṣṇavā bhaktasaṃgena kramātsadyoginaḥ parāḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

40-49. The son of Suta then said: “All is well (with me) with the grace of your feet. I am coming from the siddha-ksetra and shall go to the Narayanasrama. I have arrived here in order to pay my respects to the group of the Brahmanas and to perform the pilgrimage of the sacred place of Naimisaranya. The one who does not bow in reverence finding a good, a Brahmana and the teacher, he is consigned to the terrible hell and has to remain there till the sun and the moon last. Vishnu roams about the earth in the form of a Brahmana. Therefore a noble person bows in reverence by the grace of Vishnu to a Brahmana. O Sacred Soul, whatever has been asked by you, I have understood thoroughly. Brahmavaivarta is the gist of the Puranas. This is the Purana, which removes all the doubts about the Puranas and the upa-Puranas. It bestows devotion of Hari and develops the knowledge of all the elements. It grants pleasure to pleasure-seekers and salvation or truth to truth-seekers. It bestows the unfailing devotion on the Vaishnavas and is like the kalpavrksa for them. The eternal Brahman is enshrined therein as a seed of the globe. The yogis and Vaishnavas meditate on him. О Shaunaka, there is hardly any difference between a yogi and a Vaisnaiva saint. The humans become Vaishnavas or yogis on the basis of the knowledge they possess. They gradually become great yogis.

## \|\| 1.50-53 \|\|

*yatrodbhavaśca devānāṃ devīnāṃ sarvajīvinām \|*  
*tataḥ prakṛtikhaṇḍe ca devīnāṃ caritaṃ śubham \|\| 50 \|\|*

*jīvakarmavipākaśca śāligrāmanirūpaṇam \|*  
*tāsāṃ ca kavaca stotramantrapūjānirūpaṇam \|\| 51 \|\|*

*prakṛterlakṣaṇaṃ tatra kalāṃśānāṃ nirūpaṇam \|*  
*kīrtterutkīrtanaṃ tāsāṃ prabhāvaśca nirūpitaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

*sukṛtīnāṃ duṣkṛtīnāṃ yadyatsthānaṃ śubhāśubham \|*  
*varṇanaṃ narakāṇāṃ ca rogāṇāṃ mokṣaṇaṃ tataḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

50-53. Thereafter follows the Prakriti-khanda, which deals with the gods, the goddesses besides the genesis and evolution of all the creatures of the universe in addition to the sacred stories about the female deities. It also deals with the deeds of the humans and Saligrama. The kavachas, stotras, mantras and the methods of their adoration are propounded therein. In the Prakriti-khanda the symptoms of the nature, their amshas and the stories related thereto are also narrated, besides the glory and their influence on humans. It also describes the merits and demerits one derives by good or bad deeds. The hells which the degraded souls achieve because of their misdeeds on earth and the duration of stay in each hell have been defined therein.

## \|\| 1.54-55 \|\|

*tato gaṇeśakhaṇḍe ca tajjanma parikīrtitam \|*  
*atīvāpūrvacaritaṃ śrutivedasudurlabham \|\| 54 \|\|*

*gaṇeśabhṛgusaṃvāde sarvatattvanirūpaṇam \|*  
*nigūḍhakavacastotramantratantra nirūpaṇam \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. This is followed by Ganesha-khanda which contains the details about the birth of Ganesha and the precious stories related to his life. In the dialogue between Ganesha and Bhrigu, the life events of Ganesha, which are quite secret in nature are brought out. The secret kavachas of Ganesha, the mantras and tantras are discussed therein.

## \|\| 1.56-61 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇajanmakhaṇḍaṃ ca kīrtitaṃ ca tataḥ param \|*  
*bhārate puṇyakṣetre ca śrīkṛṣṇajanma karma ca \|\| 56 \|\|*

*bhuvo bhārāvataraṇaṃ krīḍākautukamaṅgalam \|*  
*satāṃ setuvidhānaṃ ca janmakhaṇḍe nirūpitam \|\| 57 \|\|*

*idaṃ te kathitaṃ vipra purāṇapravaraṃ param \|*  
*catuḥkhaṇḍaiḥ parimitaṃ sarvadharmanirūpaṇam \|\| 58 \|\|*

*sarveṣāmīpsitaṃ śrīdaṃ sarvāśāpūrṇakāraṇam \|*  
*brahmavaivartakaṃ nāma sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradam \|\| 59 \|\|*

*sārabhūtaṃ purāṇeṣu kevalaṃ vedasaṃmitam \|*  
*vivṛtaṃ brahmakārtsnyaṃ ca kṛṣṇena yatra śaunaka \|\| 60 \|\|*

*brahmavaivartakaṃ tena pravadanti purāvidaḥ \|*  
*\|\| 61 \|\|*

56-61. Thereafter follows the recitation of the Krishna-janma Khanda (the part relating to the birth of Krishna). It deals with the performance of Krishna, to relieve the land of Bharata of the burden of the evil people, the pleasure sports of the lord which serve like a bridge for the noble people to cross the ocean of the universe. This is the gist of all religions. The Purana is adored by all and grants them riches besides fulfilling all their desires, earning for it the title of Brahmavaivarta. О Shaunaka, Shri Krishna has propounded the entire philosophy of Brahman in it and this is also the reason why it is called the Brahmavaivarta.

## \|\| 1.62-67 \|\|

*idaṃ purāṇasūtraṃ ca purā dattaṃ ca brahmaṇe \|*  
*nirāmaye ca goloke kṛṣṇena paramātmanā \|\| 62 \|\|*

*mahātīrthe puṣkare ca dattaṃ dharmāya brahmaṇā \|*  
*dharmeṇa dattaṃ putrāya prītyā nārāyaṇāya ca \|\| 63 \|\|*

*nārado vyāsadevāya pradadau jāhnavītaṭe \|\| 64 \|\|*

*vyāsaḥ purāṇasūtraṃ tatsaṃvyasya vipulaṃ mahat \|*  
*mahyaṃ dadau siddhakṣetre puṇyadeśe manoharam \|\| 65 \|\|*

*mayedaṃ kathitaṃ brahmaṃstatsamagraṃ niśāmaya \|*  
*aṣṭādaśasahasraṃ tu vyāsenedaṃ purāṇakam \|\| 66 \|\|*

*purāṇakārtsnyaśravaṇe yatphalaṃ labhate naraḥ \|*  
*tatphalaṃ labhate nūnamadhyāyaśravaṇena ca \|\| 67 \|\|*

62-67. In the early times, the brief of this Purana was handed down by lord Krishna to Brahma. Thereafter, Brahma narrated the same to Dharma at the sacred place of Puskara. Dharma in turn imparted the sacred knowledge to Narayana with a pleasant mind. Lord Narayana recited this Purana to Narada, who narrated the same to the sage Vyasa at the banks of the Ganga. Analysing the text further and developing it pleasantly, Vyasa imparted its knowledge to me. The merit

## Chapter 2

## \|\| 2.1 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kimapūrvaṃ śrutaṃ saute paramādbhutamīpsitam \|*  
*sarvaṃ kathaya saṃvyasya brahmakhaṇḍamanuttamam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shaunaka said: “O Son of Suta, which is the astonishing and the unprecedented Purana you have come across? You better narrate the Brahmakhanda in detail (to enlighten us on the various subjects dealt with therein).

## \|\| 2.2-9 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*vande guroḥ pādapadmaṃ vyāsasyāmitatejasaḥ \|*  
*hariṃ devāndvijānnatvā dharmānvakṣye sanātanān \|\| 2 \|\|*

*yacchrutaṃ vyāsavaktreṇa brahmakhaṇḍamanuttamam \|*  
*ajñānāndhatamodhvaṃsi jñānavartmapradīpakam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*jyotiḥsamūhaṃ pralaye purā''sītkevalaṃ dvija \|*  
*sūryyakoṭiprabhaṃ nityamasaṃkhyaṃ viśvakāraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*svecchāmayasya ca vibhostajjyotirujjvalaṃ mahat \|*  
*jyotirabhyantare lokatrayameva manoharam \|\| 5 \|\|*

*teṣāmupari golokaṃ nityamīśvaravad dvija \|*  
*trikoṭiyojanāyāmavistīrṇaṃ maṇḍalākṛti \|\| 6 \|\|*

*tejaḥsvarūpaṃ sumahadratnabhūmimayaṃ param \|*  
*adṛśyaṃ yogibhiḥ svapne dṛśyaṃ gamyaṃ ca vaiṣṇavaiḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*yogena dhṛtamīśena cāntarikṣasthitaṃ varam \|*  
*ādhivyādhijarāmṛtyuśokabhītivivarjitam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*sadratnaracitāsaṃkhyamandiraiḥ pariśobhitam \|*  
*laye kṛṣṇayutaṃ sṛṣṭau gopagopībhirāvṛtam \|\| 9 \|\|*

2-9. Sauti said: “I bow in reverence at the feet of the glorious teacher Vyasa. Then after bowing at the feet of Vishnu, the Brahmanas, I am going to narrate the ancient religions, the sacred Brahmakhanda which was recited to me by Vyasa; it destroys the darkness of ignorance and leads to the bright path of knowledge. О Brahmana, there was a ball of light in the beginning, after the dissolution, the lustre of which resembled crores of suns. The same ball of light was quite vast and had been the cause of the universe. The light of the selfbom lord is full of lustre. All the three worlds are enshrined in this ball of light quite pleasantly. О Brahmana the Goloka is located above all the three worlds which is eternal like the lord himself. It is spread into crores of yojanas. It exists in the form of a globe. It is quite illustrious and its land is studded with gems. The yogis cannot visualise it even in dream, while the Vaishnava not only visualise it but also achieve it. The great soul has held the best of the lokas with his yogic practices. The Goloka is free from bodily ailments, mental ailments, death, grief and terror. Its grandeur it increased by innumerable gem-studded vast mansions. At the time of dissolution, Lord Krishna alone resides there. During the normal times the place is crowded with the cowherds and cowherdesses.

## \|\| 2.10 \|\|

*tadadho dakṣiṇe savye pañcāśatkoṭiyojanān \|*  
*vaikuṇṭhaṃ śivalokaṃ tu tatsamaṃ sumanoharam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Below the Goloka and at the distance of fifty crores of yojanas, Vaikuntha is located to its south. Shivaloka is located to the west. Both the Vaikuntha and Shivaloka are as beautiful as the Goloka. Vaikuntha is located in a circular wa\> covering an area of a crore of yojanas.

## \|\| 2.11-16 \|\|

*koṭiyojanavistīrṇaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ maṇḍalākṛti \|*  
*laye śūnyaṃ ca sṛṣṭau ca lakṣmīnārāyaṇānvitam \|\| 11 \|\|*

*caturbhujaiḥ pārṣadaiśca jarāmṛtyvādivarjitam \|*  
*savye ca śivalokaṃ ca koṭiyojanavistṛtam \|\| 12 \|\|*

*laye śūnyaṃ ca sṛṣṭau ca sapārṣadaśivānvitam \|*  
*golokābhyantare jyotiratīva sumanoharam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*paramāhlādakaṃ śaśvatparamānandakārakam \|*  
*dhyāyante yoginaḥ śaśvadyogena jñānacakṣuṣā \|\| 14 \|\|*

*tadevānandajanakaṃ nirākāraṃ parātparam \|*  
*tajjyotirantare rūpamatīva sumanoharam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*navīnanīradaśyāmaṃ raktapaṅkajalocanam \|*  
*śāradīyapārvaṇenduśobhitaṃ cāmalānanam \|\| 16 \|\|*

11-16. During the time of dissolution, it remains deserted and during the normal times Vishnu and Lakshmi reside there attended by the four armed attendants. Vaikuntha too is from death and old age. To the west of it is located the Shivaloka which is spread over an area of a crore of yojcinas. During the. time of dissolution, the Shivaloka also remains un-inhabited and during the normal times, Shiva and Parvati have their abode there. The Goloka possesses the divine light which bestows pleasure on all. The ascetics always meditate upon with their divine yogic sight that light which is blissful, invisible or having no form and represents the form of Brahmana. The lord remains present in that light in quite a pleasant form, having a dark complexion like a new cloud. His eyes resemble the red lotus. His spotless face shines like the full moon of the winter.

## \|\| 2.17-21 \|\|

*koṭikandarpalāvaṇyaṃ līlādhāma manoramam \|*  
*dvibhujaṃ muralīhastaṃ sasmitaṃ pītavāsasam \|\| 17 \|\|*

*sadratnabhūṣaṇaughena bhūṣitaṃ bhaktavatsalam \|*  
*candanokṣitasarvāṅgaṃ kastūrīkuṅkumānvitam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*śrīvatsavakṣaḥsaṃbhrājatkaustubhena virājitam \|*  
*sadratnasāraracitakirīṭamukuṭojjvalam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca vanamālāvibhūṣitam \|*  
*tadeva paramaṃ brahma bhagavantaṃ sanātanam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*svecchāmayaṃ sarvabījaṃ sarvādhāraṃ parātparam \|*  
*kiśoravayasaṃ śaśvadgopaveṣavidhāyakam \|\| 21 \|\|*

17-21. His beauty resembles crores of Kamadevas. His beautiful form is the abode of divine plays. He has two arms and holds a flute. He has a smiling face and is clad with a yellow lower garment. He is adorned with beautiful gem-studded ornaments. His limbs are adorned with sandal-paste, kasttirl and saffron. His chest is adorned with the Srivatsa mark. His head is adorned with the kirtta-mukuta which is studded with gems. He is seated over the gem-studded throne. He indeed is Parabrahman, the great lord. He is self-born, the cause of the creation of all the universe and the absolute Brahman. He always remains in the tender age moving in the form of a cowherd.

## \|\| 2.22-27 \|\|

*koṭipūrṇenduśobhāḍhyaṃ bhaktānugrahakārakam \|*  
*nirīhaṃ nirvikāraṃ ca paripūrṇatamaṃ vibhum \|\| 22 \|\|*

*rāsamaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ śāntaṃ rāseśvaraṃ varam \|*  
*māṅgalyaṃ maṅgalārhaṃ ca māṅgalyaṃ maṅgalapradam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*paramānandabījaṃ ca satyamakṣaramavyayam \|*  
*sarvasiddheśvaraṃ sarvasiddhirūpaṃ ca siddhidam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*prakṛteḥ paramīśānaṃ nirguṇaṃ nityavigraham \|*  
*ādyaṃ puruṣamavyaktaṃ puruhūtaṃ puruṣṭutam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*satyaṃ svatantramekaṃ ca paramātmasvarūpakam \|*  
*dhyāyante vaiṣṇavāḥ śāntāḥ śāntaṃ tanparamāyaṇam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*evaṃrūpaṃ paraṃ bibhradbhagavāneka eva saḥ \|*  
*digbhiśca nabhasā sārddhaṃ śūnyaṃ viśvaṃ dadarśa ha \|\| 27 \|\|*

22-27. He is endowed with the beauty of crores of full-moons. He is compassionate towards his devotees. He is spotless, unattached, complete Brahman, omnipresent, remains present in the divine dance, peaceful, Raseshvara, the one who extends welfare to all, abode of welfare, seed of blissfulness, truthful, indestructible, imperishable or eternal, lord of all the achievements, the form of all the achievements, beyond nature, Isvara, formless, form of the great soul, calm and is the refuge of all. The calm-looking Vaishnavas meditate on him. Therefore it is one lord alone who takes to many forms. He witnessed the complete void everywhere at the time of dissolution”.

## Chapter 3

## \|\| 3.1-3 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā śūnyamayaṃ viśvaṃ golokaṃ ca bhayaṅkaram \|*  
*nirjantu nirjalaṃ ghoraṃ nirvātaṃ tamasā vṛtam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*vṛkṣaśailasamudrādivihīnaṃ vikṛtākṛtam \|*  
*nirmuktikaṃ ca nirdhātu niḥsasyaṃ nistṛṇaṃ dvija \|\| 2 \|\|*

*ālocya manasā sarvameka evāsahāyavān \|*  
*svecchayā sraṣṭumārebhe sṛṣṭiṃ svecchāmayaḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. Sauti said: “О Brahmana, the self born lord found the Goloka had been looking horrible, the universe was a great void, devoid of all the creatures and the water, terrible, covered with darkness, deprived of trees, mountains and the oceans; defonned and had been deprived of the dust, metals, greenery and grass. The lord then contemplating in his mind deeply started the creation of his own accord.

## \|\| 3.4-9 \|\|

*āvirbabhūvuḥ sargādau puṃso dakṣiṇapārśvataḥ \|*  
*bhavakāraṇarūpāśca mūrtimantastrayo guṇāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*tato mahānahaṅkāraḥ pañcatanmātra eva ca \|*  
*rūparasagandhasparśaśabdāścaivetisaṃjñakāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*āvirbabhūva paścātsvayaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*śyāmo yuvā pītavāsā vanamālī caturbhujaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*śaṃkhacakragadāpadmadharaḥ smeramukhāmbujaḥ \|*  
*ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣāḍhyaḥ śārṅgī kaustubhabhūṣaṇaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*śrīvatsavakṣāḥ śrīvāsaḥ śrīvidhiḥ śrīvibhāvanaḥ \|*  
*śāradenduprabhāmṛṣṭasukhendusumano haraḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*kāmadevaprabhāmṛṣṭarūpalāvaṇyasundaraḥ \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇapurataḥ sthitvā tuṣṭāva taṃ puṭāñjaliḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

4-9. All the virtues (elements) emerged at the beginning of the universe, from the right side of the great soul. The great tattvas, arrogance Pancatanmatras the form, taste, small, touch and sound then emerged out of him. Thereafter Narayana himself appeared having a dark complexion, clad in a yellow lower garment, having four arms holding conch, dies, club and lotus, wearing a serene smile over the face, adorned with the gem-studded ornaments, carrying the divine Sarnga-bow, wearing the kaustubha gem and the Shrivatsa mark on the chest, beloved of Lakshmi, glorious, adored by Sri, the face having the lustre of the full moon of the winter season, the body having the beauty of the god of love, stood before the Lord Krishna and started eulogising him with both the hands folded.

## \|\| 3.10-13 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*varaṃ vareṇyaṃ varadaṃ varārhaṃ varakāraṇam \|*  
*kāraṇaṃ kāraṇānāṃ ca karma tatkarmakāraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|*

*tapastatphaladaṃ śaśvattapasvīśaṃ ca tāpasam \|*  
*vande navaghanaśyāmaṃ svātmārāmaṃ manoharam \|\| 11 \|\|*

*niṣkāmaṃ kāmarūpaṃ ca kāmaghnaṃ kāmakāraṇam \|*  
*sarvaṃ sarveśvaraṃ sarvaṃ bījarūpamanuttamam \|\| 12 \|\|*

*vedarūpaṃ vedabhavaṃ vedoktaphaladaṃ phalam \|*  
*vedajñaṃ tadvidhānaṃ ca sarvavedavidāṃvaram \|\| 13 \|\|*

10-13. Narayana said: “You are the best of all, adorable, bestower of the boons, the cause of granting the boon, cause of all the causes, form of all the actions, form of tapas, the one who always grants reward of the tapas, tapas and the lord of all the sages, having the dark complexion like the new cloud beautiful, free from desires, form of all the desires, remover of the cowardice, cause of the birth of Kamadeva (the god of love), truthful, lord of all, form of all the seeds, the best of all, the form of the Vedas, the seed of the Vedas, bestower of the result of the reciting of the Vedas, well-versed in the Vedas, well aware of the provisions of the Vedas, besides being the best of all the people well versed in the Vedas; I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 3.14-17 \|\|

*ityuktvā bhaktiyuktaśca sa uvāsa tadājñayā \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsane ramye purataḥ paramātmanaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*nārāyaṇakṛtaṃ stotraṃ yaḥ paṭhetsusamāhitaḥ \|*  
*trisaṃdhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhennityaṃ pāpaṃ tasya na vidyate \|\| 15 \|\|*

*putrārthī labhate putraṃ bhāryyārthī labhate priyām \|*  
*bhraṣṭarājyo labhedrājyaṃ dhanaṃ bhraṣṭadhano labhet \|\| 16 \|\|*

*kārāgāre vipadgrastaḥ stotreṇānena mucyate \|*  
*rogātpramucyate rogī dhruvaṃ śrutvā ca saṃyataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

14-17. Saying this, Lord Narayana, seated himself over the gem-studded lion throne, at the command of Lord Krishna. Whosoever recites this stotra recited by Narayana with a devoted mind in the morning, noon and the evening, thrice a day, he is relieved of all the sins. By reciting this, those desirous of getting a son, get the same; the seekers of a wife get the same, the deposed kings get back the kingdom and whosoever is deprived of the prowers, gets the same. The person who is overpowered with miseries, is relieved of them with the reciting of this prayer. The one who recites this prayer for a year, is relieved of all ailments. Here ends prayer of Krsna, offered by Narayana, in the Brahmavaivarta Purana.

## \|\| 3.18-23 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*āvirbabhūva tatpaścādātmano vāmapārśvataḥ \|*  
*śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaḥ pañcavaktro digambaraḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

*taptakāñcanavarṇābha jaṭābhāradharo varaḥ \|*  
*īṣaddhāsyaprasannāsyastrinetraścandraśekharaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*triśūlapaṭṭiśadharo japamālākaraḥ paraḥ \|*  
*sarvasiddheśvaraḥ siddho yogīndrāṇāṃ gurorguruḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*mṛtyormṛtyurīśvaraśca mṛtyurmṛtyuñjayaḥ śivaḥ \|*  
*jñānānando mahājñānī mahājñānapradaḥ paraḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*pūrṇacandraprabhāmṛṣṭamukhadṛśyo manoharaḥ \|*  
*vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca pravaraḥ prajvalanbrahmatejasā \|\| 22 \|\|*

*śrīkṛṣpurataḥ sthitvā tuṣṭāva taṃ puṭāñjaliḥ \|*  
*pulakāṅkitasarvāṅgaḥ sāśrunetro'tigadgadaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

18-23. Sauti said: “Then from the left of his back side, five-faced Shiva, having the lustre of a crystal, appeared. He was adorned with matted locks of hair having the complexion of heated gold. He wore a serene smile over his face, wore a crescent over his forehead, holding a trident in his hands, served by the siddhas with the garlands of victory, the teacher of the teachers and the yogis, the one who overpowers even death, bestower of welfare, blissful, possessor of great knowledge, the best of all and the bestower of the great knowledge, having the face illumined with the lustre of the full moon, pleasant to look at, the chief of the Vaishnavas and emitted the divine light. He then stood before Lord Krishna with folded hands and started praying. His entire body was filled with emotions; the eyes were filled with tears and his voice was choked.

## \|\| 3.24-26 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*jayasvarūpaṃ jayadaṃ jayeśaṃ jayakāraṇam \|*  
*pravaraṃ jayadānāṃ ca vande tamaparājitam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*viśvaṃ viśveśvareśaṃ ca viśveśaṃ viśvakāraṇam \|*  
*viśvādhāraṃ ca viśvastaṃ viśvakāraṇakāraṇam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*viśvarakṣākāraṇaṃ ca viśvaghnaṃ viśvajaṃ param \|*  
*phalabījaṃ phalādhāraṃ phalaṃ ca tatphalapradam \|*  
*tejaḥsvarūpaṃ tejodaṃ sarvatejasvināṃ varam \|\| 26 \|\|*

24-26. Mahadeva said: “You are the form of victory, bestower of victory, cause of victory, the best of those who bestow the victory. I bow before you, О Lord. The one who is of the universal form, the lord of the universe, the cause of the universe, the support of the universe, pervading of the universe, the cause of the universe, the destroyer of the universe, the best of the seed of the creation of the universe, the cause of the fruit, bestower of the reward, form of the lustre, the granter of the lustre and the best of all those possesing the lustre.

## \|\| 3.27-29 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā taṃ natvā ratnasiṃhāsane vare \|*  
*nārāyaṇaṃ ca saṃbhāṣya sa uvāsa tadājñayā \|\| 27 \|\|*

*iti śambhukṛtaṃ stotraṃ yo janaḥ saṃyataḥ paṭhet \|*  
*sarvasiddhirbhavettasya vijayaṃ ca pade pade \|\| 28 \|\|*

*santataṃ varddhate mitraṃ dhanamaiśvaryyameva ca \|*  
*śatrusainyaṃ kṣayaṃ yāti duḥkhāni duritāni ca \|\| 29 \|\|*

27-29. Saying this, he bowed in reverence and with his permission, he took his seat over the gem-studded in order to converse with Narayana. The one who recites Narayana’s stotra by Shiva, with concentrated mind, he achieves success at every step and all his desires are fulfilled. There is always an increase in his riches and the friends. The forces of his enemies, his miseries and sins get destroyed. Here ends prayer of Shri Krishna, recited by Shiva.

## \|\| 3.30-34 \|\|

*iti brahmavaivarte śambhukṛtaṃ śrīkṛṣṇastotram \|*  
*sautiruvāca \|*  
*āvirvabhūva tatpaścātkṛṣṇasya nābhipaṅkajāt \|*  
*mahātapasvī vṛddhaśca kamaṇḍalukaro varaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*śuklavāsāḥ śukladantaḥ śuklakeśaścaturmukhaḥ \|*  
*yogīśaḥ śilpināmīśaḥ sarveṣāṃ janako guruḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*tapasāṃ phaladātā ca pradātā sarvasampadām \|*  
*sraṣṭā vidhātā karttā ca harttā ca sarvakarmaṇām \|\| 32 \|\|*

*dhātā caturṇāṃ vedānāṃ jñātā vedaprasūpatiḥ \|*  
*śāntaḥ sarasvatīkāntaḥ suśīlaśca kṛpānidhiḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

*śrīkṛṣṇapurataḥ sthitvā tuṣṭāva taṃ puṭāñjaliḥ \|*  
*pulakāṃkitasarvāṃgo bhaktinamrātmakandharaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

30-34. Sauti said: “At that point of time, from the navel of the Lord Krishna, the best and the illustrious, Brahma emerged, holding a kamandalu, seated over the lotus. He had white teeth and bright costumes. He was the best of the yogis, the lord of the artisans, creator of all, the teacher, bestower of the reward for performing the tapas, bestower of all the riches, the creator, destiny, performer of all the actions, remover of all the ills, holder of all, well-versed in all the four Vedas, calm, the lord of Sarasvati, noble and merciful. He stood before Lord Krishna and offered his prayer to him. At that point of time he felt emotional and his neck was bent in devotion before the lord.

## \|\| 3.35-37 \|\|

*. brahmovāca \|*  
*kṛṣṇaṃ vande guṇātītaṃ govindamekamakṣaram \|*  
*avyaktamavyayaṃ vyaktaṃ gopaveṣavidhāyinam \|\| 35 \|\|*

*kiśoravayasaṃ śāntaṃ gopīkāntaṃ manoharam \|*  
*navīnanīradaśyāmaṃ koṭikandarpasundaram \|\| 36 \|\|*

*vṛndāvanavanābhyarṇe rāsamaṇḍalasaṃsthitam \|*  
*rāseśvaraṃ rāsavāsaṃ rāsollāsasamutsukam \|\| 37 \|\|*

35-37. Brahma said: “I bow in reverence to Lord Krishna, who is beyond all the virtues, is known as Govinda alone, is indestructible, eternal, omnipresent, appearing in the form of a cowherd, appearing in tender age, calm, lord of cowherdesses, good looking, beautiful to look at, more beautiful than the lord of love, participating in the celestial dance with the cowherdesses in Braja, Raseshvara, the one who resides with the celestial dance and always eager to enjoy pleasure.

## \|\| 3.38-40 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā taṃ natvā ratnasiṃhāsane varam \|*  
*nārāyaṇeśau saṃbhāṣya sa uvāsa tadājñayā \|\| 38 \|\|*

*iti brahmakṛtaṃ stotraṃ prātarutthāya yaḥ paṭhet \|*  
*pāpāni tasya naśyanti duḥsvapnaḥ susvapno bhavet \|\| 39 \|\|*

*bhaktirbhavati govinde śrīputrapautravarddhinī \|*  
*akīrtiḥ kṣayamāpnoti satkīrttirvarddhate ciram \|*  
*iti brahmavaivartte brahmakṛtaṃ śrīkṛṣṇastotram \|\| 40 \|\|*

38-40. Thus saying, Brahma, bowing before Shri Krishna and with his permission took his seat besides Narayana and Shiva. Whosoever recites the prayer offered by Brahma, all his sins vanish and the bad dreams turn into good dreams and he achieves the devotion of Govinda, which increases the sons and the grand sons. His ill-fame disappears and his fame lasts for long. Here ends prayer offered by Brahma to Krishna in the Brahmavaivarta Purdna.

## \|\| 3.41-44 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*āvirbabhūva tatpaścādvakṣasaḥ paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*sasmitaḥ puruṣaḥ kaścicchuklavarṇo jaṭādharaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*sarvasākṣī ca sarvajñaḥ sarveṣāṃ sarvakarmaṇām \|*  
*samaḥ sarvatra sadayo hiṃsākopavivarjitaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*dharmajñānayuto dharmo dharmiṣṭho dharmado bhavet \|*  
*sa eva dharmiṇāṃ dharmaḥ paramātmā phalodbhavaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*śrīkṛṣṇapurataḥ sthitvā praṇamya daṇḍavadbhuvi \|*  
*tuṣṭāva paramātmānaṃ sarveśaṃ sarvakāmadam \|\| 44 \|\|*

41-44. Sauti said: “Thereafter out of the chest of Krishna, a divine figure of white complexion wearing the locks of hair over his heads. He wore a serene smile over his face. He was witness to all the deeds of all the creatures, was the knower of everything, who always remained unmoved, having good nature, devoid of anger and terror, filled with knowledge and Dharma, quite religious, religion of the religious people and was the great soul and the bestower of the reward. He then stood before Lord Krishna and then prostrated before him and started offering his prayer for him.

## \|\| 3.45-47 \|\|

*śrīdharma uvāca \|*  
*kṛṣṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ vāsudevaṃ paramātmānamīśvaram \|*  
*govindaṃ paramānandamekamakṣaramacyutam \|\| 45 \|\|*

*gopeśvaraṃ ca gopīśaṃ gopaṃ gorakṣakaṃ vibhum \|*  
*gavāmīśaṃ ca goṣṭhasthaṃ govatsapucchadhāriṇam \|\| 46 \|\|*

*gogopagopīmadhyasthaṃ pradhānaṃ puruṣottamam \|\| 47 \|\|*

45-47. Dharma said: “Krishna, Vishnu, Vasudeva, Paramatma, Ishvara, Govinda, extremely blissful, one is imperishable and grants the reward. He is lord of the cowherds, cowherd, protector of the cows, omnipresent, lord cf the cows and is the one who resides in the home of the cow. He bears the tails of the calves and lives amongst the cowherdesses. He is the chief of all, the best of the humans and the one who cannot be killed, sinless, dark complexioned, calm and is pleasant to look at I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 3.48-52 \|\|

*ityuccāryya samuttiṣṭhanratnasiṃhāsane vare \|*  
*brahmaviṣṇumaheśāṃstānsambhāṣya sa uvāsa ha \|\| 48 \|\|*

*caturviśatināmāni dharmavaktrodgatāni ca \|*  
*yaḥ paṭhetprātarutthāya sa sukhī sarvato jayī \|\| 49 \|\|*

*mṛtyukāle harernāma tasya sādhyaṃ labhed dhuvam \|*  
*sa yātyante hareḥ sthānaṃ haridāsyaṃ bhaveddhruvam \|\| 50 \|\|*

*nityaṃ dharmastaṃ ghaṭate nādharme tadratirbhavet \|*  
*caturvarga phalaṃ tasya śaśvatkaragataṃ bhavet \|\| 51 \|\|*

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvapāpāni palāyante bhayena ca \|*  
*bhayāni caiva duḥkhāni vainateyamivoragāḥ \|*  
*iti brahmavaivartte dharmakṛtaṃ śrīkṛṣṇastotram \|\| 52 \|\|*

48-52. Saying this Dharma got up. Thereafter, with the permission of the lord, he took his seat with Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva. Whosoever recites the twenty-four names of the lord spoken by Dharma, early in the morning, he always attains happiness and pleasure besides being victorious. At the end he reaches the abode of the lord and attains his devotion. He always achieves Dharma and is never attracted towards sinful ways. He achieves the four elements of Dharma, Artha, Kama and Moksa. All the sins disappear at his very sight as the snakes disappear at the sight of Garuda. Here ends prayer of Dharma for Krsna, recited in the Brahmavaivarta Purana

## \|\| 3.53-55 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*āvirbabhūva kanyaikā dharmasya vāmapārśvataḥ \|*  
*mūrttirmūrtimatī sākṣāddvitīyā kamalālayā \|\| 53 \|\|*

*āvirbabhūva tatpaścānmukhataḥ paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*ekā devī śuklavarṇā vīṇāpustakadhāriṇī \|\| 54 \|\|*

*koṭipūrṇenduśobhā ḍhyā śaratpaṅkajalocanā \|*  
*vahniśuddhāṃśukādhānā ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitā \|\| 55 \|\|*

53-55. Then Sauti said: “At that point of time a beautiful girl emerged out of the left part of Dharma who resembled completely with Lakshmi. She was known by the name of Murti. Thereafter, a female with white complexion emerged out of the mount of the lord, holding a book and a lute. She had the lustre of crores of suns. Her eyes resembled the lotus flowers of the winter season. She had bright costumes and was adorned with the bright gold ornaments.

## \|\| 3.56-59 \|\|

*sasmitā sudatī śyāmā sundarīṇāṃ ca sundarī \|*  
*śreṣṭhā śrutīnāṃ śāstrāṇāṃ viduṣāṃ jananī parā \|\| 56 \|\|*

*vāgadhiṣṭhātṛdevī sā kavīnāmiṣṭadevatā \|*  
*śuddhasattvasvarūpā ca śāntarūpā sarasvatī \|\| 57 \|\|*

*govindapurataḥ sthitvā jagau prathamataḥ sukham \|*  
*tannāmaguṇakīrtiṃ ca vīṇayā sā nanartta ca \|\| 58 \|\|*

*kṛtāni yāni karmāṇi kalpekalpe yugeyuge \|*  
*tāni sarvāṇi hariṇā tuṣṭāva saṃpuṭāṃjaliḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

56-59. She wore a serene smile on her face and she had beautiful teeth. She was the damsel of sixteen year’s age. She was the best of all the beauties in the universe and all the shrutis, Shastras and the intelligence emerged out of him. She controlled the speech, the goddess, of all the poets, an incarnation of purity and was known as Sarasvati with a peaceful look. Standing before Krishna, she played on Vina, and then danced. She sang in praise of the lord, highlighting all the chief events of his earlier incarnations.

## \|\| 3.60-64 \|\|

*sarasvatyuvāca \|*  
*rāsamaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ rāsollāsasamutsukam \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|\| 60 \|\|*

*rāseśvaraṃ rāsakaraṃ varaṃ rāseśvarīśvaram \|*  
*rāsādhiṣṭhātṛdaivaṃ ca vande rāsavinodinam \|\| 61 \|\|*

*rāsāyāsapariśrāntaṃ rāsarāsavihāriṇam \|*  
*rāsotsukānāṃ gopīnāṃ kāntaṃ śāntaṃ manoharam \|\| 62 \|\|*

*praṇamya ca tamityuktvā prahṛṣṭavadanā satī \|*  
*uvāsa sā sakāmā ca ratnasiṃhāsane vare \|\| 63 \|\|*

*iti vāṇīkṛtaṃ stotraṃ prātarutthāya yaḥ paṭheta \|*  
*buddhimāndhanavānso'pi vidyāvānputravānsadā \|*  
*iti brahmavaivarte sarasvatīkṛtaṃ śrīkṛṣṇastotram \|\| 64 \|\|*

60-64. Sarasvati spoke. “Being present in the divine dance, anxious to enjoy the pleasure of the divine dance, seated over the gem-studded throne, Raseshvara and the performer of the divine dance, the beloved of Radhika, lover of the divine dance and the one who enjoys the divine dance, I bow in reverence to him. The one who gets tired of the divine dance, but is still anxious to perform the divine dance. I bow in reverence to Lord Krishna who is quite pleasant to look at. Thus bowing before the lord, Sarasvati, with a mind filled with pleasure, got seated over a lion throne. The one who recites the stotra of Sarasvati, he would achieve wisdom, treasures, riches, intelligence and progeny. Here ends prayer recited by Sarasvati, in favour of Krishna in the Brahmavaivarta Parana.

## \|\| 3.65-67 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*āvirbabhūva manasaḥ kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*ekā devī gauravarṇā ratnālaṃkārabhūṣitā \|\| 65 \|\|*

*pītavastraparīdhānā sasmitā navayauvanā \|*  
*sarvaiśvaryyādhidevī sā sarvasampatphalapradā \|*  
*svarge ca svargalakṣmīśca rājalakṣmīśca rājasu \|\| 66 \|\|*

*sā hareḥ purataḥ sthitvā paramātmānamīśvaram \|*  
*tuṣṭāva praṇatā sādhvī bhaktinamrātmakandharā \|\| 67 \|\|*

65-67. Sauti said: “A fair complexioned lady also appeared from the mind of Lord Krishna, who was adorned with gem-studded ornaments. She was clad in yellow garments and wore a serene smile on her face. She was the goddess of all the riches and could grant all treasures. She was the gold complexioned Lakshmi and remained as treasure with the kings. She stood before the lord and bowed in reverence to him. She then bowed her back in devotion and offered her prayers to the Lord.

## \|\| 3.68 \|\|

*mahālakṣmīruvāca \|*  
*satyasvarūpaṃ satyeśaṃ satyabījaṃ sanātanam \|*  
*satyādhāraṃ ca satyajñaṃ satyamūlaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Maha-Lakshmi said: “You are the form of truth, its lord, seed of the truth the very foundations of the truth, knower of the truth and you are the cause of the truth. I bow in reverence to you.

## \|\| 3.69-70 \|\|

*ityuktvā śrīhariṃ natvā sā covāsa sukhāsane \|*  
*taptakāṃcanavarṇābhā bhāsayantī diśastviṣā \|\| 69 \|\|*

*āvirbabhūva tatpaścād buddheśca paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*sarvādhiṣṭhātṛdevī sā mūlaprakṛtirīśvarī \|\| 70 \|\|*

69-70. You have the lustre of molten gold and illuminating all the directions with her lustre, the goddess Lakshmi then took her seat, bowing before the lord. Thereafter Prakriti emerged out of the body of the lord who is adored by all the deities.

## \|\| 3.71-76 \|\|

*taptakāñcanavarṇābhā sūryyakoṭisamaprabhā \|*  
*īṣaddhāsyaprasannāsyā śaratpaṅkajalocanā \|\| 71 \|\|*

*raktavastraparīdhānā ratnābharaṇabhūṣitā \|*  
*nidrātṛṣṇā kṣutpipāsā dayā śraddhā kṣamādikāḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

*tāsāṃ ca sarvaśaktīnāmīśādhiṣṭhātṛdevatā \|*  
*bhayaṅkarī śatabhujā durgā durgārtināśinī \|\| 73 \|\|*

*ātmanaḥ śaktirūpā sā jagatāṃ jananī parā \|*  
*triśūlaśaktiśārṅgaṃ ca dhanuḥkhaḍgaśarāṇi ca \|\| 74 \|\|*

*śaṃkha cakragadāpadmamakṣamālāṃ kamaṇḍalum \|*  
*vajramaṅkuśapāśaṃ ca bhuśuṇḍīdaṇḍatomaram \|\| 75 \|\|*

*nārāyaṇāstraṃ brahmāstraṃ raudraṃ pāśupataṃ tathā \|*  
*pārjjanyaṃ vāruṇaṃ vāhnaṃ gāndharvaṃ bibhratī satī \|*  
*kṛṣṇasya purataḥ sthitvā tuṣṭāva taṃ mudā'nvitā \|\| 76 \|\|*

71-76. She having the lustre of the molten gold, was putting to shame the lustre of crores of Suns. He face wore the serene smile on her face. Her eyes resembled the lotus flowers of the winter season. She was clad in red costumes and was adorned with the gem-studded ornaments. All other goddesses like the goddess of sleep, lust, hunger, thirst compassion, faith and forgiveness, owe their origin to her, besides all other shaktis. She is of terrific form with hundreds of arms and is also known as Durga who removes all miseries. She is the strength of the soul and is the mother of the entire universe. She holds a trident, a sword, a bow, arrows, conch, disc, club, lotus, vajra, kamandalu, arikusha, pasa, bhusundl, danda, tomara, narayanastra, brahmastra, rudrastra, parjanyastra, varunastra, agneyastra and gandharvastra are held by her in her hands as her attributes. She, the chaste one, stood before Krishna and started reciting prayers to him.

## \|\| 3.77 \|\|

*prakṛtiruvāca \|*  
*ahaṃ prakṛtirīśānā sarveśā sarvarūpiṇī \|*  
*sarvaśaktisvarūpā ca mayā ca śaktimajjagat \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. Prakriti then spoke: “I am the nature, Isvarl, Sarveshvari, possessing all the forms and omnipotent. The entire universe moves because of me.

## \|\| 3.78 \|\|

*tvayā sṛṣṭā na svatantrā tvameva jagatāṃ patiḥ \|*  
*gatiśca pātā sraṣṭā ca saṃharttā ca punarvidhiḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. You are not the exclusive creator of this universe, but you are its lord, the movement, protector, creator, destroyer and its creator again.

## \|\| 3.79-80 \|\|

*sraṣṭuṃ sraṣṭā ca saṃhartuṃ saṃhartā vedhasā vidhiḥ \|*  
*paramānandarūpaṃ tvāṃ vande cānandapūrvakam \|*  
*cakṣurnimeṣakāle ca brahmaṇaḥ patanaṃ bhavet \|\| 79 \|\|*

*tasya prabhāvamatulaṃ varṇituṃ kaḥ kṣamo vibho \|*  
*bhrūbhaṅgalīlāmātreṇa viṣṇukoṭiṃ sṛjettu yaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

79-80. For the creation, you become the creator, the destroyer for distraction and even Brahma is born out of you. I therefore bow in reverence and pleasure to the blissful one like you. О lord, Brahma fall from the grace with the fist twinkling of an eye. The one who can create crores of Vishnu with the strength of his arms, who would be able to recite the glory of such a great personality like you.

## \|\| 3.81 \|\|

*carācarāṃśca viśveṣu devānbrahmapurogamān \|*  
*madvidhāḥ kati vā devīḥ sraṣṭuṃ śaktaśca līlayā \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. Similarly you are competent enough to create innumerable moveable and immovable creatures, Brahma and other gods, besides the goddesses like me in the universe, quite playfully.

## \|\| 3.82 \|\|

*paripūrṇatamaṃ svīḍyaṃ vande cānandapūrvakam \|*  
*mahānvirāḍ yatkalāṃśo viśvasaṃkhyāśrayo vibho \|*  
*vande cānandapūrvaṃ taṃ paramātmānamīśvaram \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. You have the complete form and I therefore bow in reverence to you. You possess the universal form which is the base of the universe in which resides the god of death. I bow in reverence to the great soul like you.

## \|\| 3.83 \|\|

*yaṃ ca stotumaśaktāśca brahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ \|*  
*vedā ahaṃ ca vāṇī ca vande taṃ prakṛteḥ param \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. The one whose prayers are beyond the recitation of Brahma, Vishnu, Shiva, the Vedas and Sarasvati and the one who is beyond the nature, I adore the same lord with reverence.

## \|\| 3.84 \|\|

*vedāśca viduṣāṃ śreṣṭhāḥ stotuṃ śaktāśca na kṣamāḥ \|*  
*nirlakṣyaṃ kaḥ kṣamaḥ stotuṃ taṃ nirīhaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. Even the best of the well-read persons besides Sarasvati are unable to recite his glory. The one who is aimless and beyond attraction, who could eulogise him. Therefore I bow in reverence to the great lord.

## \|\| 3.85 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā sā durgā ratnasiṃhāsane vare \|*  
*uvāsa natvā śrīkṛṣṇaṃ tuṣṭuvustāṃ sureśvarāḥ \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Thus praying and bowing to Lord Krishna, the goddess Durga took her seat over the lion throne. Thereafter the gods eulogised the goddess Durga.

## \|\| 3.86-87 \|\|

*iti durgākṛtaṃ stotraṃ kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*yaḥ paṭhedarccanākāle sa jayī sarvataḥ sukhī \|\| 86 \|\|*

*durgā tasya gṛhaṃ tyaktvā naiva yāti kadācana \|*  
*bhavābdhau yaśasā bhāti yātyante śrīhareḥ puram \|\| 87 \|\|*

86-87. Thus the one who recites the prayer in the morning, composed by the goddess Durga in favour of Lord Krishna, at the time of adoration, he achieves victory and pleasure everywhere. The goddess Durga never deserts him. He attains glory in the ocean of the universe and ultimately he reaches the abode of Hari.

## Chapter 4

## \|\| 4.1-3 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*āvirbabhūva tatpaścātkṛṣṇasya rasanāgrataḥ \|*  
*śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśā devī caikā manoharā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*śuklavastraparīdhānā sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitā \|*  
*bibhratī japamālāṃ ca sā sāvitrī prakīrtitā \|\| 2 \|\|*

*sā tuṣṭāva puraḥ sthitvā paraṃ brahma sanātanam \|*  
*puṭāṃjaliparā sādhvī bhaktinamrātmakandharā \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. Sauti said: “Thereafter, from the tip of the tongue emerged a beautiful goddess who was spotless like the crystal, pleasant and quite glorious. She was clad in white garments and was holding a garland of victory in her hands. She was known as Savitri. The chaste goddess stood before the lord with folded hands and she started offering her prayers to the lord with her neck cast downwards.

## \|\| 4.4-5 \|\|

*sāvitryuvāca \|*  
*namāmi sarvabījaṃ tvāṃ brahmajyotiḥ sanātanam \|*  
*parātparataraṃ śyāmaṃ nirvikāraṃ nirañjanam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*ityuktvā sasmitā devī ratnasiṃhāsane vare \|*  
*uvāsa śrīhariṃ natvā punareva śrutiprasūḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. Savitri said: “You are the seed of all the creatures and are the flame of the eternal Brahman. I bow in reverence to you. The one who is beyond the reach of everyone, you are dark complexioned and spotless, Brahman. Saying this and smiling, Savitri the mother of the Vedas, bowed before lord Hari, took her seat over the gem-studded lion throne.

## \|\| 4.6-7 \|\|

*āvirbabhūva tatpaścātkṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*mānasācca pumānekastaptakāṃcanasannibhaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*mano mathnāti sarveṣāṃ pañcabāṇena kāminām \|*  
*tannāma manmathaṃ tena pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. Thereafter, out of the mind of Lord Krishna, a male deity having the lustre of molten gold,emerged, who could pierce through the minds of all with his five passionate arrows. That is why the well-read people call him the god of Love or Kamadeva.

## \|\| 4.8-9 \|\|

*tasya puṃso vāmapārśvātkāmasya kāminī varā \|*  
*babhūvātīva lalitā sarveṣāṃ mohakāriṇī \|\| 8 \|\|*

*ratirbabhūva sarveṣāṃ tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sasmitāṃ satīm \|*  
*ratīti tena tannāma pravadaṃti manīṣiṇaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. From the back of the god of love, an extremely beautiful damsel who happened to be a paragon of beauty emerged. She could attract all the people. The mind of all the people felt attracted towards that beautiful damsel. She was therefore given the name of Rati.

## \|\| 4.10-13 \|\|

*hariṃ stutvā tayā sārddhaṃ sa uvāsa hareḥ puraḥ \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsane cāsya pañcabāṇo dhanurddharaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*māraṇaṃ stambhanaṃ caiva jṛmbhaṇaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ tathā \|*  
*unmādanaṃ pañcabāṇanpañcabāṇo bibharti saḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*bāṇāṃścikṣepa sarvāṃśca kāmo bāṇaparīkṣayā \|*  
*sadyaḥ sarve sakāmāśca babhūvurīśvarecchayā \|\| 12 \|\|*

*ratiṃ dṛṣṭvā brahmaṇaśca retaḥ pāto vabhūva ha \|*  
*tatra tasthau mahāyogī vastreṇācchādya lajjayā \|\| 13 \|\|*

10-13. Both of them bowed in reverence to lord Krishna and thereafter they took their seats over the gem-studded thrones. Kamadeva carried a bow of flowers and the arrows which were known as Marana, Stambhana, Jrmbhana, Sosana and Unmadana. In order to test the effectiveness of his arrows, Kamadeva shot all the five arrows at the same time. As a result of this all the people became passionate. Finding Rati there, the semen of Brahma fell but Brahma, the best of the yogis, concealed the same with his costumes out of shame.

## \|\| 4.14-15 \|\|

*vastraṃ dagdhvā samuttasthau jvaladagniḥ sureśvaraḥ \|*  
*koṭitālapramāṇaśca saśikhaśca samujjvalan \|\| 14 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇastadvarddhanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sasarjāpaḥ svalīlayā \|*  
*niḥśvāsavāyunā sārddhaṃ mukhabinduṃ samudgiran \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. Thereafter that costume was flown by the lord of fire with his rising flames like the tall tala trees. Finding the flames of fire rising, Lord Krishna created water with the mesas of his yogic powers. He then started dropping the drops of water with his breathing.

## \|\| 4.16-18 \|\|

*viśvaughaṃ plāvayāmāsa mukhabiṃdujalaṃ dvija \|*  
*tatra kiñcijjalakaṇaṃ vahniṃ śāntaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 16 \|\|*

*tataḥprabhṛti tenāgnistoyānnirvāṇatāṃ vrajet \|*  
*āvirbhūtaḥ pumānekastatastadadhidevatā \|\| 17 \|\|*

*uttasthau tajjalādekaḥ pumānsa varuṇaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*jalādhiṣṭhātṛdevo'sau sarveṣāṃ yādasāmpatiḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

16-18. О Brahmana, with the drops of water emerging from his mouth, the entire universe was submerged in water. A few drops of that water extinguished even the fire. The fire was extinguished with that water ultimately. Thereafter with the touch of the water, the water god appeared in person who was given the name of Varuria. The water thereafter became the abode of all the aquatic animals.

## \|\| 4.19-20 \|\|

*āvirbabhūva kanyaikā tadvahnervāmapārśvataḥ \|*  
*sā svāhā vahnipatnī tāṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*jaleśasya vāmapārśvātkanyā caikā babhūva sā \|*  
*varuṇānīti vikhyātā varuṇasya priyā satī \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. Then a girl was born out of the left side of the god of fire and was known as the wife of the god of fire. She was known by the name of Svaha, out of the left side of Varuna a girl emerged, who was known as Varunam, the spouse of Varuna.

## \|\| 4.21-22 \|\|

*babhūva pavanaḥ śrīmānvibhorniśvāsavāyutaḥ \|*  
*sa ca prāṇāśca sarveṣāṃ niḥśvāsastatphalodbhavaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*tasya vāyorvāmapārśvātkanyā caikā babhūva ha \|*  
*vāyoḥ patnī sā ca devī vāyavī parikīrtitā \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. The wind god was born out of the breathing of the lord who indeed is the life of everyone. The air one inhales while breathing is reflects him indeed. A girl also emerged out of the left side of the wind god known as Vayavl, the spouse of wind.

## \|\| 4.23-25 \|\|

*kṛṣṇasya kāmabāṇena retaḥpāto babhūva ha \|*  
*jale tadrecanaṃ cakre lajjayā sura saṃsadi \|\| 23 \|\|*

*sahasravatsarānte taḍḍimbharūpaṃ babhūva ha \|*  
*tato mahānvirāḍ jajñe viśvaughādhāra eva saḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

*yasyaika lomavivare viśvaikasya vyavasthitiḥ \|*  
*sthūlātsthūlataraḥ so'pi mahānnānyastataḥ paraḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

23-25. Thereafter because of the arrows of love, the semen of Lord Krishna also fell but he placed the same in the water И that assembly. After a thousand years it emerged in the form of an egg. From that egg a gigantic universal form came out, which happens to be the base of the entire Universe. His single hair-pit could accommodate a complete universe. It is harder than the hardest and nothing else is bigger than it.

## \|\| 4.26-27 \|\|

*sa eva ṣoḍaśāṃśo'pi kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*mahāviṣṇuḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sarvādhāraḥ sanātanaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*mahārṇave śayānaḥ sa padmapatraṃ yathā jale \|*  
*babhūvatustau dvau daityau tasya karṇamalodbhavau \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. It happens to be the sixteenth amsha of Lord Krishna and is also known as Maha-Vishnu and who happens to be the base of all. He reclines in the waters of the ocean like the lotus leaf floating over the water surface. From out of way of his ears two demons were born.

## \|\| 4.28-29 \|\|

*tau jalācca samutthāya brahmāṇaṃ hantumudyatau \|*  
*nārāyaṇaśca bhagavāñjaghane tau jaghāna ha \|\| 28 \|\|*

*babhūva medinī kṛtsnā kārtsnyena medasā tayā \|*  
*tatraiva santi viśvāni sā ca devī vasundharā \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. Both the demons then intended to kill Brahma, but Narayana placing them over his thighs and killed them. The earth was born out of their bodies, earning her the title of Medini. She accommodates the entire universe. She is also known by the name of Vasundhara.

## Chapter 5

## \|\| 5.1 \|\|

*. śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*gogopagopyo goloke kiṃ nityāḥ kiṃnu kalpitāḥ \|*  
*mama saṃdehabhedārthaṃ tanme vyākhyātumarhasi \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shaunaka said: “Are the cows and the cowherdesses also available in the Goloka or are they imaginary? Please, tell me to remove my doubts.

## \|\| 5.2-3 \|\|

*. sautiruvāca \|*  
*sarvādisṛṣṭau tāḥ klṛptāḥ pralaye pralaye sthitāḥ \|*  
*sarvādisṛṣṭikathanaṃ yanmayā kathitaṃ dvija \|\| 2 \|\|*

*sarvādisṛṣṭau klṛptau ca nārāyaṇamaheśvarau \|*  
*pralaye pralaye vyaktau sthitau tau prakṛtiśca sā \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. Sauti said: “O Brahmana, the cows, cowherds and the cowherdesses reside in the initial creation, as I have already told you and they merge in the form of Krishna at the time of dissolution. In the beginning of the universe, only Maheshvara and Narayana alone are present. Both of them with the nature remain at the time of dissolution.

## \|\| 5.4-6 \|\|

*sarvādau brahmakalpasya caritaṃ kathitaṃ dvija \|*  
*vārāhapādmakalpau dvau kathayiṣyāmi śroṣyasi \|\| 4 \|\|*

*brāhmavārāhapādmāśca kalpāśca trividhā mune \|*  
*yathāyugāni catvāri krameṇa kathitāni ca \|\| 5 \|\|*

*satyaṃ tretā dvāparaṃ ca kaliśceti caturyugam \|*  
*triśataiśca ṣaṣṭyadhikairyugairdivyaṃ yugaṃ smṛtam \|\| 6 \|\|*

4-6. О Brahmana, I have narrated first of all the characters of the Brahmakalpa. I shall now describe the Varaha-kalpa and Padma-kalpa. You please listen to it. Because of the differentiation of Brahma, Varaha' and Padma there are three types of kalpa. As the four yugas like Satya, Treta, Dvapara and Kali move in a chronological manner, similarly the kalpas too move accordingly. A divine yuga consists of three hundred and sixty yugas.

## \|\| 5.7 \|\|

*manvantaraṃ tu divyānāṃ yugānāmekasaptatiḥ \|*  
*caturdaśeṣu manuṣu gateṣu brahmaṇo dinam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. A manvantara comprises of seventy-one divine yugas. After the passing of fourteen Manus, a day of Brahma is counted.

## \|\| 5.8-9 \|\|

*triśataiśca ṣaṣṭyadhikairdinairvarṣaṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ \|*  
*aṣṭottaraṃ varṣaśataṃ vidherāyurnirūpitam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*etannimeṣakālastu kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*brahmaṇaścāyuṣā kalpaḥ kālavidbhirnirūpitaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. After the expiry of three hundred and sixty days, a year of Brahma is completed. Thus Brahma is believed to live for a hundred and eight years. The same period has been prescribed by Lord Krishna. The people well-versed in the scriptures have prescribed the age of Brahma, equivalent to a kalpa.

## \|\| 5.10 \|\|

*kṣudrakalpā bahutarāste saṃvartādayaḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*saptakalpāṃtajīvī sa mārkaṇḍeyaśca tanmataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. There are several small kalpas known as Samvarta. Markandeya is known to have lived for seven kalpas.

## \|\| 5.11 \|\|

*brahmaṇaśca dinenaiva sa kalpaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|*  
*vidheśca saptadivase munerāyurnirūpitam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. But this kalpa equates with a single day of Brahma. Therefore in the seven days of Brahma, Markandeya completes his age.

## \|\| 5.12 \|\|

*brāhmavārāhapādmāśca trayaḥ kalpā nirūpitāḥ \|*  
*kalpatraye yathā sṛṣṭiḥ kathayāmi niśāmaya \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. There are only three kalpas known as Brahma, Varaha and Padma. Now you listen to the creation of each one of these kalpas.

## \|\| 5.13 \|\|

*brāhme ca medinīṃ sṛṣṭvā sraṣṭā sṛṣṭiṃ cakāra saḥ \|*  
*madhukaiṭabhayoścaiva medasā cājñayā prabhoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. During the Brdhma-kalpa, the earth was created out of the bodies of Madhu and Kaitabha. After the creation of the earth, the creator created the creatures on earth with the permission of the Lord Krishna.

## \|\| 5.14 \|\|

*vārāhe tāṃ samuddhṛtya luptāṃ magnāṃ rasātalāt \|*  
*viṣṇorvarāharūpasya dvārā cātiprayatnataḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. In the Varnh-kalpa, the earth which had been submerged in water after dissolution Lord Vishnu, taking the form of a boar, rescued the earth and the world was inhabited thereafter.

## \|\| 5.15 \|\|

*pādme viṣṇornābhipadme sraṣṭā sṛṣṭiṃ vinirmame \|*  
*trilokīṃ brahmalokāntāṃ nityalokatrayaṃ vinā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Thereafter in the Padma-kalpa the creator, created the universe seated over the lotus emerging from the navel of Lord Vishnu. All the three worlds up to Brahmaloka were created by him and not the three lokas of the upper region.

## \|\| 5.16 \|\|

*etattu kālasaṃkhyānamuktaṃ sṛṣṭinirūpaṇe \|*  
*kiṃcinnirūpaṇaṃ sṛṣṭe kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. I have told this story relating to the creation of the Universe. What else do you want to listen to?

## \|\| 5.17 \|\|

*. śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ kiṃ cakāra bhagavānsātvatāṃpatiḥ \|*  
*etānsṛṣṭvā kiṃ cakāra tanme vyākhyātumarhasi \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Shaunaka said: “After this, what did Krishna do and what other universe did he create? Please enlighten me about the same.

## \|\| 5.18-19 \|\|

*. sautiruvāca \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ tu goloke golokeśo mahānprabhuḥ \|*  
*etānsṛṣṭvā jagāmāsau suramyaṃ rāsamaṇḍalam \|*  
*etaiḥ sametairbhagavānatīva kamanīyakam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*ramyāṇāṃ kalpavṛkṣāṇāṃ madhye'tīva manoharam \|*  
*suvistīrṇaṃ ca susamaṃ susnigdhaṃ maṇḍalīkṛtam \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. Sauti said: “Thereafter, Lord Krishna, the lord of Goloka, went to the place of the divine dance, after completing the above creation. The place of the divine dance was located in the beautiful grove of the kalpavrksas (or wish fulfilling trees). The Rasamandala was arranged in a circular fashion and looked quite charming (from a distance.)

## \|\| 5.20-22 \|\|

*candanāgurukastūrīkuṅkumaiśca susaṃskṛtam \|*  
*dadhilājasaktudhānyadūrvāparṇapariplutam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*paṭṭasūtragranthiyuktaṃ navacandanapallavaiḥ \|*  
*saṃyuktarambhāstambhānāṃ samūhaiḥ pariveṣṭitam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*sadratnasāranirmāṇamaṇḍapānāṃ trikoṭibhiḥ \|*  
*ratnapradīpajvalitaiḥ puṣpadhūpādivāsitaiḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

20-22. It was decorated with the sandal-wood trees, aguru, kasturl and vermilion. The curd, boiled paddy, powdered barley and rice were showered over it. The sandal leaves tied in silken cord were serving as an item of decoration of the place. It was decorated with the trunks of the banana tree. Three crores of mandapas were decorated there with the best of gems illumining the earth. The lamps made of gems were lighted there. The place had the fragrance of flowers and incense.

## \|\| 5.23-24 \|\|

*śṛṃgārārhabhogavastusamūhapariveṣṭitam \|*  
*atīva lalitākalpatalpayuktaiḥ suśobhitam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*tatra gatvā ca taiḥ sārdhaṃ samuvāsa jagatpatiḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā rāsaṃ vismitāste babhūvurmunisattama \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. A large quantity of cosmetics and perfumes was stored therein. Lord Krishna arrived there with all his companions. All of those present there were immensely surprised at the sight of the place.

## \|\| 5.25 \|\|

*āvirbabhūva kanyaikā kṛṣṇasya vāmapārśvataḥ \|*  
*dhāvitvā puṣpamānīya dadāvarghyaṃ prabhoḥ pade \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. At the same time, a girl emerged out of the left side of the Lord Krishna. She rushed at once and brought the flowers and she started offering them at every step of the lord.

## \|\| 5.26 \|\|

*rāse saṃbhūya goloke sā dadhāva hareḥ puraḥ \|*  
*tena rādhā samākhyātā purāvidbhirdvijottama \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О best of the sage, she was given the name of Radha by the intellectual because she was born in the stage of dance and had started running about the lord.

## \|\| 5.27 \|\|

*prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛdevī sā kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūva prāṇebhyaḥ prāṇebhyo'pi garīyasī \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. She became the lady of the life of Krishna and she became dearest to him because she had emerged out of his own body.

## \|\| 5.28 \|\|

*devī ṣoḍaśavarṣīyā navayauvanasaṃyutā \|*  
*vahniśuddhāṃśukādhānā sasmitā sumanoharā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. She was youthful and of sixteen years of age. She was clad in the garment of the complexion of the molten gold. She was quite beautiful and youthful and a smile over her face.

## \|\| 5.29-32 \|\|

*sukomalāṃgī lalitā sundarīṣu ca sundarī \|*  
*bṛhannitambabhārārttā pīnaśroṇipayodharā \|\| 29 \|\|*

*bandhujīvajitā raktasundaroṣṭhā dharāvarā \|*  
*muktāpaṅktijitā cārudantapaṃktirmanoharā \|\| 30 \|\|*

*śaratpārvaṇakoṭīnduśobhāmṛṣṭaśubhānanā \|*  
*cārusīmantinīcāruśaratpaṅkajalocanā \|\| 31 \|\|*

*khagendracañcuvijitacārunāsā manoharā \|*  
*svarṇagaṇḍūkavijite gaṇḍayugme ca bibhratī \|\| 32 \|\|*

29-32. She had extremely tender limbs. She was the best of all the beauties. She had developed breasts and the pelvic region. Her face was like the flower and the lips were red. She had the teeth like the pearls and her face possessed the lustre of crores of moons. Her eyes were beautiful and looked like the lotus of the winter season. Her nose was more beautiful than the beak of Garuda. Both her cheeks were shining like a mirror.

## \|\| 5.33-35 \|\|

*dadhatī cārukarṇe ca ratnābharaṇabhūṣite \|*  
*candanāgurukastūrīyuktakuṃkumabindubhiḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

*sindūrabindusaṃyuktasukapolā manoharā \|*  
*susaṃskṛtaṃ keśapāśaṃ mālatīmālyabhūṣitam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*sugandhakabarībhāraṃ sundaraṃ dadhatī satī \|*  
*sthalapadmaprabhāmuṣṭaṃ pādayugmaṃ ca bibhratī \|\| 35 \|\|*

33-35. She was adorned with all the gem-studded ornaments which decorated her ears. The cosmetics like sandal-paste, aguru, kastml, collyrium etc., were applied on her face which added to her beauty. Her well arranged hair was decorated with the jasmine flowers. The beautiful damsel had arranged her hair in a tuft.

## \|\| 5.36-39 \|\|

*gamanaṃ kurvatī sā ca haṃsakhañjanagañjanam \|*  
*sadratnasāranirmāṇāṃ vanamālī manoharām \|\| 36 \|\|*

*hāraṃ hīrakanirmāṇaṃ ratnakeyūrakaṅkaṇam \|*  
*sadratnasāranirmāṇaṃ pāśakaṃ sumanoharam \|\| 37 \|\|*

*amūlyaratnanirmāṇaṃ kvaṇanmañjīrarañjitam \|*  
*nānāprakāracitrāḍhyaṃ sundaraṃ paribibhratī \|\| 38 \|\|*

*sā ca sambhāṣya govindaṃ ratnasiṃhāsane vare \|*  
*uvāsa sasmitā bhartuḥ paśyantī mukhapaṅkajam \|\| 39 \|\|*

36-39. Her walking was graceful. She wore the garlands of gems, the best of forest flowers, gem-studded armlets and anklets and several other ornaments of gems and precious stones. She talked to Lord Krishna and with his permission looking smilingly at the face of her lord she got herself seated over the gem-studded lion throne.

## \|\| 5.40 \|\|

*tasyāśca lomakūpebhyaḥ sadyo*  
*gopāṅganāgaṇaḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūva rūpeṇa veśenaiva ca tatsamaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. At the same time the cowherdesses emerged from her body, who were as beautiful as she was.

## \|\| 5.41 \|\|

*lakṣakoṭīparimitaḥ śaśvatsusthirayauvanaḥ \|*  
*saṃkhyāvidbhiśca saṃkhyāto goloke gopikāgaṇaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. They were crores in number. They were all youthful. The intellectuals in Goloka have spelt out the number of Gopikas like this.

## \|\| 5.42 \|\|

*kṛṣṇasya lomakūpebhyaḥ sadyo gopagaṇo mune \|*  
*āvirbabhūva rūpeṇa veṣeṇaiva ca tatsamaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. At that very moment, the cowherds also appeared from the body of Lord Krishna, who had a similar beauty and form.

## \|\| 5.43 \|\|

*triṃśatkoṭiparimitaḥ kamanīyo manoharaḥ \|*  
*saṃkhyāvidbhiśca saṃkhyāto ballavānāṃ gaṇaḥ śrutau \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The Shrutis pronounce that the number of the cowherds in Goloka is thirty crores.

## \|\| 5.44 \|\|

*kṛṣṇasya lomakūpebhyaḥ sadyaścāvirbabhūva ha \|*  
*nānāvarṇo gogaṇaśca śaśvatsusthirayauvanaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. At the same time several young cows also emerged out of the body of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 5.45 \|\|

*balīvardāḥ surabhyaśca vatsā nānāvidhāḥ śubhāḥ \|*  
*atīva lalitāḥ śyāmā bahvyo vai kāmadhenavaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Which included the bulls and the cows of high breed of the lineage of Surabhl, besides the calves. Some of them were the Kamadhenu cows.

## \|\| 5.46 \|\|

*teṣāmekaṃ balīvardaṃ koṭisiṃhasamaṃ bale \|*  
*śivāya pradadau kṛṣṇo vāhanāya manoharam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Lord Krishna gave away a beautiful bull which was quite strong resembled to crore of lions to lord Shiva, to serve him as his mount.

## \|\| 5.47-48 \|\|

*kṛṣṇāṃghrinakharandhebhyo haṃsapaṅktirmanoharā \|*  
*āvirbabhūva sahasā strīpuṃvatsasamanvitā \|\| 47 \|\|*

*teṣāmekaṃ rājahaṃsaṃ mahābalaparākramam \|*  
*vāhanāya dadau kṛṣṇo brahmaṇe ca tapasvine \|\| 48 \|\|*

47-48. Thereafter the geese were born out of the feet of lord Krishna which included males and females. Out of those, the one who was the most beautiful was given over by the lord to ascetic Brahma.

## \|\| 5.49-50 \|\|

*vāmakarṇasya vivarātkṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*gaṇaḥ śvetaturaṅgāṇāmāvirbhūto manoharaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*teṣāmekaṃ ca śvetāśvaṃ dharmārthaṃ vāhanāya ca \|*  
*dadau gopāṅganeśaśca saṃprītyā surasaṃsadi \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. The white complexioned horses emerged from the hole of left ear of the lord Krishna. The lord of the cowherdesses, delightfully gave away one of the white horses to Dharma, to serve him as his vehicle in the assembly of gods.

## \|\| 5.51 \|\|

*dakṣakarṇasya vivarātpuṃsaśca surasaṃsadi \|*  
*āvirbhūtā siṃhapaṅktirmahābalaparākramā \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Thereafter in the assembly of gods from the hole of right ear of the lord a group of lions emerged which was very mighty and strong.

## \|\| 5.52 \|\|

*teṣāmekaṃ dadau kṛṣṇaḥ prakṛtyai paramādaram \|*  
*amūlyaratnamālyaṃ ca varaṃ yadabhivāñchitam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. He handed over one of the lions to the goddess Durga, besides a boon and a garland of precious gems, in addition to a boon.

## \|\| 5.53 \|\|

*kṛṣṇo yogena yogīndraścakāra rathapañcakam \|*  
*śuddharatnendranirmāṇaṃ manoyāyi manoharam \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Krishna, the lord of the yogis, created fine divine chariots which were made of pure gems and which moved with the speed of the mind.

## \|\| 5.54-55 \|\|

*lakṣayojanamūrdhve ca prasthe ca śatayojanam \|*  
*lakṣacakraṃ vāyurahaṃ lakṣakrīḍāgṛhānvitam \|\| 54 \|\|*

*śṛṅgārārhaṃ bhogavastutalpāsaṃkhyasamanvitam \|*  
*ratnapradīpalakṣāṇāṃ vājibhiśca virājitam \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. The height of each one of them exceeded a lakh of yojanas and the width was a hundred yojanas. Each chariot had a lakh of wheels which moved with the speed of the wind. Each one of them had a lakh of sport halls, make-up rooms and innumerable materials for consumption and the beds were innumerable. They were adorned with innumerable lamps and the horses.

## \|\| 5.56-58 \|\|

*nānācitravicitrāḍhathaṃ sadratnakalaśojjvalam \|*  
*ratnadarpaṇabhūṣāḍhyaṃ śobhitaṃ śvetacāmaraiḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

*vahniśuddhāṃśukaiścitrairmuktājālairvibhūṣitam \|*  
*maṇīndramuktāmāṇikyahīrahāravirājitam \|\| 57 \|\|*

*āraktavarṇaratnendrasāranirmāṇakṛtrimaiḥ \|*  
*paṅkajānāmasaṃkhyaiśca sundaraiśca suśobhitam \|\| 58 \|\|*

56-58. They contained various types of paintings. They were further adorned with charming kalashas (vases), the mirrors of gems, ornaments and the fly-whisks. Like the gold heated in the fire, costumes, the illuminating astonishingly beautiful garlands of gems, besides the gems, decorated the place.

## \|\| 5.59 \|\|

*dadau nārāyaṇāyaikaṃ teṣāṃ madhye dvijottamaḥ \|*  
*ekaṃ dattvā rādhikāyai rarakṣāśeṣamātmane \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. О best of the Brahmanas, Lord Krishna then handed over a set of gems and ornaments each to lord Narayana and Radhika and the rest were kept by him for himself.

## \|\| 5.60-61 \|\|

*āvirbabhūva kṛṣṇasya guhyadeśāttataḥ param \|*  
*piṅgalaśca pumānekaḥ piṅgalaiśca gaṇaiḥ saha \|\| 60 \|\|*

*āvirbhūtā yato guhyāttena te guhyakāḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*yaḥ pumān sa kuberaśca dhaneśo guhyakeśvaraḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

60-61. Thereafter a Pingala purusa emerged out of the private parts of lord Krishna, together with others of his companions. Because of their birth out of the secret parts of lord Krishna, they were known as Guhyakas. The chief among them was later known as the lord of riches as well as the Guhyakas.

## \|\| 5.62 \|\|

*babhūva kanyakā caikā kubere vāmapārśvataḥ \|*  
*kuberapatnī sā devī sundarīṇāṃ manoramā \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. A girl was born out of the left side of Kubera. That beautiful lady was subsequently known as the wife of Kubera.

## \|\| 5.63-65 \|\|

*bhūtapretapiśācāśca kūṣmāṇḍabrahmarākṣasāḥ \|*  
*vetālā vikṛtāstasyāvirbhūtā guhyadeśataḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

*śaṅkhacakragadāpadmadhāriṇo vanamālinaḥ \|*  
*pītavastraparīdhānāḥ sarve śyāmacaturbhujāḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

*kirīṭinaḥ kuṇḍalino ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitāḥ \|*  
*āvirbhūtāḥ pārṣadāśca kṛṣṇasya mukhato mune \|\| 65 \|\|*

63-65. The goblins, Pishacas, evil spirits, Kflsmandas, Brahma-raksasas and Vetalas also appeared from the secret parts of the lord. О sage, then some attendants emerged out of the mouth of lord Kys na. They all held conch, disc, club and locus; and were clad in the yellow lower garment. They were four armed and were of dark complexion. All of them were adorned with the kirifa crowns, Kundalas and the gem-studded ornaments.

## \|\| 5.66 \|\|

*caturbhujānpārṣadāṃśca dadau nārāyaṇāya ca \|*  
*guhyakānguhyakeśāya bhūtādīñchaṅkarāya ca \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Lord Krishna gave away the four armed attendant to lord Narayana. Similarly the goblins, Pretas, were presented to lord Shiva, while the Guhyakas were given over to Kubera.

## \|\| 5.67-69 \|\|

*dvibhujāḥ śyāmavarṇāśca japamālākarā varāḥ \|*  
*dhyāyantaścaraṇāmbhojaṃ kṛṣṇasya satataṃ mudā \|\| 67 \|\|*

*dāsyo niyuktā dasāścaivārghyamādāya yatnataḥ \|*  
*āvirbhūtā vaiṣṇavāśca sarve kṛṣṇaparāyaṇāḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

*pulakāṅkitasarvāṅgāḥ sāśrunetrāḥ sagadgadā \|*  
*āvirbhūtāḥ pādapadmātpādapadmaikamānasāḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

67-69. Thereafter two armed attendants emerged out of the feet of lord Krishna. They had dark eomplexion and held the garlands of victory in their hands. The best of these attendants always were devoted to the feet of lord Krishna. Shri Krishna entrusted them with the duties of attendants. They emerged for faithfully serving the gods. All of them felt emotional with tears flowing out of their eyes and their speech was choked. Their minds were completely devoted to the auspicious and tender feet of the lord.

## \|\| 5.70-71 \|\|

*āvirbabhūvuḥ kṛṣṇasya dakṣanetrādbhayaṅkarāḥ \|*  
*triśūlapaṭṭiśadharāstrinetrāścandraśekharāḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*digambarā mahākāyā jvaladagniśikhopamāḥ \|*  
*te bhairavā mahābhāgāḥ śivatulyāśca tejasā \|\| 71 \|\|*

70-71. Terrific looking men holding tridents and patt isa also emerged out of the right eye of Lord Krishna. All of them possessed three eyes each and were adorned with crescent type of crowns over their heads. They were all of gigantic size and had no costumes on their bodies and illumined the place like dazzling fire flames. They were called Mahakala Bhairavas and resembled Shiva in glory.

## \|\| 5.72 \|\|

*rurusaṃhārakālākhyā asitakrodhabhīṣaṇāḥ \|*  
*mahābhairavakhaṭvāṅgāvityaṣṭau bhairavāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Rudra, the god of death, destruction, time, anger, terror, Mahabhairava and Khatvanga are known as the eight Bhairavas.

## \|\| 5.73-74 \|\|

*āvirbabhūva kṛṣṇasya vāmanetrādbhayaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*triśūlapaṭṭiśavyāghracarmāmbaragadādharaḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

*digambaro mahākāyastriṇetraścandraśekharaḥ \|*  
*sa īśāno mahābhāgo dikpālānāmadhīśvaraḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

73-74. A terrific man was born out of the left eye of lord Krishna. He held a trident, pattisa and a club. He was clad in tiger’s skin. He was Digambara and Mahakaya and possessed three eyes. His head was adorned with the crescent crown and was known as Ishana. He happened to be the leader of the Dikpalas.

## \|\| 5.75 \|\|

*ḍākinyaścaiva yoginyaḥ kṣetrapālāḥ sahasraśaḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūvuḥ kṛṣṇasya nāsikāvivarodarāt \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. Thereafter Dakinis and Yoginis emerged out of the nostrils of lord Krishna, besides hundreds of Ksetrapalas.

## \|\| 5.76 \|\|

*surāstrikoṭisaṃkhyātā divyamūrtidharāvarāḥ \|*  
*āvirbarbhūvuḥ sahasā puṃso vai pṛṣṭhadeśataḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Then three crores of gods emerged out of his back side having the best and the divine appearance.

## Chapter 6

## \|\| 6.1-4 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*atha kṛṣṇo mahālakṣmīṃ sādaraṃ ca sarasvatīm \|*  
*nārāyaṇāya pradadau ratnendraṃ mālayā saha \|\| 1 \|\|*

*sāvitrīṃ brahmaṇe prādānmūrtiṃ dharmāya sādaram \|*  
*ratiṃ kāmāya rūpāḍhyāṃ kuberāya manoramām \|\| 2 \|\|*

*anyāśca yā yā anyabhyo yāśca yebhyaḥ samudbhavāḥ \|*  
*tasmai tasmai dadau kṛṣṇastāṃ tāṃ rūpavatīṃ satīm \|\| 3 \|\|*

*tataḥ śaṃkaramāhūya sarveśo yogināṃ gurum \|*  
*uvāca priyamityevaṃ gṛhṇīyāḥ siṃhavāhinīm \|\| 4 \|\|*

1-4. Sauti said, “Thereafter lord Krishna handed over to Narayana, Lakshmi and Sarasvati, quite gracefully, adorned with all the ornaments besides the precious garlands of gems and pearls. Similarly he handed over Savitri to Brahma, Mtirti to Dharma, Rati to Kamadeva and the Beautiful Manorama to Kubera. Thereafter lord Krishna handed over the wives of all the gods. The one who emerged out of the body of a god was given over to the same god. Thereafter lord Krishna summoned lord Shiva, the best of the yogis and said to him, “you better accept the goddess who rides on the lion.

## \|\| 6.5 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇasya vacaḥ śrutvā prahasannīlalohitaḥ \|*  
*uvāca bhītaḥ praṇataḥ prāṇeśaṃ prabhumacyutam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Shiva smiled on hearing the words of lord Krishna and he meekly spoke to lord Krishna.

## \|\| 6.6-7 \|\|

*śrīmaheśvara uvāca \|*  
*adhunāhaṃ na gṛhṇāmi prakṛtiṃ prākṛto yathā \|*  
*tvadbhaktyaikavyavahitāṃ dāsyamārgavirodhinīm \|\| 6 \|\|*

*tattvajñānasamācchannāṃ yogadvārakapāṭikām \|*  
*muktīcchādhvaṃsarūpāṃ ca sakāmāṃ kāmavarddhinīm \|\| 7 \|\|*
