6-7. Lord Shiva said, “Like an ordinary man I am unable to accept (the goddess of) nature, because she is going to detract my mind from your devotion and would obstruct the path of serving you. She can overshadow the true knowledge; she is the door of the yogic practices, can suppress the desire for salvation. She is passionate and could increase passion in a person.

## \|\| 6.8-9 \|\|

*tapasyācchannarūpāṃ ca mahāmohakaraṇḍikām \|*  
*bhavakārāgṛhe ghore dṛḍhāṃ nigaḍarūpiṇīm \|\| 8 \|\|*

*śaśvadvibuddhijananīṃ sadbuddhicchedakāriṇīm \|*  
*śaśvadvibhogasārāṃ ca viṣayecchāvivarddhinīm \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. She can destroy the tapas, store of illusion; she is the strong base of the terrific worldly bondages; all leads to the evil ways, destroy of the noble ideas, always deprived of the noble path.

## \|\| 6.10 \|\|

*necchāmi gṛhiṇīṃ nātha varaṃ dehi madīpsitam \|*  
*yasya yadvāñchitaṃ tasmai taddadāti sadīśvaraḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Because of this I need no spouse. I am desirous of seeking a boon as it suits my mind. You may kindly grant it, because the lord always grants the wishes of all.

## \|\| 6.11 \|\|

*tvadbhaktiviṣaye dāsye lālasā varddhate'niśam \|*  
*tṛptirna jāyate nāma japane pādasevane \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Му devotion for you is always on the increase. I do not feel satisfied by serving you reciting the name of Rama.

## \|\| 6.12 \|\|

*tvannāmapañcavaktreṇa guṇaṃ sanmaṅgalālayam \|*  
*svapne jāgaraṇe śaśvadgāyangāyan bhramāmyaham \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. While awake or asleep, I intend to recite your name with all my five mouths and wander in the universe.

## \|\| 6.13 \|\|

*ākalpakoṭikoṭiṃ ca tadrūpadhyānatatparam \|*  
*bhogecchāviṣayenaiva yoge tapasi manmanaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. For crores of kalpas I meditate on you. I was therefore never attracted towards passion.

## \|\| 6.14-16 \|\|

*tvatsevane pūjane ca vandane nāma kīrtane \|*  
*sadollasitameṣāṃ ca viratau viratiṃ labhet \|\| 14 \|\|*

*smaraṇaṃ kīrttanaṃ nāma guṇayoḥ śravaṇaṃ japaḥ \|*  
*tvaccārurūpadhyānaṃ tvatpādasevābhivandanam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*samarpaṇaṃ cātmanaśca nityaṃ naivedyabhojanam \|*  
*varaṃ vareśa dehīdaṃ navadhā bhaktilakṣaṇam \|\| 16 \|\|*

14-16. By serving you, adoring you, reciting your name, I derive the divine pleasure. A short detraction from your meditation unnerves me. О Lord of the boons, in order to recite your qualities and your name, japam, klrtanam and to get engrossed in your beautiful form, to serve at your feet, bowing in reverence at your feet and to eat after offering you first, are the nine types of Bhakti (devotion) which may be granted to me, ”

## \|\| 6.17 \|\|

*sārṣṭisālokyasārūpyasāmīpyaṃ sāmyalīnatām \|*  
*vadanti ṣaḍvidhāṃ muktiṃ muktāmuktivido vibho \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. О Brahmanas, those who are well aware of salvation and hell, to stay in the world, to achieve a stage equivalent to the lord and to get absorbed in him, are known as the six types of salvation.

## \|\| 6.18-24 \|\|

*aṇimālaghimāprāptiḥ prākāmyaṃ mahimā tathā \|*  
*īśitvaṃ ca vaśitvaṃ ca sarvakāmāvasāyitā \|\| 18 \|\|*

*sarvajña dūraśravaṇaṃ parakāyapraveśanam \|*  
*vāksiddhiḥ kalpavṛkṣatvaṃ sraṣṭuṃ saṃhartumīśatā \|\| 19 \|\|*

*amaratvaṃ ca sarvāgryaṃ siddhayo'ṣṭādaśa smṛtāḥ \|*  
*yogāstapāṃsi sarvāṇi dānāni ca vratāni ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

*yaśaḥkīrtirvacaḥ satyaṃ dharmāṇyanaśanāni ca \|*  
*bhramaṇaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu snānamanyasurārcanam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*surārcā darśanaṃ saptadvīpasaptapradakṣiṇam \|*  
*snānaṃ sarvasamudreṣu sarvasvargapradarśanam \|\| 22 \|\|*

*brahmatvaṃ caiva rudratvaṃ viṣṇutvaṃ ca paraṃ padam \|*  
*ato'nirvacanīyāni vāñchanīyāni santi vā \|\| 23 \|\|*

*sarvāṇyetāni sarveśa kathitāni ca yāni ca \|*  
*tava bhaktikalāṃśasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 24 \|\|*

18-24. То achieve the tiny or small fonns, to attain something, failure to express the desires, glory, attain godhood, overpower, to become free from all the desires, all knowledgeable, to be capable of listening to the words spoken at a long distance, vak-siddhi, getting all the desire fulfilled, capability of creation and destruction, to be eternal, to become the best or the foremost of all are known as the eighteen types of achievements. The yoga, tapas and all types of charities, vows, glory, truthfulness, fasting, visiting all the holy places and having a bath there, adoration at other sacred place, worship of Devas, cirucumambulation of the seven continents seven times, to have a bath at all the oceans, to visit all the heavens, the Brahmapada, Rudrapada, Vishnupada and the Supremepada and all other things which are beyond the human thought do not equate with even the smallest particle of your glory.

## \|\| 6.25 \|\|

*śarvasya vacanaṃ śrutvā kṛṣṇastaṃ yogināṃ gurum \|*  
*prahasyovāca vacanaṃ satyaṃ sarvasukhapradam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. On listening the words of the teacher of the yogis, lord Krsna smiled and pleasantly spoke the sweet words.

## \|\| 6.26-27 \|\|

*. śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*matsevāṃ kuru sarveśa śarvasarvavidāṃvara \|*  
*kalpakoṭiśataṃ yāvatpūrṇaṃ śaśvadaharniśam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*varastapasvināṃ tvaṃ ca siddhānāṃ yogināṃ tathā \|*  
*jñānināṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca surāṇāṃ ca sureśvara \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Lord Krishna Said, “O Shiva, the best of the intellectuals, you serve me regularly during day and night, for a hundred crores of kalpas. О lord of the gods, you are the best of the ascetics, siddhas, yogis, intellects, Vaishnavas and the gods.

## \|\| 6.28-30 \|\|

*amaratvaṃ labha bhava bhava mṛtyuṃjayo mahān \|*  
*sarvasiddhiṃ ca vedāṃśca sarvajñatvaṃ ca madvarāt \|\| 28 \|\|*

*asaṃkhyabrahmaṇāṃ pātaṃ līlayā vatsa paśyasi \|*  
*adyaprabhṛti jñānena tejasā vayasā śiva \|\| 29 \|\|*

*parā krameṇa yaśasā mahasā matsamo bhava \|*  
*prāṇānāmapadhikastvañca na bhaktastvatparo mama \|\| 30 \|\|*

28-30. Overcoming death you became eternal. Therefore with my boon you achieve all the siddhis, knowledge of the four Vedas and the knowledge of all the four worlds, with the boon granted by one you would be able to witness the fall of innumerable Brahmas. О Shiva, from today itself, you shall possess the intellect, glory, age, prowess, valour and strength like me, because you are dearer to me than my life even and there is no other person more devoted to me than you.

## \|\| 6.31-32 \|\|

*tvatparo nāsti me preyāṃstvaṃ madīyātmanaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*ye tvāṃ nindanti pāpiṣṭhā jñānahīnā vicetanāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*pacyante kālasūtreṇa yāvaccandradivākarau \|*  
*kalpakoṭiśatānte ca grahīṣyasi śivāṃ śiva \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. You are greater than my soul even. No one else is dearer to me than you. The sinners, foolish and un-enlightened people who denounce you, they suffer in terrific hell till the sun and the moon last. О Shiva, you will achieve Prakriti after the lapse of a hundred crores of years..

## \|\| 6.33-35 \|\|

*mamāvyarthaṃ ca vacanaṃ pālanaṃ kartumarhasi \|*  
*tvanmukhānnirgataṃ vākyaṃ na karomyadhuneti ca \|\| 33 \|\|*

*madvākyaṃ ca svavākyaṃ ca pālanaṃ tatkariṣyasi \|*  
*gṛhītvā prakṛtiṃ śambho divyaṃ varṣasahasrakam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*sukhaṃ mahacca śṛṅgāraṃ kariṣyasi na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*na kevalaṃ tapasvī tvamīśvaro matsamo mahān \|\| 35 \|\|*

33-35. You therefore comply with my truthful words. I shall not agree to your present request. О Shiva, you will obey my words and you will perform according to your words when you accept Prakfti as your spouse and enjoy the love sports and the worldly pleasures with her for a divine thousand years. There is no doubt about the same. You are not a recluse alone, but you are an Ishvara as well like me.

## \|\| 6.36-41 \|\|

*kāle gṛhī tapasvī ca yogī svecchāmayo hi yaḥ \|*  
*duḥkhaṃ ca dārasaṃyoge yattvayā kathitaṃ śiva \|\| 36 \|\|*

*kustrī dadāti duḥkhaṃ ca svāmine na pativratā \|*  
*kule mahati yā jātā kulajā kulapālikā \|\| 37 \|\|*

*karoti pālanaṃ snehātsatputrasya samaṃ patim \|*  
*patirbandhuḥ patirbharttā daivataṃ kulayoṣitām \|\| 38 \|\|*

*patito'patito vāpi kṛpaṇaśceśvaro'thavā \|*  
*asatkulaprasūtāyāḥ pitrorduśśīlamiśritāḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

*dhruvaṃ tāḥ parabhogyāśca patiṃ nindanti santatam \|*  
*āvayoratiriktaṃ ca yā paśyati patiṃ satī \|\| 40 \|\|*

*goloke svāminā sārddhaṃ koṭikalpaṃ pramodate \|*  
*bhavitā sā śivā śaivī prakṛtivaiṣṇavī śiva \|\| 41 \|\|*

36-41. The self born Isvara has to perform at times as householder, a tapas and a yogi. The mental agony you have stated in relation to dwelling with a wife one gets only in company of the degraded ladies and not the chaste one. The one who is born in a well-established family, has a spotless parentage and the one who abides by the family norms, such a lady serves her husband like a good son. Because for the ladies born in the high families, the husband is the closest relative, the lord and the god even though he might be the degraded one, poor and the one devoid of all the riches. While the ladies born in the lower class whose parents are not good nature, they become immoral indeed. Such women always denounce their husbands. The chaste lady who considers her husband even greater than both of us, she enjoys the company of her husband for crores of kalpas in the Goloka, О Shiva, the Vaishnavl sakti shall bestow welfare on you while remaining in your company.

## \|\| 6.42-47 \|\|

*madājñayā ca tāṃ sādhvīṃ grahīṣyasi bhavāya ca \|*  
*prakṛtyā yonisaṃyuktaṃ tvalliṅgaṃ tīrthamṛtkṛtam \|\| 42 \|\|*

*tīrthe sahasraṃ saṃpūjya bhaktyā pañcopacārataḥ \|*  
*sadakṣiṇaṃ saṃyato yaḥ pavitraśca jitendriyaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*koṭikalpaṃ ca goloke modate ca mayā saha \|*  
*lakṣaṃ tīrthe pūjayedyo vidhivatsādhudakṣiṇam \|\| 44 \|\|*

*na cyutistasya golokātsa bhavedāvayoḥ samaḥ \|*  
*mṛdbhasmagośakṛtpiṇḍaistīrthe vālukayā'pi vā \|\| 45 \|\|*

*kṛtvā liṅgaṃ sakṛtpūjya vasetkalpāyutaṃ divi \|*  
*prajāvānbhūmimānvidvānputravāndhanavāṃstathā \|\| 46 \|\|*

*jñānavānmuktimānsādhuḥ śivaliṅgārcanādbhavet \|*  
*śivaliṅgārcanaṃ sthānaṃ mattīrthaṃ tīrthameva tat \|*  
*bhavettatra mṛtaḥ pāpī śivalokaṃ sa gacchati \|\| 47 \|\|*

42-47. With my command you accept that chaste lady as your wife. Those who shall make the linga and yoni\_from the earth of the holy places and worship the same, such people who have controlled all their senses, adore them in the prescribed style of Pancopacara, he resides with me in the Goloka for a crore of years. He never falls back from the Goloka and he ultimately becomes like us. Thus by making the linga from day, dust and ashes, collected from the holy places, adores it, dwells in the heaven for ten thousand kalpas. By adoring Shivalinga the noble people achieve the people, land, knowledge, sons, riches divine knowledge and salvation with the adoration of Shivalinga even an unholy place gets sanctified and even when a sinner dies at that place, he is carried to the place of Shiva.

## \|\| 6.48 \|\|

*mahādeva mahādeva mahādevetivādinaḥ \|*  
*paścādyāmi mahātrasto nāmaśravaṇalobhataḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. By repeating the name of Mahadeva thrice or the one who recites his name, all his misfortunes vanish.

## \|\| 6.49 \|\|

*śiveti śabdamuccāryya prāṇāṃstyajati yo naraḥ \|*  
*koṭijanmārjitātpāpānmukto muktiṃ prayāti saḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The one who breathes our repeating the name of Shiva, he is relieved of all the sins accumulated for the last several births and achieves salvation.

## \|\| 6.50 \|\|

*śivakalyāṇavacanaṃ kalyāṇaṃ muktivācakam \|*  
*yatastatprabhavettena sa śivaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The word Shiva devotes welfare and welfare leads to salvation. By reciting the name of Shiva, one achieves welfare or salvation. This is the reason why Mahadeva has been given the name of Shiva.

## \|\| 6.51 \|\|

*vicchede dhanabandhūnāṃ nimagnaḥ śokasāgare \|*  
*śiveti śabdamuccāryya labhetsarvaśivaṃ naraḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. At the lord of the treasure and the close relatives, if the grief stricken recites the name of Shiva he achieves welfare.

## \|\| 6.52-53 \|\|

*pāpaghne vartate śiśca vaśca muktiprade tathā \|*  
*pāpaghno mokṣado nṝṇāṃ śivastena prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

*śiveti ca śivaṃ nāma yasya vāci pravarttate \|*  
*koṭijanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ tasya naśyati niścitam \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. The word Shiva consists of two letters which means the destroyer of sins and which means the one who bestows salvation. This is the reason why Shiva is called the bestower of welfare and salvation. Those who always recite the name of Shiva, are deprived of all the sins earned by them in the crores of births.

## \|\| 6.54 \|\|

*ityuktvā śūline kṛṣṇo dattvā kalpataruṃ manum \|*  
*tattvajñānaṃ mṛtyujayamavocatsiṃhavāhinīm \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Lord Krishna, thus speaking to Shiva, the holder of Sula, bestowed on him the hymn of Mrtyunjaya (The one that overpowers death) which was like the wish fulfilling tree. He then spoke to Prakriti, Durga who rides the lion.

## \|\| 6.55 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*adhunā tiṣṭha vatse tvaṃ goloke mama sannidhau \|*  
*kāle bhajiṣyasi śivaṃ śivadaṃ ca śivāyanam \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Lord Krishna, said “O Damsel, you remain here with me for the present in the Goloka. After sometime you will serve lord Shiva who bestows welfare on all.

## \|\| 6.56 \|\|

*tejaḥsu sarvadevānāmāvirbhūya varānane \|*  
*saṃhṛtya daityānsarvāṃśca bhavitā sarvapūjitā \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. You would appear from the tejas of all the gods, you shall kill the demons and shall then be adored by the gods.

## \|\| 6.57-58 \|\|

*tataḥ kalpaviśeṣe ca satyaṃ satyayuge sati \|*  
*bhavitā dakṣakanyā tvaṃ suśīlā śambhugehinī \|\| 57 \|\|*

*tataḥ śarīraṃ saṃtyajya yajñe bhartuśca nindayā \|*  
*menāyāṃ śailabhāryyāyāṃ bhavitā pārvatīti ca \|\| 58 \|\|*

57-58. Thereafter in Satyayuga in a particular kalpa you shall be born as the daughter of Yaksa and then shall become the spouse of Shiva. Thereafter you will end your life in the yajna of Daksha in protest against his denouncing Shiva and shall be reborn as Parvati from the womb of Mena, the wife of Himalaya.

## \|\| 6.59 \|\|

*divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ ca vihariṣyasi śambhunā \|*  
*pūrṇaṃ tataḥ sarvakālamamedaṃ tvaṃ labhiṣyasi \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. You shall enjoy the company of Shiva for a thousand years. Thereafter, you shall always be identified with your husband Shiva.

## \|\| 6.60-61 \|\|

*kāle sarveṣu viśveṣu mahāpūjāsu pūjite \|*  
*bhavitā prativarṣe ca śāradī yā sureśvari \|\| 60 \|\|*

*grāmeṣu nagareṣveva pūjitā grāmadevatā \|*  
*bhavatī bhavitetyevaṃ nāmabhedena cāruṇā \|\| 61 \|\|*

60-61. О Goddess of all the gods, every year you will be adofed at appropriate times by the people in the winter season. You will be adored in all the villages and towns and you will be known by different names at different places.

## \|\| 6.62-63 \|\|

*madājñayā śivakṛtaistantrairnānāvidhairapi \|*  
*pūjāvidhiṃ vidhāsyāmi kavacaṃ stotrasaṃyutam \|\| 62 \|\|*

*bhaviṣyanti mahāntaśca tavaiva paricārakāḥ \|*  
*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ siddhāśca phalabhāginaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. At my command, you will be adored with the prayers and Tantras composed by Shiva. I shall arrange for stotras and the kavachas for your sake, by which your devotees shall achieve great glory besides achieving dharma, artha and moksa.

## \|\| 6.64-65 \|\|

*ye tvāṃ mātarbhajiṣyanti puṇyakṣetre ca bhārate \|*  
*teṣāṃ yaśaśca kīrtiśca dharmaiśvaryyaṃ ca varddhate \|\| 64 \|\|*

*ityuktvā prakṛtiṃ tasyai mantramekādaśākṣaram \|*  
*dattvā sakāmabījaṃ ca mantrarājamanuttamam \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. О Mother, those of the auspicious people who will adore you in the sacred land of Bharata vars a, their glory, popularity, devotion and riches shall go on increasing. Saying this lord Krishna, presented her and bestowed on her eleven mantras together with kdmablja. Each one of this mantra is the chief of the mantras.

## \|\| 6.66-67 \|\|

*cakāra vidhinā dhyānaṃ bhaktaṃ bhaktānukampayā \|*  
*śrīmāyākāmabījāḍhyaṃ dadau mantraṃ daśākṣaram \|\| 66 \|\|*

*sṛṣṭyaupayaugikīṃ śaktiṃ sarvasiddhiṃ ca kāmadām \|*  
*tadviśiṣṭotkṛṣṭatattvaṃ jñānaṃ tasyai dadau vibhuḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

66-67. The lord then advised her of meditating in a formal way and thereafter the-ten-letter mantra (or Dasaksara) was projected before her. Side by side the things useful on earth like the Prowess, all the siddhis, which fulfil all the desires, the best of the knowledge of the elements, were also bestowed on her.

## \|\| 6.68 \|\|

*trayodaśākṣaraṃ mantraṃ dattvā tasmai jagatpatiḥ \|*  
*kavacaṃ stotrasahitaṃ śaṅkarāya tathā dvija \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. О Brahmanas, thus lord Krishna, gave away to Shiva the Trayodasaksara mantra and also the kavachas with the stotras.

## \|\| 6.69-70 \|\|

*dattvā dharmāya taṃ mantraṃ siddhijñānaṃ tadeva ca \|*  
*kāmāya vahnaye caiva kuberāya ca vāyave \|\| 69 \|\|*

*evaṃ kuberādibhyastu dattvā mantrādikaṃ param \|*  
*vidhiṃ provāca sṛṣṭyarthaṃ vidhāturvidhireva saḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

69-70. Then the knowledge of accomplishment (,siddhi-jnanam), the best of the knowledge of Tantras and mantras were also given away to Kamadeva, Agni, Kubera and the wind-god. Thereafter mantras were also given away to Kubera and others. Then lord Krishna, who happened to be the creator of the creator, commanded Brahma the creator, for the creation of the universe.

## \|\| 6.71-72 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*madīyaṃ ca tapaḥ kṛtvā divyaṃ varṣasahasrakam \|*  
*sṛṣṭiṃ kuru mahābhāga vidhe nānāvidhāṃ parām \|\| 71 \|\|*

*ityuktvā brahmaṇe kṛṣṇo dadau mālāṃ manoramām \|*  
*jagāma sārddhaṃ gopībhirgopairvṛndāvanaṃ vanam \|\| 72 \|\|*

71-72. Lord Krishna said, “O Lord of destiny, by performing tapas for me for several years, you create various types of creature in the universe”. Saying this Shri Krishna handed over a divine garland of gems to Brahma, lord Krishna then left the place accompanied by the cowherds and cowherdesses under the shade of the sandal tree.

## Chapter 7

## \|\| 7.1-5 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*tadā brahmā tapaḥ kṛtvā siddhiṃ prāpya yathepsitām \|*  
*sasṛje pṛthivīmādau madhukaiṭabhamedasā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*sasṛje parvatānaṣṭau pradhānānsumanoharān \|*  
*kṣudrānasaṃkhyānkiṃ brūmaḥ pradhānākhyāṃ niśāmaya \|\| 2 \|\|*

*sumeruṃ caiva kailāsaṃ malayaṃ ca himālayam \|*  
*udayaṃ ca tathā'staṃ ca suvelaṃ gandhamādanam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*samudrānsasṛje sapta nadānkatividhā nadīḥ \|*  
*vṛkṣāṃśca grāmanagaraṃ samudrākhyā niśāmaya \|\| 4 \|\|*

*lavaṇekṣusurāsarpirdadhidugdhajalārṇavān \|*  
*lakṣayojanamānena dviguṇāṃśca parātparān \|\| 5 \|\|*

1-5. Brahma then performed tapas with the concentration of his mind and achieved the desired goal. First of all the earth was created with the feet of Madhu and Kaitabha; them the eight high mountains together with many small hills attached to them were created. What could be said about their names? Listen to the names of the chief mountains, which include Sumeru, Kailasa, Malaya, Himalaya, Udayacala, Astacala, Suvela and Gandhamadana, which are known as the eight chief mountains. Brahma then created seven oceans, several rivers, rivulets, streams, trees, villages and cities. The oceans included of saltish water, sugarcane, wine, ghee, curd, milk and pure water. The first of them was spread in an area of a lakh of yojanas. The rest of them become double in size respectively.

## \|\| 7.6-7 \|\|

*saptadvīpāṃśca tadbhūmimaṇḍale kamalākṛte \|*  
*upadvīpāṃstathā saptasīmāśailāṃśca sapta ca \|\| 6 \|\|*

*nibodha vipra dvīpākhyāṃ purā yā vidhinā kṛtā \|*  
*jambūśākakuśaplakṣakrauñcanyagrodhapauṣkarān \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. There are seven continents (islands) which are surrounded by these oceans. Their lands resemble those of the lotus leaves. The sub-continents and the respective mountains too are also seven in number. О Brahmana, I now speak out the name of these islands which are JambQ, Saka, Icusa, Plaksa, Kraunca, Nyagrodha and Pus kara.

## \|\| 7.8 \|\|

*meroraṣṭasu śṛṅgeṣu sasṛje'ṣṭau purī prabhuḥ \|*  
*aṣṭānāṃ lokapālānāṃ vihārāya manoharāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Thereafter Brahma built up eight cities over the peaks of the Meru mountain for the sport of Dikpalas (which are eight in number).

## \|\| 7.9 \|\|

*mūle'nantasya nagarīṃ nirmāya jagatāṃ patiḥ \|*  
*ūrdhve svargāṃśca saptaiva teṣāmākhyā niśāmaya \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The lord of the universe created the abode of Shesha and created seven islands in the nether world.

## \|\| 7.10-12 \|\|

*bhūrlokaṃ ca bhuvarlokaṃ svarlokaṃ sumanoharam \|*  
*janolokaṃ tapolokaṃ satyalokaṃ ca śaunaka \|\| 10 \|\|*

*śṛṅgamūrdhni brahmalokaṃ jarādiparivarjitam \|*  
*tadūrdhve dhruvalokaṃ ca sarvataḥ sumanoharam \|\| 11 \|\|*

*tadadhaḥ sapta pātālānnirmame jagadīśvaraḥ \|*  
*svargātiriktabhogāḍhyānadhodhaḥ kramato mune \|\| 12 \|\|*

10-12. These were named as Bhti-loka, Bhuvar-loka,1 the most pleasing heaven, Janaloka, Tapaloka and Satyaloka. Thereafter Brahma created Brahmaloka a top the Meru mountain, which was free from death and old age. Above that the charming Dhruvaloka was built. Down below seven Patala lokas were created for the lord of the universe. It was more equipped with items of cosmetics and pleasure as compared to the heaven.

## \|\| 7.13 \|\|

*atalaṃ vitalaṃ caiva sutalaṃ ca talātalam \|*  
*mahātalaṃ ca pātālaṃ rasātalamadhastataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. There names are given as under-Atala, Vitala, Sutala, Talatala, Mahatala, Patala and Rasatala.

## \|\| 7.14-15 \|\|

*saptadvīpaiḥ saptanākaiḥ saptapātālasaṃjñakaiḥ \|*  
*ebhirlokaiśca brahmāṇḍaṃ brahmādhikṛtameva ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

*evañcāsaṃkhyabrahmāṇḍaṃ sarvaṃ kṛtrimameva ca \|*  
*mahāviṣṇośca lomnāṃ ca vivareṣu ca śaunaka \|\| 15\|\|*

14-15. The seven dvipas (Islands), seven Patalas in the universe are controlled by Brahma. О Shaunaka! innumerable globes are found in the hair pits on the body of lord Vishnu.

## \|\| 7.16 \|\|

*prativiśveṣu dikpālā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ \|*  
*surā narādayaḥ sarve santi kṛṣṇasya māyayā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. With the illusion of lord Krishna, in each globe is infested with the Dikpalas, Vishnu, Shiva, Brahma, the gods and the humans.

## \|\| 7.17 \|\|

*brāhmāṇḍagaṇanāṃ kartuṃ na kṣamo jagatāṃ patiḥ \|*  
*na śaṅkaro na dharmaśca na ca viṣṇuśca ke surāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Brahma, the lord of the universe is unable to count the population of the universe. Not only this even Shiva, Vishnu and other gods are unable to do so.

## \|\| 7.18 \|\|

*saṃkhyātumīśvaraḥ śakto na saṃkhyātuṃ tathāpi saḥ \|*  
*viśvākāśadiśāṃ caiva sarvato yadyapi kṣamaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Though Ishvara is unable to count them, still it is quite difficult for them to manage the daily routine of the universe, including the sky and directions.

## \|\| 7.19 \|\|

*kṛtrimāṇi ca viśvāni viśvasthāni ca yāni ca \|*  
*anityāni ca viprendra svapnavannaśvarāṇi ca \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O best of the Brahmanas, all the things which exist in the arti^cial world, they are all perishable and are all like the dreams.

## \|\| 7.20 \|\|

*vaikuṇṭhaḥ śivalokaśca golokaśca tayoḥ paraḥ \|*  
*nityo viśvabahirbhūtaścātmākāśadiśo yathā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Goloka, which is located beyond Shivaloka and Vishnuloka, is the eternal place. Like the soul, the sky and directions, these are eternal and are beyond the artificial globe.

## Chapter 8

## \|\| 8.1-4 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*brahmā viśvaṃ vinirmāya sāvitryāṃ varayoṣiti \|*  
*cakāra vīryyādhānaṃ ca kāmukyāṃ kāmuko yathā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*sā divyaṃ śatavarṣaṃ ca dhṛtvā garbhaṃ sudussaham \|*  
*suprasūtā ca suṣuve caturvedānmanoharān \|\| 2 \|\|*

*vividhāñśāstrasaṃghāṃśca tarkavyākaraṇādikān \|*  
*ṣaṭtriṃśatsaṃkhyakā divyā rāgiṇīḥ sumanoharāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*ṣaḍrāgānsundarāṃścaiva nānātāla samanvitān \|*  
*satyatretādvāparāṃśca kaliṃ ca kalahapriyam \|\| 4 \|\|*

1-4. Sauti said, “Brahma, after the creation of the universe, implanted the seed in the beautiful Savitri as a passionate person, implants the seed in his wife. Savitri then held the seduce of Brahma in her womb for a hundred divine years and gave birth to charming RSginls. Thereafter six beautiful Ragas were created together with the rhythms Savitri then gave birth of Satyayuga, Dvdpara, Tretayuga and Kaliyuga.

## \|\| 8.5-6 \|\|

*varṣamāsamṛtuṃ caiva tithiṃ daṇḍakṣaṇādikam \|*  
*dinaṃ rātriṃ ca vārāṃśca sandhyāmuṣasameva ca \|\| 5 \|\|*

*puṣṭiṃ ca devasenāṃ ca medhāṃ ca vijayāṃ jayām \|*  
*ṣaṭkṛttikāśca yogāṃśca karaṇaṃ ca tapo dhanaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. O best of the ascetics thereafter, years, months, seasons, days, dandas moments, besides days, nights, days of the week, daybreak, Push, Devasena, Medha, Vijaya, Jaya, six Krttikas and Karana were also produced by her.

## \|\| 8.7-9 \|\|

*devasenāṃ mahāṣaṣṭhīṃ kārttikeyapriyāṃ satīm \|*  
*mātṛkāsu pradhānā sā bālānāmiṣṭadevatā \|\| 7 \|\|*

*brāhmaṃ pādmaṃ ca vārāhaṃ kalpatrayamidaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*nityaṃ naimittikaṃ caiva dviparārddhaṃ ca prākṛtam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*caturvidhaṃ ca pralayaṃ kālaṃ vai mṛtyukanyakām \|*  
*sarvānvyādhigaṇāṃścaiva sā prasūya stanaṃ dadau \|\| 9 \|\|*

7-9. MahasastI Devasena, the spouse of Karttikeya, the chief among the Matrkas, was also born out of Savitri, She is also the deity of the children. The Brahma, Padma and Varaha kalpas, the four types of the times of dissolution, everlasting, were also created by her. She then fed them with the breast milk.

## \|\| 8.10 \|\|

*atha dhātuḥ pṛṣṭhadeśādadharmaḥ samajāyata \|*  
*alakṣmīstadvāmapārśvādbabhūvātyantakāminī \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Thereafter out of the back side of Brahma adharma emerged and from his left side the most passionate ALakshmi or the goddess of misfortune emerged.

## \|\| 8.11-12 \|\|

*nābhideśādviśvakarmā jāto vai śilpināṃ guruḥ \|*  
*mahāntau vasavo'ṣṭau ca mahābalaparākramāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*atha dhātuśca manasa āvirbhūtāḥ kumārakāḥ \|*  
*catvāraḥ pañcavarṣīyā jvalanto brahmatejasā \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. Vishvakarma, the teacher of the architects was born besides the eight valorous Vasus. Then four Kumaras were born out of the mind of Brahma, who always looked of five years of age and possessed great glory.

## \|\| 8.13 \|\|

*sanakaśca sanandaśca tṛtīyaśca sanātanaḥ \|*  
*sanatkumāro bhagavāṃścaturtho jñānināṃ varaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The first one was given the name of Sanaka, the second one Sananda, the third one Sanatana and the fourth one was Sanatkumara, the best of the intellectuals.

## \|\| 8.14-15 \|\|

*āvirbabhūva mukhataḥ kumāraḥ kanakaprabhaḥ \|*  
*divyarūpadharaḥ śrīmānsastrīkaḥ sundaro yuvā \|\| 14 \|\|*

*kṣattriyāṇāṃ bījarūpo nāmnā svāyambhuvo manuḥ \|*  
*yā strī sā śatarūpā ca rūpāḍhyā kamalā kalā \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. A child emerged out of his mouth having the lustre of gold. Having the divine form, illustrious and was accompanied with the spouse. He happened to be the seed of the young people. Ks atriyas. He was known as the Svayambhu-Manu who was accompanied with Satrnpa his spouse who was beautiful like Lakshmi and her body was quite artistic.

## \|\| 8.16-17 \|\|

*sastrīkaśca manustasthau dhātrājñā paripālakaḥ \|*  
*svayaṃ vidhātā putrāṃśca tānuvāca praharṣitān \|\| 16 \|\|*

*sṛṣṭiṃ karttuṃ mahābhāgo mahābhāgavatāndvija \|*  
*jagmuste ca nahītyuktvā taptuṃ kṛṣṇaparāyaṇāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. Manu then obeyed the command of Brahma with his spouse. Thereafter Brahma, with a pleasant mind commanded the illustrious lady for the creation of the universe. But the Kumaras refused to obey the command of Brahma and with their minds devoted towards Lord Krishna, they left for performing tapas.

## \|\| 8.18-22 \|\|

*cukopa hetunā tena vidhātā jagatāṃ patiḥ \|*  
*kopāsaktasya ca vidherjvalato brahmatejasā \|\| 18 \|\|*

*āvirbhūtā lalāṭācca rudrā ekādaśa prabho \|*  
*kālāgnirudraḥ saṃhartā teṣāmekaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*sarveṣāmeva viśvānāṃ sa tāmasa iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*rājasaśca svayaṃ brahmā śivo viṣṇuśca sāttvikau \|\| 20 \|\|*

*golokanāthaḥ kṛṣṇaśca nirguṇaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ \|*  
*paramajñānino mūrkhā vadantaṃ tāmasaṃ śivam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*śuddhasattvasvarūpaṃ ca nirmalaṃ vaiṣṇavāgraṇīm \|*  
*śṛṇu nāmāni rudrāṇāṃ vedoktāni ca yāni ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

18-22. This enraged, Brahma, the creator of the universe. О Lord, on getting enraged, Brahma, a Rudra appeared out of his forehead. He is said to be Kalagni Rudra and is known as the one with tamoguna element in the universe. Brahma is known as rajas, besides, Shiva and Vishnu are taken to be Satvikas. The great Krishna, the lord of Goloka is formless and beyond nature. The foolish people, term Shiva as possessing the tamasis elements, but he is spotless, truthful and the foremost of the Vaispavas. Now listen to the names of Rudras as spelt out in the Vedas.

## \|\| 8.23 \|\|

*mahānmahātmā matimānbhīṣaṇaśca bhayaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*ṛtudhvajaścordhvakeśaḥ piṅgalākṣo ruciḥ śuciḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. They are known as Mahan, Mahatma, Matiman, Bhisana, Bhayankara, Rtudhvaja, Urdhvakesa, Pinglakso, Ruci and Suci.

## \|\| 8.24-25 \|\|

*pulastyo dakṣakarṇācca pulaho vāmakarṇataḥ \|*  
*dakṣanetrāttathā'triśca vāmanetrātkratuḥ svayam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*araṇirnāsikārandhrādaṅgirāśca mukhādruciḥ \|*  
*bhṛguśca vāmapārśvācca dakṣo dakṣiṇapārśvataḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. Out of the right ear of Brahma emerged Pulastya, Pulaha from the left ear, Atri from the right eye, Svayamkratu from the left eye, Arani and Angira from the navel, Ruci from the mouth, Bhrigu form the thighs and from the right side of the back Daksha was born.

## \|\| 8.26-28 \|\|

*chāyāyāḥ kardamo jāto nābheḥ pañcaśikhastathā \|*  
*vakṣasaścaiva voḍhuśca kaṇṭhadeśācca nāradaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*marīciḥ skandhadeśāccaivāpāntaratamā galāt \|*  
*vasiṣṭho rasanādeśātpracetā adharoṣṭhataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

*haṃsaśca vāmakukṣeśca dakṣakukṣeryatiḥ svayam \|*  
*sṛṣṭiṃ vidhātuṃ sa vidhiścakārājñāṃ sutānprati \|*  
*piturvākyaṃ samākarṇya tamuvāca sa nāradaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

26-28. Then Kardama was born out of the shadow of Brahma, Pancasikha out of the navel, Vodhu out of the chest, Narada from the neck, Marichi from the shoulders, Apantratma from the throat, Vasist ha from the tongue, Praceta from lower lip, the goose from the left waist, Yati from right waist. Brahma them commanded his sons to create the universe. On hearing the command of his father, Narada said.

## \|\| 8.29 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*pūrvamānaya majjyeṣṭhānsanakādīnpitāmaha \|*  
*kārayitvā dārayuktānasmānvada jagatpate \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Narada Said, “O Great Sire, О Lord of the universe, you summon first of all our elder brothers, who are known as Sanaka etc. and arrange for their marriage. Then only you issue the command to us.

## \|\| 8.30 \|\|

*pitrā te tapase yuktāḥ saṃsārāya vayaṃ katham \|*  
*aho hanta prabhorbuddhirviparītāya kalpate \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. When the father himself allowed them to disown the universe, then why are we being entangled in the bondages of the world. It is surprising as well as a pity that the mind of the lord is moving in the different direction.

## \|\| 8.31-35 \|\|

*kasmai putrāya pīyūṣātparaṃ dattaṃ tapo'dhunā \|*  
*kasmai dadāsi viṣayaṃ viṣamaṃ ca viṣādhikam \|\| 31 \|\|*

*atīva nimne ghore ca bhavābdhau yaḥ patetpitaḥ \|*  
*niṣkṛtistasya nāstīti koṭikalpe gate'pi ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

*nistārabījaṃ sarveṣāṃ bījaṃ ca puruṣotamam \|*  
*sarvadaṃ bhaktidaṃ dāsyapradaṃ satyaṃ kṛpāmayam \|\| 33 \|\|*

*bhaktaikaśaraṇaṃ bhaktavatsalaṃ svacchameva ca \|*  
*bhaktapriyaṃ bhaktanāthaṃ bhaktānugrahakārakam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*bhaktārādhyaṃ bhaktasādhyaṃ vihāya parameśvaram \|*  
*mano dadhāti ko mūḍho viṣaye nāśakāraṇe \|\| 35 \|\|*

31-35. Because sons are being made to enjoy the nectar of tapas while others are being made to consume the terrific poison. О Father, whosoever, would fall into the horrible ocean of the universe, he could not be rescued out of it for crores of kalpas , because it is lord Purusottama alone, who bestows all the riches, devotion, slavery, benevolence and is the only abode of rescue for his devotees. He loves his devotees, is spotless, beloved of the devotees, lord of the devotees and showers his compassion over his devotees. His is adorable by the devotees. You tell me who would be the fool who leaving such a compassionate lord shall divert his mind to the worldly pleasures.

## \|\| 8.36 \|\|

*vihāya kṛṣṇasevāṃ ca pīyūṣādadhikāṃ priyām \|*  
*ko mūḍho viṣamaśnāti viṣamaṃ viṣayābhidham \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Who would be the fool enough to discord the devotion of lord Krishna involve himself in the wordily pleasures, which are like the horrible poison.

## \|\| 8.37 \|\|

*svapnavannaśvaraṃ tucchamasatyaṃ mṛtyukāraṇam \|*  
*yathā dīpaśikhāgraṃ ca kīṭānāṃ sumanoharam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. As the tip of the lamp flame, becomes the cause of destruction for the fire-flies, inspite of being quite beautiful to look as, similarly the worldly pleasures are also short lived, degraded, perishable and destructive.

## \|\| 8.38 \|\|

*yathā baḍiśamāṃsaṃ ca matsyāpātasukhapradam \|*  
*tathā viṣayiṇāṃ tāta viṣayaṃ mṛtyukāraṇam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. As the piece of flesh tied to the fish rod, delights and fish for an instant and them becomes the cause of its destination, similarly the worldly pleasures for a person in the universe is quite short-lived and become the cause of his destruction”.

## \|\| 8.39 \|\|

*ityuktvā nāradastatra virarāma vidheḥ puraḥ \|*  
*tasthau tātaṃ namaskṛtya jvaladagniśikhopamaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Narada spoke like the fire flame before Brahma and then kept quiet. Then he bowed in reverence to Brahma and stood in attention before him.

## \|\| 8.40 \|\|

*brahmā kopaparītaśca śaśāpa tanayaṃ dvija \|*  
*uvāca kampitāṅgaśca raktāsyaḥ sphuritādharaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. О Brahmana, Brahma them getting enraged pronounced a curse on Narada. He was shaking in anger and his face became red.

## \|\| 8.41 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*bhavitā jñānalopaste macchāpena ca nārada \|*  
*krīḍāmṛgaśca tvaṃ sādhyo yoṣillubddhadhaśca lampaṭaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Brahma said, “You will be deprived of your intelligence because of my curse. You will become the playful dear of the damsels and shall become passionate for women.

## \|\| 8.42-44 \|\|

*sthirayauvanayuktānāṃ rūpāḍhyānāṃ manoharaḥ \|*  
*pañcāśatkāminīnāṃ ca bhartā ca prāṇavallabhaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*śṛṅgāraśāstravettā ca mahāśṛṅgāralolupaḥ \|*  
*nānāprakāraśṛṅgāranipuṇānāṃ gurorguruḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*gandharvāṇāṃ ca suvaraḥ susvaraśca sugāyanaḥ \|*  
*vīṇāvādanasandarbhaniṣṇātaḥ sthirayauvanaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

42-44. You will become the beautiful husband of fifty youthful and charming damsels. You will get well-versed in the art of love-sport, extremely passionate, master of several of the love sports and teachers of these engaged in making love. You will be good singe and excel in the playing of vwa. You will always remain youthful.

## \|\| 8.45-46 \|\|

*prājño madhuravāk śāntaḥ suśīlaḥ sundaraḥ sudhīḥ \|*  
*bhaviṣyasi na sandeho nāmataścopabarhaṇaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*tābhirdivyaṃ lakṣayugaṃ vihṛtya nirjane vane \|*  
*punarmadīyaśāpena dāsī putraśca tatparaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. But at the same time you will be an intellect, sweet tongued, calm, humble, beautiful and full of wisdom. There is no doubt about it. You will be known by the name of upabrahana. You will roam about with those youthful damsels for a lakh of years in the lonely forest. Thereafter you will be born of a maid servant because of my curse.

## \|\| 8.47 \|\|

*vatsa vaiṣṇavasaṃsargādvaiṣṇavocchiṣṭabhojanāt \|*  
*punaḥ kṛṣṇaprasādena bhaviṣyasi mamātmajaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. О Son, thereafter, after coming in contact with the noble people and after consuming their food you will achieve the grace of lord Krishna you will get yourself established as my son.

## \|\| 8.48 \|\|

*jñānaṃ dāsyāmi te divyaṃ punareva purātanam \|*  
*adhunā bhava naṣṭastvaṃ matsuto nipata dhuvam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. At that point of time I shall bestow on you the divine and ancient knowledge. But present you will get destroyed inspite of your being my son and will surely fall down.

## \|\| 8.49 \|\|

*brahmetyuktvā sutaṃ vipra virarāma jagatpatiḥ \|*  
*ruroda nāradastātamavocatsaṃpuṭāñjaliḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Brahma, the lord of the universe then kept quiet after so speaking to Narada. Narada them spoke to his father while crying.

## \|\| 8.50 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*krodhaṃ saṃhara saṃhartastāta tāta jagadguro \|*  
*sraṣṭustapasvīśasyāho krodho'yaṃ mayyanākaraḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Narada said, “O Father, О Teacher of the universe. You please shed away your anger and be calm. You are the creator and the lord of the ascetics. You have showered your anger on me without any reason.

## \|\| 8.51 \|\|

*śapetparityajedvidvānputramutpathagāminam \|*  
*tapasvinaṃ sutaṃ śaptuṃ kathamarhasi paṃḍita \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. О Great Intellect, you are a well-read personality. The people curse the wicked sons and disown them. Then how do you fell justified in cursing and disowning your do you feel justified in cursing and disowning you son who is a recluse.

## \|\| 8.52 \|\|

*janirbhavatu me brahmanyāsu yāsu ca yoniṣu \|*  
*na jahātu harerbhaktirmā vedaṃ dehi me varam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O Brahmana, in whatever forms I am born in future, I should never be deprived of the devotion of the lord. Your also bestow this boon to me.

## \|\| 8.53 \|\|

*putraścejjagatāṃ dhāturnāsti bhaktirhareḥ pade \|*  
*sūkarādatiriktaśca so'dhamo bhārate bhuvi \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Because even when be the son of the creator of the universe, if he is not devoted at the feet of the lord, then he is considered to be worse than a pig on earth.

## \|\| 8.54 \|\|

*jātismaro harerbhaktiyuktaḥ sūkarayoniṣu \|*  
*janirlabhetsa pravaro golokaṃ yāti karmaṇā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. By remembering the deeds of his earlier births and with his mind filled with the devotion of the lord, even if one is born as a boar the said person with his deeds can achieve Goloka,

## \|\| 8.55-57 \|\|

*govindacaraṇāmbhojabhaktimādhvīkamīpsitam \|*  
*pibatāṃ vaiṣṇavādīnāṃ sparśapūtā vasundharā \|\| 55 \|\|*

*tīrthāni sparśamicchanti vaiṣṇavānāṃ pitāmaha \|*  
*pāpānāṃ pāpitattvānāṃ kṣālanāyātmanāmapi \|\| 56 \|\|*

*mantropadeśamātreṇa narā muktāśca bhārate \|*  
*paraiśca koṭipuruṣaiḥ pūrvaiḥ sārdhaṃ hareraho \|\| 57 \|\|*

55-57. Because the earth gets purified by the people who by consuming the dust from the lotus-like feet of the lord and keeping company of the Vaishnavas. With the discourses of mantras, the people get redeemed with crores of earlier generations.

## \|\| 8.58 \|\|

*koṭijanmārjitātpāpānmantragrahaṇamātrataḥ \|*  
*muktāḥ śudhyanti yatpūrvaṃ karma nirmūlayanti ca \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. By reciting mantras one gets relived of the crores of accumulated sins because the Mantras uproot all the earlier sins..

## \|\| 8.59 \|\|

*putrāndārāṃśca śiṣyāṃśca sevakānbāndhavāṃstathā \|*  
*yo darśayati sanmārgaṃ sadgatistaṃ labheddhruvam \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Thus whosoever leads his coming generations to the righteous path, including the sons, wife, pupils servants and relatives, he surely achieves the highest place in the heaven.

## \|\| 8.60 \|\|

*yo darśayatyasanmārgaṃ śiṣyairviśvāsito guruḥ \|*  
*kumbhīpāke sthitistasya yāvaccandradivākarau \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. The teacher who misleads the faithful pupil, he gets dumped in the kumbhipaka hell till the sun and the moon shine an earth.

## \|\| 8.61 \|\|

*sa kiṃguruḥ sa kiṃtātaḥ sa kiṃsvāmī sa kiṃsutaḥ \|*  
*yaḥ śrīkṛṣṇapadāmbhoje bhaktiṃ dātumanīśvaraḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The teacher, brother, father, lord and the son are to be denounced, who are unable to bestow the devotion of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 8.62 \|\|

*śapto niraparādhena tvayā'haṃ caturānana \|*  
*mayā śaptuṃ tvamucito ghnantaṃ ghnantyapi paṇḍitāḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. О Four faced one, you have cursed me for no fault of mine. It would therefore be proper for me to pronounce a curse on you as well, because one who indulges in violence has to face violence even from the noble people.

## \|\| 8.63 \|\|

*kavacastotrapūjābhiḥ sahitaste manurmanoḥ \|*  
*lupto bhavatu macchāpātprativiśveṣu niścitam \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Because of my curse, your stotras, kavachas, mantra and adoration shall disappear from the earth.

## \|\| 8.64-65 \|\|

*apūjyo bhava viśveṣu yāvatkalpatrayaṃ pitaḥ \|*  
*gateṣu triṣu kalpeṣu pūjyapūjyo bhaviṣyasi \|\| 64 \|\|*

*adhunā yajñabhāgaste vratādiṣvapi suvrata \|*  
*pūjanaṃ cāstu māmaikaṃ vandyo bhava surādibhiḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. О Father you shall remain unadorable in all the three worlds in the three kalpas. Of course you will be adored again after the lapse of the three kalpas. Presently you will be deprived of your part in the yajnas. You will not be adored even in the religion vows. You will only be adored by the gods”.

## \|\| 8.66-68 \|\|

*ityuktvā nāradastatra virarāma pituḥ puraḥ \|*  
*tasthau sabhāyāṃ sa vidhirhṛdayena vidūyatā \|\| 66 \|\|*

*upabarheṇa gandharvo nāradastena hetunā \|*  
*dāsīputraśca śāpena pitureva ca śaunaka \|\| 67 \|\|*

*tataḥ punarnāradaśca sa babhūva mahānṛṣiḥ \|*  
*jñānaṃ prāpya pituḥ paścātkathayiṣyāmi cādhunā \|\| 68 \|\|*

66-68. Saying Narada kept quiet before his father, Brahma, however, continued to remain there calmly. Narada was then turned as Gandharva known by the names of Upabarhana. Then he became the son of the slave girl. Thereafter receiving knowledge from Brahma, he became Narada. I shall speak on the subject shortly.

## Chapter 9

## \|\| 9.1-2 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*atha brahmā svaputrāṃstānādideśa ca sṛṣṭaye \|*  
*sṛṣṭiṃ pracakruste sarve viprendra nāradaṃ vinā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*marīcermanaso jātaḥ kaśyapaśca prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*atrernetramalāccandraḥ kṣīrode ca babhūva ha \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Sauti Said, “O best of the Brahmanas, thereafter, Brahma commanded his sons to start creation and all the sons started creation except Narada. From the mind of Marlca, Prajapati, Kashyapa, was born. From the eyes of the sage Atri, moon was born in Ksirasagara (ocean of milk).

## \|\| 9.3 \|\|

*pracetaso'pi manaso gautamaśca babhūva ha \|*  
*pulastyamānasaḥ putro maitrāvaruṇa eva ca \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Gautama was born out of the mind of Praceta and Maitra-vamna appeared from Pulastya.

## \|\| 9.4-5 \|\|

*manośca śatarūpāyāṃ tisraḥ kanyāḥ prajajñire \|*  
*ākūtirdevahūtiśca prasūtistāḥ pativratāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*priyavratottānapādau dvau ca putrau manoharau \|*  
*uttānapādatanayo dhruvaḥ paramadhārmikaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. From Manu and Satariipa, three daughters were born, in the name of AkUti, Devahuti and Prasuti, who were all well-virtuous. In addition to the daughters, Priyavrata and Uttanapada two' sons were also born to them. The most religions Dhruva was the son of Uttanapada.

## \|\| 9.6 \|\|

*ākūtiṃ rucaye prādāddakṣāyātha prasūtikām \|*  
*devahūtiṃ kardamāya yatputraḥ kapilaḥ svayam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Akuti was married to Ruci, Prasuti to Daksha and Devahuti to Kardama. Kapila was born of Devahuti.

## \|\| 9.7-8 \|\|

*prasūtyāṃ dakṣabījena ṣaṣṭikanyāḥ prajajñire \|*  
*aṣṭau dharmāya sa dadau rudrāyaikādaśa smṛtāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*śivāyaikāṃ satīṃ prādātkaśyapāya trayodaśa \|*  
*saptaviṃśati kanyāśca dakṣaścandrāya dattavān \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. With the semen of Daksha, sixty daughters were born from the womb of Prasuti. Out of these sixty daughters, he gave away in marriage, eight to Dharma, eleven to Rudra one name Sati to Shiva, thirteen to Kashyapa and twenty seven to the moon.

## \|\| 9.9 \|\|

*iti śrībahmavaivarte mahāpurāṇe sautiśaunakasaṃvāde brahmakhaṇḍe navamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О Brahmana, I am reciting the names of the wives of Dharma. These were Sauti, Pusti, Dhrti, Ksama, Sraddha, Mati and Smrti.

## \|\| 9.10 \|\|

*śānteḥ putraśca santoṣaḥ puṣṭeḥ putro mahānabhūt \|*  
*dhṛtedhairyyaṃ ca tuṣṭeśca harṣadarpau sutau smṛtau \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. A son named Samtosa was born to Santi and Mahan was a son of Pusti. Dhairya was born to Dhrti and Harsa and Darpa to Tusti.

## \|\| 9.11 \|\|

*kṣamāputraḥ sahiṣṇuśca śraddhāputraśca dhārmikaḥ \|*  
*materjñānābhidhaḥ putraḥ smṛterjātismaro mahān \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Similarly Sahisnu was born of Ksama, DhSrmika from Sraddha, Jnana from Mati and Jatismara from Smrti were born.

## \|\| 9.12 \|\|

*pūrvapatnyāṃ ca mūrtyāṃ ca naranārāyaṇāvṛṣī \|*  
*babhūvurete dharmiṣṭhā dharmaputrāśca śaunaka \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O Shaunaka, from Mati, the first wife of Dharma, two sages known as Nara and Narayana and several other religions sons were born.

## \|\| 9.13-14 \|\|

*nāmāni rudrapatnīnāṃ sāvadhānaṃ nibodha me \|*  
*kalā kalāvatī kāṣṭhā kālikā kalahapriyā \|\| 13 \|\|*

*kandalī bhīṣaṇā rāsnā pramocā bhūṣaṇā śukī \|*  
*etāsāṃ bahavaḥ putrā babhūvuḥ śivapārṣadāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. I shall now narrate the names of the wives of Rudras. Listen to me attentively. These were Kala, Kalavati, Kastha, Kalika, Kalahapriya, Kandalx, Bhlsana, Rasna, Pramoca, Bhusana and Sukl. Many sons were born to them who became the attendants of Shiva.

## \|\| 9.15 \|\|

*sā satī svāminindāyāṃ tanuṃ tatyāja yajñataḥ \|*  
*punarbhūtvā śailaputrī lebhe sā śaṅkaraṃ patim \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Because of the denouncing of her husband, Shiva, who was the lord of yajnas in the function in the abode of Daksha Sati ended her life and was born again in the house of Himalayas as the daughter of Mena and married Shiva again as her husband.

## \|\| 9.16-18 \|\|

*kaśyapasya priyāṇāṃ ca nāmāni śṛṇu dhārmika \|*  
*aditirdevamātā vai daityamātā ditistathā \|\| 16 \|\|*

*sarpamātā tathā kadrūrvinatā pakṣisūstathā \|*  
*surabhiśca gavāṃ mātā mahiṣāṇāṃ ca niścitam \|\| 17 \|\|*

*sārameyādijantūnāṃ saramā sūścatuṣpadām \|*  
*danuḥ prasūrdānavānāmanyāścetyevamādikāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

16-18. О Religions ones, now you listen to the names of the wives of Kashyapa, who were, Aditi, the mother of gods, Kadru the mother of serpents, Vinata the mother of the birds, Surabhi, the mother of cows and bulls; Sarama daughter of dogs and other four footed animals, Danu the mother of the Danavas, besides several other wives who gave birth to many sons and daughters.

## \|\| 9.19 \|\|

*indraśca dvādaśādityā upendrādyāḥ surā mune \|*  
*kathitāścāditeḥ putrā mahābalaparākramāḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. О Brahman, Indra, the twelve Adityas, Upendra (Vishnu) and other gods, are believed to be the sons of Aditi.

## \|\| 9.20-21 \|\|

*indraputro jayantaśca brahmañśacyāmajāyata \|*  
*ādityasya savarṇāyāṃ kanyāyāṃ viśvakarmaṇaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*śanaiścarayamau putrau kālindī kanyakā tathā \|*  
*upeṃdravīryyātpṛthvyāṃ tu maṅgalaḥ samajāyata \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. Jayanta was born of Shachi, the spouse of Indra. Sani and Yama were born of Suvana, the daughter of Vishvakarma besides a daughter named Yamuna. Similarly the planet Mangala was born out of the earth from the seed of Upendra.

## \|\| 9.22 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ saute sa copedrānmaṅgalaḥ samajāyata \|*  
*vasundharāyāṃ balavāṃstanme vyākhyātumarhasi \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Shaunaka said, “O Son of Suta, tell me, how was the valorous Mangala planet born of the earth. Kindly tell me.

## \|\| 9.23-27 \|\|

*uedrarūpamālokya kāmārtaṃ ca vasuṃdharā \|*  
*vidhāya sundarīveṣamakṣatā prauḍhayauvanā \|\| 23 \|\|*

*malaye nirjane ramye cārucandanapallave \|*  
*candanokṣitasarvāṅgaṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*taṃ suśīlaṃ śayānaṃ ca śāntaṃ sasmitamīpsitam \|*  
*sasmitā tasya talpe ca sahasā samupasthitā \|\| 25 \|\|*

*suramyāṃ mālatīmālāṃ dadau tasmai varānanā \|*  
*sugandhi candanaṃ cāru kastūrīkuṅkumānvitam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*upendrastanmano jñātvā kāminīṃ kāmapīḍitām \|*  
*nānāprakāraśṛṅgāraṃ cakāra ca tayā saha \|\| 27 \|\|*

23-27. Sauti said, “once the earth-became passionate at the sight of the beauty of Upendra. She then turned herself into the form of a beautiful damsel and went to the lonely place of Malyavan mountain which abounded with the sandal-wood trees having tender leaves. Her entire body was plastered with the sandal-paste. She was adorned with the gem-studded ornaments and wore a serene and calm out look and a smile on her face. She wore a beautiful garland of fragrant flowers, besides the perfume of saffron and kastitri emitted from her body. She found her beloved sleeping over a bed and infected with passion she herself reached him she then presented him with beautiful garland of fragrant jasmine flowers together with the perfume of kastUri and saffron.

## \|\| 9.28 \|\|

*tadaṅgasaṅgasaṃsaktā mūrchāṃ prāpa satī tadā \|*  
*mṛteva nidritevāsau bījādhānaṃ kṛte harau \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. By her union with him she felt intoxicated and was about to faint. When the seed of Vishnu was implanted in her she felt like a half dead person.

## \|\| 9.29-30 \|\|

*tāṃ vilagnāṃ ca suśroṇīṃ sukhasambhogamūrchitām \|*  
*bṛhanmuktanitambāṃ ca sa smitāṃ vipulastanīm \|\| 29 \|\|*

*kṣaṇaṃ vakṣasi kṛtvā tāṃ tadoṣṭhaṃ ca cucumba ha \|*  
*vihāya tatra rahasi jagāma puruṣottamaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. Thereafter, the damsel with wide waist and developed breasts, fatigued with the performing of the love sport, she was smiling. Upendra them embraced her and kissed her lips. Thereafter, Purusottama left the place leaving her alone there.

## \|\| 9.31 \|\|

*urvaśī pathi gacchantī bodhayāmāsa tāṃ mune \|*  
*sā ca papraccha vṛttāntaṃ kathayāmāsa bhūśca tām \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O sage, per chance TJrvasI, passed by the way. She enquired about her welfare and brought her to senses. The earth goddess narrated to her the entire story.

## \|\| 9.32-33 \|\|

*vīryyasaṃvaraṇaṃ kartuṃ sā cāśaktā ca durbalā \|*  
*pravālasyākare trastā vīryyanyāsaṃ cakāra sā \|\| 32 \|\|*

*tena pravālavarṇaśca kumāraḥ samapadyata \|*  
*tejasā sṛryyasadṛśo nārāyaṇa suto mahān \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. Thereafter the weak earth was unable to bear the seed of Vishnu. She then dropped the semen in the mine of gems and the planet Mangala was born out of it. That son of Narayana was known as great illustrious like the sun.

## \|\| 9.34-35 \|\|

*maṅgalasya priyā medhā tasya ghaṇṭeśvaro mahān \|*  
*vraṇadātā'titejasvī viṣṇutulyo babhūva ha \|\| 34 \|\|*

*diterhiraṇyakaśipuhiraṇyākṣau mahābalau \|*  
*kanyā ca siṃhikā vipra saiṃhikeyaśca tatsutaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. The spouse of Mangala was known as Medha and their sons were also illustrious like the lord Vishnu. Two sons were born to Diti, who were known as Hiranyaksa and Hiranyakas'ipu besides a daughter named Simhika. Rahu was the son of Simhika.

## \|\| 9.36 \|\|

*nirṛtiḥ siṃhikā sā ca tena rāhuśca nairṛtaḥ \|*  
*sūkareṇa hiraṇyākṣo'pyanapatyo mṛto yuvā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Simhika was also known as Nirrti because of which Rahu was also known as Nairrta. Hiranyaksa was issueless. He was killed young by the Varaha incarnation of Vishnu.

## \|\| 9.37 \|\|

*hiraṇyakaśipoḥ putraḥ prahrādo vaiṣṇavāgraṇīḥ \|*  
*virocanaśca tatputrastatputraśca baliḥ svayam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Prahlada, the son of Hiranyakasipu was the best of the Vaishnavas. His son was known as Virocana and Bali was the son of Virocana.

## \|\| 9.38 \|\|

*baleḥ putro mahāyogī jñānī śaṅkarakiṃkaraḥ \|*  
*ditervaṃśaśca kathitaḥ kadrūvaṃśaṃ nibodha me \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Banasura was the son of Bali who was a great yogi and great devotee of Shiva. Thus I have spelt out the details about the family of Diti. Now I speak about the race of KadrQ. Which you please listen.

## \|\| 9.39-41 \|\|

*anantaṃ vāsukiṃ caiva kālīyaṃ ca dhanañjayam \|*  
*karkoṭakaṃ takṣakaṃ ca padmamairāvataṃ tathā \|\| 39 \|\|*

*mahāpadmaṃ ca śaṃkuṃ ca śaṃkhaṃ saṃvaraṇaṃ tathā \|*  
*dhṛtarāṣṭraṃ ca durddharṣaṃ durjayaṃ durmukhaṃ balam \|\| 40 \|\|*

*gokṣaṃ gokārmukaṃ caiva virūpādīṃśca śaunaka \|*  
*na teṣāṃ pravarāścaiva yāvatyaḥ sarpajātayaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

39-41. Ananta, Vasuki, Kallya, Dhananjaya, Karkot aka, Taksaka, Padama, Airavata, Mahapadma, Sadakum, Saihkha, Samvarana, Dhrtarastra, Durddhasa, Durjya, Durmukha, Bala, Moksa, Gokarmuka and Virapa are the chiefs of all the races of the serpents.

## \|\| 9.42 \|\|

*kanyakā manasā devī kamalāṃśasamudbhavā \|*  
*tapasvinīnāṃ pravarā mahātejasvinī śubhā \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The girl born of Lakshmi was given the name of Manasa, who happens to be the best of the female ascetics. She is illustrious and bestows welfare.

## \|\| 9.43 \|\|

*yatpatiśca jaratkārurnārāyaṇakulodbhavaḥ \|*  
*āstīkastanayo yasyā viṣṇutulyaśca tejasā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Jaratkam happens to be her husband who was born out of the amsa of Vishnu. Astlka is the son of Jaratkam who too is illustrious like Vishnu.

## \|\| 9.44 \|\|

*eteṣāṃ nāmamātreṇa nāsti nāgabhayaṃ nṛṇām \|*  
*kadrūvaṃśo nigadito vinatāyāḥ śṛṇuṣva me \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. By reciting their names the humans are relieved of the danger from snakes. I have apprised you of the details of the family of Kadrn and shall now enlighten you about the race of Vinata.

## \|\| 9.45 \|\|

*vainateyāruṇau putrau viṣṇutulyaparākramau \|*  
*tau babhūvuḥ krameṇaiva yāvatyaḥ pakṣijātayaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Aruna and Garuda were the two sons of Vinata. Both of them were valorous like Vishnu. All the spices of the birds emerged out of them.

## \|\| 9.46-47 \|\|

*gāvaśca mahiṣāścaiva surabhipravarā ime \|*  
*sarve vai sārameyāśca babhūvuḥ saramāsutāḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

*dānavāśca danorvaṃśā anyāssāmānyajātayaḥ \|*  
*uktaḥ kaśyapavaṃśaśca candrākhyānaṃ nibodha me \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. The cows and buffaloes were born out of Surabhi. All the dogs were born of Sarama. The sons born of Danu were known as Danavas and the rest of the races were born out of different females. Thus describing about the race of Kashyapa I now speak about the solar race.

## \|\| 9.48 \|\|

*nāmāni candrapatnīnāṃ sāvadhānaṃ niśāmaya \|*  
*atyapūrvaṃ ca caritaṃ purāṇeṣu purātanam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. First of all you listen about the wives of the moon and their stories which have been highlighted in the Puranas.

## \|\| 9.49-53 \|\|

*aśvinī bharaṇī caiva kṛttikā rohiṇī tathā \|*  
*mṛgaśīrṣā tathā''rdrā ca pūjyā sādhvī punarvasuḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*puṣyā''śleṣā maghā pūrvaphalgunyuttaraphalgunī \|*  
*hastā citrā tathā svātī viśākhā cānurādhikā \|\| 50 \|\|*

*jyeṣṭhā mūlā tathā pūrvā''ṣāḍhā caivottarā smṛtā \|*  
*śravaṇā ca dhaniṣṭhā ca tathā śatabhiṣak śubhā \|\| 51 \|\|*

*pūrvā bhādrottarābhādrā revatyantā vidhupriyāḥ \|*  
*tāsāṃ madhye ca subhagā rohiṇī rasikā varā \|\| 52 \|\|*

*santataṃ rasabhāvena cakāra śaśinaṃ vaśam \|*  
*rohiṇyupagataścandro na yātyanyāṃ ca kāminīm \|\| 53 \|\|*

49-53. The spouses of the moon include, Ashvini, BharanI, Krttika, Rohini, MrgasTra, Ardra, Punarvasu, Pusya, As'lesa, Magha, Piirvaphalgunl, UttaraphalgunI, Hasta, Chitra, Svati, Visakha, Anuradha, Jyestha, Mula, PQrvasadha, Uttarasadha, Sravana, Dhanistha, Satabhisa, Piirva Bhadrapada, Uttara Bhadrapada and Revati. These are the twenty seven in number of all these the passionate and beautiful Rohini is dearest to him, because she had always overpowered the moon because of her live and devotion. The moon then neglected his other wives considerably.

## \|\| 9.54 \|\|

*sarvā bhaginyaḥ pitaraṃ kathayāmāsurādṛtāḥ \|*  
*sapatnīkṛtasantāpaṃ prāṇanāśakaraṃ param \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Thereafter the neglected sisters complained to his father collectively that they were jeered'at by the co-wives.

## \|\| 9.55 \|\|

*dakṣaḥ prakupitaścandramaśapanmantrapūrvakam \|*  
*drutaṃ śvaśuraśāpena yakṣmagrasto babhūva saḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. The emerged father then pronounced a curse on the moon. Because of the curse of his father-in-law, the moon suffered from consumption.

## \|\| 9.56-57 \|\|

*dine dine yakṣmaṇā sa kṣīyamāṇaśca duḥkhitaḥ \|*  
*vapuṣyarddhaṃ kṣīyamāṇe śaṃkaraṃ śaraṇaṃ yayau \|\| 56 \|\|*

*dṛṣṭvā candraṃ śaṃkaraśca kleśitaṃ śaraṇāgatam \|*  
*karuṇāsāgarastasmai kṛpayā cābhayaṃ dadau \|\| 57 \|\|*

56-57. Gradually his strength and lustre went on decreasing. Ultimately the moon took refuge with Shiva. The compassionate Shiva, finding the moon so suffering he freed him from the danger.

## \|\| 9.58 \|\|

*nirmuktaṃ yakṣmaṇā kṛtvā svakapole sthalaṃ dadau \|*  
*amaro nirbhayo bhūtvā sa tasthau śivaśekhare \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. He relieved the moon from the disease of consumption and placed him over his head. The moon this became eternal and was lodged over the head of Shiva, which freed the moon of all the danger.

## \|\| 9.59 \|\|

*taṃ śivaḥ śekhare kṛtvā cābhavaccandraśekharaḥ \|*  
*nāsti deveṣu lokeṣu śivāccharaṇapaṃjaraḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Thereafter, Shiva came to be known a Chandrasekhara because of his accommodating the moon over his head. О Gods, there is no one else who could be more compassionate then Shiva.

## \|\| 9.60-61 \|\|

*dakṣakanyāḥ patiṃ muktaṃ dṛṣṭvā ca ruruduḥ punaḥ \|*  
*ājagmuḥ śaraṇaṃ tātaṃ dakṣaṃ tejasvināṃ varam \|\| 60 \|\|*

*uccaiśca rurudurgatvā nihatyāṅgaṃ punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*tamūcuḥ kātaraṃ dīnā dīnanāthaṃ vidheḥ sutam \|\| 61 \|\|*

60-61. Then the daughters of Daksha finding the moon having been freed from the ailment, again started crying and reached Daksha, the illustrious one. They then started lamenting while crying and beating their breasts. They cried aloud feeling miserable and spoke to Daksha, the son of Brahma.

## \|\| 9.62 \|\|

*dakṣakanyā ūcuḥ \|*  
*svāmisaubhāgyalābhāya tvamukto'smābhireva ca \|*  
*saubhāgyamastu nastāta gataḥ svāmī guṇānvitaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The daughter of Daksha said, “0 Father, we had prayed to you for seeking the grace of our husband. But instead of getting his grace, our lord has left us.

## \|\| 9.63 \|\|

*sthite cakṣuṣi he tāta dṛṣṭaṃ dhvāntamayaṃ jagat \|*  
*vijñātamadhunā strīṇāṃ patireva hi locanam \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. O Father, we find darkness everywhere in the universe inspite of having eyes. We have now come to realise that the husband only is the eyes for the ladies.

## \|\| 9.64 \|\|

*patireva gatiḥ strīṇāṃ patiḥ prāṇāśca sampadaḥ \|*  
*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ hetuḥ seturbhavārṇave \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Not only this, the movement of the ladies, their life and the riches are vested in the husband. He alone is the cause of dharma, artha, kama and moksa and the means to cross the ocean of the universe.

## \|\| 9.65 \|\|

*patirnārāyaṇaḥ strīṇāṃ vrataṃ dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ karma vṛthā tāsāṃ svāmināṃ vimukhāśca yāḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. For ladies, husband alone is Narayana, the vow and the ancient religion. Because of this, the religion functions performed by the ladies after antagonising the husbands are of no avail.

## \|\| 9.66-67 \|\|

*snānaṃ ca sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu dakṣiṇā \|*  
*sarvadānāni puṇyāni vratāni niyamāśca ye \|\| 66 \|\|*

*devārcanaṃ cānaśanaṃ sarvāṇi ca tapāṃsi ca \|*  
*svāminaḥ pādasevāyāḥ kalāṃ nārhaṃti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 67 \|\|*

66-67. Bathing at all the holy places and the daksina given after performing of the yajnas, noble deeds, vratas adoration of gods, holy fasting and all the types of meditation do not equate with even the sixteenth ray as compared to the serving at the feet of the husband.

## \|\| 9.68 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ bāndhavānāṃ ca priyaḥ putraśca yoṣitām \|*  
*sa eva svāminoṃ'śaśca śataputrātparaḥ patiḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Son is the dearest of all the relatives but the same son happens only to be a part of the husband. Therefore the husband is far more important than the hundreds of sons..

## \|\| 9.69 \|\|

*asadvaṃśaprasūtā yā sā dveṣṭi svāminaṃ sadā \|*  
*yasyā manaścalaṃ duṣṭaṃ santataṃ parapūruṣe \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. A lady born in an evil family always remains environs her husband because her mind always remains unstable and she feels attracted towards the evil and wicked people.

## \|\| 9.70 \|\|

*patitaṃ rogiṇaṃ duṣṭaṃ nirdhanaṃ guṇahīnakam \|*  
*yuvānaṃ caiva vṛddhaṃ vā bhajettaṃ na tyajetsatī \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. But the chaste ladies serve her husband’s even when he is mean, degraded, sickly, wicked, poor deprived of virtues and whether young or old.

## \|\| 9.71-72 \|\|

*saguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ vāpi dveṣṭi yā saṃtyajetpatim \|*  
*pacyate kālasūtre sā yāvaccaṃdradivākarau \|\| 71 \|\|*

*kīṭaiḥ śunakatulyaiśca bhakṣitā sā divāniśam \|*  
*bhuṅkte mṛtavasāmāṃsaṃ pibenmūtraṃ ca tṛṣṇayā \|\| 72 \|\|*

71-72. A woman who envies a poor or virtuous husband and deserts him, she suffers in the horrible hell till the sun and moon shine on earth, where insects eat her up like dogs day and night. When hungry, she has to consume the flesh of dead bodies and has to consume the urine to quench her thirst.

## \|\| 9.73 \|\|

*gṛdhraḥ koṭisahasrāṇi śatajanmāni sūkaraḥ \|*  
*śvāpadaḥ śatajanmāni sā bhavedbandhuhā tataḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. She then is born crores of times as vulture, female pig for a hundred years and carnivore for a hundred births and ultimately she destroys her own family.

## \|\| 9.74 \|\|

*tato mānavajanmāni labheccetpūrvakarmaṇaḥ \|*  
*vidhavā dhanahīnā ca rogayuktā bhaved dhruvam \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Again, because of her good deeds, in case she is reborn as a human, she becomes a widow, a pauper and remains sickly. This is certain.

## \|\| 9.75 \|\|

*dehi naḥ kāntadānaṃ ca kāmapūraṃ vidheḥ suta \|*  
*vidhātrā sadṛśastvaṃ ca punaḥ sraṣṭuṃ kṣamo jagat \|\| 75\|\|*

75\. О Son of Brahmana, you better restore our husband to us because you can create the universe like Brahma himself?”.

## \|\| 9.76-77 \|\|

*kanyānāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā dakṣaḥ śaṃkarasannidhim \|*  
*jagāma śambhustaṃ dṛṣṭvā samutthāya nanāma ca \|\| 76 \|\|*

*dakṣastasyāśiṣaṃ kṛtvā samuvāca kṛpānidhim \|*  
*tatyāja kopaṃ durddhaṣo dṛṣṭvā ca praṇataṃ śivam \|\| 77 \|\|*

76-77. On hearing the words of his daughters, Daksha went to Shiva, who got up from his seat and bowed in reverence before Daksha, Daksha then blessed Shiva, the compassionate one. Finding Shiva’s humble behaviour, Daksha’s anger disappeared.

## \|\| 9.78 \|\|

*dakṣa uvāca \|*  
*dehi jāmātaraṃ śambho madīyaṃ prāṇavallabham \|*  
*matsutānāṃ ca prāṇānāṃ parameva priyaṃ patim \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. Daksha said, “O Lord Shiva, you please return my son in law who is dearer to my daughters than their lives even.

## \|\| 9.79 \|\|

*na ceddadāsi jāmātarmama jāmātaraṃ vidhum \|*  
*dāsyāmi dāruṇaṃ śāpaṃ tubhyaṃ tvaṃ kena mucyase \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. You also happen to be my son in law. In case you do not return my son-in-law to me, I shall pronounce a horrible curse on you and you will not be able to free yourself from it.

## \|\| 9.80 \|\|

*dakṣasya vacanaṃ śrutvā tamuvāca kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*sudhādhikaṃ ca vacanaṃ brahmañśaraṇapañjaraḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. О Brahmana, on hearing the words of Daksha, Shiva spoke the words which were sweeter than the nectar even.

## \|\| 9.81-82 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*karoṣi bhasmasāccenmāṃ dadyā vā śāpameva ca \|*  
*nāhaṃ dātuṃ samarthaśca candraṃ ca śaraṇāgatam \|\| 81 \|\|*

*śivasya vacanaṃ śrutvā dakṣastaṃ śaptumudyataḥ \|*  
*śivaḥ sasmāra govindaṃ vipanmokṣaṇakārakam \|\| 82 \|\|*

81-82. Shiva said, “Whether your reduce me to ashes or pronounce a curse on me, but I am unable to return the moon who has taken the refuge under me.” On hearing the words of Shiva, Daksha was about to pronounce a curse of Shiva. At that point of time, Shiva remembered of Govinda.

## \|\| 9.83 \|\|

*etasminnantare kṛṣṇo vṛddhabrāhmaṇarūpadhṛk \|*  
*samāyayau tayormūlaṃ tau taṃ ca namatuḥ kramāt \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. At that very moment, lord Krishna appeared on the scene in the form of an old Brahmana, who happened to be the creation of both of them. Both of them bowed before him in reverence.

## \|\| 9.84 \|\|

*dattvā śubhāśiṣaṃ tau sa brahmajyotiḥ sanātanaḥ \|*  
*uvāca śaṅkaraṃ pūrvaṃ paripūrṇatamo dvija \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. О Brahmana, he blessed both of them with his divine light and he then spoke to Shiva first.

## \|\| 9.85 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*na cātmanaḥ priyaḥ kaściccharva sarveṣu bandhuṣu \|*  
*ātmānaṃ rakṣa dakṣāya dehi candraṃ sureśvara \|\| 85\|\|*

85\. The lord said, “O Shiva, nothing is dearer to them the soul in all the creatures, therefore О Lord of the gods, you better same yourself by giving away the moon the Daksha.

## \|\| 9.86 \|\|

*tapasvināṃ varaḥ śāntastvamevaṃ vaiṣṇavāgraṇīḥ \|*  
*samaḥ sarveṣu jīveṣu hiṃsākrodhavivarjitaḥ \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. You are the best of the recluses, are calm, first among the Vaishnavas and you treat ail the creatures in one and the same way. You are free from violence and anger.

## \|\| 9.87 \|\|

*dakṣaḥ krodhī ca durddharṣastejasvī brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ \|*  
*śiṣṭo bibheti durddharṣaṃ na durddharṣaśca kaṃcana \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. Daksha is full of anger, terrific and is an illustrious son of Brahma. A noble person gives in before a terrific person who never gets afraid of anyone”.

## \|\| 9.88 \|\|

*nārāyaṇavacaḥ śrutvā hasitvā śaṅkaraḥ svayam \|*  
*uvāca nītisāraṃ ca nītibījaṃ parātparam \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. Hearing the words of Narayana, Shiva smiled and spoke quite appropriate words which were the gist of the politics.

## \|\| 9.89 \|\|

*śaṅkara uvāca \|*  
*tapo dāsyāmi tejaśca sarvasiddhiṃ ca sampadam \|*  
*prāṇāṃśca na samartho'haṃ pradātuṃ śaraṇāgatam \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. Shiva said, “I can sacrifice my tapas, glory, all the achievements, the riches and even my life but cannot give away the person who had taken refuge with me.

## \|\| 9.90 \|\|

*yo dadāti bhayenaiva prapannaṃ śaraṇāgatam \|*  
*taṃ ca dharmaḥ parityajya yāti śaptvā sudāruṇam \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. Because the one who disown the person who had taken refuge with him, he is also abandoned by the Dharma pronouncing a curse of him.

## \|\| 9.91 \|\|

*sarvaṃ tyaktuṃ samartho'haṃ na svadharmaṃ jagatprabho \|*  
*yaḥ svadharmavihīnaśca sa ca sarvabahiṣkṛtaḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. Therefore, О lord of the universe. I can disown anyone except the Dharma. Because the one who is deprived of the Dharma of his own is discarded by all the Dharma.

## \|\| 9.92 \|\|

*yaśca dharmaṃ sadā rakṣeddharmastaṃ parirakṣati \|*  
*dharmaṃ vedeśvara tvaṃ ca kiṃ māṃ brūhi svamāyayā \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. The one who protest Dharma, he is also protected Dharma. О Lord you are well aware of the Dharma and as such why do you speak such words influenced with your illusion.

## \|\| 9.93 \|\|

*tvaṃ sarvamātā sraṣṭā ca hantā ca pariṇāmataḥ \|*  
*tvayi bhaktirdṛḍhā yasya tasya kasmādbhayaṃ bhavet \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. You are the producer and the creator of all besides being the destroyer. The one who is deeply devoted to you cannot be afraid of anyone”.

## \|\| 9.94 \|\|

*śaṅkarasya vacaḥ śrutvā bhagavānsarvabhāvavit \|*  
*candraṃ candrādviniṣkṛṣya dakṣāya pradadau hariḥ \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. The lord who is well aware of the sentiments of others listened to the words of Shiva quite attentively. He then took out the moon from his head and gave him away to Daksha.

## \|\| 9.95-98 \|\|

*pratasthāvarddhacandraśca nirvyādhiḥ śivaśekhare \|*  
*nijagrāha paraṃ candraṃ viṣṇudattaṃ prajāpatiḥ \|\| 95 \|\|*

*yakṣmagrastaṃ ca taṃ dṛṣṭvā dakṣastuṣṭāva mādhavam \|*  
*pakṣe pūrṇaṃ kṣataṃ pakṣe taṃ cakāra hariḥ svayam \|\| 96 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇastebhyo varaṃ dattvā jagāma svālayaṃ dvija \|*  
*dakṣaścandraṃ gṛhītvā ca kanyābhyaḥ pradadau punaḥ \|\| 97 \|\|*

*candrastāśca pariprāpya vijahāra divāniśam \|*  
*samaṃ dadarśa tāḥ sarvāstatprabhṛtyeva kampitaḥ \|\| 98 \|\|*

95-98. The half them the half portion of the moon which was quite healthy was placed over the head of Shiva, while the other half which was ailing went to Daksha, who finding the same infested with the disease of consumption, prayed to lord Krishna, who so managed that the moon would become full during one fortnight and lustre less during the other fortnight. Thus blessing both of them lord Krishna retreated to his abode. Daksha on his part entrusted the care of the moon to his daughters. The moon on the other hand treated all his wives equally from that time on wards and enjoyed all the pleasures of their company.

## \|\| 9.99 \|\|

*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ kiñcitsṛṣṭikramaṃ mune \|*  
*śrutaṃ ca guruvaktreṇa puṣkare munisaṃsadi \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. О Brahmana, I have narrated to you all whatever was hard by me at Psskara from the mouth of my teacher..

## Chapter 10

## \|\| 10.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*bhṛgoḥ putraśca cyavanaḥ śukraśca jñānināṃ vara \|*  
*kratorapi kriyā bhāryyā vālakhilyānasūyata \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said, “Bhrigu, Cyayana and Sukra happen to be the best of the intellectuals. Kratu’s wife named Kriya gave birth to the sages known as Balakhilyas.

## \|\| 10.2 \|\|

*trayaḥ putrāścāṅgiraso babhūvurmunisattamāḥ \|*  
*bṛhaspatirutathyaśca śambaraścāpi śaunaka \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. О Shaunaka, Brihaspati the best of the sages was born of Angira, besides Utathya and Sambara.

## \|\| 10.3 \|\|

*vasiṣṭhasya sutaḥ śaktiḥ śakteḥ putraḥ parāśaraḥ \|*  
*parāśarasutaḥ śrīmānkṛṣṇadvaipāyano hariḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Shakti was born to Vasistha. Parasara was the son of sakti and Parashara’s son was Krishna Dvaipayana Vyasa, who was considered to be the partial incarnation of Vishnu.

## \|\| 10.4 \|\|

*vyāsaputraḥ śivāṃśaśca śukaśca jñānināṃ varaḥ \|*  
*viśvaśravāḥ pulastyasya yasya putro dhaneśvaraḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The great intellect Suka was born to Vyasa who is believed to be the arhsa of Shiva. Pulastya had a son named Vishvasrava and he had a son named Kubera.

## \|\| 10.5-6 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*aho purāṇa vaduṣāmatyantaṃ durgamaṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*na buddhaṃ vacanaṃ kiṃciddhaneśotpattipūrvakam \|\| 5 \|\|*

*adhunā kathitaṃ janma dhaneśasyeśvarādidam \|*  
*punarbhiṃnnakramaṃ janma bravīṣi kathameva mām \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Shaunaka said, “It is surprising that the words of the people well-versed in the Puranas are difficult to be understood because first now you have stated that Kubera was born out of lord Krishna, them how have spoken to me the other source of the birth of Kubera. (How could Kubera be the son of Vishvasrava).

## \|\| 10.7 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*babhūvurete dikpālāḥ purā ca parameśvarāt \|*  
*punaśca brahmaśāpena sa ca viśvaśravassutaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Sauti said, “In the ancient times all the Dikpalas were born of the lord but then with the curse of the Brahma were born as the sons of Vishvasrava.

## \|\| 10.8-9 \|\|

*gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dātumutathyaśca dhaneśvaram \|*  
*yayāce koṭisauvarṇaṃ yatnataśca pracetase \|\| 8 \|\|*

*dhaneśo viraso bhūtvā tasmai taddātumudyataḥ \|*  
*cakāra bhasmasādvipra punarjanma lalābha saḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. Once Utathya demanded a crores of gold coins form Kubera to pay gurudaksina to his teacher. Kubera is turn behaved with him quite harshly. At this Utathya reduced Kubera to ashes as a result of which Kubera was to be reborn.

## \|\| 10.10 \|\|

*tena viśravasaḥ putraḥ kuberaśca dhanādhipaḥ \|*  
*rāvaṇaḥ kumbhakarṇaśca dhārmikaśca vibhīṣaṇaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Thus Kubera was born again of Vishvashrava and the brother of Ravana, Kumbhakama and Vibhisana who was quite a religious one.

## \|\| 10.11 \|\|

*pulahasya suto vātsyaḥ śāṇḍilyaśca ruceḥ sutaḥ \|*  
*sāvarṇirgautamājjajñe munipravara eva saḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Agastya was the son of Pulaha Vatsya, Sandilya was born of Ruci and Savarni was born of the great sage Gautama.

## \|\| 10.12 \|\|

*kāśyapaḥ kaśyapājjāto bharadvājo bṛhaspateḥ \|*  
*( svayaṃ vātsyaśca pulahātsāvarṇirgautamāttathā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Kashyapa was born to Kashyapa and Bhardvaja was the son of Brihaspati. Vatsya himself was born of the sage Pulaha. The great sage Sandilya was born of Ruci.

## \|\| 10.13 \|\|

*śāṇḍilyaśca ruceḥ putro munistejasvināṃ varaḥ \|) babhūvuḥ pañcagotrāśca eteṣāṃ pravarā bhave \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Five gotras were associated with these five families. О Shaunaka, other castes of Brahmanas were born out of the mouth of Brahma. They were all spread over to different countries and are devoid of the gotras.

## \|\| 10.14 \|\|

*babhūvurbrahmaṇo vaktrādanyā brāhmaṇajātayaḥ \|*  
*tāḥ sthitā deśabhedeṣu gotraśūnyāśca śaunaka \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Similarly the moon, the sun and the Kshatriyas born of Manu are the best. The Kshatriyas of other castes were born out of the anns of Brahma. The Vaisays were born out of his eyes and the Shudras emerged out of his feet. With the contact of the Siidra women with the men of other castes varnas sukaras were born. О best of the Brahmanas, the cowherds, barbers, sweet meat sellers, the weavers, the black smiths, the gold smiths and the traders are known as sat-Shudras. With the union of Vaishya with Siidra a female of double caste is born and is called Ambastha.

## \|\| 10.15-18 \|\|

*candrādityamanūnāṃ ca pravarāḥ kṣatriyāḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*brahmaṇo bāhudeśāccaivānyāḥ kṣatriyajātayaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*ūrudeśācca vaiśyāśca pādataḥ śūdrajātayaḥ \|*  
*tāsāṃ saṅkarajātena babhūvurvarṇasaṅkarāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*gopanāpitabhillāśca tathā modakakūbarau \|*  
*tāmbūlisvarṇakārau ca vaṇigjātaya eva ca \|\| 17 \|\|*

*ityevamādyā viprendra sacchūdrāḥ parikīrttitāḥ \|*  
*śūdrāviśostu karaṇo'mbaṣṭho vaiśyadvijanmanoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

15-18. Vishvakanna implanted the seed in the knowledge (Vidya) and nine sons were born of her, who were known as the artisans like, the gardener, Carpenter, maker of Sarhkha, weaver, potter and blacksmith. All these six types of artisans are known to be the best of all.

## \|\| 10.19-20 \|\|

*viśvakarmā ca śūdrāyāṃ vīryyādhānaṃ cakāra saḥ \|*  
*tato babhūvuḥ putrāśca navaite śilpakāriṇaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*mālākāraḥ śaṅkhakāraḥ karmakāraḥ kuvindakaḥ \|*  
*kumbhakāraḥ kāṃsyakāraḥ ṣaḍete śilpināṃ varāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. The carpenter, sculptor and the gold-smith are the ones who had fallen on earth due to the curse of Brahma and are known as Vamasankaras besides being unsuitable to perfonn the yajnas.

## \|\| 10.21 \|\|

*sūtrakāraścitrakāraḥ svarṇakārastathaiva ca \|*  
*patitāste brahmaśāpādayājyā varṇasaṅkarāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Shaunaka said, “How could Vishvakarma implant his see in a Shudra woman? How were they fallen? Why did Brahma pronounce a curse on them. О best of these possessing the knowledge of the Puranas, you please tell us.

## \|\| 10.22-23 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ devo viśvakarmā vīryyādhānaṃ cakāra saḥ \|*  
*śūdrāyāmadhamāyāṃ ca kathaṃ te patitāstrayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*kathaṃ teṣu brahmaśāpo hyabhavatkena hetunā \|*  
*he purāṇavidāṃ śreṣṭha tannaḥ śaṃsitumarhasi \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. Sauti said, “Once an Apsara named GhrtacI, infested with passion and clad in attractive garments was proceeding to the abode of Kamadeva. Viswakarma spotted her on the way near Psskara.

## \|\| 10.24 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*ghṛtācī kāmataḥ kāmaṃ veṣaṃ cakre manoharam \|*  
*tāmapaśyadviśvakarmā gacchantīṃ puṣkare pathi \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. On seeing her, the mind of Vishvakarma was filled with pleasure. Infested with passion he sought to make love with her. At that point of time she was adorned with all the ornaments. All her limbs looked tender. She, having an eternal youthfulness looked like a damsel of sixteen years. She had broad hips. She could attract even the recluses. She stood unstable because of casting a sharp side glance and looked quite passionate, infested with love. Her waist was well developed. Her costumes were being flown by the wind. Both her breasts were well developed, raised and hard. She wore a smile on her face and the lustre of her body put even to moon to shame. Her beautiful lips resembled the ripe bimba fruits and were red in colour. Her forehead was plastered with coryllium mixed with kasturl. The precious gem-studded kundalas were hanging and shining over her cheeks. Vishvakarma, the master of Love sport, spoke to her the words which were sweet like the nectar.

## \|\| 10.25-31 \|\|

*āgacchattadvilokācca prasādotphullamānamaḥ \|*  
*tāṃ yayāce sa śṛṅgāraṃ kāmena hṛtacetanaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*ratnālaṅkārabhūṣāḍhyāṃ sarvāvayavakomalām \|*  
*yathā ṣoḍaśavarṣīyāṃ śaśvatsusthirayauvanām \|\| 26 \|\|*

*bṛhannitamba bhārārtāṃ munimānasamohinīm \|*  
*ativegakaṭākṣeṇa lolāṃ kāmātipīḍitām \|\| 27 \|\|*

*tacchroṇīṃ kaṭhināṃ dṛṣṭvā vāyunā'pahṛtāṃśukām \|*  
*atīvoccaiḥ stanayugaṃ kaṭhinaṃ vartulaṃ param \|\| 28 \|\|*

*susmitaṃ cāru vaktraṃ ca śaraccandravinindakam \|*  
*pakvabimbaphalāraktasvoṣṭhādharamanoharam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*sindūrabindusaṃyuktaṃ kastūrībindusaṃyutam \|*  
*kapolamujjvalaṃ śaśvanmahārhamaṇikuṇḍalam \|\| 30 \|\|*

*tāmuvāca priyāṃ śāntāṃ kāmaśāstraviśāradaḥ \|*  
*kāmāgnivarddhanodyogi vacanaṃ śrutisundaram \|\| 31 \|\|*

25-31. Vishvakarma said, “O Beautiful one and dearer than my life, where are you going after stealing away my life out of my body. О Beautiful, wait for a while.

## \|\| 10.32 \|\|

*viśvakarmovāca \|*  
*ayi kva yāsi lalite mama prāṇādhike priye \|*  
*mama prāṇāṃścāpahatya tiṣṭha kānte kṣaṇaṃ śubhe \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. I have been roaming about the world over in search of you. Failing to find you I had decided to enter the fire to end my life.

## \|\| 10.33 \|\|

*tavaivānveṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā bhramāmi jagatītalam \|*  
*svaprāṇāṃstyaktumiṣṭo'haṃ tvāṃ na dṛṣṭvā hutāśane \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. I have heard from the mouth of Rambha that you are proceeding to the abode of Kamadeva. That is why I have arrived here to intercept you.

## \|\| 10.34 \|\|

*tvaṃ kāmalokaṃ yāsīti śrutvā rambhāmukhoditam \|*  
*āgacchamahamevādya cāsminvartmanyavasthitaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. О Beautiful one, you enjoy my company in the orchard located over the bank of the river Sarasvati, who fragrant breeze is always blowing. You cohabit with a beautiful person like me because a clever man would always like to make love with a clever lady.

## \|\| 10.35-36 \|\|

*aho sarasvatītīre puṣpodyāne manohare \|*  
*sugandhimandaśītena vāyunā surabhīkṛte \|\| 35 \|\|*

*yabha kānte mayā sārddhaṃ yūnā kāntena śobhane \|*  
*vidagdhāyā vidagdhena saṅgamo guṇavānbhavet \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. You are the most beautiful lady in the world and your youthfulness is eternal. You are attractive having tender limbs and the most beautiful of all the beautiful women on earth.

## \|\| 10.37 \|\|

*sthirayauvanasaṃyuktā tvameva cirajīvinī \|*  
*kāmukī komalāṅgī ca sundarīṣu ca sundarī \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. With the blessing of Shiva I have already won over the daughter of the lord of death. I have also been granted a boon Kubera, when I went to his abode. Similarly Varuna has bestowed me with the gift of the garland of gems, female ornaments from the wind-god, the sanctified garments from the god of fire and the love sports from Kamadeva, which is the mode of pleasure for all the ladies. I have learnt the art of decoration from the moon.

## \|\| 10.38-40 \|\|

*mṛtyuṃjayavareṇaiva mṛtyukanyā jitā mayā \|*  
*kuberabhavanaṃ gatvā dhanaṃ labdhaṃ kuberataḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

*ratnamālā ca varuṇādvāyoḥ strīratnabhūṣaṇam \|*  
*vahniśuddhaṃ vastrayugaṃ vahneḥ prāptaṃ mahaujasa. \|\| 39 \|\|*

*kāmaśāstraṃ kāmadevādyoṣidrañjanakāram \|*  
*śṛṅgāraśilpaṃ yatkiñcillabdhaṃ candrācca durlabham \|\| 40 \|\|*

38-40. I had resolved to hand over the garland of gems and all the ornaments. After keeping them safely in my abode, I had come out in search of you. I shall hand them all over to you after enjoying your company”.

## \|\| 10.41-42 \|\|

*ratnamālāṃ vastrayugmaṃ sarvāṇyābharaṇāni ca \|*  
*tubhyaṃ dātuṃ hṛdi kṛtaṃ prāptaṃ tatkṣaṇameva ca \|\| 41 \|\|*

*gṛhe tāni ca saṃsthāpya cāgato'nveṣaṇe bhave \|*  
*virāme sukhasambhoge tubhyaṃ dāsyāmi sāmpratam \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. Listening to the words of the passionate Vishvakarma, GhrtacI smilingly spoke the appropriate words.

## \|\| 10.43 \|\|

*kāmukasya vacaḥ śrutvā ghṛtācī sasmitā mune \|*  
*dadau pratyuttaraṃ śīghraṃ nītiyuktaṃ manoharam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. GhrtacI said, “O Passionate one, I accept all whatever have you spoken to me. But presently, I would like to speak to you some meaningful words.

## \|\| 10.44 \|\|

*ghṛtācyuvāca \|*  
*tvayā yaduktaṃ bhadraṃ tatsvīkaromyadhunā param \|*  
*kintu sāmayikaṃ vākyaṃ bravīṣyāmi smarātura \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Currently I am moving to Kamadeva’s abode, so well dressed. The day on which I use a particular dress for some one, that day is meant for that particular person. Today therefore I am the wife of Kamadeva and the wife of your teacher because you have just now told me that Kamadeva had been your teacher.

## \|\| 10.45-46 \|\|

*kāmadevālayaṃ yāmi kṛtaveṣā ca tatkṛte \|*
