*yaddine yatkṛte yāmo vayaṃ teṣāṃ ca yoṣitaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*adyāhaṃ kāmapatnī ca gurupatnī tavādhunā \|*  
*tvayoktamadhunedaṃ ca paṭhitaṃ kāmadevataḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. The one who imparts the knowledge and the one who imparts the knowledge in mantras enjoys a position a lakh times greater than the father a thousand times greater than the mother. There is none else comparable to the teacher.

## \|\| 10.47 \|\|

*vidyādātā mantradātā gururlakṣaguṇaiḥ pituḥ \|*  
*mātuḥ sahasraguṇavānnāstyanyastatsamo guruḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. О Intelligent one, I have heard from the Vedas that the teacher and the wife of the teacher are hundred times more respectable as a mother is hundred times more respectable than the father.

## \|\| 10.48 \|\|

*guroḥ śataguṇaiḥ pūjyā gurupatnī śrutau śrutā \|*  
*pituḥ śataguṇaṃ pūjyā yathā mātā vicakṣaṇa \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The quantity of sins one earns by cohabiting with one’s own mother is a lakh times more than cohabiting with the wife of the teacher.

## \|\| 10.49 \|\|

*mātrā samāgame sūnoryāvāndoṣaḥ śrutau śrutaḥ \|*  
*tato lakṣaguṇo doṣo gurupatnīsamāgame \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. A person who talks to someone addressing her as a mother, in reality she becomes like his mother because Dharma is witness for all the noble people.

## \|\| 10.50 \|\|

*mātarityeva śabdena yāṃ ca sambhāṣate naraḥ \|*  
*sa mātṛtulyā satyena dharmaḥ sākṣī satāmapi \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Thus the one who cohabits with her, has to suffer horrible pains in the terrific hell till the sun and the moon shine on earth.

## \|\| 10.51 \|\|

*tayā hi saṃgato yassyātkālasūtraṃ prayāti saḥ \|*  
*tatra ghore vasatyeva yāvaccandradivākarau \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. One earns four times more sin for cohabiting with his mother and by so doing with the wife of a teacher one earns a lakh of time more sins.

## \|\| 10.52 \|\|

*mātrā saha samāyoge tato doṣaścaturguṇaḥ \|*  
*sārddhaṃ ca gurupatnyā ca tallakṣaguṇa eva ca \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. and he suffers in the horrible hell till the life of Brahma. No remedy has been prescribed in the Vedas for such sins and is rather unheard of.

## \|\| 10.53 \|\|

*kumbhīpāke patatyeva yāvadvai brahmaṇo vayaḥ \|*  
*prāyaścittaṃ pāpinaśca tasya naiva śrutau śrutam \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The sinners are thrown in the kumbhipaka hell, which moves like the wheel of the potters, sharp like sword, filled with flesh, urine refuse filled with the insects while bite like the tridents, burning like the fire flames and is boiling hot..

## \|\| 10.54-55 \|\|

*cakrākāraṃ kulālasya tīkṣṇadhāraṃ ca khaṅgavat \|*  
*vasāmūtrapurīṣaiśca paripūrṇaṃ sudustaram \|\| 54 \|\|*

*śūlavatkṛmisaṃyuktaṃ taptamagnisamaṃ dravat \|*  
*pāpināṃ tadvihāraṃ ca kumbhīpākaprakīrtitam \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. The sin that accrues to a person for enjoying the company of the wife of the teacher the same quantity of sin is earned by the wife of the teacher as well in case she enjoys the company of others in a passionate manner..

## \|\| 10.56 \|\|

*yāvāndoṣo hi puṃsāṃ ca gurupatnīsamāgame \|*  
*tāvāṃśca gurupatnyāṃ vai tatra cetkāmukī yadi \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Today, I happen to be the beloved of Kamadeva. That is why I am going to him. I shall come for your sake in that is why I am going to him. I shal' come for your sake in a more attractive costume on some other day”..

## \|\| 10.57 \|\|

*adya yāsyāmi kāmasya mandiraṃ tasya kāminī \|*  
*veṣaṃ kṛtvā gamiṣyāmi tvatkṛte'haṃ dināntare \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. On hearing the words of GhrtacI, Vishvakarma was enraged and pronounced a curse on her saying that she would be born on earth in the house of a Shudra..

## \|\| 10.58 \|\|

*ghṛtācīvacanaṃ śrutvā viśvakarmā ruroṣa tām \|*  
*śaśāpa śūdrayoniṃ ca vrajeti jagatītale \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Shaunaka said, “O sage, how did she bear the seed of Vishvakarma? Where were the hundred sons bom? For how long did she remain on earth.

## \|\| 10.59 \|\|

*ghṛtācī tadvacaḥ śrutvā taṃ śaśāpa sudāruṇam \|*  
*labha janma bhave tvaṃ ca svargabhraṣṭo bhaveti ca \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. GhrtacI too on hearing the curse of Vishvakarma, also pronounced a terrific curse on him, “you fall from heaven and be born on earth.

## \|\| 10.60 \|\|

*ghṛtācī kārumuktvā ca sā'gacchatkāmamandiram \|*  
*kāmena surataṃ kṛtvā kathayāmāsa tāṃ kathām \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Thus cursing Vishvakarma, GhrtacI reached the abode of Vishvakarma. After enjoying the love sport with Kamadeva, the narrated the details of what had happened to her..

## \|\| 10.61-62 \|\|

*sā bhārate ca kāmoktyā gopasya madanasya ca \|*  
*patnyāṃ prayāge nagare lebhe janma ca śaunaka \|\| 61 \|\|*

*jātismarā tatprasūtā babhūva ca tapasvinī \|*  
*varaṃ na vavre dharmiṣṭhā tapasyāyāṃ mano dadhau \|\| 62 \|\|*

61-62. О Shaunaka! After informing Kamadeva, GhrtacI took birth in the house of a cowherd named Madana, in the town of Prayaga. Even after her birth on earth, she could remember the happenings of her earlier birth. She therefore instead of entering into the marriage, resorted to sever tapas.

## \|\| 10.63 \|\|

*tapaścakāra tapasā taptakāñcanasannibhā \|*  
*divyaṃ ca śatavarṣaṃ sā gaṅgātīre manorame \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. GhrtacI having the complexion of the heated gold performed tapas over the bank of the Gangs for a hundred years.

## \|\| 10.64 \|\|

*vīryeṇa surakārośca nava putrānprasūya sā \|*  
*punaḥ svalokaṃ gatvā ca sā ghṛtācī babhūva ha \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Thereafter GhrtacI after giving birth to a hundred sons born out of the semen of Vishvakarma retreated to heaven.

## \|\| 10.65 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ vīryyaṃ sā dadhāra surakārostapasvinī \|*  
*putrānnava prasūtā ca kutra vā kati vāsarān \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. Sauti said, “Vishvakarma was grieved at the curse of GhrtacI and with a painful heart, he want to Brahma to whom he narrated the details of what had happened. Thereafter with the permission of Brahma, he was born on earth in the Brahmana family. Even after having been born in a Brahmana family, he worked as an artisan, as a result of which he worked variously in the royal palaces as well as the houses of other people.

## \|\| 10.66-68 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*viśvakarmā tu tacchāpaṃ samākarṇya ruṣā'nvitaḥ \|*  
*jagāma brahmaṇaḥ sthānaṃ śokena hṛtacetanaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

*natvā stutvā ca brahmāṇaṃ kathayāmāsa tāṃ kathām \|*  
*lalābha janma brāhmaṇyāṃ pṛthivyāmājñayā vidheḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

*sa eva brāhmaṇo bhūtvā bhuvi kārurbabhūva ha \|*  
*nṛpāṇāṃ ca gṛhasthānāṃ nānāśilpaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 68 \|\|*

66-68. He always performed the job of an artisan and the quality of his artistic skill was indeed astonishing and pleasant.

## \|\| 10.69 \|\|

*śilpaṃ ca kārayāmāsa sarvebhyaḥ sarvataḥ sadā \|*  
*vicitraṃ vividhaṃ śilpamāścaryyaṃ sumanoharam \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Once he after attending his job at the royal palaces, went to the bank of the Ganga for having a bath. There he spotted a beautifully female recluse engaged in tapas. Vishvakarma, who was well aware of the happenings of his past birth recognised her.

## \|\| 10.70-71 \|\|

*ekadā tu prayāge ca śilpaṃ kṛtvā nṛpasya ca \|*  
*snātuṃ jagāma gaṅgāṃ sa cāpaśyattatra kāminīm \|\| 70 \|\|*

*ghṛtācīṃ navarūpāṃ ca yuvatiṃ tāṃ tapasvinīm \|*  
*jātismarāṃ tāṃ bubudhe sa ca jātismaro dvija \|\| 71 \|\|*

70-71. On seeing her, he was suddenly infested with passion; He, however, became calm and in a sweet tone, he spoke to the female ascetic.

## \|\| 10.72 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā sakāmaḥ sahasā babhūva hṛtacetanaḥ \|*  
*uvāca madhuraṃ śāntaḥ śāntāṃ tāṃ ca tapasvinīm \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. The Brahmana, said, “O GhrtacI, possessing the beautiful body, you are here at present, О Beautiful one having the things like the trunk of the banana tree, I am Vishvakarma. Can you recognise me?

## \|\| 10.73 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*aho'dhunā tvamatraiva ghṛtāci sumanohare \|*  
*mā māṃ smarasi rambhoru viśvakarmā'hameva ca \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. О Beautiful one, I shall relieve you of the curse. You cohabit with me. Because of you I have been burning from within.

## \|\| 10.74 \|\|

*śāpamokṣaṃ kariṣyāmi bhaja māṃ tava sundari \|*  
*tvatkṛte'tidahatyeva mano me sa ca manmathaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. On hearing the words of the Brahmana, GhrtacI, took to new form and in a calm mood, spoke to him quite appropriately this.

## \|\| 10.75 \|\|

*dvijasya vacanaṃ śrutvā ghṛtācī navarūpiṇī \|*  
*uvāca madhuraṃ śāntā nītiyuktaṃ paraṃ vacaḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. The cowherdesses said, “on that day, I was the wife of Kamadeva and now a recluse. Thus how can I cohabit with you in Bharata over the bank of the Ganga.

## \|\| 10.76 \|\|

*gopikovāca \|*  
*taddine kāmakāntā'hamadhunā ca tapasvinī \|*  
*kathaṃ tvayā saṃgatā syāṃ gaṃgātīre ca bhārate \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Because this Bharata, О Vishvakarma is a sacred place and what ever good and evil deeds are performed here, one has to face their result.

## \|\| 10.77 \|\|

*viśvakarmannidaṃ puṇyaṃ karmakṣetraṃ ca bhāratam \|*  
*atra yatkriyate karma bhogo'nyatra śubhāśubham \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. The great ascetics prefer to be born on earth and they perform infested with the illusion of lord Vishnu.

## \|\| 10.78 \|\|

*dharmī mokṣakṛte janma pralabhya tapasaḥ phalāt \|*  
*nibaddhaḥ kurute karma mohito viṣṇumāyayā \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. Because on whom so ever, the illusion of Vishnu gets pleased, lord Krishna and showers his devotion and the desired mantra.

## \|\| 10.79 \|\|

*māyā nārāyaṇīśānā parituṣṭā ca yaṃ bhavet \|*  
*tasmai dadāti śrīkṛṣṇo bhaktiṃ tanmantramīpsitam \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. The god who having been hom in Bharata, involves himself in worldly pleasures and passions are apparently overpowered by the illusion of Vishnu.

## \|\| 10.80 \|\|

*yo mūḍho viṣayāsakto labdhajanmā ca bhārate \|*  
*vihāya kṛṣṇaṃ sarveśaṃ sa mugdho viṣṇumāyayā \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. I have been reminded of all the happenings of my past birth. I am the divine apsara named GhrtacI of the earlier birth and an currently the daughter of a cowherd.

## \|\| 10.81 \|\|

*sarvaṃ smarāmi devāhamaho jātismarā purā \|*  
*ghṛtācī suraveśyā'hamadhunā gopakanyakā \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. I am performing tapas over the sacred bank of the Ganga for the achieving of the salvation. Therefore О Passionate one, you better remain calm at the moment, because this is not the place for making love sport.

## \|\| 10.82 \|\|

*tapaḥ karomi mokṣārthaṃ gaṅgātīre supuṇyade \|*  
*nātra sthalaṃ ca krīḍāyāḥ sthirastvaṃ bhava kāmuka \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. The sins which are performed elsewhere are washed away by having a bath in the water of the Ganga but the sin perfonned over the bank of the Ganga gets multiplied a lakh of times in an instant.

## \|\| 10.83 \|\|

*anyatra yatkṛtaṃ pāpaṃ gaṃgāyāṃ tadvinaśyati \|*  
*gaṃgātīre kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ sadyo lakṣaguṇaṃ bhavet \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. The same sin can vanish with the performing of the tapas of Narayana. The sin committed intentionally or unintentionally also disappears with the tapas.

## \|\| 10.84 \|\|

*tattu nārāyaṇakṣetre tapasā ca vinaśyati \|*  
*yadyeva kāmataḥ kṛtvā nivṛttaśca bhavetpunaḥ \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. Vishvakarma, of the form of a wind, then proceeded on to the Malayacala mountain with Ghrtachi.

## \|\| 10.85 \|\|

*ghṛtācīvacanaṃ śrutvā viśvakarmā'nilākṛtiḥ \|*  
*jagāma tāṃ gṛhītvā ca malayaṃ candanālayam \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Reaching the Malaya mountain, a beautiful bed of flowers was arranged. The fragrant coal was blowing. In the lonely forest, both of them enjoyed the love sport, over the flowery bid. They continued their love-sport for twelve years unmindful of the passing of the days or nights.

## \|\| 10.86-87 \|\|

*ramyāyāṃ malayadroṇyāṃ puṣpatalpe manorame \|*  
*puṣpacandanavātena saṃtataṃ surabhīkṛte \|\| 86 \|\|*

*cakāra sukhasambhogaṃ tayā sa vijane vane \|*  
*pūrṇaṃ dvādaśavarṣaṃ ca bubudhe na divāniśam \|\| 87 \|\|*

86-87. Thereafter the lustful woman became pregnant with well-nourished foetus and she gave birth to a nine beautiful sons at that very place. О Shaunaka, Vishvakarma, imparted adequate training in various arts to all his sons and they became intellects, competent valorous and wise. He then entrusting then the jobs of gardeners, carpenters, conch-makers, weavers, potters, gold smiths and painters and also blessed them variously. He then dedicated all of them to the universe and discarding the human body, Vishvakarma went to his heavenly abode.

## \|\| 10.88-91 \|\|

*babhūva garbhaḥ kāminyāḥ paripūrṇaḥ sudurvahaḥ \|*  
*sā suṣāva ca tatraiva putrānnava manoharān \|\| 88 \|\|*

*kṛtaśikṣitaśilpāṃśva jñānayuktāṃśca śaunaka \|*  
*pūrṇaprāktanato yugyānbalayuktānvicakṣaṇān \|\| 89 \|\|*

*mālākārānkarmakārāñchaṅkhakārānkuvindakān \|*  
*kumbhakārānsūtrakārānsvarṇacitrakarāṃstathā \|\| 90 \|\|*

*tau ca tebhyo varaṃ dattvā tānsaṃsthāpya mahītale \|*  
*mānavīṃ tanumutsṛjya jagmaturnijamandiram \|\| 91 \|\|*

88-91. О best of the Brahmanas, the goldsmith, fell from the position of the Brahmanahood by stealing the gold of the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 10.92 \|\|

*svarṇakāraḥ svarṇacauryādbrāhmaṇānāṃ dvijottama \|*  
*babhūva patitaḥ sadyo brahmaśāpena karmaṇā \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. The woodcutter, being unable to provide wood to the Brahmanas, for yajna in time was fallen from his position with the curse of Brahmana, similarly the painter also had to suffer because of erroneous making of the paintings with the curse of the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 10.93-94 \|\|

*sūtrakāro dvijānāṃ tu śāpena patito bhuvi \|*  
*śīghraṃ ca yajñakāṣṭhāni na dadau tena hetunā \|\| 93 \|\|*

*vyatikrameṇa citrāṇāṃ sadyaścitrakarastathā \|*  
*patito brahmaśāpena brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kopataḥ \|\| 94 \|\|*

93-94. A special type of trader too because of by stealing the gold in league with the goldsmiths, also fell from grace with the curse of the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 10.95 \|\|

*kaścidvaṇigviśeṣaśca saṃsargātsvarṇakāriṇaḥ \|*  
*svarṇacauryyādidoṣeṇa patito brahmaśāpataḥ \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. With the planting of seed of a painter in the unchaste Shudra woman the mason was born.

## \|\| 10.96 \|\|

*kulaṭāyāṃ ca śūdrāyāṃ citrakārasya vīryyataḥ \|*  
*babhūvāṭṭālikākāraḥ patito jāradoṣataḥ \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. Because of his birth from an unchaste woman, he also fell from the grace.

## \|\| 10.97 \|\|

*aṭṭālikākārabījātkumbhakārasya yoṣiti \|*  
*babhūva koṭakaḥ sadyaḥ patito gṛhakārakaḥ \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. The potter’s seed was planted into the womb of an unchaste kotaka woman and an oil-crusher was born. He was also treated to be degraded one.

## \|\| 10.98 \|\|

*kumbhakārasya bījena sadyaḥ koṭakayoṣiti \|*  
*babhūva tailakāraśca kuṭilaḥ patito bhuvi \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. Tlvara was born of the seed of a Kshatriya implanted into the womb of the wife of a Rajaputra. He was also considered to be a degraded one because of the illicit relation.

## \|\| 10.99 \|\|

*sadyaḥ kṣatriyabījena rājaputrasya yoṣiti \|*  
*babhūva tīvaraścaiva patito jāradoṣataḥ \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. With the planting of the seed of Tlvara into the womb of the wife of an oil-man, a degraded thief was born who was known as Leta. Six sons were born of the daughter of Tlvara, with the seed of beta, who were known by the names of Malla, Mantra, Matara, Bhanda, Kola and Kalandara.

## \|\| 10.100-101 \|\|

*tīvarasya tu bījena tailakārasya yoṣiti \|*  
*babhūva patito dasyurleṭaśca parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 100 \|\|*

*leṭastīvarakanyāyāṃ janayāmāsa ṣaṭ sutān \|*  
*māllaṃ mantraṃ mātaraṃ ca bhaṇḍaṃ kolaṃ kalandaram \|\| 101 \|\|*

100-101. Because of the illicit relations, with seed of Shudras planted in a Brahmana woman, Chandala was born.

## \|\| 10.102 \|\|

*brāhmaṇyāṃ śūdravīryyeṇa patito jāradoṣataḥ \|*  
*sadyo babhūva caṇḍālaḥ sarvasmādadhamo'śuciḥ \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. When the semen of Tlvara was planted in the Chandala woman, she gone birth to cobbler. The wife of the skinner when impregnated by a Chandala, a hunter is born.

## \|\| 10.103 \|\|

*tīvareṇa ca caṇḍālyāṃ carmakāro babhūva ha \|*  
*carmakāryyāṃ ca caṇḍālānmāṃsacchedo babhūva ha \|\| 103 \|\|*

103\. With the union of a hunter and the fisher woman, Konca (bird catcher) was born with the union of the wife of the bird-catcher and Kaivarta, KarttHra was born.

## \|\| 10.104 \|\|

*māṃsacchedyāṃ tīvareṇa koñcaśca parikīrtitaḥ \|*  
*koñcastriyāṃ tu kaivarttātkarttāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 104 \|\|*

104\. О Shaunaka, with the semen of Leta implanted in the womb of a Chandala girl, two wicked sons named Haddi and Dam were born.

## \|\| 10.105 \|\|

*sadyaścaṇḍālakanyāyāṃ leṭavīryyeṇa śaunaka \|*  
*babhūvatustau dvau putrau duṣṭau haḍḍiḍamau tathā \|\| 105 \|\|*

105\. Then from the daughter of Haddi with the seed of Chandala, five wicked sons were born. They were known as the forest dwellers.

## \|\| 10.106 \|\|

*krameṇa haḍḍikanyāyāṃ sadyaścaṇḍālavīryyataḥ \|*  
*babhūvuḥ pañca putrāśca duṣṭā vanacarāśca te \|\| 106 \|\|*

106\. О Shaunaka, the son who was born of the daughter of Tlvara with the seed of Leta, over the bank of the Ganga, was knower as Gangaputra.

## \|\| 10.107 \|\|

*leṭāttīvarakanyāyāṃ gaṅgātīre ca śaunaka \|*  
*babhūva sadyo yo bālo gaṅgāputraḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 107 \|\|*

107\. From the daughter of Gangaputra and with the seed of Vesadharl, was known as Yungi.

## \|\| 10.108 \|\|

*gaṅgāputrasya kanyāyāṃ vīryyādvai veṣadhāriṇaḥ \|*  
*babhūva veṣadhārī ca putro yuṅgī prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 108 \|\|*

108\. Sundl was born with the union of a trader and the daughter of Tlvara and with the union of Sundl and a Vaisya, Paundraka was born.

## \|\| 10.109 \|\|

*vaiśyāttīvarakanyāyāṃ sadyaḥ śuṇḍī babhūva ha \|*  
*śuṇḍīyoṣiti vaiśyānu pauṇḍrakaśca babhūva ha \|\| 109 \|\|*

109\. With the union of a Kshatriya and Karana-kanya, Rajaputra was born. With the union of Karana and the daughter of Rajaputra Agarl was born.

## \|\| 10.110 \|\|

*kṣatrātkaraṇakanyāyāṃ rājaputro babhūva ha \|*  
*rājaputryāṃ tu karaṇādāgarīti prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 110 \|\|*

110\. Kaivarta was born with the union of a Kshatriya with the wife of a Vaisya. Patita was born with the union of Kali and Tlvara who was a degraded one.

## \|\| 10.111 \|\|

*kṣatravīryyeṇa vaiśyāyāṃ kaivarttaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|*  
*kalau tīvarasaṃsargāddhīvaraḥ patito bhuvi \|\| 111 \|\|*

111\. The washerman was born with the union of Dhlvara with the wife of Tlvara. Koyali was born out of the union of a washer woman and the Tivara.

## \|\| 10.112 \|\|

*tīvaryāṃ dhīvarātputro babhūva rajakaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*rajakyāṃ tīvarāccaiva koyālīti babhūva ha \|\| 112 \|\|*

112\. Sarvasvl was born as a son with the union of Napita with a cowherdesses who was quite valorous but the killer of animals.

## \|\| 10.113 \|\|

*nāpitādgopakanyāyāṃ sarvasvī tasya yoṣiti \|*  
*kṣatrādbabhūva vyādhaśca balavānmṛgahiṃsakaḥ \|\| 113 \|\|*

113\. Tivara with his union with the Sundl girl seven sons were born, who by keeping the company of Haddi were turned into robbers.

## \|\| 10.114 \|\|

*tīvarācchuṇḍikanyāyāṃ babhūvuḥ sapta putrakāḥ \|*  
*te kalau haḍḍisaṃsargādbabhūvurdasyavaḥ sadā \|\| 114 \|\|*

114\. A Brahmana woman came in contact with a sage on the first day of the period. A son was born thereafter, who was known by the name of Kudar.

## \|\| 10.115 \|\|

*brāhmaṇyāmṛṣivīryyeṇa ṛtoḥ prathamavāsare \|*  
*kutsitaścodare jātaḥ kūdarastena kīrtitaḥ \|\| 115 \|\|*

115\. Such a person is equated with an unclean Brahmana. He fell from grace because of his mother’s fault during the period and because of his union with a kotaka woman, he was further down graded.

## \|\| 10.116 \|\|

*tadaśaucaṃ vipratulyaṃ patitaścartudoṣataḥ \|*  
*sadyaḥ koṭakasaṃsargādadhamo jagatītale \|\| 116 \|\|*

116\. Similarly the wife of a Kshatriya in union with a Kshatriya on the first day of the period gave birth to a son who was quite valorous but became a great robber as well as the archer. He deliberately acted against the words of the Ks atriyas and was therefore known as vdgatlta right from his birth.

## \|\| 10.117-118 \|\|

*kṣatravīryyeṇa vaiśyāyāmṛtoḥ prathamavāsare \|*  
*jātaḥ putro mahādasyurbalavāṃśca dhanurddharaḥ \|\| 117 \|\|*

*cakāra vāgatītaṃ ca kṣatriyeṇāpi vāritaḥ \|*  
*tena jātyāḥ sa putraśca vāgatītaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 118 \|\|*

117-118. Because of the sin of the menses cycle, a Kshatriya’s seed implanted in the womb of a

## \|\| 10.119 \|\|

*kṣattravīryyeṇa śūdrāyāmṛtudoṣeṇa pāpataḥ \|*  
*balavanto durantāśca babhūvurmlecchajātayaḥ \|\| 119 \|\|*

119\. Shudra woman the terrific Mlecchas caste was born.

## \|\| 10.120 \|\|

*aviddhakarṇāḥ krūrāśca nirbhayā raṇadurjayāḥ \|*  
*śaucācāravihīnāśca durddharṣā dharmavarjitāḥ \|\| 120 \|\|*

120\. These Mlecchas without the ear-holes. They were cmel, heartless, who live with difficulty in the battle, were devoid of cleanliness and good manners, besides being horrible and were irreligious.

## \|\| 10.121 \|\|

*mlecchātkuvindakanyāyāṃ jolā jātirbabhūva ha \|*  
*jolātkuvindakanyāyāṃ śarāṅkaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 121 \|\|*

121\. The Jola caste was born from the union of a Mleccha and the kuvinda woman and with the union of kuvinda’s girl and Jola, Saranka was born.

## \|\| 10.122 \|\|

*varṇasaṅkaradoṣeṇa bahvyaścāśrutajātaya \|*  
*tāsāṃ nāmāni saṃkhyāśca ko vā vaktuṃ kṣamo dvija \|\| 122 \|\|*

122\. Thus О Brahmana, because of the fault of Varnasankara (ill legitimate relations) several casts which were earlier unheard of were born and no one would be able to narrate their names.

## \|\| 10.123-124 \|\|

*vaidyo'śvinīkumāreṇa jāto viprasya yoṣiti \|*  
*vaidyavīryyeṇa śūdrāyāṃ babhūvurbahavo janāḥ \|\| 123 \|\|*

*te ca grāmyaguṇajñāśca mantrauṣadhiparāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*tebhyaśca jātāḥ śūdrāyāṃ ye vyālagrāhiṇo bhuvi \|\| 124 \|\|*

123-124. From a Brahmana woman Asvanlkumaras produced the Vaidyas. The Vaidyas on the other hand produced many sons from the Shudra women. These people were well versed in the village rituals as well as the indigenous medicines. They in turn produced the snake charmers by cohabiting with the Shudra-woman.

## \|\| 10.125 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ brāhmaṇapatnyāṃ tu sūryyaputro'śvinīsutaḥ \|*  
*aho kenāvivekena vīryyādhānaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 125\|\|*

125\. Shaunaka said, “How did AshvanI Kumara the son of Surya venture to cohabit with a Brahmana woman? In which form did he implant his seed in her”?

## \|\| 10.126-128 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*gacchantīṃ tīrthayātrāyāṃ brāhmaṇīṃ ravinandanaḥ \|*  
*dadarśa kāmukaḥ śāntaḥ puṣpodyāne ca nirjane \|\| 126 \|\|*

*tayā nivārito yatnādbalena balavānsuraḥ \|*  
*atīva sundarīṃ dṛṣṭvā vīryyādhānaṃ cakāra saḥ \|\| 127 \|\|*

*drutaṃ tatyāja garbhaṃ sā puṣpodyāne manohare \|*  
*sadyo babhūva putraśca taptakāñcanasannibhaḥ \|\| 128 \|\|*

126-128. Sauti said, “A Brahmana lady was on her way to pilgrimage. When she reached a lonely orchard with beautiful flower beds, AsvanI Kumara felt attracted towards her. Finding her most beautiful and attractive, AsvanI Kumara, forcibly cohabited with her against her wishes. She dropped the focus then and there in the orchard, but a son with the complexion of the heated gold was born.

## \|\| 10.129-131 \|\|

*saputrā svāmino gehaṃ jagāma vrīḍitā sadā \|*  
*svāminaṃ kathayāmāsa yanmārge daivasaṅkaṭam \|\| 129 \|\|*

*vipro roṣeṇa tatyāja taṃ ca putraṃ svakāminīm \|*  
*saridbabhūva yogena sā ca godāvarī smṛtā \|\| 130 \|\|*

*putraṃ cikitsāśāstraṃ ca pāṭhayāmāsa yatnataḥ \|*  
*nānāśilpaṃ ca maṃtraṃ ca svayaṃ sa ravinandanaḥ \|\| 131 \|\|*

129-131. Thereafter the woman, much disgraced, reached her husband’s home with the child. She then narrated to her husband, what ever had happened on the way. In rage, the Brahmana turned out his wife and the child from his home. Thereafter the Brahmana woman, with the use of her divine powers was turned into a stream known as Godavari. AshvanI Kumara patronised the boy and trained him in the use of medicines, mantras and various types of arts.

## \|\| 10.132-133 \|\|

*vipraśca vetanājyotirgaṇanācca nirantaram \|*  
*vedadharmaparityakto babhūva gaṇako bhuvi \|\| 132 \|\|*

*lobhī vipraśca śūdrāṇāmagre dānaṃ gṛhītavān \|*  
*grahaṇe mṛtadānānāmagrādāno babhūva saḥ \|\| 133 \|\|*

132-133. But the Brahmana by constant counting the constellations and by receiving the wages, fell from the Vedic Dhanna and started roaming on the earth in the form of a dancer. The greedy Brahmana accepted the charities given at the time of solar or lunar eclipses and also those offered at the time of death and as such he came to be known as Agradanl.

## \|\| 10.134 \|\|

*kaścitpumānbrahmayajñe yajñakuṇḍātsamutthitaḥ \|*  
*sa sūto dharmavaktā ca matpūrvapuruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 134 \|\|*

134\. A man was born of the fire altar of the Brahmana. He came to be known as the speaker on Dharma or Sota. The same spokesman on Dharma or Suta is one ancestor.

## \|\| 10.135 \|\|

*purāṇaṃ pāṭhayāmāsa taṃ ca brahmā kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*purāṇavaktā sūtaśca yajñakuṇḍasamudbhavaḥ \|\| 135\|\|*

135\. The compassionate Brahma imported him the knowledge of the Puranas. Thus the person born out of the fire-altar, also known as Suta became the speaker on the Puranas.

## \|\| 10.136-137 \|\|

*vaiśyāyāṃ sūtavīryyeṇa pumāneko babhūva ha \|*  
*sa bhaṭṭo vāvadūkaśca sarveṣāṃ stutipāṭhakaḥ \|\| 136 \|\|*

*evaṃ te kathitaḥ kiṃcitpṛthivyāṃ jātinirṇaya \|*  
*varṇasaṅkaradoṣeṇa bahvyo'nyāḥ santi jātayaḥ \|\| 137 \|\|*

136-137. With the semen of Suta implanted in the Vaisya woman a man was born, who happened to be a good orator and was known as a Bhatta in the world. He used to praise everyone. Thus I have narrated the genesis of some of the castes on earth. Several castes born out of illicit relations have been left out.

## \|\| 10.138 \|\|

*sabandho yeṣu yeṣāṃ yaḥ sarvajātiṣu sarvataḥ \|*  
*tattvaṃ bravīmi vedoktaṃ brahmaṇā kathitaṃ purā \|\| 138 \|\|*

138\. I shall now speak about the inter-linking of some castes, which was earlier spelt out by Brahma and given the descriptions in the Vedas.

## \|\| 10.139-140 \|\|

*pitā tātastu janako janmadātā prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*ambā mātā ca jananī janayitrī prasūrapi \|\| 139 \|\|*

*pitāmahaḥ pitṛpitā tatpitā prapitāpahaḥ \|*  
*ata ūrdhvaṃ jñātayaśca sagotrāḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 140 \|\|*

139-140. The father is also called as Janaka and also the one who gives birth. Similarly the grand father happens to be the father of the father and the great grand father happens to be the father of the grand father. There relations are called to be in the some gotra or the family

## \|\| 10.141-142 \|\|

*mātāmahaḥ pitā mātuḥ pramātāmaha eva ca \|*  
*mātāmahasya janakastatpitā vṛddhapūrvakaḥ \|\| 141 \|\|*

*pitāmahī piturmātā tacchvaśrūḥ prapitāmahī \|*  
*tacchvaśrūśca parijñeyā sā vṛddhaprapitāmahī \|\| 142 \|\|*

141-142. The father of the mother is called the maternal grand father and his father is known as the great grand maternal grand father and his father is known as the great paternal grandfather.

## \|\| 10.143-144 \|\|

*mātāmahī mātṛmātā mātṛtulyā ca pūjitā \|*  
*pramātāmahīti jñeyā pramātāmahakāminī \|\| 143 \|\|*

*vṛddhamātāmahī jñeyā patpituḥ kāminī tathā \|*  
*pitṛbhrātā pitṛ vyaśca mātṛbhrātā ca mātulaḥ \|\| 144 \|\|*

143-144. The mother of the mother is called the grand maternal mother, her mother-in-law is known as the great maternal grand mother and is as adorable as the mother herself. The wife of the grand father is known as the grand mother and the wife of his father is known as the great grand mother. The brother of the father is known as the uncle, whereas the brother of the mother is known as maternal uncle.

## \|\| 10.145-146 \|\|

*pitṛṣvasā piturmātṛṣvasā mātussvasā smṛtā \|*  
*sūnuśca tanayaḥ putro dāyādaścātmajastathā \|\| 145 \|\|*

*dhanabhāgvīryyajaścaiva puṃsi janye ca varttate \|*  
*janyāyāṃ duhitā kanyā cātmajā parikīrtitā \|\| 146 \|\|*

145-146. The sister of the father is known as paternal aunt and the sister of the mother is called the maternal aunt. The words like Sunu, Tanaya, Dayada and Atmaja are synonymous for the son. One’s own son is known as Dhanabhag and VTryaja or the daughter who is born to a person is known as Duhila, Kanya and Atmaja.

## \|\| 10.147-150 \|\|

*putrapatnī vadhū jñeyā jāmātā duhituḥ patiḥ \|*  
*patiḥ priyaśca bhartā ca svāmīkānte ca vartate \|\| 147 \|\|*

*devaraḥ svāmino bhrātā nanāṃdā svāminaḥ svasā \|*  
*śvaśuraḥ svāminastātaḥ śvaśrūśca svāminaḥ prasūḥ \|\| 148 \|\|*

*bhāryyā jāyā priyā kāntā strī va patnī prakīrtitā \|*  
*patnībhrātā śyālakaśca svasā patnyāśca śyālikā \|\| 149 \|\|*

*patnīmātā tathā śvaśrūstatpitā śvaśuraḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*sagarbhaḥ sodaro bhrātā sagarbhā bhaginī smṛtā \|\| 150 \|\|*

147-150. The wife of the son is called (daughter-in-law) and the daughter’s husband is called (Son-in-law). A woman’s spouse is called the husband, priya, bharta, svami and kanta. The husband’s brother is called devara and the sister of the husband is called nanada. Similarly the father of the husband is called svasura (father-in-law) and his mother is known as svasrii (mother-in-law). The wife is also called bharyci, jaya, priya, kanta, Strl and patnl. The brother of the wife is known as syalaka (brother-in-law). The wife’s sister is known as shyalika (sister-in-law). The mother of the wife is also known as svasrii (mother-in-law) and the wife’s father is known as svasura (father-in-law). The real brother is called sodara and the real sister is called sodara.

## \|\| 10.151-152 \|\|

*bhaginījo bhāgineyo bhrātṛjo bhrātṛputrakaḥ \|*  
*āvutto bhaginīkānto bhaginīpatireva ca \|\| 151 \|\|*

*śyālīpatistu bhrātā ca śvaśuraikatvahetunā \|*  
*śvaśurastu pitā jñeyo janmadātuḥ samo mune \|\| 152 \|\|*

151-152. The son of the sister is called bhagineya and brother’s son is called bhatrja. Sister’s husband is called avutta, bhaginikanta and bhaginipati and the husband of the sister-in-law is also called the brother. The father-in-law of both of them have to be treated as one’s own father.

## \|\| 10.153 \|\|

*annadātā bhayatrātā patnītātastathaiva ca \|*  
*vidyādātā janmadātā paṃcaite pitaro nṛṇām \|\| 153 \|\|*

153\. The. one who gives birth, food one who relieves from danger, wife’s father and the one who imparts knowledge, are known as the father.

## \|\| 10.154-155 \|\|

*annadātuśca yā patnī bhaginī gurukāminī mātā ca tatsapatnī ca kanyā putrapriyā tathā \|\| 154 \|\|*

*māturmātā piturmātā śvaśrūpitroḥ svasā tathā \|*  
*pitṛvyastrī mātulānī mātaraśca cartudaśa \|\| 155 \|\|*

154-155. The wife or sister of the one who gives food, wife of a teacher, mother and step mother, daughter, daughter-in-law, mother’s mother, grand mother, daughter-in-law, mother’s sister, father’s sister, aunt and maternal aunt are fourteens, all treated to be mothers.

## \|\| 10.156 \|\|

*pautrastu putraputre ca prapautrastatsute'pi ca \|*  
*tatputrādyāśca ye vaṃśyāḥ kulajāśca prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 156 \|\|*

156\. The son of the son is called the grand son and his sons are called the great grand son and his sons are called varisya and kulaja.

## \|\| 10.157 \|\|

*kanyāputraśca dauhitrastatputrādyāśca bāṃdhavāḥ \|*  
*bhāgineyasutādyāśca puruṣā bāṃdhavāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 157 \|\|*

157\. The son of the daughter is called dauhitra and his sons are known as the bandhava.

## \|\| 10.158 \|\|

*bhrātṛputrasya putrādyāste punarjñātayaḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*guruputrastathā bhrātā poṣyaḥ paramabāndhavaḥ \|\| 158 \|\|*

158\. The sons of the brother belong to the same caste is called jnati. One own brother as well as the son of the teacher are to be brought up (if need be).

## \|\| 10.159 \|\|

*gurukanyā ca bhaginī poṣyā mātṛsamā mune \|*  
*putrasya ca gururbhrātā poṣyaḥ susnigdhabāndhavaḥ \|\| 159 \|\|*

159\. О sage, the teacher’s daughter and the sister, both are like one’s own mother and should be served will. The son's teacher should be treated like one’s own brother. This is called the intimate relation.

## \|\| 10.160 \|\|

*putrasya śvaśuro bhrātā bandhurvaivāhikaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*kanyāyāḥ śvaśure caiva tatsambandhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 160 \|\|*

160\. The father-in-law of the son should be treated like one’s own brother. Similar is the case with the father-in-law of the daughter.

## \|\| 10.161-162 \|\|

*guruśca kanyakāyāśca bhrātā susnigdhabāndhavaḥ \|*  
*guruśvaśurabhrātṝṇāṃ gurutulyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 161 \|\|*

*bandhutā yena sārddha ca tanmitraṃ parikīrtitam \|*  
*mitraṃ sukhapradaṃ jñeyaṃ duḥkhado ripurucyate \|\| 162 \|\|*

161-162. The teacher of the daughter is also to be treated as the respectable relative. The brothers of the teacher and the father-in-law are to be extended the same respect and with them one has to maintain brotherly relations. He is called a friend. The one who extends pleasure should be treated as the friend and the one who showers miseries should be treated as the enemy.

## \|\| 10.163-164 \|\|

*bāndhavo duḥkhado daivānnissambandho'sukhapradaḥ \|*  
*sambandhāstrividhāḥ puṃsāṃ viprendra jagatītale \|\| 163 \|\|*

*vidyājo yonijaścaiva prītijaśca prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*mitraṃ tu prītijaṃ jñeyaṃ sa sambadhaḥ sudurlabhaḥ \|\| 164 \|\|*

163-164. О best of the Brahmanas, sometimes even brother becomes the cause of miseries and the one with whom one may have no relation at all becomes the cause of all the pleasures. There are three types of relations with the people on earth which are due to exchange of knowledge, physical intercourse and these attracted due to mental attachment. Of these the relations with a friend is based on the mutual love and affection. Which is quite difficult to get.

## \|\| 10.165 \|\|

*mitramātā mitrabhāryyā mātṛtulyā na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*mitrabhrātā mitrapitā bhrātṛtātasamau nṛṇām \|\| 165 \|\|*

165\. The mother and the wife of a friend are like one’s own mother. There is no doubt about it. The father of a friend and the brother of a friend are to be treated like one’s own father and brother and brother.

## \|\| 10.166 \|\|

*caturthaṃ nāmasambandhamityāha kamalodbhavaḥ \|*  
*jāraścopapartirbandhurduṣṭā sambhogakarttari \|\| 166 \|\|*

166\. Brahma, who was born of the lotus has also described an other relation based on the name. Besides one develops intimacy with the forcible intercourse in illicit relation.

## \|\| 10.167 \|\|

*upapatnyāṃ navajñā ca preyasī cittahāriṇī \|*  
*svāmitulyaśca jāraśca navajñā gṛhiṇī samā \|\| 167 \|\|*

167\. The beloved who attracts the mind of someone is known as the co-wife. The lover is at times treated like the husband and the beloved like the wife (some times).

## \|\| 10.168 \|\|

*sambandho deśabhede ca sarvadeśe vigarhitaḥ \|*  
*avaidiko ninditastu viśvāmitreṇa nirmitaḥ \|\| 168 \|\|*

168\. This type of illicit relation has been denounced in our country as well as the countries of the world. Such types of (illicit) relations were started by the sage Vishvamitra.

## \|\| 10.169-170 \|\|

*dustyajaśca mahadbhistu deśabhede vidhīyate \|*  
*akīrtijanakaḥ puṃsāṃ yoṣitāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ \|\| 169 \|\|*

*tejīyasāṃ na doṣāya vidyamāne yuge yuge \|\| 170 \|\|*

169-170. Such a type of relationship is prohibited for the high ranking people even. Such types of relationship brings disgrace not only for the men but also the women. But sometime in certain circumstances, such relationship is allowed for highly placed people.

## Chapter 11

## \|\| 11.1 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*dvijaḥ sa bhāryyāṃ saṃtyajya kiñcakāra viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*aśvinorvā mahābhāga kiṃ nāma kasya vaṃśajau \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shaunaka said, “what did the Brahmana do after disowning his own wife? What is the name of the son born of Asvinlkumaras. To which race do they belong?

## \|\| 11.2-4 \|\|

*sotiruvāca \|*  
*dvijaśca sutapā nāma bhāradvājo mahāmuniḥ \|*  
*tapaścakāra kṛṣṇasya lakṣavarṣaṃ himāliye \|\| 2 \|\|*

*mahātapasvī tejasvī prajvalanbrahmatejasā \|*  
*jyotirdadarśa kṛṣṇasya gagane sahasā kṣaṇam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*varaṃ sa vavre nirliptamātmānaṃ prakṛteḥ param \|*  
*na ca mokṣaṃ yayāce taṃ dāsyaṃ bhaktiṃ ca niścalām \|\| 4 \|\|*

2-4. Sauti said, Sutapa was the name of that Brahmana ascetic. He had been the great sage in the family of Bharadvaja. He went to the Himalaya mountain and performed tapas there for a lakh of years. The great tapas illumined himself with the lustre of his penance. Once he witnessed the lustre of Shri Krishna in the sky for a while and he sought for a boon for his eternal devotion in the lord. He did not seek for salvation.

## \|\| 11.5 \|\|

*babhūvākāśavāṇīti kuru dāraparigraham \|*  
*paścāddāsyaṃ pradāsyāmi bhaktiṃ bhogakṣaye dvija \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Then the divine from the sky declared, “O Brahman, you marry yourself. After you enjoy your worldly life, I shall bestow upon you my eternal devotion forever.

## \|\| 11.6 \|\|

*pitṝṇāṃ mānasīṃ kanyāṃ dadau tasmai vidhiḥ svayam \|*  
*tasyāṃ kalyāṇamitraśca babhūva munipuṅgava \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Thereafter Brahma himself gave him a girl named Manasl. О best of the sages, with his union with her a son named Kalyanamitra was born.

## \|\| 11.7 \|\|

*yasya smaraṇamātreṇa na bhavetkuliśādbhayam \|*  
*na draṣṭavyaṃ bandhumātraṃ nūnaṃ tatsmaraṇāllabhet \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. With the reciting of whose name, a person is relieved of the danger like the fall of lightening or vajra. With the reciting of the name of Kalyanamitra, one also gets his own relatives restored to him, who are otherwise difficult to get.

## \|\| 11.8-9 \|\|

*kalyāṇamitrajananīṃ parityajya mahāmuniḥ \|*  
*śaśāpa sūryyaputraṃ ca yajñabhāgvarjjito bhava \|\| 8 \|\|*

*sa sodaraśca vā pūjyo bhaveti ca surādhama \|*  
*vyādhigrasto jaḍāṅgaśca bhūyātte'kīrtimāniti \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. Thereafter, the great sage Sutapa, after disowning his wife also pronounced a curse over AshvanI Kumara, the son of Surya, “you will be deprived of the place of.grace with your brothers and also the share in the yajna. Your body willbe infested with decease and will earn a black spot.

## \|\| 11.10 \|\|

*ityuktvā sutapā gehaṃ pratasthe sūnunā saha \|*  
*aśvibhyāṃ sahitaḥ sūryyaḥ prayayau ca tadantikam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Saying this Sutapa went to his abode carrying the child with him. The sun of the other hand also reached the sage with his sons.

## \|\| 11.11 \|\|

*putrābhyāṃ vyādhiyuktābhyāṃ sūryyastrijagatāṃ patiḥ \|*  
*munīndraṃ vai sutapasaṃ sa tuṣṭāva ca śaunaka \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. О Shaunaka, Surya, the lord of the three worlds, reached the abode of Sutapa with his ailing sons. All of them then started eulogising the sage.

## \|\| 11.12 \|\|

*sūryya uvāca \|*  
*kṣamasva bhagavanvipra viṣṇurūpa yuge yuge \|*  
*mama putrāparādhaṃ ca bhāradvāja munīśvara \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Sun said, “O Brahmana, I beg forgiveness О lord you represent the form of Vishnu in all the three yugas. О graet sage Bhardvaja, you forgive the fault of my sons.

## \|\| 11.13-14 \|\|

*brahmaviṣṇumaheśādyāḥ surāḥ sarve ca santatam \|*  
*bhuñjate vipradattaṃ tu phalapuṣpajalādikam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇāvāhitā devāḥ śaśvadviśveṣu pūjitāḥ \|*  
*na ca viprātparo devo viprarūpī svayaṃ hariḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. О Brahmana, all the three gods like Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva, gladly consume the fruits the fruits, flowers and the water offered by the Brahmanas. All the gods who are adored by the Brahmanas are always adored there. No other birth is better than the birth of a Brahmana because the Brahmanas are the form of lord himself.

## \|\| 11.15 \|\|

*brāhmaṇe parituṣṭe ca tuṣṭo nārāyaṇaḥ svayam \|*  
*nārāyaṇe ca santuṣṭe santuṣṭāḥ sarvadevatāḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. With the pleasing of a Brahmana, the lord himself gets pleasant and with the pleasing of the Lord Narayana all the gods are pleased.

## \|\| 11.16 \|\|

*nāsti gaṅgāsamaṃ tīrthaṃ na ca kṛṣṇātparaḥ suraḥ \|*  
*na śaṅkarādvaiṣṇavaśca na sahiṣṇurdharā parā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. There is no place more sacred them the Ganga. There is no other god better than Krishna. There is no one else who could bear the pain more peacefully them the earth.

## \|\| 11.17 \|\|

*na ca satyātparo dharmo na sādhvī pārvatīparā \|*  
*na daivādbalavānkaścinna ca putrātparaḥ priyaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. There is no dharma greater than the truthfulness. There is no lady more chaste than Parvati and no one is dearer in the universe than the son.

## \|\| 11.18 \|\|

*na ca vyādhisamaḥ śatrurna ca pūjyo guroḥ paraḥ \|*  
*nāsti mātṛsamo bandhurna ca mitraṃ pituḥ param \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. There is no enemy greater than the ailment; no one mere respectable than the teacher, no relative is greater than the mother and there is no friend greater than the father.

## \|\| 11.19 \|\|

*ekādaśīvratānnānyattapo nānaśanātparam \|*  
*paraṃ sarvadhanaṃ ratnaṃ vidyā ratnaṃ paraṃ tataḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Ekadashi-vrata is the best of all the vratas and there is no tapas is greater than the fasting. The gems are best of all the riches and of all the gems, the gem of knowledge is most precious.

## \|\| 11.20 \|\|

*sarvāśramaiḥ paro vipro nāsti viprasamo guruḥ \|*  
*vedavedāṅgatattvajña ityāha kamalodbhavaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Brahmana is best of all the castes and there is no teacher better than a Brahmana. These are the words spoken by Brahma, who was born out of the lotus”.

## \|\| 11.21-24 \|\|

*sūryyasya vacanaṃ śrutvā bhāradvājo nanāma tam \|*  
*nīrujau cāpi tatputrau cakāra tapasaḥ phalāt \|\| 21 \|\|*

*paścācca tava putrau ca yajñabhājau bhaviṣyataḥ \|*  
*ityuktvā taṃ ca sutapāḥ praṇamyāhaskaraṃ muniḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*jagāma gaṅgāṃ saṃtrasto harisevanatatparaḥ \|*  
*putrābhyāṃ sahitaḥ sūryyo jagāma nija mandiram \|\| 23 \|\|*

*babhūvatustau pūjyau ca yajñabhājau dvijāśiṣā \|*  
*etatsūryakṛtaṃ viprastotraṃ yo mānavaḥ paṭhet \|*  
*viprapādaprasādena sarvatra vijayī bhavet \|\| 24 \|\|*

21-24. Listening to thawords of thc.sumgod, Sutapa, b

## \|\| 11.25 \|\|

*brāhmaṇebhyo nama iti prātarutthāya yaḥ paṭhet \|*  
*sa snātaḥ sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Early in the morning who bows at the feet of Brahmana, he earns the merit of having a both at alt the holy places.

## \|\| 11.26-27 \|\|

*pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni tāni tīrthāni sāgare \|*  
*sāgare yāni tīrthāni viprapādeṣu tāni ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

*viprapādodakaṃ pītvā yāvattiṣṭhati medinī \|*  
*tāvatpuṣkarapātreṣu pibaṃti pitaro jalam \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. There are several sacred places in the earth and an equal number of them do exist in the oceans. An equal number of the sacred places are enshrined in the feet of the Brahmanas. Therefore who so ever, sips the water of the Brahmana’s feet, their ancestors drink water in the lotus leaves till the earth lasts.

## \|\| 11.28 \|\|

*viprapādodakaṃ puṇyaṃ bhaktiyuktaśca yaḥ pibet \|*  
*sa snātaḥ sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The one who sips the sacred water of a Brahmana’s feet, he achieves the merit of bathing at all the holy places and the dlksa at the

## \|\| 11.29 \|\|

*mahārogī yadi pibedviprapādodakaṃ dvija \|*  
*mucyate sarvarogācca māsamekaṃ tu bhaktitaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. О Brahmana, even if a single person sips with devotion the caniamrta of a Brahmana for a mouth, he is relieved of all ailments.

## \|\| 11.30-31 \|\|

*avidyo vā savidyo vā saṃdhyāpūto hi yo dvijaḥ \|*  
*sa eva viṣṇusadṛśo na harau vimukho yadi \|\| 30 \|\|*

*ghnantaṃ vipraṃ śapantaṃ vā na hanyānna ca taṃ śapet \|*  
*gobhyaḥ śataguṇaṃ pūjyo haribhaktaśca sa smṛtaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

30-31. Whether one is an intellect or a fool, a Brahmana, who gets himself sanctified by perfonning Sandhya-vandana and adores the lord with devotion, he can be equated with Vis nu. A Brahmana should not be attacked while running or while pronouncing a curse, should not be attacked. A Brahmana who is devoted to the lord, is hundred times more adorable than the cows.

## \|\| 11.32 \|\|

*pādodakaṃ ca naivedyaṃ bhuṅkte viprasya yo dvija \|*  
*nityaṃ naivedyabhojī yo rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Those who drink the carnamrta and naivaidya from the Brahmana they achieve the merit of perfonning the RajasUya yajita.

## \|\| 11.33 \|\|

*ekādaśyāṃ na bhuṅkte yo nityaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ samarcayet \|*  
*tasya pādodakaṃ prāpya sthalaṃ tīrthaṃ bhaved dhruvam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. One who does not eat the food on the Ekadashi day and adores lord Krishna daily, his caranodaka when consumed becomes like the sacred place.

## \|\| 11.34 \|\|

*yo bhuṅkte bhojanocchiṣṭaṃ nityaṃ naivedyabhojanam \|*  
*kṛṣṇadevasya pūto'sau jīvanmukto mahītale \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The one who consumes the naivedya of lord Krishna daily such a great soul gets purified on earth and is freed from the cycle of the birth and death.

## \|\| 11.35 \|\|

*annaṃ viṣṭhā payo mūtraṃ yadviṣṇoraniveditam \|*  
*dvijānāṃ kulajātānāmityāha kamalodbhavaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Brahma, who emerged from the lotus also stated that the food from noble Brahmana which is not offered to lord Krishna is like the refuse and the milk is like the urine.

## \|\| 11.36 \|\|

*brahmā ca brahmaputrāśca sarve viṣṇuparāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇastatkule jāto vimukhaśca harau katham \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Brahma and his sons are the devotees of Vishnu and Brahmanas are born in his race. Then how can he disregard the lord?

## \|\| 11.37 \|\|

*pitrormātāmahādīnāṃ saṃsargasya gurośca vā \|*  
*doṣeṇa vimukhāḥ kṛṣṇe viprā jīvanmṛtāśca te \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Not only the Brahmana, but also his father, grandfather and great grandfather, who remain adverse to lord Krishna they are considered to be dead even when they still remain alive on earth.

## \|\| 11.38 \|\|

*sa kiṃguruḥ sa kiṃtātaḥ sa kiṃputraḥ sa kiṃsakhā \|*  
*sa kiṃrājā sa kiṃbandhurna dadyādyo harau matim \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The teacher, parents, friend, relative and anyone else who does not inspire a person to be devoted to lord Krishna, have to denounced.

## \|\| 11.39-40 \|\|

*avaiṣṇavāddvijādvipra caṇḍālo vaiṣṇavo varaḥ \|*  
*sagaṇaḥ śvapaco mukto brāhmaṇo narakaṃ vrajet \|\| 39 \|\|*

*sandhyāhīno'śucirnityaṃ kṛṣṇe vā vimukho dvijaḥ \|*  
*sa eva brāhmaṇābhāso viṣahīno yathoragaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. О Brahmana, A Vaishnava Chandala is better than the universe Brahmana. Therefore a Chandala Vaishnava, helps in achieving salvation and A Vaishnava Brahmana achieves the hell. Such of the Brahmanas who are unclean and fools and are not devoted to Vishnu they can be called as the Brahmanas for name sake only like a snake which is without the poison.

## \|\| 11.41-42 \|\|

*guruvaktrādviṣṇumaṃtro yasya karṇe praviśyati \|*  
*taṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ mahāpūtaṃ jīvanmuktaṃ vadedvidhiḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*puṃsāṃ mātāmahādīnāṃ śataiḥ sārddhaṃ hareḥ padam \|*  
*prayāti vaiṣṇavaḥ puṃsāmātmanaḥ kulakoṭibhiḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. When the name of Vishnu spoken by a teacher enters the ear, it is treated to be quite sacred by Brahma. Such a Vaishnava redeems several of the generation in the maternal and paternal faces and enters the abode of Vishnu.

## \|\| 11.43 \|\|

*brahmakṣattriyaviṭśūdrāścatasro jātayo yathā \|*  
*svatantrā jātirekā ca viśvasminvaiṣṇavābhidhā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Though there are four castes viz.: Brahmana, Kshatriya, Vaisya and Shudra, but Vaishnavas of the world belong to one and the same caste.

## \|\| 11.44-45 \|\|

*dhyāyanti vaiṣṇavāḥ śaśvadgovindapadapaṅkajam \|*  
*dhyāyate tāṃśca govindaḥ śaśvatteṣāṃ ca sannidhau \|\| 44 \|\|*

*sudarśanaṃ saṃniyojya bhaktānāṃ rakṣaṇāya ca \|*  
*tathāpi na hi niścinto'vatiṣṭhedbhaktasannidhau \|\| 45 \|\|*

44-45. The Vaishnavas are always devoted at the lotus-like feet of the lord and lord Krishna, takes care of them remaining besides them and he does not hesitate the use of Sudarsana Cakra in order to save them. He always remains with his devotees.

## Chapter 12

## \|\| 12.1-3 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*ṛṣivaṃśaprasaṅgena babhūvurvividhāḥ kathāḥ \|*  
*upālambhena prastāvātkautukena śrutā mayā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*prajā vā sasṛjuḥ ke vā ūrdhvaretāśca kaścana \|*  
*pitrā saha virodhena nāradaḥ kiṃ cakāra saḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*pituḥ śāpena putrasya kiṃ babhūva virodhataḥ \|*  
*piturvā putraśāpena saute tatkathyatāṃ śubham \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. There are several stories a bout the races of the sages. I have narrated them already which I heard from others quite playfully. It will, therefore, not be appropriate to repeat them. Out of the sons of Brahma who were those who participated in the creation of the universe? What did Narada do after his opposing the command of his father? What happened to the son after he was cursed by the father? What happened after the son pronounced a curse on the father? О son of Suta, you narrate this story in detail.

## \|\| 12.4-5 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*haṃso yatiścāraṇiśca voḍhuḥ pañcaśikhastathā \|*  
*apāntaratamāścaiva sanakādyāśca śaunaka \|\| 4 \|\|*

*etairvinā'nye bahavo brahmaputrāśca santatam \|*  
*sāṃsārikāḥ prajāvanto gurvājñāparipālakāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. Suta replied, “All Shaunaka except Hansa, Yati, AranI, Vodhu, Pancasikha, Apantaratama and Sanakadi are the sages and are other sons of Brahma who participated in the creation of universe. They always followed the command of Brahma.

## \|\| 12.6 \|\|

*apūjyaḥ putraśāpena svayaṃ brahmā prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*tenaiva brahmaṇo mantraṃ nopāsante vipaścitaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Brahma himself was fallen from graces as a result of cure of his son Narada. He become unadorable. That is why the wise people did not adore Brahma at all.

## \|\| 12.7 \|\|

*nārado guruśāpena gandharvaśca babhūva saḥ \|*  
*kathayāmi suvistīrṇaṃ tadvṛttāntaṃ niśāmaya \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Narada on the other hand was also cursed by Brahma. I am narrating that story to you in details. You please listen to

## \|\| 12.8-9 \|\|

*gandharvarājaḥ sarveṣāṃ gandharvāṇāṃ varo mahān \|*  
*paramaiśvaryyasaṃyuktaḥ putrahīno hi karmaṇā \|\| 8 \|\|*

*gurvājñayā puṣkare sa parameṇa samādhinā \|*  
*tapaścakāra śambhośca kṛpaṇo dīnamānasaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. During those times, there was a king of Gandharvas who was quite respectable and a great king. He had enough of riches with him but he had no son. The beleaguered king with a pam in his heart went to his priest and with his permission he started a yajna in the holy place of Puskara. He started adoring with a devoted heart Lord Shiva.

## \|\| 12.10-11 \|\|

*śivasya kavacaṃ stotraṃ mantraṃ ca dvādaśākṣaram \|*  
*dadau gandharvarājāya vasiṣṭhaśca kṛpānidhiḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*jajāpa paramaṃ mantraṃ divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ mune \|*  
*puṣkare sa nirāhāraḥ putraduḥkhena tāpitaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. The most compassionate Vasistha gave him the kavacha, the prayer and a hymn of 12 letters to the king of the Gandharvas. О sage, with his mind offering prayer, having no sons, the king of Gandharvas remained without food for a hundred divine years reciting the mantras.

## \|\| 12.12 \|\|

*virāme śatavarṣasya dadarśa purataḥ śivam \|*  
*bhāsayaṃtaṃ daśa diśo jvalantaṃ brahmatejasā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. After, a hundred years, Shiva himself appeared before him which illuminated all the ten directions with his lustre.

## \|\| 12.13-14 \|\|

*mahattejassvarūpaṃ ca bhagavantaṃ sanātanam \|*  
*īṣaddhāsaṃ prasannāsyaṃ bhaktānugrahakārakam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*taporūpaṃ tapobījaṃ tapasyāphaladaṃ phalam \|*  
*śaraṇāgatabhaktāya dātāraṃ sarvasampadām \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. His face was bearing a serene smile. He is compassionate to his devotees in the form of tapas and also is seed of tapas. He gave proper reward for his tapas and he himself performs tapas. He fulfils all the wishes of his devotees who come to take refuge under him.

## \|\| 12.15-18 \|\|

*triśūlapaṭṭiśadharaṃ vṛṣabhasthaṃ digambaram \|*  
*śuddha sphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ trinetraṃ candraśekharam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*taptasvarṇaprabhājuṣṭajaṭājāladharaṃ varam \|*  
*nīlakaṇḍaṃ ca sarvajñaṃ nāgayajñopavītakam \|\| 16 \|\|*

*saṃhartāraṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kālaṃ mṛtyuṃjayaṃ param \|*  
*grīṣmamadhyāhnamārttaṇḍakoṭisaṃkāśamīśvaram \|\| 17 \|\|*

*tattvajñānapradaṃ śāṃtaṃ muktidaṃ haribhaktidam \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā nanāma sahasā gandharvo daṇḍavadbhuvi \|\| 18 \|\|*

15-18. He was holding a trident, a pattisa and other weapons. He was mounted over his bull and was without clothes. He had lustre of spotless crystal, three eyes, a crescent over the head and had matted locks of hair resembling the complexion of the molten gold. He had a blue mark over a neck and serpents, were adorning his shoulders. Thus the omnipresent Shiva appears in the form of a child, destroyer of all, the care who overcomes death, had the lustre of crores of sun shining at moon time in summer. He was peaceful and the figure of the bestower of tattvajnana, salvation and devotion of Hpri. Finding Shiva before him, the Gandharvas prostrated before him and started reciting prayer in his favour.

## \|\| 12.19 \|\|

*vasiṣṭhadattastotreṇa tuṣṭāva parameśvaram \|*  
*varaṃ vṛṇuṣveti śivastamuvāca kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*sa yayāce hare bhaktiṃ putraṃ paramavaiṣṇavam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Thereafter the most compassionate Shiva said to him, “Ask for a boon.” The king of Gandharvas then sought for a son who could be a perfect Vaishnava and be devoted to the lord.

## \|\| 12.20 \|\|

*gandharvasya vacaḥ śrutvā cāha sīccandraśekharaḥ \|*  
*uvāca dīnaṃ dīneśo dīnabandhuḥ sanātanam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. On hearing these words of the king of Gandharvas, Lord Shiva having the crescent over him head spoke to him smilingly thus.

## \|\| 12.21 \|\|

*śrīmahādeva uvāca \|*  
*kṛtārthastvaṃ varādekādanyaccarvitacarvaṇam \|*  
*gandharvarāja vṛṇuṣe ko vā tṛpto'timaṅgale \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Lord Mahadeva said, “O king of Gandharvas, you have not satisfied with only one boon. The second boon is therefore like repeating the stuff which has already been crushed. Even otherwise, whatever the boon has been desired by you is also all right. Who will be satisfied with salvation alone? (Because after whatever one gets, he desires much more beyond that)

## \|\| 12.22-23 \|\|

*yasya bhaktirharau vatsa sudṛḍhā sarvamaṅgalā \|*  
*sa samarthaḥ sarvaviśvaṃ pātuṃ karttuṃ ca līlayā \|\| 22 \|\|*

*ātmanaḥ kulakoṭiṃ ca śataṃ mātāmahasya ca \|*  
*puruṣāṇāṃ samuddhṛtya golokaṃ yāti niścitam \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. O Son, the one how is extremely devoted to the lord Hari, he can protect the entire universe and even re-create it. With his devotion, he can redeem a hundred of his earlier generations.

## \|\| 12.24 \|\|

*trividhāni ca pāpāni koṭijanmārjitāni ca \|*  
*nihatya puṇyabhogaṃ ca haridāsyaṃ labhed dhruvam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He is relieved of the sins commuted by him in his earlier birth and achieves the privilege of serving the lord.

## \|\| 12.25 \|\|

*tāvatpatnī sutastāvattāvadaiśvaryyamīpsitam \|*  
*sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ nṛṇāṃ tāvadyāvatkṛṣṇe na mānasam \|\| 25\|\|*

25\. One remained unprivileged in the world like his family, the wife, the son and other worldly riches till such time his mind is not attracted and devoted towards lord Krishna.

## \|\| 12.26-27 \|\|

*kṛṣṇe manasi saṃjāte bhaktikhaḍgo duratyayaḥ \|*  
*narāṇāṃ karmavṛkṣāṇāṃ mūlacchedaṃ karotyaho \|\| 26 \|\|*

*bhavedyeṣāṃ sukṛtināṃ putrāḥ paramavaiṣṇavāḥ \|*  
*kulakoṭiṃ ca teṣāṃ te uddharantyeva līlayā \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Because the sword the devotion of lord Krishna cuts off all the fetters of actions of a human being like the trees. This is quite surprising. The wise people get quite illustrious sons. Those sons redeem their future generation.

## \|\| 12.28 \|\|

*caritārthaḥ pumānekādvaramicchurvarādaho \|*  
*kiṃ vareṇa dvitīyena puṃsāṃ tṛptirna maṅgale \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Though a noble persons gets satisfied with a single boon but instead of that he becomes desirous or getting an other one. This is quite surprising. What is the use of second boon? One is not satisfied with the achieving of the welfare.

## \|\| 12.29-31 \|\|

*dhanaṃ saṃcitamasmākaṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ sudurlabham \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇe bhaktidāsyaṃ ca na vayaṃ dātumutsukāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*varayānyaṃ varaṃ vatsa yatte manasi vāṃchitam \|*  
*indratvamamaratvaṃ vā brahmatvaṃ labha durlabham \|\| 30 \|\|*

*sarvasiddhiṃ mahāyogaṃ jñānaṃ mṛtyujayādikam \|*  
*sukhena sarvaṃ dāsyāmi haridāsyaṃ tyaja dhruvam \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. We have the forest of welfare preserved for the Vaishnavas. One does not became anxious to bestow that devotion and dedication of lord Krishna on others. Therefore, you ask for another appropriate boon. I can bestow etemalship or the place of Indra and even the eternal bliss, all the siddhis and the knowledge to overcome the death, with pleasures. You, however, shed away the desires to be a slave of lord Hari”.

## \|\| 12.32 \|\|

*śaṅkarasya vacaḥ śrutvā śuṣkakaṇṭhoṣṭhatālukaḥ \|*  
*uvāca dīno dīneśaṃ dātāraṃ sarvasampadām \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. On hearing these words of Shiva, the lips, the throat and the mouth of the poor king of Gandharvas dried up. Then mustering courage he appealed to the lord who could bestow of the successes to all.

## \|\| 12.33 \|\|

*gandharva uvāca \|*  
*yatpakṣmacālanenaiva brahmaṇaḥ patanaṃ bhavet \|*  
*tadbrahmatvaṃ svapnatulyaṃ kṛṣṇabhakto na cecchati \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The Gandharva said, “The one who fall with the meagre sight of Brahma, that Brahmana is like a dream and short lived. Nobody will desire for it.

## \|\| 12.34-36 \|\|

*indratvamamaratvaṃ vā siddhiyogādikaṃ śiva \|*  
*jñānaṃ mṛtyujayādyaṃ vā na hi bhaktasya vāñchitam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*sālokyasārṣṭisāmīpyasāyujyaṃ śrīharerapi \|*  
*tatra nirvāṇamokṣaṃ ca na hi vāñchanti vaiṣṇavāḥ \|\| 35\|\|*

*śaśvattatra dṛḍhā bhaktirharidāsyaṃ sudurlabham \|*  
*svapne jāgaraṇe bhaktā vāñchantyevaṃ varaṃ varam \|\| 36 \|\|*

34-36. О Lord, Shiva, the place of Indra, the successes, the Yogic powers, the powers to overcome the death are of no use for a devotee. The four types of the salvation of the lord like Salokya, Sariipya, Samipya, Sayujya and the complete redemption can hardly be desired by a Vaisnnava. I desire the everlasting devotion for lord Krishna in which I should remain engrossed all the times while sleeping or while awake. Therefore, you kindly grant us this boons alone.

## \|\| 12.37 \|\|

*taddāsyaṃ vaiṣṇavasutaṃ dehi kalpataro varam \|*  
*tvāṃ prāpya labhate tuṣṭaṃ varaṃ sarvavaro'varaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. О kalpavrksa, this is the reason, why I need a son who should be devoted to Vishnu and be his slave also. You kindly grant this boon to me because after getting such a boon because with your pleasure in case some one also seeks another boon it is simple cruelty.

## \|\| 12.38 \|\|

*na dāsyasīdaṃ cecchambho varaṃ duṣkṛtinaṃ ca mām \|*  
*kṛtvā hi svaśiraśchedaṃ pradāsyāmi hutāśane \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. О Lord Shiva, in case you do not grant me the desired boon, I shall cut off my head and offer it as a offering in the burning fire”.

## \|\| 12.39 \|\|

*gandharvavacanaṃ śrutvā tamuvāca kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*bhaktaṃ dīnaṃ ca bhakteśo bhaktānugrahakārakaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Thereafter, the compassionate lord, taking a pity on his devotee granted the king of Gandharvas, the boon desired.

## \|\| 12.40-42 \|\|

*śrīśaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*haribhaktiṃ harerdāsyaṃ putraṃ paramavaiṣṇavam \|*  
*cirāyuṣaṃ ca guṇinaṃ śaśvatsusthirayauvanam \|\| 40 \|\|*

*jñāninaṃ sundaravaraṃ gurubhaktaṃ jitendriyam \|*  
*gandharvarājapravaraṃ varemaṃ labha mā śucaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*ityuktvā śaṃkarastasmājjagāma svālayaṃ mune \|*  
*gandharvarājaḥ santuṣṭa ājagāma svamandiram \|\| 42 \|\|*

40-42. Lord Shiva said, “you will get a son who will be a perfect Vaishnava devoted to the lord. You achieve these boons and don’t worry in your mind. Besides being a Vaisanava your son will also be a great devotee of lord Hari. He will remain always useful, an intellectual most beautiful, devoted to the teachers and shall well control all his senses”. Saying this lord Shiva went to his abode and the Gandharvas also went back home.

## \|\| 12.43 \|\|

*praphullamānasāḥ sarve mānavāḥ siddhakarmaṇaḥ \|*  
*nāradastasya bhāryyāyāṃ lebhe janma ca bhārate \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. On meeting with successes the mind of all the people get delighted blossoming like a lotus flower. Narada was born out the womb of the devoted wife of the king of Gandharva.

## \|\| 12.44 \|\|

*suṣāva putraṃ sā vṛddhā parvate gandhamādane \|*  
*gururvasiṣṭho bhagavānnāma cakre yathocitam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The old wife of king of Gandharvas gave birth to a child at Gandhamadana mountain and a teacher Vasistha performed the yajnopavita ceremony of the son.

## \|\| 12.45 \|\|

*bālakasya ca tasyaiva maṅgalaṃ maṅgale dine \|*  
*upa śabdo'dhikārthaśca pūjye ca barhaṇaḥ pumān \|*  
*pūjyānāmadhiko bālastenopabarhaṇābhidhaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. This child has been quite devoted to the lord. Therefore, he was given the name of Upabarhana, by Vasistha.

## Chapter 13

## \|\| 13.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*putrotsave ca ratnāni dhanāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*gandharvarājaḥ pradadau brāhmaṇebhyo mudā'nvitaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said, the king of Gandharva on the birth of son distributed several type of gems and riches among the Brahmanas and others.

## \|\| 13.2 \|\|

*upabarhaṇastu kālena harermantraṃ sudurlabham \|*  
*vasiṣṭhena tu samprāpya sa cakre duṣkaraṃ tapaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. On coming to age the boy Upabarhana acquired through the aid of the teacher Vasistha acquired a mantra and he started performing severe austerities.

## \|\| 13.3 \|\|

*ekadā gaṇḍakītīre taṃ ca samprāptayauvanam \|*  
*gandharvapatnyo dadṛśurmūrcchāmāpuśca tatkṣaṇam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Once after becoming a youth when the Upabarhana was roaming about the bank of river Gandakl. He was supported by a large number of Gandharvas themselves. All of them were fainted at the sight of the boy.

## \|\| 13.4 \|\|

*tāśca tīvraṃ tapaḥ kṛtvā prāṇānsaṃtyajya yogataḥ \|*  
*pañcāśattā babhūvuśca kanyāścitrarathasya ca \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Thereafter thus people themselves it is there entered their life after performing great tapas. Thereafter they were as the daughter of Gandharva king Chitraratha.

## \|\| 13.5 \|\|

*upabarhaṇagandharvaṃ tāśca taṃ vavrire patim \|*  
*mudā mālā dadustasmai kāmukyaḥ piturājñayā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Thereafter those girls thereafter they married the Gandharva name Upabarhana with the permission of their father they garlanded the Gandharva boy.

## \|\| 13.6 \|\|

*gṛhītvā tāśca gandharvo yuvā susthirayauvanaḥ \|*  
*divyaṃ trilakṣavarṣaṃ ca reme rahasi kāmukaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The possessionate Gandharva also started reciting in lonely place after marring those girls. He spent three lakh of divining years with them and enjoyed the youthful life.

## \|\| 13.7-8 \|\|

*tato'pi suciraṃ rājyaṃ kṛtvā tābhiḥ sahāniśam \|*  
*jagāma brahmaṇaḥ sthānaṃ harigāthāṃ jagau mune \|\| 7 \|\|*

*dṛṣṭvā sa rambhārambhorunartane kaṭhinaṃ stanam \|*  
*babhūva skhalanaṃ tasya gandharvasya mahātmanaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. О sage, thereafter he was crowned as a king and started enjoying the life pleasure with those ladies. Once he went to Brahma and joined a function there reciting his glory. He found there Ramba dancing in a most attractive manner whose body was like a trunk of banana tree. Her breast well developed and quite hard. The semen of that Gandharva felt at the sight of Rambha.

## \|\| 13.9-10 \|\|

*drutaṃ tatyāja saṅgītaṃ mūrcchāṃ prāpa sabhātale \|*  
*uccaiḥ prajahasurdevā brahmā kopācchaśāpa tam \|\| 9 \|\|*

*vraja tvaṃ śūdrayoniṃ ca gāndharvīṃ tanumutsṛja \|*  
*kāle vaiṣṇavasaṃsargānmatputrastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. As a result of this not only was he deprived of the musical concert but also was fainted there. All the guards started laughing aloud. Thereafter Brahma pronouncing a curse of him said shady, the body of Gandharva, you will busy was a Shudra thereafter while keeping the company of Vaishnava your believe as my son and get will established in that position.

## \|\| 13.11 \|\|

*vinā vipattermahimā puṃsāṃ naiva bhavetsuta \|*  
*sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ krameṇa prabhavediti \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O Son, without facing the adversities the people don’t achieve gold. All the people of the world alternatively gets the pleasures and pains.

## \|\| 13.12 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā sa vidhiragacchatpuṣkarād gṛham \|*  
*upabarhaṇagandharvassa jahau tāṃ tanuṃ tadā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Saying this, Brahma then went to the holly place of Puskara. О Upabarhana, the Gandharva then died in instantaneously.

## \|\| 13.13-18 \|\|

*mūlādhāraṃ svādhiṣṭhānaṃ maṇipūramanāhatam \|*  
*viśuddhamājñākhyaṃ ceti bhittvā ṣaṭcakrameva ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

*iḍāṃ suṣumnāṃ medhāṃ ca piṅgalāṃ prāṇahāriṇīm \|*  
*sarvajñānapradāṃ caiva manassaṃyaminīṃ tathā \|\| 14 \|\|*

*viśuddhāṃ ca niruddhāṃ ca vāyusaṃcāriṇīṃ tathā \|*  
*tejaśśuṣkakarīṃ caiva balapuṣṭikarīṃ tathā \|\| 15 \|\|*

*buddhisaṃcāriṇīṃ caiva jñānajṛmbhanakāriṇīm \|*  
*sarvaprāṇaharāṃ caiva punarjīvanakāriṇīm \|\| 16 \|\|*

*etāḥ ṣoḍaśadhā nāḍīrbhittvā vai haṃsameva ca \|*  
*manasā sahitaṃ brahmarandhramānīya yogataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*sthitvā muhūrtamātmānamātmanyeva yuyoja ha \|*  
*jātismaraśca yogīndraḥ saṃprāpa brahma śaunaka \|\| 18 \|\|*

13-18. He first of all penetrated through the Satcakra name Muladhara, Svadhisthana Manipura, Anahata, Vis'uddha and Ajna developed ida, Sus umna, Medha, Pingala, Pranaharini, Sarvajnanaprada, Manahsamymini, Visuddha, Niruddha, Vayu-sancarini and which derives the lustre and one which increase the powers, increase intelligence, developed wisdom, causes death to all and grants rebirth, served these sixteen types of vein. Thereafter with the application of yogic practices he brought the mind (Manas) with Prana into Brahmarandhra and he established himself firmly in yogic posture for some time. О Shaunaka! thereafter that best of the Yogis, remembering his previous birth Upabarhana achieved the state of salvation.

## \|\| 13.19-21 \|\|

*vīṇāṃ tritantrīṃ duṣprāpyāṃ vāmaskandhe nidhāya ca \|*  
*śuddhasphaṭikamālāṃ ca vidhṛtvā dakṣiṇe kare \|\| 19 \|\|*

*saṃjalpanparamaṃ brahma vedasāraṃ parātparam \|*  
*paraṃ nistārabījaṃ ca kṛṣṇa ityakṣaradvayam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*prācyāṃ kṛtvā śiraḥsthānaṃ paścime caraṇadvayam \|*  
*nidhāya darbhaśayane śayānaḥ puruṣo yathā \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. О Shaunaka, he then started reciting the name of two letters i.e. Krishna holding a flute on three strings in his right hand and a garland of pure crystal bits which is the absence of Vedas and can redeem the people at large. He then slept other the bed at Kushasana keeping his head towards the east and the legs towards the west as if somebody was sleeping.

## \|\| 13.22-25 \|\|

*gandharvarājastaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhāryyayā saha tatkṣaṇam \|*  
*yogena brahma samprāpa śrīkṛṣṇaṃ manasā smaran \|\| 22 \|\|*

*patnyaśca bāndhavāḥ sarve vilapya rurudurbhuśam \|*  
*jagmuḥ krameṇa śokārttā mohitā viṣṇumāyayā \|\| 23 \|\|*

*pañcāśadyoṣitāṃ madhye pradhānā mahiṣī ca yā \|*  
*sādhvī mālāvatī nāmnā paramā preyasī varā \|\| 24 \|\|*

*uccai ruroda sā tīvraṃ kāntaṃ kṛtvā ca vakṣasi \|*  
*ityuvāca ca śokārttā kāntaṃ saṃbodhya caiva hi \|\| 25 \|\|*

22-25. When his father, the king of Gandharva found him meeting an end with his life in this manner he himself with his wife ended his life recited the name of Krishna, by yogic practices. They thus achieved the place of Lord eternal. Thereafter all the relations and brothers of Upabarhana started crying aloud lamenting at same time. Influenced with the illusion of Vishnu they were filled with grieve they went to place where his body was placed. Of all the fifty spouses of Upabarhana, Malavati happened to be a keep queen. She was quite chaste and was quite deer to her husband. She started crying keeping the head of her husband in her lap.

## \|\| 13.26 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*he nātha ramaṇa śreṣṭha vidagdha rasikeśvara \|*  
*darśanaṃ dehi māṃ bandho nimagnāṃ śokasāgare \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Malavati said, “O Lord, you are the best of all the relation. I am getting drowned in the ocean of grieves. You kindly appear before me.

## \|\| 13.27-33 \|\|

*visrambhake suvasane ramye candanakānane \|*  
*puṣpabhadrānadītīre puṣpodyāne manohare \|\| 27 \|\|*

*candanācalasānnidhye cārucandanakānane \|*  
*puṣpacandanatalpe ca candanānilavāsite \|\| 28 \|\|*

*gandhamādanaśailaikadeśe ramye nadītaṭe \|*  
*puṃskokilanināde ca mālatījālaśālini \|\| 29 \|\|*

*śrīśaile śrīvane divye śrīnivāsaniṣevite \|*  
*śrīyukte śrīpadāmbhoje pūte'cyutakṛte śubhe \|\| 30 \|\|*

*purā yā yā kṛtā krīḍā vasante rahasi tvayā \|*  
*mayā ca durhṛdā sārddhaṃ tayā vai dūyate manaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*sudhātulyena vacasā siktā'haṃ ca purā tvayā \|*  
*dūyate satataṃ tena paramātmā'tidāruṇam \|\| 32 \|\|*

*sādhunā saha saṃsargo vaikuṇṭhādapi durlabhaḥ \|*  
*aho tato'tivicchedo maraṇādapi duṣkaraḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

27-33. In the painted and charming place in the creation of sandal trees over the bank of river Bhadra in the beautiful grove near the Malaya mountain and served by the breeze having the fragrance of sandal over the bed of sandal of flowers in the region of the Gandhamadana mountain there is a place which is quite charming over the bank of river. It is infested with the Cuckoos. It is filled with milky flowers and clean water. It happened to be a place of Vishnu that also Lakshmi. All the spots which you had with me are easy my mind initially you use to serve him with your nectar-like speech without it my entire body is suffering from pain. The mind of noble person is better then the pleasure of the Vainkutha. Having been deprived such a noble person which becomes more painful.

## \|\| 13.34-35 \|\|

*tasmātteṣāṃ ca vicchedaḥ sādhuśokakaraḥ paraḥ \|*  
*tato'pi bandhuvicchedaḥ śokaḥ paramadāruṇaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*tato'patyaviyogo hi maraṇādatiricyate \|*  
*sarvasmātpatibhedo hi tatparaṃ nāsti saṅkaṭam \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. Therefore, the wicket people are bound to vanish and for the noble people it is quite painful. It is more painful. It is more painful for the close relations to see the death of someone deer to them besides this the separation of one own son is more painful of all the grieves the separation from someone more painful. There is no other misery greater then this.

## \|\| 13.36 \|\|

*śayane bhojane snāne svapne jāgaraṇe'pi ca \|*  
*svāmivicchedaduḥkhaṃ ca nūtanaṃ ca dine dine \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Because sleeping eating, waving, sleeping and awake the separation from the husband gets more and more painful with the passage of time.

## \|\| 13.37 \|\|

*sarvaśokaṃ vismaretstrī svāmisaṃyogamātrataḥ \|*  
*bandhumanyaṃ na paśyāmi yaṃ dṛṣṭvā vismaretpatim \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The woman forgets every grieves in the company of her husband but I do not find any other relation who could make the forget my husband.

## \|\| 13.38 \|\|

*nāto viśiṣṭaṃ paśyāmi bāndhavaṃ svāminā vinā \|*  
*sādhvīnāṃ kulajātānāmityāha kamalodbhavaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Brahma himself emphasised on this that for a chaste lady on high quite family there is no other better relation then the husband.

## \|\| 13.39 \|\|

*he digīśāśca dikpālā he dharma tvaṃ prajāpate \|*  
*girīśa kamalākānta patidānaṃ ca dehi me \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. О the directions and the ruling Dikpalas, О Dharma, О Prajapati, О Lordship О Lord of Laks ml, you kindly given me back my husband.

## \|\| 13.40 \|\|

*ityuktvā virahārttā sā kanyā citrarathasya ca \|*  
*mūrcchāṃ saṃprāpa tatraiva durgame gahane vane \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Thus spoking the daughter of Citraratha afflicted by seperation felted in the dense forest.

## \|\| 13.41 \|\|

*vicetanā tatra tasthau kāntaṃ kṛtvā svavakṣasi \|*  
*paripūrṇaṃ divānaktaṃ sarvadevaiśca rakṣitā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. She remains in that unconscious condition placing the head of her husband over her chest for a day and a night. The Gods protected her at that time.

## \|\| 13.42 \|\|

*prabhāte cetanāṃ prāpya vilalāpa bhṛśaṃ muhuḥ \|*  
*ityuvāca punastatra hariṃ saṃbodhya sā satī \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The nest morning after regaining her sense she started lamenting again and again. Thereafter the chaste lady spoke again addressing the lord thus.

## \|\| 13.43-44 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*he kṛṣṇa jagatāṃ nātha nātha nāhaṃ jagadbahiḥ \|*  
*tvameva jagatāṃ pātā māṃ na pāsi kathaṃ prabho \|\| 43 \|\|*

*ayaṃ bharttā'sya bhāryyā'haṃ mameti tava māyayā \|*  
*tvameva sambhavo bharttā sarveṣāṃ sarvakāraṇaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. Malavati said, “O Lord, Krishna you are the lord of the entire universe. I also a part of the universe. О Lord, you protect the world. Then why am I deprived of the protection”? My husband is lying here and I am his wife. This feeling of mine is your individual. You are the lord of everyone. It happen like this. What else can I say. Because you happened to be cause of every things.

## \|\| 13.45 \|\|

*gandharvaḥ karmaṇā kāntaḥ kāntā'haṃ cāsya karmaṇā \|*  
*kva gataḥ karmabhogānte kutra saṃsthāpya māṃ priyām \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Because of my own deed Gandharva became my husband quite because of my own deeds I became his wife and after the end influence of the good deeds he left a dearest wife like me.

## \|\| 13.46 \|\|

*ko vā kasya patiḥ putraḥ kā vā kasya priyā prabho \|*  
*saṃyunakti vidhātā ca vinayukti ca karmaṇā \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О Lord, who would happened to be once husband or the son and who happens to be the believed of anyone. It is destiny which connects the people in relationship.

## \|\| 13.47 \|\|

*saṃyoge paramānando viyoge prāṇasaṅkaṭam \|*  
*śaśvajjagati mūrkhasya nātmārāmasya niścitam \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. All the pleasures of the world are achieved by people by providence and in the separation the life become miserable. Such if the noble people who are well-versed in the movement of the sole are free from grief.

## \|\| 13.48 \|\|

*naśvaro viṣayaḥ satyaṃ bhuvi bhogaśca bāndhavaḥ \|*  
*svayaṃ tyaktaḥ sukhāyaiva duḥkhāya tyājitaḥ paraiḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. It is true that all the pleasures of world and the relatives are short lives. It would, therefore, be better to warrant disowned them because if someone else makes such deeds those pleasures it became painful.

## \|\| 13.49 \|\|

*tasmātsantaḥ svayaṃ tyaktvā paramaiśvaryamīpsitam \|*  
*dhyāyante satataṃ kṛṣṇa pādapadmaṃ nirāpadam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Therefore, the simple people voluntarily disowned and pleasure and devoted their mind towards the devotion of Lord Krishna and always they all the lotus-like feet of the Lord.

## \|\| 13.50 \|\|

*sarvatra jñāninaḥ santaḥ kā strī jñānavatī bhuvi \|*  
*tato mahyaṃ vimūḍhāyai dātumarhati vāñchitam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. There are unable quite well-versed intellectual people everywhere on earth but one quite hardly come across and. Therefore, for a foolish persons like me you grant me desired wish.

## \|\| 13.51 \|\|

*na me vāñchā'maratve ca śakratve mokṣavartmani \|*  
*imaṃ kāntaṃ varaṃ dehi caturvargakaraṃ param \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. I am not a desirous eternity, the place of Brahma, the place of Indra or the salvation. Therefore, I should be given back this husband of mine whose like four varnas (castes) for me.

## \|\| 13.52 \|\|

*yāvatī kāminījātirjagatyāṃ jagadīśvara \|*  
*kasyaicinna hi dattaśca tena dhātredṛśaḥ patiḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. О Lord of the universe, all the ladies in the world none as a virtuous husband given by Brahma, as I have.

## \|\| 13.53 \|\|

*tasmai dattā guṇāḥ sarve rūpāṇi vividhāni ca \|*  
*suśīlāni ca sarvāṇi cāmaratvaṃ vinā hare \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. О Lord, Brahma has bestow the all virtues, the beauty and the charming nature to them except the immortality.

## \|\| 13.54 \|\|

*rūpeṇa ca guṇenaiva tejasā vikrameṇa ca \|*  
*jñānena śāntyā santuṣṭyā haritulyaḥ prabhurmama \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. My husband possess the beauty, the glory, the virtues, the prowess, the intelligence, the peaceful, nature and the satisfaction, thus he is like the lord himself.

## \|\| 13.55-56 \|\|

*haribhakto harisamo gāmbhīrye sāgaro yathā \|*  
*dīptimānsūryyatulyaśca śuddho vahnisamastathā \|\| 55 \|\|*

*candratulyaḥ sudṛśyaśca kandarpasamasundaraḥ \|*  
*buddhyā bṛhaspatisamaḥ kāvye kavisamastathā \|\| 56 \|\|*

55-56. My husband is like the devotees of Lord Hari and is like a ocean in death. He is illustrious like sun, shining like the fire, charming like the moon, beautiful like the Lord of love, extremely intelligent and illiteracy feel he can be compared with Sukracarya.

## \|\| 13.57-59 \|\|

*vāṇī ca sarvaśāstrajñā pratibhāyāṃ bhṛgoriva \|*  
*kuberatulyo dhanavānmahāndātā manoriva \|\| 57 \|\|*

*dharme dharmasamo dharmī satye satyavratādhikaḥ \|*  
*kumāratulyastapasā svācāre brahmaṇā samaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

*aiśvaryye śakratulyaśca sahiṣṇuḥ pṛthivīsamaḥ \|*  
*evambhūto mṛtaḥ kāntaḥ prāṇā yānti na me katham \|\| 59 \|\|*

57-59. His speech is the essence of all the scriptures. He is like bright in appearance and in riches he is like Kubera. He is the great person is charity like Manu and in the performing of religious rights he is like Dharma himself. He is quite truthful and is a great tapasvl like Sanaka and others. In conduct, he gets Brahma and possess grace like Indra himself. He is patient like the earth. Such a husband of mine is lying died here is about to leave me.

## \|\| 13.60 \|\|

*are surā yajñabhājo ghṛtaṃ bhoktuṃ kṣamā bhuvi \|*  
*kṣaṇenāyajñabhājaśca kariṣyāmi svalīlayā \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. О Gods, you perceive your share of yajna performed on earth I shall now deprived of your share of yajna that I performed..

## \|\| 13.61-62 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa jagatkānta nāhameva jagadbahiḥ \|*  
*śīghraṃ jīvaya matkāntamanyathā tvāṃ śapāmyaham \|\| 61 \|\|*

*prajāpate putraśāpāttvamapūjyo mahītale \|*  
*tavaivānadhikāritvaṃ kariṣyāmyadhunā bhave \|\| 62 \|\|*

61-62. О Narayana, О lord of the entire universe, I am also a stream with the universe. You bring back to life my husband otherwise I am going to pronounce a curse on you. О Prajapati, you are not adorned honoured because of the curse of your son. I shall now deprive you of the right of honour by pronouncing a curse.

## \|\| 13.63 \|\|

*he śambho jñānalopaṃ te kariṣyāmi śapena ca \|*  
*dharmalopaṃ ca dharmasya kariṣyāmyeva līlayā \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. О Lord Shiva, I shall deprived you of all your glory and by pronouncing a curse on you and also separate the actions of dharma.

## \|\| 13.64 \|\|

*yamādhikāraṃ dūre ca kariṣyāmi na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*satyaṃ kālaṃ śapiṣyāmi mṛtyukanyāṃ suniṣṭhurām \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. I shall deprive dharma of a rights. There is no doubt about it. Similarly I can also pronounce a curse on cruel God of death and also his daughter.

## \|\| 13.65 \|\|

*śapāmi sarvānatraiva jarāṃ vyādhiṃ vinā'dhunā \|*  
*vyādhinā jarayā mṛtyurna hyabhūcca patermama \|\| 65\|\|*

65\. -My husband has not meet with his end because of old age or my ailment. Therefore living this to, I shall pronounce a curse on all adults.

## \|\| 13.66-67 \|\|

*ityuktvā kauśikītīre cāgacchacchaptumeva tān \|*  
*mālāvatī mahāsādhvī śavaṃ kṛtvā svavakṣasi \|\| 66 \|\|*

*tāṃ śaptumudyatāṃ dṛṣṭvā brahmā devapurogamaḥ \|*  
*jagāma śaraṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ tīraṃ kṣīrapayonidheḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

66-67. Thus saying the great industrious and chaste Malavati keeping the head of her dead husband in her lap got ready to pronounce a curse on all the gods. With the end in voice she then and went to bank of the KausikI river. Finding her ready to pronounce a curse all the Gods including Brahma rushed to the ocean of Milk and took refuge under Lord Vishnu.

## \|\| 13.68 \|\|

*tatra snātvā ca tuṣṭāva paramātmānamīśvaram \|*  
*viṣṇuṃ brahmā jagatkāntamityuvāca ha bhītavat \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Taking his bath there the panicky Brahma started offering prayers to lord Vishnu who is considered the Lord of the Universe.

## \|\| 13.69 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*upabarhaṇapatnī sā kanyā citrarathasya ca \|*  
*kāntahetośca mā devāñchapettvaṃ rakṣa mādhava \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Brahma said, О Mahadeva, Malavati the daughter of Chitraratha and the wife of Upabarhana is going to pronounce a curse on me and all the Gods. You protect us from her curse.

## \|\| 13.70 \|\|

*smaranti sādhavaḥ santo japanti munayo mudā \|*  
*svapne jāgaraṇe caiva sarvakāryyeṣu mādhavam \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. All saints and sages recite the name of Lord Krishna pleasantly while sleeping or while awake.

## \|\| 13.71 \|\|

*śaraṇāgatadīnārttaparitrāṇaparāyaṇa \|*  
*rakṣa rakṣa hṛṣīkeśa vrajāmaḥ śaraṇaṃ vayam \|\| 71 \|\|*
