71\. O Lord of all the organ of senses, you always protect thus occasion and take refuge under you. You protect us, we took refuge under you.

## \|\| 13.72 \|\|

*pūjā me putraśāpena vihatā sāmprataṃ prabho \|*  
*adhikārahataṃ māṃ ca kurute mālatī satī \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. О Lord, my adoration always come to an end because of the curse of my own son. Now I am going to be deprived of all my rights with the pronounce of the curse by Malavati.

## \|\| 13.73 \|\|

*sarvādhikāro brahmāṇḍe tvayā dattaḥ purā prabho \|*  
*sampadetādṛśī nātha yāsyatyevādhunā mama \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. О Lord, you have distributed all the rights of mine. In the entire universe, О Lord now I am going to be deprived this as a whole.

## \|\| 13.74 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*tvayā dattaṃ mahājñānaṃ guptaṃ sarveṣu durlabham \|*  
*śatamanvantaratapaḥphalena puṣkare purā \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Brahma said, in earlier time I perform tapas as in the region of Puskara for a period of 100 manvantaras as a result of which I achieve the eternal knowledge. It is quite secret and rare.

## \|\| 13.75 \|\|

*aiśvaryaṃ vā dhanaṃ vā'pi vidyā vā vikramo'thavā \|*  
*jñānasya paramārthasya kalāṃ nārhaṃti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. The riches, intelligence, knowledge and prowess cannot be equated with the 16lh part of that knowledge.

## \|\| 13.76 \|\|

*sarvājñātaṃ sarvaguptamatyantaṃ durlabhaṃ param \|*  
*mama tattvajñānaratnaṃ śāpānniryāti yoṣitaḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. The most secret and rarest and the best of the knowledge in tattvajnana it is going to vanish because of the curse of a Lady.

## \|\| 13.77 \|\|

*aho pativratātejaḥ sarveṣāṃ tejasāṃ param \|*  
*tejo'nalena dagdhaṃ māṃ rakṣa rakṣa hare hare \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. This is really surprising that the glory of chaste lady is so powerful. Therefore, О Lord, I am going to burnt out in the fire of time glory. Therefore, you kindly protect me.

## \|\| 13.78 \|\|

*dharma uvāca \|*  
*sarvaratnātparaṃ ratnaṃ dharma eva sanātanaḥ \|*  
*yāsyatyevaṃvidho dharmastvayā dattaḥ purā prabho \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. Dharma said: О Lord, in the ancient times Dharma was bestow which is the best of all the gems and eternal. It is being snatched away from me.

## \|\| 13.79 \|\|

*saptamanvantaratapaḥphalena parameśvara \|*  
*prāpto dharmo 'dhunā yāti śāpena yoṣitaḥ prabhau \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. О Lord of all the universe ! I was bestowed with the same after performing of tapas for seven manvantaras. But О Lord that Dharma to is being snatched away from me because О the curse of that chaste lady.

## \|\| 13.80 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*yajñabhājo ghṛtabhujo vayameva tvayā kṛtāḥ \|*  
*yoṣicchāpena tatsarvamadhunā yāti mādhava \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. The God said: О Mahadeva, the duty of consuming of ghee, the clarified butter in the fire utter was interested by you. Because of the curse of the lady we are going to be deprived of it.

## \|\| 13.81-82 \|\|

*ityuktvā saṃyatāḥ sarve tasthustatra bhayārditāḥ \|*  
*etasminnantare'kasmādvāgbabhūvāśarīriṇī \|\| 81 \|\|*

*yūyaṃ gacchata tanmūlaṃ viprarūpī janārdanaḥ \|*  
*paścādyāsyati śāntyarthamiti vo rakṣaṇāya ca \|\| 82 \|\|*

81-82. The panic-stricken Gods stood there attentively after speaking of this. At the same time the divine voice from the sky declared, as all of you should go to Malavati thereafter the Lord JanaMana will also reach there in the form of Brahma.

## \|\| 13.83 \|\|

*śrutvā tadvacanaṃ devāḥ prahṛṣṭamanasonmukhāḥ \|*  
*jagmurmālāvatīsthānaṃ kauśikītīramīśvarāḥ \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. On hearing the divine voice mind of all the Gods filled with delight. All of them then reach the bank of the Kausiki river, there the chaste lady was present.

## \|\| 13.84-90 \|\|

*tāmeva dadṛśurdevā devīṃ mālāvatīṃ satīm \|*  
*ratnasārendrabhūṣābhirujjvalāṃ kamalākalām \|\| 84 \|\|*

*vahniśuddhāṃśukādhānāṃ sindūrabindubhūṣitām \|*  
*śaraccandraprabhāṃ śāntāṃ dyotayantīṃ diśastviṣā \|\| 85\|\|*

*patisevāmahādharmacirasañcitatejasā \|*  
*prajvalantīṃ supradīptaśikhāṃ vahnerivottamām \|\| 86 \|\|*

*yogāsanaṃ kurvatī ca śavavakṣaḥsthalasthitām \|*  
*suramyāṃ svāmino vīṇāṃ bibhratīṃ dakṣiṇe kare \|\| 87 \|\|*

*tarjanyaṅguṣṭhakoṭibhyāṃ śuddhasphaṭikamālikām \|*  
*bhaktyā snehena kāntasya bibhratīṃ yogamudrayā \|\| 88 \|\|*

*cārucampakavarṇābhāṃ bimboṣṭhīṃ ratnamālinīm \|*  
*yathā ṣoḍaśavarṣīyāṃ śaśvatsusthirayauvanām \|\| 89 \|\|*

*bṛhannitambabhārārttāṃ pīnaśroṇipayodharām \|*  
*paśyantīṃ śavamīśasya śubhadṛṣṭyā punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 90 \|\|*

84-90. Не was bearing all the ornaments studded with gems and she looked like the Goddess Lakshmi as they were purified in a fire. Her forehead was adorned with Kumkum. She had a lustre of the moon in the winter season. Her lustre illumined of the directions. She performed great religious feet and was having a glory like the burning fire. She was seated there embracing the body of her husband on the ground and she holds the charming flute on her husband in a right hand. She was appearing the beautiful garland of flowers and looked like a damsel of sixteen years old and having a sound. Her chest was broad and stiff. She was presently looking the body of her husband.

## \|\| 13.91 \|\|

*evambhūtāṃ ca tāṃ dṛṣṭvā devāste vismayaṃ yayuḥ \|*  
*sthagitāṃ ca kṣaṇaṃ tatra dhārmikā dharmabhīravaḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. Finding Malavati in this condition all the gods were extremely surprised. All of them were quite noble and therefore, they stood their consoling themselves for sometimes.

## Chapter 14

## \|\| 14.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*tatra sthitvā kṣaṇaṃ devā brahmeśānapurogamāḥ \|*  
*yayurmālāvatīmūlaṃ paraṃ maṅgaladāyakāḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said – With Brahma and Shiva made to lead the gods and bestow welfare to all, they reached the place of Malavati in a moment.

## \|\| 14.2 \|\|

*mālāvatī surāndṛṣṭvā praṇanāma prativratā \|*  
*ruroda kāntaṃ saṃsthāpya devānāṃ sannidhau mune \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. О Saga, The chaste lady Malavati bowed in reverence to the gods on meeting them and placing the body of her husband before them started crying.

## \|\| 14.3-6 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra kaścidbrāhmaṇabālakaḥ \|*  
*ājagāma surāṇāṃ ca sabhāmatimanoharaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*daṇḍī chatrī śuklavāsā bibhrattilakamujjvalam \|*  
*dīrghapustakahastaśca supraśāntaśca susmitaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*candanokṣitasarvāṅgaḥ prajvalanbrahmatejasā \|*  
*surānsaṃbhāṣya tatraiva vismitānviṣṇumāyayā \|\| 5 \|\|*

*tatrovāsa sabhāmadhye tārāmadhye yathā śaśī \|*  
*uvāca devānsarvāṃśca mālatīṃ ca vicakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

3-6. In the meantime, in the assembly of the gods, a beautiful Brahmana boy arrived, holding a staff and an umbrella in his hands, wearing white garments, with his forehead painted with sandal-paste, carrying a huge manuscript in his hand, wearing a peaceful appearance and a serene smile on the face. All his limbs were painted with the paste of sandalwood. The boy having the lustre of Brahmana started talking to the gods reaching there, who appeared to have been seated with the illusion of lord Vishnu. In the assembly of the gods, the Brahmana boy looked like the moon among the stars. He then spoke to Malavati.

## \|\| 14.7 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*kathamatra surāḥ sarve brahmeśānapurogamāḥ \|*  
*svayaṃ vidhātā jagatāṃ sraṣṭā vai kena karmaṇā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Brahmana said: “Why have the gods arrived with Brahma, the creator of the universe and Shiva, at this place”?

## \|\| 14.8-10 \|\|

*sarvabrahmāṇḍasaṃharttā śambhuratra svayaṃ vibhuḥ \|*  
*aho trijagatāṃ sākṣī dharmo vai sarvakarmaṇām \|\| 8 \|\|*

*kathaṃ raviḥ kathaṃ candraḥ kathamatra hutāśanaḥ \|*  
*kathaṃ kālo mṛtyukanyā kathaṃ vā'tra yamādayaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*he mālāvati te kroḍe ko'tiśuṣkaśśavo'naghe \|*  
*jīvitāyāḥ kathaṃ mūle yoṣitaśca pumāñchavaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

8-10. Shiva who is the destroyer of the entire universe is also present here and surprisingly Dharma too who stands witness to the deeds of all, it also present here. Why have the gods like Surya, the Moon, the fire, Yama, the god of death have arrived here? О Malavati, О sinless one, whose dead body which is dried up, are you carrying in your lap? “How can a lady keep the dead body of a man like this?”

## \|\| 14.11 \|\|

*ityuktvā tāṃśca tāṃ vipro virarāma sabhātale \|*  
*mālāvatī taṃ praṇamya samuvāca vicakṣaṇam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. After speaking to the gods and Malavati he kept quiet. Thereafter Malavati spoke offering her salutation to the Brahmana.

## \|\| 14.12 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*ānandapūrvakaṃ vande viprarūpaṃ janārdanam \|*  
*tuṣṭā devā haristuṣṭo yasya puṣpajalena ca \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Malavati said — “O Janardana, you have come in the fonn of a Brahmana. I bow in reverence to you with a pleasing mind. The gods and lord Vishnu feel pleased with the flowers and water offered to him.

## \|\| 14.13 \|\|

*avadhānaṃ kuru vibho śokārtāyā nivedane \|*  
*samā kṛpā satāṃ śaśvadyogyā yogye kṛpāvatām \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. О lord, you kindly listen attentively to the prayer of a grief-stricken person like me, becausethe grace of the noble people benefits equally the competent and incompetent people.

## \|\| 14.14 \|\|

*upabarhaṇabhāryyā'haṃ kanyā citrarathasya ca \|*  
*sarve mālāvatīṃ kṛtvā vadante viprapuṅgava \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O best of the Brahmanas I am the daughter of Chitraratha and the wife of Upabarhana; I am known by the name of Malavati.

## \|\| 14.15-16 \|\|

*divyaṃ lakṣayugaṃ ramye sthāne sthāne manohare \|*  
*kṛtā svacchandataḥ krīḍā cānena svāminā saha \|\| 15 \|\|*

*priye sneho hi sādhvīnāṃ yāvānviprendra yoṣitām \|*  
*sarva śāstrānusāreṇa jānāsi tvaṃ vicakṣaṇa \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. I have roamed about in the company of this husband of mine at the attractive and beautiful places for a lakh of divine years. О best of the Brahmanas, О intelligent one, the chaste women are extremely attached to their husbands. This is well known in the scriptures as well as to you.

## \|\| 14.17 \|\|

*akasmādbrahmaṇaḥ śāpātprāṇāṃstatyāja matpatiḥ \|*  
*devānuddiśya vilape yathā jīvati matpatiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Му husband has met with his end because of the curse of Brahma. I am lamenting before the gods in order to bring back my husband to life.

## \|\| 14.18 \|\|

*svakāryyasādhane sarve vyagrāśca jagatītale \|*  
*bhāvābhāvaṃ na jānanti kevalaṃ svārthatatparāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Because all the people in the universe get devoted to the accomplishment of their tasks. No one is concerned about the profit or the loss but everyone is concerned with the meeting of his own selfish ends.

## \|\| 14.19-20 \|\|

*sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ bhayaṃ śokaṃ santāpaḥ karmaṇāṃ nṛṇām \|*  
*aiśvaryyaṃ paramānando janma mṛtyuśca mokṣaṇam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*devāśca sarvaja nakā dātāraḥ karmaṇāṃ phalam \|*  
*karttāraḥ karmavṛkṣāṇāṃ mūlacchedaṃ ca līlayā \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. The pleasures, the pain, the grief, the fear, lamenting, riches, bliss, birth, death and salvation are achieved by the people as a result of their own deeds. The gods are the creators of everyone and they also bestow the results of ones deeds. Simultaneously with your own divine play, they can also uproot the tree of one’s actions.

## \|\| 14.21-22 \|\|

*na hi devātparo bandhurna hi devātparo balī \|*  
*dayāvānna hi devācca na ca dātā tataḥ paraḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*sarvāndevānahaṃ yāce patidānaṃ mamepsitam \|*  
*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ phaladāṃśca suradrumān \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. Because no one else is a better friend than the gods, no one possesses greater prowess than the gods, nor can there be anyone more compassionate than the gods. Besides there is no one else who could perform charities greater than the gods. Therefore I seek the indulgence of the gods who bestow Dharma, Artha, Kama and Moksa. They are like wish-fulfilling trees and I urge them to bless my with my desire of getting a husband of me own liking.

## \|\| 14.23-24 \|\|

*yadi dāsyanti devā me kāntadānaṃ yathepsitam \|*  
*bhadraṃ tadā'nyathā tebhyo dāsyāmi strīvadhaṃ dhruvam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*śapiṣyāmi ca sarvāṃśca dāruṇaṃ durnivārakam \|*  
*durnivāryyaḥ satīśāpastapasā kena vāryyate \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. О gods, if you grant me the desired boon, then it is alright; otherwise I shall make you responsible for the killing of a lady and pronounce a terrific curse on all the gods. The curse of a chaste woman can never be infructuous. No one can be relieved of the ill-effects of the curse pronounced by performing any amount of tapas”.

## \|\| 14.25 \|\|

*ityuktvā mālatī sādhvī śokārtā surasaṃsadi \|*  
*virarāma dvijaśreṣṭhastāmuvāca ca śaunaka \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. О Shaunaka, thus speaking the grieved Malavati kept quiet in the assembly of gods. Thereafter the best of the Brahmana spoke.

## \|\| 14.26 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*karmaṇāṃ phaladātāro devāḥ satyaṃ ca mālati \|*  
*na sadyaḥ sucireṇaiva dhānyaṃ kṛṣakavannṛṇām \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Brahmana said — О Malavati this is true that the gods give the humans the result of their deeds, but they cannot do so at once in the same way as the seed sown by a farmer does not bear fruit at once and it takes time for it to grow.

## \|\| 14.27 \|\|

*gṛhī ca kṛṣakadvārā kṣatre dhānyaṃ vapetsati \|*  
*tadaṅkuro bhavetkāle kāle vṛkṣaḥ phalatyapi \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The house-holders sow the seed in the field. It sprouts in due course of time. Further in due course of time, it bears fruit.

## \|\| 14.28 \|\|

*kāle supakvaṃ bhavati kāle prāpnoti tadgṛhī \|*  
*evaṃ sarvaṃ samunneyaṃ cireṇa karmaṇaḥ phalam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. At the appropriate time the fruit ripens up and the house-holder will get it in due course of time. Similarly it takes some time to reap the fruits of one’s deeds.

## \|\| 14.29 \|\|

*aṣṭhī vapati saṃsāre gṛhastho viṣṇumāyayā \|*  
*kāle tadaṅkuro vṛkṣaḥ kāle prāpnoti tatphalam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Infatuated with the illusion of lord Vishnu a house-holder implants the seed which gets sprouted in time and further in due course of time it bears fruit which is consumed by the house-holders.

## \|\| 14.30 \|\|

*puṇyavānpuṇyabhūmau ca karoti suciraṃ tapaḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ ca phaladātāro devāḥ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The noble people who perform tapas for a long time at the sacred places receive the fruit of the same through the gods only. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 14.31 \|\|

*brāhmaṇānāṃ mukhe kṣetre śreṣṭhe'nūṣara eva ca \|*  
*yo yajjuhoti bhaktyā ca sa tatprāpnoti niścitam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. In the mouth of Brahmana and the ploughed earth, if a person drops the food or the seed with devotion, he surely gets the fruits of it.

## \|\| 14.32-37 \|\|

*na balaṃ na ca saundaryaṃ naiśvaryaṃ na dhanaṃ sutaḥ \|*  
*naiva strī na ca satkāntaḥ kiṃ bhavettapasā vinā \|\| 32 \|\|*

*sevate prakṛtiṃ yo hi bhaktyā janmani janmani \|*  
*sa labhetsundarīṃ kāntāṃ vinītāṃ ca guṇānvitām \|\| 33 \|\|*

*śriyaṃ ca niścalāṃ putraṃ pautraṃ bhūmiṃ dhanaṃ prajām \|*  
*prakṛteśca vareṇaiva labhedbhakto'valīlayā \|\| 34 \|\|*

*śivaṃ śivasvarūpaṃ ca śivadaṃ śivakāraṇam \|*  
*jñānānandaṃ mahātmānaṃ paraṃ mṛtyuṃjayaṃ param \|\| 35 \|\|*

*tamīśaṃ sevate yo hi bhaktyā janmani janmani \|*  
*pumānprāpnoti satkāntāṃ kāminī cāpi satpatim \|\| 36 \|\|*

*vidyāṃ jñānaṃ sukavitāṃ putraṃ pautraṃ parāṃ śriyam \|*  
*balaṃ dhanaṃ vikramaṃ ca labheddharavareṇa saḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

32-37. When performing tapas, the prowess, beauty., the riches, the son, the wife and the beautiful husband cannot be had except by the one, who serves the goddess Durga for many lives. He gets the virtuous, humble and beautiful wife, with the blessings of the goddess; the devotee receives the riches, sons, grandsons, land, money and the people who with a devoted mind adore Shiva, the granter of welfare, who is also the cause of welfare, blissful and the best of the souls, who has overcome the lord of death. Such a person gets the beautiful wife during all his births. The lady who adores Shiva, achieves the best of the humans as her husband. With the boon of Shiva, the people achieve learnings, knowledge, the best of poetry, sons, grandsons and great prowess.

## \|\| 14.38 \|\|

*brahmāṇaṃ bhajate yo hi labhetso'pi prajāṃ śriyam \|*  
*vidyāmaiśvaryyamānandaṃ vareṇa brahmaṇo naraḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Some people adore Brahma and with the boon granted by him, get riches, learnings, wealth and bliss.

## \|\| 14.39-46 \|\|

*yo naro bhajate bhaktyā dīnanāthaṃ dineśvaram \|*  
*vidyāmārogyamānandaṃ dhanaṃ putraṃ labhed dhuvam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*gaṇeśvaraṃ yo bhajate devadevaṃ sanātanam \|*  
*sarvāgrapūjyaṃ sarveśaṃ bhaktyā janmani janmani \|\| 40 \|\|*

*vighnanāśo bhavettasya svapne jāgaraṇe'niśam \|*  
*paramānandamaiśvaryyaṃ putraṃ pautraṃ dhanaṃ prajāḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*jñānaṃ vidyāṃ sukavitāṃ labhate tadvareṇa ca \|*  
*bhajate yo hi viṣṇuṃ ca lakṣmīkāntaṃ sureśvaram \|\| 42 \|\|*

*varārthī cellabhetsarvaṃ nirvāṇamanyathā dhruvam \|*  
*śāntaṃ niṣevya pātāraṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ labhennaraḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*sarvaṃ tapaḥ sarvadharmaṃ yaśaḥ kīrtimanuttamām \|*  
*viṣṇuṃ niṣevya sarveśaṃ yo mūḍho labhate varam \|\| 44 \|\|*

*viḍambito vidhātrā'sau mohito viṣṇumāyayā \|*  
*māyā nārāyaṇīśānā sarvaprakṛtirīśvarī \|\| 45 \|\|*

*sā kṛpāṃ kurute yaṃ ca viṣṇumantraṃ dadāti tam \|*  
*dharmaṃ yo bhajate dharmī sarvadharmaṃ labhed dhruvam \|\| 46 \|\|*

39-46. Those who adore lord Surya also, achieve learnings, a body free from ailments, bliss, wealth and sons. The one who adores with reverence lord Ganesha who is chief of all the ganas and is adorable first of all, remover of obstachies while sleeping or awake, besides achieves eternal bliss, riches, sons, grandsons, wealth, friends, learning, knowledge and beautiful poetry as a boon. Those who adore lord Vishnu the spouse of Lakshmi, achieve their desired boon or otherwise they achieve salvation by adoring the peaceful looking lord Vishnu who is the protector as well bestower of Dharma, glory and great reputation. A fool after serving lord Vishnu desires in return for a boon and it appears that he is-cheated by the destiny of the illusion of lord Vishnu. The illusion of Narayana is competent enough to do anything besides being the cause of action and if the great goddess is pleased with anyone, she bestows the Vishnu mantra on him. Such of the religious people who follow the path of Dharma ultimately achieve all the Dharmas.

## \|\| 14.47-51 \|\|

*iha loke sukhaṃ bhuktvā yāti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padam \|*  
*yo yaṃ devaṃ bhajedbhaktyā sa cādau labhate ca tam \|\| 47 \|\|*

*kāle paścāttena sārddhaṃ paraṃ viṣṇoḥ padaṃ labhet \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇaṃ bhajate yo hi nirguṇaṃ prakṛteḥ param \|\| 48 \|\|*

*brahmaviṣṇuśivādīnāṃ sevyaṃ bījaṃ parātparam \|*  
*akṣaraṃ paramaṃ brahma bhagavantaṃ sanātanam \|\| 49 \|\|*

*sākāraṃ ca nirākāraṃ jyotiḥ svecchāmayaṃ vibhum \|*  
*sarvādhāraṃ ca sarveśaṃ paramānandamīśvaram \|\| 50 \|\|*

*nirliptasākṣirūpaṃ ca bhaktānugrahavigraham \|*  
*jīvanmuktaḥ sa satyaṃ hi na varaṃ labhate sudhīḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

47-51. After leading a life of pleasure in this world, they ultimately achieve the abode of lord Vishnu. Whosoever adores anyone of the gods with devotion he achieves initially the same god. Thereafter, he proceeds to the ultimate abode of lord Vishnu. Lord Krishna is beyond nature and is free from all the three gunas. The gods like Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva are his initial cause, Paratpara indestructible, Parabrahma and eternal lords. He is visible as well as invisible, the form of flame, moves according to his own sweet will. He is all-pervading and is above all, lord of all, blissful; he is un-involved and all-seeing god; he incarnates in human form in order to bestow his grace on his devotees. Those who adore him, achieve salvation in reality. A Vaishnava does not seek for any other boon.

## \|\| 14.52-54 \|\|

*sa sarvaṃ manyate tucchaṃ sālokyādicatuṣṭayam \|*  
*brahmatvamamaratvaṃ vā mokṣaṃ yattucchavatsati \|\| 52 \|\|*

*aiśvaryaṃ loṣṭatulyaṃ ca naśvaraṃ caiva manyate \|*  
*indratvaṃ ca manutvaṃ ca cirajīvitvameva vā \|\| 53\|\|*

*jala budbudavad buddhyā cātitucchaṃ na gaṇyate \|*  
*svapne jāgaraṇe vā'pi śaśvatsevāṃ ca vācchati \|\| 54 \|\|*

52-54. He considers four types of salvation including Salokya to be of no consequence besides the Brahman-hood; becoming eternal and achieving of salvation are of little importance to him. He considers worldly riches as dust. Similarly the position of Indra, the highest position as a human and long life, are considered by him to be short-lived, like water bubbles. They serve lord Krishna while sleeping or awake.

## \|\| 14.55-61 \|\|

*dāsyaṃ vinā na yāceta śrīkṛṣṇasya padaṃ param \|*  
*tatpādābje dṛḍhāṃ bhaktiṃ labdhvā pūrṇo nirantaram \|\| 55\|\|*

*paripūrṇatamaṃ brahma niṣevyaṃ susthiraḥ sadā \|*  
*ātmanaḥ kulakoṭiṃ ca śataṃ mātāmahasya ca \|\| 56 \|\|*

*śvaśurasya śataṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya cāvalīlayā \|*  
*dāsaṃ dāsīṃ prasūṃ bhāryāṃ putrādapi paraṃ śatam \|\| 57 \|\|*

*uddharetkṛṣṇabhaktaśca golokaṃ yāti niścitam \|*  
*tāvadgarbhe vasetkāmī tāvatī yamayātanā \|\| 58\|\|*

*tāvadgṛhī ca bhogārthī yāvatkṛṣṇaṃ na sevate \|*  
*guruvaktrādviṣṇumantro yasya karṇe praviśyati \|\| 59 \|\|*

*yamastallikhanaṃ dūraṃ karoti tatkṣaṇaṃ bhiyā \|*  
*madhuparkādikaṃ brahmā puraiva tanniyojayet \|\| 60 \|\|*

*aho vilaṅghya mallokaṃ mārgeṇānena yāsyati \|*  
*tasya vai niṣkṛtirnāsti kalpakoṭiśatairapi \|\| 61 \|\|*

55-61. A Vaishnava does not aspire after the highest stage with,lord Krishna except for becoming his slave. He feels that all his wishes are fulfilled by devoting his mind whole-heatedly at the feet of lord Krishna. The devotee of lord adores the eternal Brahman with great devotion. He redeems crores of the ancestor of his race and playfully redeems also his servants, slave girls, mothers, wives and sons. He ultimately achieves the Goloka. The one who indulges in passions, remains in the womb of his mother and bears all the pain to consume the fruit of his deeds, till such time as he does not adore the lord. Whenever a Mantra of Vishnu recited by the teachers enters into the ears of a person, the very moment Yama, the god of death gets terrified and removes the writings of his destiny from his account. Thus Brahma always remains eager to welcome him with Madhuparka and other offerings. Such a fellow sill crosses my abode and travels onward to the higher regions and dwells there for crores of kalpas.

## \|\| 14.62 \|\|

*duritāni ca bhītāni koṭijanmakṛtāni ca \|*  
*taṃ vihāya palāyante vainateyaṃ yathoragāḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. As the serpents disappeared at the sight of Garuda, similarly all the sins accumulated for crores of births disappear at the sight of a devotee of Krishna.

## \|\| 14.63 \|\|

*purātanaṃ kṛtaṃ karma yadyattasya śubhāśubham \|*  
*chinatti kṛṣṇaścakreṇa tīkṣṇadhāreṇa santatam \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. All the good and bad deeds performed by him vanish by the grace of lord Krishna’s disc.

## \|\| 14.64-65 \|\|

*taṃ vihāya jarā mṛtyuryāti cakrabhiyā sati \|*  
*anyathā śatakhaṇḍaṃ tāṃ kurute ca sudarśanaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

*niḥśaṅko yāti golokaṃ vihāya mānavīṃ tanum \|*  
*gatvā divyāṃ tanuṃ dhṛtvā śrīkṛṣṇaṃ sevate sadā \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. Old age and death leave him for fear of the caki-a of Krishna otherwise the cakra of Krishna reduces them into pieces. The devotee of Krishna leaving his human body reaches the Goloka without any interruption and taking to a divine form serves lord Krishna there.

## \|\| 14.66 \|\|

*yāvatkṛṣṇo hi goloke tāvadbhakto vasetsadā \|*  
*nimeṣaṃ manyate dāso naśvaraṃ brahmaṇo vayaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Till such time as lord Krishna resides in the Goloka his devotee also resides there. The devotee of lord Krishna cares the least for the age prescribed by Brahma.

## Chapter 15

## \|\| 15.1 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*kena rogeṇa hi mṛto'dhunā sādhvi tava priyaḥ \|*  
*sarvarogacikitsāṃ ca jānāmi ca cikitsakaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Brahmana said — О chaste lady, you tell us the disease which resulted into the death of your husband? I am a physician and I am well aware of the treatment of all diseases.

## \|\| 15.2 \|\|

*mṛtatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ rogātsaptāhābhyantare sati \|*  
*mahājñānena taṃ jīvaṃ jīvayāmyavalīlayā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O chaste lady, in case anyone becomes like that by any ailment or in case it so happens within a week, then I can extend treatment with my great knowledge, can revive him to life in the twinkling of an eye.

## \|\| 15.3 \|\|

*rājamṛtyuṃ yamaṃ kālaṃ vyādhimānīya tvatpuraḥ \|*  
*nibadhya dātuṃ śakto'haṃ vyādho baddhvā paśuṃ yathā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. I can capture old age, death, Yama, time and ailment and can produce them before you, like a hunter captures animal and produces it before you.

## \|\| 15.4 \|\|

*yato na sañcaredvyādhirdeheṣu dehadhāriṇām \|*  
*vyādhīnāṃ kāraṇaṃ yadyatsarvaṃ jānāmi sundari \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О beautiful one, I am well aware of the means by which to relieve a human being of the ailment of the body and for relieving the body of an ailment.

## \|\| 15.5 \|\|

*yato na sañcaredvyādhibījaṃ duṣṭamamaṅgalam \|*  
*tadupāyaṃ vijānāmi śāstratattvānusārataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. According to the theory of the scriptures I also know the means by which the seeds of ailments would never sprout in a human body.

## \|\| 15.6 \|\|

*yo vā yogena khedena dehatyāgaṃ karoti ca \|*  
*tasya taṃ jīvanopāyaṃ jānāmi yogadharmataḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Whosoever may have ended his life by yogic practices or in any other way, I am well aware of the means to revive him back to life with the influence of my yogic knowledge.”

## \|\| 15.7 \|\|

*brāhmaṇasya vacaḥ śrutvā sphītā mālāvatī satī \|*  
*sasmitā snigdhacittā sā tamuvāca praharṣitā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On hearing the words of the Brahmana, Malavati felt immensely pleased and with her mind filled with delight, she said.

## \|\| 15.8 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*aho śrutaṃ kimāścaryyaṃ vacanaṃ vālavaktrataḥ \|*  
*vayasā'tiśiśurdṛṣṭo jñānaṃ yogavidāṃ param \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Malavati said — what a surprising talk am I listening to from the mouth of the Brahmana boy? Compared to his age, he looks like a child, but his knowledge in the Yoga, exceeds that of everyone.

## \|\| 15.9 \|\|

*tvayā kṛtā pratijñā ca kāntaṃ jīvayituṃ mama \|*  
*viparītaṃ na sadvākyaṃ tatkṣaṇaṃ jīvitaḥ patiḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О lord you have taken a resolve to revive my husband back to life. These words cannot be changed. I, therefore, at once believe that my husband will come to life again.

## \|\| 15.10 \|\|

*jīvayiṣyati matkāntaṃ paścādvedavidāṃ varaḥ \|*  
*yadyatpṛcchāmi sandehāttadbhavānvaktumarhati \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. You are the best among those well-versed in the Vedas. You bring my husband back to life but I am putting you some questions because of certain doubts in my mind. You kindly clarify them.

## \|\| 15.11-13 \|\|

*sabhāyāṃ jīvite kānte tasya tīvrasya sannidhau \|*  
*tvāṃ hi praṣṭuṃ na śaktā'haṃ vidyamāne madīśvare \|\| 11 \|\|*

*ete brahmādayo devā vidyamānāśca saṃsadi \|*  
*tvaṃ ca vedavidāṃ śreṣṭho na ca kaścinmadīśvaraḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*nārīṃ rakṣati bharttāṃ cenna ko'pi khaṇḍituṃ kṣamaḥ \|*  
*śāstiṃ karoti yadi sa na ko'pi rakṣitā bhuvi \|\| 13 \|\|*

11-13. Because I shall not be able to ask you any questions when my husband comes back to life, as he is of harsh temperament. Those in the assembly of the gods, including Brahma and others, are present besides you are well-versed in the Vedas; but none of them is my husband. In case a lady is protected by her husband, then no one can contradict her. In case he disciplines his wife or punishes her then no one on earth can provide protection to her.

## \|\| 15.14-15 \|\|

*evaṃ deveṣu no śaktiḥ śakre vā brahmarudrayoḥ \|*  
*strī pumbhāvaśca boddhavyaḥ svāmī karttā ca yoṣitām \|\| 14 \|\|*

*svāmī karttā ca harttā ca śāstā poṣṭā ca rakṣitā \|*  
*abhīṣṭadevaḥ pūjyaśca na guruḥ svāminaḥ paraḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. Thus all the Vedas, Indra, Brahma and Rudra also have no competence to protect her. The men and the women have the relationship of husband and wife. The husband is performer, extractor, preserver, protector, family god and is adored by the lady. There is no better guru for a lady than her husband alone.

## \|\| 15.16 \|\|

*kanyā satkulajātā yā sā kāntavaśavartinī \|*  
*yā svatantrā ca sā duṣṭā svabhāvātkuṭilā dhruvam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The girl who is born in a high family always remains dependent upon her husband. But those who get independent, become wicked and degraded as well.

## \|\| 15.17 \|\|

*duṣṭā parapumāṃsaṃ ca sevate yā narādhamā \|*  
*sā nindati patiṃ śaśvadasadvaṃśaprasūtikā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Those of the wicked ladies, who take interest in others, are considered to be degraded ones and denounce their husbands. Such girls belong to the lower caste.

## \|\| 15.18 \|\|

*upabarhaṇabhāryyā'haṃ kanyā citrarathasya ca \|*  
*vadhūrgandharvarājasya kāntabhaktā sadā dvija \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. I am wife of Upabarhana and the daughter of Chitraratha. О Brahmana, I am a chaste woman and the daughter-in-law of the king of Gandharvas.

## \|\| 15.19 \|\|

*sarvaṃ kālayituṃ śaktastvaṃ ca vedavidāṃ vara \|*  
*kālaṃ yamaṃ mṛtyukanyāṃ madabhyāśaṃ samānaya \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. О great Brahmana you are competent enough to summon them all here. Therefore you summon here Yama and Mrtyukanya.

## \|\| 15.20-22 \|\|

*mālāvatīvacaḥ śrutvā vipro vedavidāṃ varaḥ \|*  
*sabhāmadhye samāhūya tānpratyakṣaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 20 \|\|*

*dadarśa mṛtyukanyāṃ ca prathamaṃ mālatī satī \|*  
*kṛṣṇavarṇāṃ ghorarūpāṃ raktāmbaradharāṃ varām \|\| 21 \|\|*

*sasmitāṃ ṣaḍbhujāṃ śāntāṃ dayāyuktāṃ mahāsatīm \|*  
*kālasya svāmino vāme catuṣṣaṣṭisutānvitām \|\| 22 \|\|*

20-22. On hearing the words of Malavati, the Brahmana who was well versed in the Vedas called all of them and made them stand there. First of all the chaste Malavati looked at Mrtyukanya who was of dark complexion and was quite horrible to look at. She was clad in red garments and she wore a serene smile on her face. She had six arms. She was peaceful, compassionate and extremely chaste. She was standing there with her sixty-four sons.

## \|\| 15.23-26 \|\|

*kālaṃ nārāyaṇāṃśaṃ ca dadarśa purataḥ satī \|*  
*mahograrūpaṃ vikaṭaṃ grīṣmasūryyasamaprabham \|\| 23 \|\|*

*ṣaḍvaktraṃ ṣoḍaśabhujaṃ caturviṃśatilocanam \|*  
*ṣaṭpādaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ ca raktāmbaradharaṃ param \|\| 24 \|\|*

*devasya devaṃ vikṛtaṃ sarvasaṃhārarūpiṇam \|*  
*kālādhidevaṃ sarveśaṃ bhagavantaṃ sanātanam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*īṣaddhāsyaprasannāsyamakṣamālākaraṃ varam \|*  
*japantaṃ paramaṃ brahma kṛṣṇamātmānamīśvaram \|\| 26 \|\|*

23-26. Thereafter, she had a look at Narayana who appeared a personified god of Death. He had a terrific appearance, looked like the sun of the winter season; he had six faces, sixteen arms, twenty four eyes and six feet. He was of dark complexion and was clad in red garments. He was the god of gods and was terrific in appearance who could destroy anyone and was respected even by the god of death, lord of all and was the eternal lord. He was wearing a pleasant and serene smile on his face. He was holding a rosary and was reciting the name of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 15.27-30 \|\|

*satī dadarśa purato vyādhisaṃghānsudurjayān \|*  
*vayasā'timahāvṛddhānstanandhānmātṛsannidhau \|\| 27 \|\|*

*sthūlapādaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ dharmiṣṭhaṃ ravinandanam \|*  
*japantaṃ paramaṃ brahma bhagavantaṃ sanātanam \|\| 28 \|\|*

*dharmādharmavicārajñaṃ paraṃ dharmasvarūpiṇam \|*  
*pāpināmapi śāstāraṃ dadarśa purato yamam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*tāṃśca dṛṣṭvā ca niḥśaṅkā papraccha prathamaṃ yamam \|*  
*mālāvatī mahāsādhvī prahaṣṭavadanekṣaṇā \|\| 30 \|\|*

27-30. Thereafter the chaste Malavati found before her all groups of ailment or diseases which were pretty old but, looked like children who encircle their mothers' breasts. Thereafter Malavati found Yaina, the son of Surya who was of black colour and had firm feet. The religious-minded son of Stirya was Parabrahma and was reciting the name of lord Krishna. He was well aware of the difference between dharma and adharma, the form of the best of Dharma and was the ruler of sinners. Looking at him the chaste Malavati with the delightful eyes doubtlessly questioned Yama.

## \|\| 15.31 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*he dharmarāja dharmiṣṭha dharmaśāstraviśārada \|*  
*kālavyatikrame kāntaṃ kathaṃ harasi me vibho \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Malavati said — “O Yama, you are devoted to the religious scriptures and are quite a religious one also. О Dharmaraja, О lord, how are you carrying my husband with you in utter disregard of the time?”

## \|\| 15.32 \|\|

*yama uvāca \|*  
*aprāptakālo mriyate na kaścijjagatītale \|*  
*īśvarājñāṃ vinā sādhvi nāmṛtaṃ cālayāmyaham \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Yama said — О chaste lady, in this universe no one dies ahead of his time or without the permission of the lord. I do not carry away anyone unless he meets with his death.

## \|\| 15.33-34 \|\|

*ahaṃ kālo mṛtyukanyā vyādhayaśca sudurjayāḥ \|*  
*niṣekeṇa prāptakālaṃ kālayantīśvarājñayā \|\| 33 \|\|*

*mṛtyukanyā vicārajñā yaṃ prāpnoti niṣekataḥ \|*  
*tamahaṃ kālayāmyeva pṛcchatāṃ kena hetunā \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. I myself, called Kala, the death and all the diseases take away the people after death with the permission of the lord. The far sighted Mrtyukanya, after the birth of a person, when she reaches someone, he alone is carried by me. Therefore you better ask her as to why she goes to anyone.

## \|\| 15.35 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*tvamapi strī mṛtyukanyā jānāsi svāmivedanam \|*  
*kathaṃ harasi matkāntaṃ jīvitāyāṃ mayi priye \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Malavati said — О Mrtyukanya, my dear friend, you are also a woman and are well aware of the pain caused with separation of a husband from a wife. Then why are you taking my husband away when I am still alive?”

## \|\| 15.36-38 \|\|

*mṛtyukanyovāca \|*  
*purā viśvasṛjā sṛṣṭā'pyahamevātra karmaṇi \|*  
*na ca kṣamā parityaktuṃ bahunā tapasā sati \|\| 36 \|\|*

*satī satīnāṃ madhye ca kācittejasvinī varā \|*  
*māmeva bhasmasātkartuṃ kṣamā yadi bhavedbhave \|\| 37 \|\|*

*sarvāpacchāntireveha tadā bhavati sundari \|*  
*putrāṇāṃ svāminaḥ paścādbhavitā yadbhaviṣyati \|\| 38 \|\|*

36-38. Mrtyukanya (death) said — In earlier times Brahma, the creator of the universe, also created me and entrusted this duty upon me. О chaste lady, I am unable to get rid of this duty even after performing great tapas. In case anyone of the chaste ladies in the universe can reduce me to ashes by pronouncing a curse, then, О beautiful one, I would have been relieved of this ugly job. After me, whatever has to happen with my husband and the sons, it could happen.

## \|\| 15.39 \|\|

*kālena preritā'haṃ ca matputrā vyādhayaśca vai \|*  
*na matsutānāṃ doṣaśca na ca me śṛṇu niścitam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Influenced by Kala, I alone perform the duty with the help of my son. It is, therefore, evident that myself and my son are not at all responsible for all this.

## \|\| 15.40 \|\|

*pṛccha kālaṃ mahātmānaṃ dharmajñaṃ dharmasaṃsadi \|*  
*tadā yaducitaṃ bhadre tatkariṣyasi niścitam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. О lady, in this assembly of the religious minded souls, you can ask the question from Kala who happens to be a great soul. Thereafter you can do whatever you think best.

## \|\| 15.41 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*he kāla karmaṇāṃ sākṣinkarmarūpa sanātana \|*  
*nārāyaṇāṃśo bhagavannamastubhyaṃparāya ca \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Malavati said – Addressing Kala, “O Kala, you are the witness of all the actions of the people; you are eternal lord and are a part of Narayana. Therefore I bow in reverence to a lord like you.

## \|\| 15.42 \|\|

*kathaṃ harasi matkāntaṃ jīvitāyāṃ mayi prabho \|*  
*jānāsi sarvaduḥkhaṃ ca sarvajñastvaṃ kṛpānidhe \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. О lord, О compassionate one, you possess all knowledge and are well aware of all miseries. Therefore, lord, “Why do you intend to take away my husband from me when I am still alive?

## \|\| 15.43 \|\|

*kālapuruṣa uvāca \|*  
*ko vā'haṃ ko yamaḥ kā ca mṛtyukanyā ca vyādhayaḥ \|*  
*vayaṃ bhramāmaḥ satatamīśājñāparipālakāḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Kalapurusa said — Who am I? Thereafter the death, Yama, Mrtyukanya and all the diseases are of no consequence. All of us always obey the command of the lord roaming about everywhere.

## \|\| 15.44-46 \|\|

*yasya sṛṣṭā ca prakṛtirbrahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ \|*  
*surā munīndrā manavo mānavāḥ sarvajantavaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

*dhyāyante tatpadāmbhojaṃ yoginaśca vicakṣaṇāḥ \|*  
*japanti śaśvannāmāni puṇyāni paramātmanaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*yadbhayādvāti vāto'yaṃ sūryyastapati yadbhayāt \|*  
*sraṣṭā brahmā''jñayā yasya pātā viṣṇuryadājñayā \|\| 46 \|\|*

44-46. He is the same lord from whom, nature, Brahma, Vishnu, Shiva and other gods, the sages, the Manus, the humans and all the creatures have emerged. All yogis and mendicants always adore at his feet. The intellectuals always recite the name of the lord regularly.

## \|\| 15.47-48 \|\|

*saṃharttā śaṅkaraḥ sarvajagatāṃ yasya śāsanāt \|*  
*dharmaśca karmaṇāṃ sākṣī yasyājñāparipālakaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

*rāśi cakraṃ grahāḥ sarve bhramanti yasya śāsanāt \|*  
*digīśāścaiva dikpālā yasyājñāparipālakāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

47-48. At whose command, the wind blows, the sun emits heat and following whose command even Brahma indulges in creation and Vishnu preserves it. At the command of whom Shiva destroys the universe and we become the witness of the deeds of the people at the command of that lord. At the command of whom all the stars, the planets and the constellation move on. Basing to the command of whom, the Dikpalas, lords of directions transact their business.

## \|\| 15.49 \|\|

*yasyājñayā ca taravaḥ puṣpāṇi ca phalāni ca \|*  
*bibhratyeva dadatyeva kāle mālāvatīsati \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. О chaste Malavati, at the command of whom the trees bear timely flowers and fruits.

## \|\| 15.50 \|\|

*yasyājñayā jalādhārā sarvādhārā vasundharā \|*  
*kṣamāvatī pṛthivī kampitā na bhayena ca \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. At the command of whom the earth is formed of the waters and comprises of all movable and immovable things. Getting afraid of whom even the earth, sometimes, shakes.

## \|\| 15.51 \|\|

*sahasā mohitā māyā māyayā yasya santatam \|*  
*sarvaprasūryā prakṛtiḥ sā bhītā yadbhayādaho \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. With the illusion of whom even the illusion is influenced and at the command of whom nature who happens to be the creator of everyone feels panicky and performs the job.

## \|\| 15.52 \|\|

*yasyāntaṃ na vidurvedā vastūnāṃ bhāvagā api \|*  
*purāṇāni ca sarvāṇi yasyaiva stutipāṭhakāḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Even the Vedas who were aware of the prowess of everyone, are unable to recite about his glory and the Puranas always offer prayers to them.

## \|\| 15.53 \|\|

*yasya nāma vidhirviṣṇuḥ sevate sumahānvirāṭ \|*  
*ṣoḍaśāṃśo bhagavataḥ sa eva tejaso vibhoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. By reciting of whose name the illustrious omnipresent lord appears in his sixteen rays in the form of Brahma, Vishnu, Shiva and the Virat (universal) form.

## \|\| 15.54 \|\|

*sarveśvaraḥ kālakālo mṛtyormṛtyuḥ parātparaḥ \|*  
*sarvavighnavināśāya taṃ kṛṣṇaṃ paricintaya \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. He is the lord of all, Kala for the Kala, death for the death, best of all the best people; therefore for the removal of all doubts, you adore at the feet of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 15.55 \|\|

*sarvābhīṣṭaṃ ca bharttāraṃ pradāsyati kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*ime yatpreritāḥ sarve sa dātā sarva sampadām \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. He alone can fulfil all your desires besides reviving your husband back to life. All these gods are influenced by him alone, therefore he is the one, who can bestow all the riches.

## \|\| 15.56 \|\|

*ityuktvā kālapuruṣo virarāma ca śaunaka \|*  
*kathāṃ kathitumārebhe punareva tu brāhmaṇaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. О Shaunaka, Kala kept quiet after speaking all this. Thereafter the Brahmana boy started speaking.

## Chapter 16

## \|\| 16.1 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*dṛṣṭaḥ kālo yamo mṛtyukanyā vyādhigaṇā aho \|*  
*kaste'dhunā ca sandehastaṃ pṛccha kanyake śubhe \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Brahmana said — “O girl, Kala, Yama, death and all other ailments have been known to you, then what more doubt is there in your mind at the moment? You ask me.”

## \|\| 16.2 \|\|

*brāhmaṇasya vacaḥ śrutvā hṛṣṭā mālāvatī satī \|*  
*yanmanoni hitaṃ praśnaṃ cakāra jagadīśvaram \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. On hearing the words of the Brahmana, the chaste Malavati felt delighted and she put her desired question to the lord of the universe.

## \|\| 16.3 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*tvayā yatkathito vyādhiḥ prāṇināṃ prāṇahārakaḥ \|*  
*tatkāraṇaṃ ca vividhaṃ sarvaṃ vede nirūpitam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Malavati said — You have told me that ailments extract the life of the people and several types of treatments for these diseases have also been spelt out in the Vedas.

## \|\| 16.4 \|\|

*yato na sañcaredvyādhirdurnivāro'śubhāvahaḥ \|*  
*tadupāyaṃ ca sākalyaṃ bhavānvaktumihārhati \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. You tell me the way by which one can protect the spread of the disease in the body. You kindly tell me the same in detail.

## \|\| 16.5 \|\|

*yadyatpṛṣmapṛṣṭaṃ vā jñātamajñātameva vā \|*  
*sarvaṃ kathaya tadbhadraṃ tvaṃ gururdīnavatsalaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. You are the one who always is compassionate to the poor and the teachers. Therefore, whatever I have asked and that whatever I have not asked, you please, tell me all that is known to you or that is unknown to me. You tell me all that is of welfare to us.

## \|\| 16.6 \|\|

*mālāvatīvacaḥ śrutvā viprarūpī janārdanaḥ \|*  
*saṃhitāṃ vaktumārebhe sahitārthāṃ ca vaidyakīm \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Thereafter lord Janardana appearing in the form of a Brahmana started speaking on the scriptures relating to medicine (Vaidyaka-samhita).

## \|\| 16.7 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*vande taṃ sarvatattvajñaṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam \|*  
*vedavedāṃgabījasya bījaṃ śrīkṛṣṇamīśvaram \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Then Brahmana said: I bow at the feet of lord Krishna, who is well aware of the secrets of all the tattvas, who is the cause of all the' causes and is the seed of all the seeds of the Vedic literature.

## \|\| 16.8 \|\|

*sa īśaścaturo vedānsasṛje maṅgalālayān \|*  
*sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalya bījarūpaḥ sanātanaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. He is the welfare of all the welfares and is the seed of all the items of welfare which were created by the lord eternal and are the basis of the four Vedas, which were revealed by him.

## \|\| 16.9 \|\|

*ṛgyajuḥsāmātharvākhyāndṛṣṭvā vedānprajāpatiḥ \|*  
*vicintya teṣāmarthaṃ caivāyurvedaṃ cakāra saḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The Vedas are known by the name of Rg, Yaju, Sama and Atharvan. By going through these Vedas and understanding their meanings, lord Brahma created the fifth Veda known as Ayurveda.

## \|\| 16.10-11 \|\|

*kṛtvā tu pañcamaṃ vedaṃ bhāskarāya dadau vibhuḥ \|*  
*svatantrasaṃhitāṃ tasmādbhāskaraśca cakāra saḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*bhāskaraśca svaśiṣyebhya āyurvedaṃ svasaṃhitām \|*  
*pradadau pāṭhayāmāsa te cakruḥ saṃhitāstataḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. Thus after creating the fifth Veda, its knowledge was bestowed on Surya and the sun-god created an independent composition of the same. Thereafter he taught the Ayurveda samhita to his pupils handing it over to them. Thereafter those pupils created many samhitas out of it.

## \|\| 16.12 \|\|

*teṣāṃ nāmāni viduṣāṃ tantrāṇi tatkṛtāni ca \|*  
*vyādhipraṇāśabījāni sādhvi matto niśāmaya \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. О noble lady, now you listen from me the names of those tantras which contained the prescriptions for the removal of the ailments.

## \|\| 16.13-14 \|\|

*dhanvantarirdivo dāsaḥ kāśīrājo'śvinīsutau \|*  
*nakulaḥ sahadevo'rkiścyavano janako budhaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*jābālo jājaliḥ pailaḥ karatho'gastya eva ca \|*  
*ete vedāṅgavedajñāḥ ṣoḍaśa vyādhināśakāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. The sixteen persons named Dhanvantri, Divodasa, Kasiraja, Asvinlkumaras, Nakula, Sahadeva, Yama, Cyavana, Janaka, Budha, Jabala, Jajali, Paila, Karatha and Agastya are well-versed in the Vedas and other Vedic literature besides their tattvas. They are also proficient in the removal of ailments.

## \|\| 16.15-18 \|\|

*cikitsātattvavijñānaṃ nāma tantraṃ manoharam \|*  
*dhanvantariśca bhaga vāṃścakāra prathamaṃ sati \|\| 15 \|\|*

*iti śrībrahmavaivarte mahāpu0 sautiśau0 brahmakhaṇḍe mālāvatīviṣṇusaṃvāde cikitsāpraṇayane ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*cikitsāsāratantraṃ ca śramaghnaṃ cāśvinīsutau \|*  
*tantraṃ vaidyakasarvasvaṃ nakulaśca cakāra saḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*cakāra sahadevaśca vyādhisindhuvimardanam \|*  
*jñānārṇavaṃ mahātantraṃ yamarājaścakāra ha \|\| 18 \|\|*

15-18. First of all, lord Dhanvantri composed a tantra known as Cikitsatattvavijnana (A treasury on the science of medicines). Thereafter Cikitsadarpana was composed by Divodasa. Kasiraja on the other hand, composed Cikitsakaumudi and both the Asvinl-kumaras composed the manuscript of Cikitsasaratantra which removed all the doubts. Similarly the work of Vaidyakasarvasva, Sahdeva composed Vyadhisindhuvimardana and Yama composed Jnandrnava which is considered to be a great tantra.

## \|\| 16.19-22 \|\|

*cyavano jīvadānaṃ ca cakāra bhagavānṛṣiḥ \|*  
*cakāra janako yogī vaidyasandehabhañjanam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*sarvasāraṃ candrasuto jābālastantrasārakam \|*  
*vedāṅgasāraṃ tantraṃ ca cakāra jājalirmuniḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*pailo nidānaṃ karathastantraṃ sarvadharaṃ param \|*  
*dvaidhanirṇayatantraṃ ca cakāra kumbhasambhavaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*cikitsāśāstrabījāni ta ntrāṇyetāni ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*vyādhipraṇāśabījāni balādhānakarīṣi \|\| 22 \|\|*

19-22. Lord Cyavana on the other hand, composed a tantra under the title of JTvadana. The yogi Janaka composed Vaidyasandehabhanjana. Budha the son of the moon composed Sarvasara. Jabala composed Tantrasara and Jajali composed Vedangasara. Paila composed Nidanatantara. Karatha composed Sarvadhara tantra and the sage Agastya composed Dvaidhanirnayatantara. The sixteen tantras and the seeds of the treatises and medicines, remove the diseases and increase strength and intelligence.

## \|\| 16.23 \|\|

*mathitvā jñānamantreṇaivāyurvedapayonidhim \|*  
*tatastasmādudājahrurnavanītāni kovidāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The intellectuals churned the ocean of Ayurveda with the churning rod of their intelligence and gave the above tantras a new form.

## \|\| 16.24 \|\|

*etāni kramaśo dṛṣṭvā divyāṃ bhāskarasaṃhitām \|*  
*āyurvedaṃ sarvabījaṃ sarvaṃ jānāsi sundari \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. О beautiful one, by going through them you will become conversant with the divine composition of the sun and Ayurveda which happens to be the seed of all.

## \|\| 16.25-27 \|\|

*vyādhestattvaparijñānaṃ vedanāyāśca nigrahaḥ \|*  
*etadvaidyasya vaidyatvaṃ na vaidyaḥ prabhurāyuṣaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*āyurvedasya vijñātā cikitsāsu yathārthavit \|*  
*dharmiṣṭhaśca dayāluśca tena vaidyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*janakaḥ sarvarogāṇāṃ durvāro dāruṇo jvaraḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktaśca yogī ca niṣṭhuro vikṛtākṛtiḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

25-27. The foremost beauty of a physician is to know pretty well about the various tattvas of the ailment and to research on the means to remove them. The physicians are not in a position to enhance the life of anyone. Such people are called physicians (Vaidyas) because of their special knowledge in the science of medicine and their application. They possess a religious mind and are compassionate. High fever is the cause of all the ailments and can be prevented with great efforts. The fever happens to be the devotee of Shiva, is yogi, the heartless one and has a deformed figure.

## \|\| 16.28 \|\|

*bhīmastripādastriśirāḥ ṣaḍbhujo navalocanaḥ \|*  
*bhasmapraharaṇo raudraḥ kālāntakayamopamaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. It has three feet, six arms and nine eyes. This terrific fever is a destroyer like Kala, Antaka and Yama, the ashes are his weapons and his god is Rudra.

## \|\| 16.29 \|\|

*mandāgnistasya janako mandāgnerjanakāstrayaḥ \|*  
*pittaśleṣmasamīrāśca prāṇināṃ duḥkhadāyakāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. It germinates from indigestion. From the same indigestion also emerges jaundice, the wind and the cough. All these trouble the people always.

## \|\| 16.30 \|\|

*vāyujaḥ pittajaścaiva śleṣmajaśca tathaiva ca \|*  
*jvarabhedāśca trividhāścaturthaśca tridoṣajaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The wind, the jaundice and the cough are created from the body and therefore the fevers also are known as Vataja (bom of the wind), Pittaja (bom of the biles), Kaphaja (bom out of cough). There is a fourth fever known as Tridosaja.

## \|\| 16.31-33 \|\|

*pāṇḍuśca kāmalaḥ kuṣṭhaḥ śothaḥ plīhā ca śūlakaḥ \|*  
*jvarātisāragrahaṇīkāsavraṇahalīmakāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*mūtrakṛcchraśca gulmaśca raktadoṣavikārajaḥ \|*  
*viṣamehaśca kabjaśca godaśca galagaṇḍakaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*bhramarī sannipātaśca viṣūcī dāruṇī sati \|*  
*eṣāṃ bhedaprabhedena catuḥṣaṣṭī rujaḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

31-33. There are several terrific ailments known as pandu, kamala, kustha, shotha, pllha, sula, jvamtisara, graham, kasa (cough), vrana (wound), hallmaka, miitrakrcchr (urinary infection), diseases born due to raktavikara (shortage of blood) is gulma, visameha (poisoning), kabja (constipation), goda, galaganda (tonsils), bhramari, sannipata, visucl (cholera) and darunl. They are said to be divided and subdivided in sixty four parts.

## \|\| 16.34 \|\|

*mṛtyukanyāsutāścaite jarā tasyāśca kanyakā \|*  
*jarā ca bhrātṛbhiḥ sārddhaṃ śaśvad bhramati bhūtalam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. They are all the sons of death and old-age is her daughter. The old age always roams about earth accompanied by her brothers.

## \|\| 16.35 \|\|

*ete copāyavettāraṃ na gacchanti ca saṃyatam \|*  
*palāyante ca taṃ dṛṣṭvā vainateyamivoragāḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. These ailments cannot approach such people who are self-disciplined and are aware of ways and means to remove them. At the sight of such persons they flee as the serpents flee at the right of Garuda.

## \|\| 16.36 \|\|

*cakṣurjalaṃ ca vyāyāmaḥ pādādhastailamardanam \|*  
*karṇayormūrdhni tailaṃ ca jarāvyādhivināśanam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. To clean the eyes with the application of water, to regularly exercise, to apply oil at the soles, the ears and the head, are some of the methods which keep the ailments away.

## \|\| 16.37-38 \|\|

*vasante bhramaṇaṃ vahnisevāṃ svalpāṃ karoti yaḥ \|*  
*bālāṃ ca sevate kāle jarā taṃ nopagacchati \|\| 37 \|\|*

*khātaśītodakasnāyī sevate candanadravam nopayāti jarā taṃ ca nidāghe'nilasevakam \|\| 38 \|\|*

37-38. In the spring season walking is quite beneficial, one who enjoys a little heat of the fire in the winter season, the old age never goes to him. Taking a bath in the summer season, the application of sandal-paste and a morning walk, keep the old age away.

## \|\| 16.39-40 \|\|

*prāvṛṣyuṣṇodakasnāyī ghanatoyaṃ na sevate \|*  
*samaye ca samāhārī jarā taṃ nopagacchati \|\| 39 \|\|*

*śaradraudraṃ na gṛhṇāti bhramaṇaṃ tatra varjayet \|*  
*khātasnāyī samāhārī jarā taṃ nopagacchati \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. To take a bath with warm water in the rainy season, to avoid the used of rain water, to take suitable food at the appropriate time, keeps the old age away. In the winter season, if one does not enjoy the sun shine, does not take a bath in a tank and the one who consumes limited food does not attract the old age.

## \|\| 16.41 \|\|

*khātasnāyī ca hemante kāle vahniṃ ca sevate \|*  
*bhuṅkte navānnamuṣṇaṃ ca jarā taṃ nopagacchati \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. In the winter season, to take a bath in the morning, to enjoy the heat of the fire timely and to consume hot food, do not attract the old age.

## \|\| 16.42 \|\|

*śiśireṃ'śukavahniṃ ca navoṣṇānnaṃ ca sevate \|*  
*yaśca voṣṇodakasnāyī jarā taṃ nopagacchati \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. In the winter season one who wears warm clothing, enjoys the fire and hot food and takes a bath with cold water the old age can never reach him.

## \|\| 16.43 \|\|

*sadyomāṃsaṃ navānnaṃ ca bālāstrīkṣīrabhojanam \|*  
*ghṛtaṃ ca sevate yo hi jarā taṃ nopagacchati \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The one who consumes fresh food, cohabits with a girl of sixteen years’ of age, takes the food with ghee and rice cooked in milk, the old age can never reach him.

## \|\| 16.44-46 \|\|

*bhuṅkte sadannaṃ kṣutkāle tṛṣṇāyāṃ pīyate jalam \|*  
*nityaṃ bhuṅkte ca tāmbūlaṃ jarā taṃ nopagacchati \|\| 44 \|\|*

*dadhi haiyaṅgavīnaṃ ca navanītaṃ tathā guḍam \|*  
*nityaṃ bhuṅkte saṃyamī yo jarā taṃ nopagacchati \|\| 45 \|\|*

*śuṣkamāṃsaṃ striyaṃ vṛddhāṃ bālārkaṃ taruṇaṃ dadhi \|*  
*saṃsevante jarā yāti prahṛṣṭā bhrātṛbhiḥ saha \|\| 46 \|\|*

44-46. To take the best of food when hungry, to drink water when thirsty and chewing of the betels and betel-nuts regularly, keep the old age away. A person who regularly consumes curd, butter, ghee and raw sugar, the old age never approaches him; the one who consumes dry meats, keeps company of old ladies and enjoys the sun-shine of Kartika month, consumes curds many days old, attracts the old age with pleasure and quickly is over powered by old age.

## \|\| 16.47-49 \|\|

*rātrau ye dadhi sevante puṃścalīśca rajasvalāḥ \|*  
*tānupaiti jarā hṛṣṭā bhrātṛbhiḥ saha sundari \|\| 47 \|\|*

*rajasvalā ca kulaṭā cāvīrā jāradūtikā \|*  
*śūdrayājakapatnī yā ṛtuhīnā ca yā sati \|\| 48 \|\|*

*yo hi tāsāmannabhojī brahmahatyāṃ labhettu saḥ \|*  
*tena pāpena sārddhaṃ sā jarā tamupagacchati \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. О beautiful one, to consume the curd during the night, to keep company of a wicked woman and a woman in menstrual period attracts the old age, where it reaches delightfully with her brothers. The woman in menstrual period, the wicked woman, the widow, the one who serves as a messenger between two lovers, the wife of the person who performs yajha for the Shdras or the women who do not have the menses; if someone takes food prepared by such women he earns the sin of Brahmahatya and because of that sin, he attracts the old age.

## \|\| 16.50-51 \|\|

*pāpānāṃ vyādhibhiḥ sārddhaṃ mitratā santataṃ dhruvam \|*  
*pāpaṃ vyādhijarābījaṃ vighnabījaṃ ca niścitam \|\| 50 \|\|*

*pāpena jāyate vyādhiḥ pāpena jāyate jarā \|*  
*pāpena jāyate dainyaṃ duḥkhaṃ śoko bhayaṅkaraḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. The sins are closely related with the ailments. The sin alone is responsible for the ailment, diseases, old age and the cause of several other obstructions. The sin attracts the ailment and also the old age. The sin also causes misery, trouble and great grief.

## \|\| 16.52 \|\|

*tasmātpāpaṃ mahāvairaṃ doṣabījamamaṅgalam \|*  
*bhārate satataṃ santo nācaranti bhayāturāḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Therefore, sin is the biggest enemy and causes misery for all. Therefore the noble people in the country never indulge in sins.

## \|\| 16.53-54 \|\|

*svadharmācārayuktaṃ ca dīkṣitaṃ harisevakam \|*  
*gurudevātithīnāṃ ca bhaktaṃ saktaṃ tapaḥsu ca \|\| 53 \|\|*

*vratopavāsayuktaṃ ca sadā tīrthaniṣevakam \|*  
*rogā dravanti taṃ dṛṣṭvā vainateyamivoragāḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. They follow their own Dharma. Those who have received dlksa, are devoted to the lord, teachers and the gods besides the guests and never attract ailments. They remain engrossed in the tapas, vows and remaining without food and are always inclined to visit the holy places and as such the ailments keep away from such people and disappear as serpents flee at the sight of the Garuda.

## \|\| 16.55 \|\|

*etāñjarā na seveta vyādhisaṃghaśca durjayaḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ bodhyamasamaye kāle sarvaṃ grasiṣyati \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. The old age and the terrific diseases never influence such people. One should therefore know about them. If one becomes ignorant of them they untimely overpower him.

## \|\| 16.56 \|\|

*jvarasya sarvarogāṇāṃ janakaḥ kathitaḥ sati \|*  
*pittaśleṣmasamīrāśca jvarasya janakāstrayaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. О chaste lady, fever is the root cause of all the ailments. I have already spoken about it. Therefore Vata, Pitta and Cough which are the main causes of the fever (should be kept away).

## \|\| 16.57 \|\|

*ete yathā saṃ caranti svayaṃ yānti ca dehiṣu \|*  
*tameva vividhopāyaṃ sādhvi matto niśāmaya \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. The way in which these ailments influence the human body, I shall make you aware of it.

## \|\| 16.58 \|\|

*kṣudhi jājvalyamānāyāmāhārābhāva eva ca \|*  
*prāṇināṃ jāyate pittaṃ cakre ca maṇipūrake \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. When one does not eat in spite of being hungry, then jaundice develop in the body.

## \|\| 16.59 \|\|

*tālabilvaphalaṃ bhuktvā jalapānaṃ ca tatkṣaṇam \|*  
*tadeva tu bhavetpittaṃ sadyaḥ prāṇaharaṃ param \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. After consuming palm and wood-apple if someone drinks water immediately, thereafter he is likely to get the ailment of jaundice.

## \|\| 16.60 \|\|

*taptodakaṃ ca śirasi bhādre tiktaṃ viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*daivagrastaśca yo bhuṅkte pittaṃ tasya prajāyate \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. When an unfortunate person drops the hot water on his head in the month of Bhadra-pada and also consumes bitter food jaundice in his body increases.

## \|\| 16.61-67 \|\|

*saśarkaraṃ ca dhānyākaṃ piṣṭaṃ śītodakānvitam \|*  
*caṇakaṃ sarvagavyaṃ ca dadhitakravivarjitam \|\| 61 \|\|*

*bilvatālaphalaṃ pakvaṃ sarvamaikṣavameva ca \|*  
*ārdrakaṃ mudgayūṣaṃ ca tilapiṣṭaṃ saśarkaram \|\| 62 \|\|*

*pittakṣayakaraṃ sadyo balapuṣṭipradaṃ param \|*  
*pittanāśaṃ ca tadbījamuktamanyannibodha me \|\| 63 \|\|*

*bhojanānantaraṃ snānaṃ jalapānaṃ vinā tṛṣā \|*  
*tilatailaṃ snigdhatailaṃ snigdhamāmalakīdravam \|\| 64 \|\|*

*paryyuṣitānnaṃ ca takraṃ ca pakvaṃ rambhāphalaṃ dadhi \|*  
*meghāmbu śarkarātoyaṃ susnigdhajalasevanam \|\| 65 \|\|*

*nārikelodakaṃ rūkṣasnānaṃ paryuṣite jale \|*  
*taruguṃjāpakvaphalaṃ supakvaṃ karkkaṭīphalam \|\| 66 \|\|*

*khātasnānaṃ ca varṣāsu mūlakaṃ śleṣmakārakam \|*  
*brahmarandhre ca tajjanma mahadvīryya vināśanam \|\| 67 \|\|*

61-67. Therefore making a powder of coriander and taking it with sugar and cold water, the jaundice subsides, the grams and articles made of cow milk, curd, ghee, cow-dung, the urine of cow and the curd or butter milk without cream or butter, the ripe wood-apples, the palm fruits and the juice of sugar-cane are the stuffs to be prepared of that, ginger, radish, green gram pulses, radish, sheshame powder mixed with sugar, when consumed during such ailment the jaundice disappears and provides a person nourishment and increases the strength. Thus the cause of jaundice and the means to remove it have been spelt out. Now I am going to tell you something more; you listen to the same. To take a bath immediately after taking food, to consume after without having been thirsty, sheshame oil, thick oil, juice of embolic myrobalan, (phyllanthus emblica), left-over food, butter milk, ripe banana, curd, rain water, sugar mixed with water, consuming of thick water, juice of coconut, left-over water, dry-wash, watermelons, ripe gourd and to take a bath in a tank during the rainy season and consuming of radish, gives rise to the creation of cough in the body. Its creation in the head ultimately destroys the semen.

## \|\| 16.68-71 \|\|

*vahnisvedaṃ bhraṣṭabhaṅgaṃ pakvatailaviśeṣakam \|*  
*bhramaṇaṃ śuṣkabhakṣaṃ ca śuṣkapakvaharītakī \|\| 68 \|\|*

*piṇḍārakamapakvaṃ ca rambhā phalamapakvakam \|*  
*vesavāraḥ sindhuvāra anāhāramapānakam \|\| 69 \|\|*

*saghṛtaṃ rocanācūrṇaṃ saghṛtaṃ śuṣkaśarkaram \|*  
*marīcaṃ pippalaṃ śuṣkamārdrakaṃ jīvakaṃ madhu \|\| 70 \|\|*

*dravyāṇyetāni gāndharvi sadyaḥ śleṣmaharāṇi ca \|*  
*balapuṣṭikarāṇyeva vāyubījaṃ niśāmaya \|\| 71 \|\|*

68-71. O daughter of Gandharva, to sweat from the wannth from the fire, to consume the dried hemp-leaves, the fried oil, aimless wandering, eating of dry food, dry black myrobalan, gum resin, unripe banana, vesavara (spices), sindhuvara (Nirgundl), fasting, non-consuming of water, taking of ghee with powder, ghee with sugar, dry ginger, jlvika (one of the great Ous adha) and honey can remove the cough and definitely provide strength and nourishment. Now listen to the cause of wind or Vata.

## \|\| 16.72-74 \|\|

*bhojanānantaraṃ sadyo gamanaṃ dhāvanaṃ tathā \|*  
*chedanaṃ vahnitāpaśca śaśvadbhramaṇamaithunam \|\| 72 \|\|*

*vṛddhastrīgamanaṃ caiva manaḥsantāpa eva ca \|*  
*atirūkṣamanāhāraṃ yuddhaṃ kalahameva ca \|\| 73 \|\|*

*kaṭu vākyaṃ bhayaṃ śokaḥ kevalaṃ vāyukāraṇam \|*  
*ājñākhyacakre tajanma niśāmaya tadauṣadham \|\| 74 \|\|*

72-74. To walk immediately after taking food, running, cutting, warming by fire, constant roaming about, enjoying conjugal pleasure, enjoying the company of an old lady, suffering from mental agony and the consuming of dry food or remaining without flood, fighting, quarrelling, speaking harsh words, person and remaining grief stricken, cause the creation of the wind. The wind is also caused by the Cakara named Ajha. I am telling you the medicine for the same also. You listen to it.

## \|\| 16.75-79 \|\|

*pakvaṃ rambhāphalaṃ caiva sabījaṃ śarkarodakam \|*  
*nārikelodakaṃ caiva sadyastakraṃ supiṣṭakam \|\| 75 \|\|*

*māhiṣaṃ dadhi miṣṭaṃ ca kevalaṃ vā saśarkaram \|*  
*sadyaḥ paryyuṣitānnaṃ ca sauvīraṃ śītalodakam \|\| 76 \|\|*

*pakvatailaviśeṣaṃ ca tilatailaṃ ca kevalam \|*  
*lāṃgalītālakharjūramuṣṇamāmalakīdravam \|\| 77 \|\|*

*śītaloṣṇodakasnānaṃ susnigdhacandanadravam \|*  
*snigdhapadmapatratalpaṃ susnigdhavyajanāni ca \|\| 78 \|\|*

*etatte kathitaṃ vatse sadyovāyupraṇāśanam \|*  
*vāyavastrividhāḥ puṃsāṃ kleśasantāpakāmajāḥ \|\| 79 \|\|*

75-79. The ripe banana fruit, sugar juice mixed with lemon juice, juice of coconut, fresh butter milk, the best of ground pulses like Kacaurl, sweet curd of buffaloes milk, the curd mixed v/ith sugar, just left over food, juice of barley, cold water, fried oil, sheshame oil, coconut, palm, juice of myrobalan, bark with hot and cold water, thick paste of sandal-wood and to lie down on the lotus leaves; all these things relieve one of the effects of wind. О daughter, I have thus mentioned all about the things which remove the ailment of the wind. There are three types of ailments concerning the wind which appear in the human beings, by mental agony, grief and passions.

## \|\| 16.80 \|\|

*vyādhisaṃghaśca kathitastantrāṇi vividhāni ca \|*  
*tāni vyādhipraṇāśāya kṛtāni sadbhireva ca \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. Thus I have given you an idea about the ailments and the means to remove them by consuming different types of medicine.

## \|\| 16.81 \|\|

*tantrāṇyetāni sarvāṇi vyādhikṣayakarāṇi ca \|*  
*rasāyanādayo yeṣu copāyāśca sudurlabhāḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. All these tantras remove ailments. Still unique types of medicines have also been prescribed therein.

## \|\| 16.82 \|\|

*na śaktaḥ kathituṃ sādhvi yathārthaṃ vatsareṇa ca \|*  
*teṣāṃ ca sarvatantrāṇāṃ kṛtānāṃ ca vicakṣaṇaiḥ \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. О chaste lady, the tantras composed by the intellectuals cannot be spelt out even in one year’s time.

## \|\| 16.83 \|\|

*kena rogeṇa tvatkānto mṛtaḥ kathaya śobhane \|*  
*tadupāyaṃ kariṣyāmi yena jīvedayaṃ sati \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. Therefore, О beautiful one, you tell me the particular ailment which caused the death of your husband. I will speak out the remedy by which he will get back his life.

## \|\| 16.84 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*brāhmaṇasya vacaḥ śrutvā kanyā citrarathasya ca \|*  
*kathāṃ kathitumārebhe sā gāndharvī praharṣitā \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. Sauti said — Listening to the words of Brahmana, Malavati the daughter of Chitraratha spoke delightedly.

## \|\| 16.85-86 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*yogena prāṇāṃstatyāja brahmaṇaḥ śāpahetunā \|*  
*sabhāyāṃ lajjitaḥ kānto mama vipra niśāmaya \|\| 85 \|\|*

*sarvaṃ śrutamapūrvaṃ ca śubhākhyānaṃ manoharam \|*  
*bhavedbhave kutaḥ keṣāṃ mahallabhyaṃ vipadvinā \|\| 86 \|\|*

85-86. Malavati said — О Brahmana listen to me. My husband feeling shy in the court of Brahma, was deprived of his life because of the curse. I have listened attentively to whatever you have said. In this world, without facing misfortune, who can get the company of a great soul like you?

## \|\| 16.87 \|\|

*adhunā matprāṇakāntaṃ dehi dehi vicakṣaṇa \|*  
*natvā vaḥ svāminā sārddhaṃ yāsyāmi svagṛhaṃ prati \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. О intelligent one, you kindly bring back to life my husband at the moment to enable me to leave for my abode with my husband bowing to you in reverence.”

## \|\| 16.88 \|\|

*mālāvatīvacaḥ śrutvā viprarūpī janārdanaḥ \|*  
*sabhāṃ jagāma devānāṃ śīghraṃ viprastadantikāt \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. On hearing the words of Malavati lord Vishnu who had taken to the fonn of a Brahmana, got up and went to the assembly of the gods.

## Chapter 17

## \|\| 17.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā dvijaṃ devasaṃghaḥ pratyutthānaṃ cakāra ca \|*  
*parasparaṃ ca sambhāṣā babhūva tatra saṃsadi \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said — Seeing him all the gods stood up and welcomed the Brahmana. Thereafter all of them started conversation among themselves.

## \|\| 17.2-3 \|\|

*mā taṃ bubudhire devāḥ śrīhariṃ viprarūpiṇam \|*  
*paurvāparyyaṃ vismṛtāśca mohitā viṣṇumāyayā \|\| 2 \|\|*

*surānsambodhya vipraśca vācā madhurayā dvija \|*  
*uvāca satyaṃ paramaṃ prāṇināṃ yacchubhāvaham \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. Infatuated with the illusion of Vishnu, the gods had forgotten about all the earlier happenings. Therefore, they could not recognise the Brahmana who was in fact lord Vishnu himself. Thereafter the Brahmana addressed the gods in sweet words which provided welfare to all the creatures.

## \|\| 17.4 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*upabarhaṇabhāryyeyaṃ kanyā citrarathasya ca \|*  
*yayāce jīvadānaṃ ca svāminaḥ śokakarṣitā \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Brahmana said — She is the wife of Upabarhana and the daughter of Chitraratha. She is plunged in grief and is begging for the life of her husband.

## \|\| 17.5 \|\|

*adhunā kimanuṣṭhānamasya kāryasya niścitam \|*  
*tanmāṃ brūta surāḥ sarve nityaṃ yatsamayocitam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. All the gods should tell me the method to be adopted for bringing back to life her husband. It should be quite appropriate and should be useful in future also.

## \|\| 17.6 \|\|

*śaptukāmā surānsarvānsādhvī tejasvinī varā \|*  
*ahaṃ kṣemāya yuṣmākamāgato bodhitā satī \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. This illustrious and the chaste lady was about to pronounce a curse on all the gods, but taking into consideration your welfare, I have dissuaded her from doing so, convincing her appropriately.

## \|\| 17.7 \|\|

*stutiḥ kṛtā ca yuṣmābhiḥ śvetadvīpe harerapi \|*  
*yuṣmākamīśo viṣṇuśca kathamevātra nāgataḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. All of you had gone to the Shveta-dvipa and had offered prayer to lord Vishnu who has not arrived here.

## \|\| 17.8 \|\|

*babhūvākāśavāṇīti paścādyāsyati keśava \|*  
*viparītaṃ kathaṃ bhūtaṃ vāṇīvākyamacañcalam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. There was a divine voice from the sky which declared that the lord would also arrive. “How has the declaration of the divine voice gone otherwise”?

## \|\| 17.9 \|\|

*brāhmaṇasya vacaḥ śrutvā svayaṃ brahmā jagadguruḥ \|*  
*uvāca vacanaṃ satyaṃ hitaṃ paramamaṅgalam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On hearing the words of the Brahmana, Brahma the teacher of the universe spoke truthful words for the benefit of all.

## \|\| 17.10 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*matputro nāradaḥ śapto gandharvaścopabarhaṇaḥ \|*  
*yogena prāṇāṃstatyāja punaḥ śāpānmamaiva hi \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Brahma said — My son Narada, after having been cursed, was turned into the form of a Gandharva named Upbarhana. And again he had to be deprived of life by yogic practices, because of the curse pronounced by me.

## \|\| 17.11 \|\|

*kālaṃ lakṣayugaṃ vyāpya sthitirasya mahītale \|*  
*śūdrayoniṃ tataḥ prāpya bhavitā matsutaḥ punaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. He would remain like this on earth for a lakh of years. Thereafter he would be born as a Shudra. Thereafter he again would become by son.

## \|\| 17.12 \|\|

*asya kālāvaśeṣasya kiñcidasti dvijottama \|*  
*tattu varṣasahasraṃ caivāyurasyāsti sāmpratam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. О best of the Brahmanas, therefore only very little time is self for his life. Presently he has to live for a thousand years more.

## \|\| 17.13-15 \|\|

*dāsyāmi jīvadānaṃ ca svayaṃ viṣṇoḥ prasādataḥ \|*  
*yathainaṃ na spṛśecchāpastatkariṣyāmi niścitam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*nāgato hariratreti tvayā yatkathitaṃ dvija \|*  
*hariḥ sarvatra sarvātmā vigrahaḥ kuta ātmanaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*svecchāmayaḥ paraṃ brahma bhaktānugrahavigrahaḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ paśyati sarvajñaḥ sarvatrāsti sanātanaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

13-15. By the grace of Vishnu I shall myself bring him back to life. I shall make appropriate efforts in this regard. This would relieve him of the curse of the gods. О Brahmana, you have asked me the reason for the absence of lord Vishnu from this place. It is not true, because Hari pervades everywhere. He is the soul of everyone and the soul has no definite form. The eternal Brahman moves according to his own sweet will. He takes to the human form in order to shower his grace over his devotees. The eternal lord is present everywhere.

## \|\| 17.16 \|\|

*viṣiśca vyāptivacano nuśca sarvatravācakaḥ \|*  
*sarvavyāpī ca sarvātmā tena viṣṇuḥ prakīrttitaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The root Vis means pervading and Nu means everywhere. Hari therefore is only present and because of that he is called Vishnu.

## \|\| 17.17 \|\|

*apavitraḥ pavitro vā sarvāvasthāṃ gato'pi vā \|*  
*yaḥ smaretpuṇḍarīkākṣaṃ sa bāhyābhyantaraḥ śuciḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. You can recite the name of the lotus-eyed Vishnu in any position, whether you are unclean or clean because the very reciting of the name of Vishnu purifies anyone everywhere.

## \|\| 17.18-19 \|\|

*karmārambhe ca madhye vā śeṣe viṣṇuṃ ca yaḥ smaret \|*  
*paripūrṇaṃ tasya karma vaidikaṃ ca bhaveddvija \|\| 18 \|\|*

*ahaṃ sraṣṭā ca jagatāṃ vidhātā saṃharo haraḥ \|*  
*dharmaśca karmaṇāṃ sākṣī yasyājñāparipālakaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. O Brahmana, at the beginning, middle and the end of his life, whosoever recites the name of Vis nu, all his Vedic practices are considered to be complete. I am the creator of the world and Shiva happens to be the destroyer. Dharma happens to be the witness of one’s deeds and obeys the command of Vishnu.

## \|\| 17.20 \|\|

*kālaḥ saṃharate lokānyamaḥ śāstā ca pāpinām \|*  
*upaiti mṛtyuḥ sarvāṃśca bhiyā yasyājñayā sadā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. With the command and the fear of whom the god of death destroys everything or Yama controls the sinful people and the death reaches everyone.

## \|\| 17.21 \|\|

*sarveśā yā ca sarvādyā prakṛtiḥ sarvasūḥ purā \|*  
*sā bhītā yasya purato yasyājñāparipālikā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Similarly the goddess of all, adorable by all the primeval energy, who creates everyone, remains terrified before him and obeys his command. It is none else but Vishnu alone.

## \|\| 17.22-24 \|\|

*maheśvara uvāca \|*  
*putrāṇāṃ brahmaṇasteṣāṃ kasya vaṃśodbhavo bhavān \|*  
*vedānadhītya bhavatā jñātaḥ kaḥ sāra eva ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

*śiṣyaḥ kasya munīndrasya kastvaṃ nāmnā ca bho dvija \|*  
*bibharttyarkātiriktaṃ ca śiśurūpo'si sāmpratam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*viḍambayasi devāṃśca viṣṇumasmākamīśvaram \|*  
*hṛdisthaṃ ca na jānāsi paramātmānamīśvaram \|\| 24 \|\|*

22-24. Maheshvara said — In which one of the rays, the sons of Brahma, were you born and what is the essence of the Vedas understood by you after reading them? О Brahmana who happens to be your teacher and what is your name? Currently you are just a child but you wear a lustre of the sun on your face. You are putting even the gods of love to shame with your lustre but surpassingly you are not aware of our lord who is recited in the minds of everyone and is the supreme soul.

## \|\| 17.25 \|\|

*yasmingate pateddeho dehināṃ paramātmani \|*  
*prayānti sarve tatpaścānnaradevānugā iva \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. After the soul departs from the body of people, all the organs of the senses and life stops functioning. In the same way, as the king is followed by all his courtiers.

## \|\| 17.26-28 \|\|

*jīvastatpratibimbaśca mano jñānaṃ ca cetanā \|*  
*prāṇāścendriyavargāśca buddhirmedhā dhṛtiḥ smṛtiḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*nidrā dayā ca tandrā ca kṣuttṛṣṇā puṣṭireva ca \|*  
*śraddhā saṃtuṣṭiricchā ca kṣamā lajjādikāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

*prayāti yatpuraḥ śaktirīśvare gamanonmukhe \|*  
*ete sarve ca śaktiśca yasyājñā paripālakāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

26-28. Life is the reflection of the same. The mind, knowledge, consciousness, life, the sense-organs, intelligence, passions, memory, sleep, compassion, state of trance, hunger, lust, development, devotion, satisfaction, desire, forgiveness and shyness are the qualities which are also followed by life. Whenever the great soul decides to part, its strength moves ahead. All the above qualities follow the prowess of god.

## \|\| 17.29 \|\|

*īśvare ca sthite dehī kṣamaśca sarvakarmasu \|*  
*gate'spṛśyaḥ śavastyājyaḥ kastaṃ dehī na manyate \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Till such time as the soul remains in the body, one can perform all the tasks and after departure of life the human body becomes untouchable and has to be abandoned. Who does not consider such a type of god Shiva to possess a human form.

## \|\| 17.30 \|\|

*svayaṃ brahmā ca jagatāṃ vidhātā sarvakārakaḥ \|*  
*pādāravindamaniśaṃ dhyāyate draṣṭumakṣamaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The creator of the universe and all the creatures of the world, is known by the name of Brahma who also adores at his feet day and night, but is unable to meet him.

## \|\| 17.31 \|\|

*yugalakṣaṃ tapastaptaṃ śrīkṛṣṇasya ca vedhasā \|*  
*tadā babhūva jñānī ca jagatsraṣṭuṃ kṣamastadā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Lord Brahma once adored lord Krishna to please him, for a lakh of yugas and could achieve intelligence and was able to create the universe.

## \|\| 17.32 \|\|

*asaṃkhyakālaṃ suciraṃ tapastaptaṃ harermayā \|*  
*tṛptiṃ jagāma na manastṛpyate kena maṅgale \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. I have also adored lord Vishnu for a long time but could not achieve the satisfaction of mind. Who could be satisfied with one’s own welfare.

## \|\| 17.33 \|\|

*adhunā pañcavaktreṇa yannāmaguṇakīrtanam \|*  
*gāyanbhramāmi sarvatra nispṛhaḥ sarvakarmasu \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Currently I am reciting his name with all the five faces and roaming about everywhere without involving myself in anything.

## \|\| 17.34 \|\|

*matto yāti ca mṛtyuśca yannāmaguṇakīrttanāt \|*  
*śaśvajapantaṃ tannāma dṛṣṭvā mṛtyuḥ palāyate \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Since I continue reciting his name and his glory the death has not reached before me because the death runs away from the person who recites his name.

## \|\| 17.35 \|\|

*sarvabrahmāṇḍasaṃharttā'pyahaṃ mṛtyuṃjayābhidhaḥ \|*  
*suciraṃ tapasā yasya guṇanāmānukīrttanāt \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. By reciting his name and performing tapas for him for a long time, I have achieved the prowess of the destruction of the globe and have conquered even the death.

## \|\| 17.36 \|\|

*kāle tatra vilīno'hamāvirbhūtastataḥ punaḥ \|*  
*na kālo mama saṃharttā na mṛtyuryatprasādataḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. At the appropriate time I get myself merged into him and thereafter I emerge from him at the appropriate time. By his grace I have been able to overcome the death and the time.

## \|\| 17.37 \|\|

*goloke yaḥ sa vaikuṇṭhe śvetadvīpe sa eva ca \|*  
*aṃśāṃśinorna bhedaśca brahmanvahnisphuliṅgavat \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O Brahmana, lord Krishna who is in Goloka also resides in Vaikuntha or Shveta-dvipa\\ as there is hardly any difference in the fire and the spark, similarly there is hardly any difference between the lord and his amsha.

## \|\| 17.38-39 \|\|

*manvantaraṃ tu divyānāṃ yugānāmekasaptatiḥ \|*  
*aṣṭāviṃśatime śakre gate ca brahmaṇo dinam \|\| 38 \|\|*

*etatsaṃkhyāviśiṣṭasya śatavarṣāyuṣo vidheḥ \|*  
*pāte locanapātaśca yadviṣṇoḥ paramātmanaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. Seventy one divine yugas stand for a manvantara and in every manvantara there are two Indras. After the expiry of the time of twenty eight Indras, a day of Brahma is formed. Similarly at the expiry of the age of a hundred years of Brahma, lord Vishnu twinkles an eye-lid.

## \|\| 17.40 \|\|

*ahaṃ kalānāmṛṣabhaḥ kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*paraṃ mahimnaḥ ko gacchenna jānāmi ca kiñcana \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Lord Krishna is the master of all the arts but no one can reach his glory. I am unaware of anything about him.

## \|\| 17.41 \|\|

*ityuktvā śaṅkarastatra virarāma ca śaunaka \|*  
*dharmaśca vaktumārebhe yaḥ sākṣī sarvakarmaṇām \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. О Shaunaka, thus speaking lord Shiva kept quiet. Thereafter Dharma who observes the deeds of all, said –

## \|\| 17.42 \|\|

*dharma uvāca \|*  
*yatpāṇipādau sarvatra cakṣuśca sarvadarśanam \|*  
*sarvāntarātmā pratyakṣo'pratyakṣaśca durātmanaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Dharma said — The one whose hand and feet are spread everywhere, whose eyes see everything, he who happens to be visible to the noble souls and for the wicked souls he remains invisible.

## \|\| 17.43 \|\|

*adhunā'pi sabhāṃ viṣṇurnāyāta iti yadvacaḥ \|*  
*tvayoktaṃ tatkayā buddhyā munīnāṃ ca matibhramaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. You have spoken that he is not present currently in the assembly. How have you said thus? These your words can mislead even the ascetics.

## \|\| 17.44 \|\|

*mahannindā bhavedyatra naiva sādhu śṛṇoti tām \|*  
*nindakaḥ śrotṛbhiḥ sārddhaṃ kumbhīpākaṃ vrajedyugam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The place were the elders are denounced, the noble people do not listen to such words, because the one who listens to such words, also proceeds to the terrific hell, with those who denounce him. He remains there suffering for yugas.

## \|\| 17.45 \|\|

*śrutvā daivānmahannindāṃ śrīviṣṇoḥ smaraṇād budhaḥ \|*  
*mucyate sarvapāpebhyaḥ puṇyaṃ prāpnoti durlabham \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. As luck would have it, listening to the denouncing of the elderly people, even the intelligent people get relieved of the sins by reciting the name of Vishnu and achieve great merit.

## \|\| 17.46-47 \|\|

*kāmato'kāmato vā'pi viṣṇunindāṃ karoti yaḥ \|*  
*śṛṇoti yo hasati vā sabhāmadhye narādhamaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

*kumbhīpāke pacati sa yāvaddhi brahmaṇo vayaḥ \|*  
*sthalaṃ bhavedapūtaṃ ca surāpātraṃ yathā dvija \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. Those who denounce lord Vishnu intentionally or unintentionally or the one who listens to the denouncing of Vishnu, sitting in an assembly and laughs at him, he remains in the terrific hell up to the life of lord Brahma. О Brahmana, like the impure vase of wine that place also becomes impure.

## \|\| 17.48 \|\|

*prāṇī ca narakaṃ yāti śrutaṃ tatraiva ceddhruvam \|*  
*viṣṇunindā ca trividhā brahmaṇā kathitā purā \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Going there whosoever listens to the denouncing of the lord, surely falls into the hell. Earlier Brahma had spelt out three types of denouncing of the lord. Firstly people denounce him in his absence, secondly there are those who don’t believe in him and thirdly there are those who compare him with other gods. Such a type of denouncement is made by people who are short of knowledge.

## \|\| 17.49-50 \|\|

*apratyakṣaṃ ca kurute kiṃ vā taṃ ca na manyate \|*  
*devānyasāmyaṃ kurute jñānahīno narādhamaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*tasyātra niṣkṛtirnāsti yāvadvai brahmaṇaḥ śatam \|*  
*gurornindāṃ yaḥ karoti piturnindāṃ narādhamaḥ \|*  
*sa yāti kālasūtraṃ ca yāvaccandradivākarau \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. The one who does so, is not redeemed even by remaining in the hell for the life of a hundred Brahmas. Similarly those who denounce their own father and teacher, fall in the hell and remain there as long as the sun and the moon continue.

## \|\| 17.51 \|\|

*viṣṇurguruśca sarveṣāṃ janako jñānadāyakaḥ \|*  
*poṣṭā pātā bhayatrātā varadātā jagattraye \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Vishnu is the lord and teacher of all the three worlds. He happens to be the father, the teacher, preserver, remover of the dangers and the one who bestows the boons.

## \|\| 17.52 \|\|

*eṣāṃ ca vacanaṃ śrutvā trayāṇāṃ viprapuṅgava \|*  
*prahasyovāca tāndevānvācā madhurayā punaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. On hearing the words of all the three, the great Brahmana smiled and looking at them, he spoke with a sweet voice.

## \|\| 17.53-54 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*kā kṛtā viṣṇunindā'ho he devā dharmaśālinaḥ \|*  
*nāgato hariratreti vyarthā''kāśasarasvatī \|\| 53 \|\|*

*iti proktaṃ mayā bhadraṃ brūta dharmārthamīśvarāḥ \|*  
*sabhāyāṃ pākṣikāḥ santo ghnanti sma śatapūruṣam \|\| 54 \|\|*
