53-54. Brahmana said — О religious minded gods, have I denounced lord Vishnu? I have only spoken that Vishnu has not arrived here. Therefore, the divine voice from the sky has been proved to be false. All of you are the lords, therefore tell me on oath, because the one, who takes sides,' in the assembly destroys his next hundred generations.

## \|\| 17.55 \|\|

*yūyaṃ ca bhāvukā brūta viṣṇuḥ sarvatra santatam \|*  
*iti cettatkathaṃ yātāḥ śvetadvīpaṃ varāya ca \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Getting emotional you speak that Vishnu is omnipresent. If it be so, why have you arrived in the Shveta-dvipa for getting the boon.

## \|\| 17.56 \|\|

*aṃśāṃśinorna bhedaścedātmanaśceti niścitam \|*  
*kalāṃ hitvā niṣevante santaḥ pūrṇatamaṃ katham \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. There is no different between Amsa and Am si and there is no difference in the soul. If this is your confirmed opinion then tell me why the best of the people discarding Amsa, adore the Arhs'fl

## \|\| 17.57 \|\|

*koṭijanmadurārādhyamasādhyamasatāmapi \|*  
*āśā balavatī puṃsāṃ kṛṣṇaṃ sevitumicchati \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. The devotion of Krishna is impossible to get the wicked people and the noble ones always try to serve the lord with devotion.

## \|\| 17.58 \|\|

*kiṃ kṣudrāḥ kiṃ mahāntaśca vāñchanti paramaṃ padam \|*  
*labdhumicchati candraṃ ca bāhubhyāṃ vāmano yathā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Holding the crescent in both the hands, irrespective of being big or small, all the people aspire for the highest place.

## \|\| 17.59-61 \|\|

*yo viṣṇurviṣayī viśve śvetadvīpanivāsakṛt \|*  
*yūyaṃ brahmeśadharmāśca dikpālāśca digīśvarāḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

*brahmaviṣṇuśivādyāśca suralokāścarācarāḥ \|*  
*evaṃ katividhāḥ santi prativiśveṣu santatam \|\| 60 \|\|*

*viśvānāṃ ca surāṇāṃ ca kaḥ saṃkhyāṃ kartumīśvaraḥ \|*  
*sarveṣāmīśvaraḥ kṛṣṇo bhaktānugrahavigrahaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

59-61. Vishnu recites at one place which is located on Shveta-dvJpa in the universe. Yourself, Brahma, Shiva, Dharma and the gods of all the directions reside there. Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva and other gods differently lodged in the different worlds who will be in a position to count the number of such globes? Shri Krishna happens to be the lord of all of them, who, in order to grace of all of them, who in order to grace his devotees, takes to divine form.

## \|\| 17.62 \|\|

*ūrdhvaṃ ca sarvabrahmāṇḍādvaikuṇṭhaṃ satyamīpsitam \|*  
*tasmādūrdhvaṃ ca golokaḥ pañcāśatkoṭiyojanam \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Satyaloka or the Vaikuntha which is desired by all, his lodged above all the globes. The Goloka is still above that which is spread over an area of fifty crores of yojanas.

## \|\| 17.63 \|\|

*caturbhujaśca vaikuṇṭhe lakṣmīkāntaḥ sanātanaḥ \|*  
*sunandanandakumudapārṣadādibhirāvṛtaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. In the Vaikuntha the eternal lord Vishnu who is the lord of Lakshmi and has four arms, resides. He is flanked by the attendants named Sunanda, Nanda, Kumuda, besides other courtiers.

## \|\| 17.64 \|\|

*goloke dvibhujaḥ kṛṣṇo rādhākāntaḥ sanātanaḥ \|*  
*gopāṅganādibhiryukto dvibhujairvopapārṣadaiḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. In the Goloka lord Krishna who is the lord of Lakshmi as well resides there having two arms only. The cowherdesses of that eternal lord having two arms only, keep surrounding him together with his courtiers.

## \|\| 17.65 \|\|

*paripūrṇatamaṃ brahma sa cātmā sarvadehinām \|*  
*svecchāmayaśca viharedrāse vṛndāvane sadā \|\| 65\|\|*

65\. The same lord Krishna is the complete Brahman himself. He is the soul of all the gods. He takes to any forms he lives and roams about in the Vrandavana engaging himself in Rasamandala.

## \|\| 17.66-68 \|\|

*tajjyotirmaṇḍalākāraṃ sūryyakoṭisamaprabham \|*  
*dhyāyante yoginaḥ santaḥ santataṃ ca nirāmayam \|\| 66 \|\|*

*navīnanīradaśyāmaṃ dvibhujaṃ pītavāsasam \|*  
*koṭikandarpalāvaṇyalīlādhāma manoharam \|\| 67 \|\|*

*kiśoranayanaṃ śaśvacchāntaṃ sasmitamī śvaram \|*  
*dhyāyante vaiṣṇavāḥ santaḥ sevante satyavigraham \|\| 68 \|\|*

66-68. The ascetics meditate upon the Jyoti of the same lord which has the lustre of crores of suns. His glory resembles that of the dark clouds. He has two arms and is clad in yellow lower garments. He possesses lustre and beauty which surpasses crores of gods of love. He is full of illusion. His beauty is quite charming. He appears of tender age. He is the peaceful great soul and spreads the lustre of his serene smile everywhere. The Vaishnava and ascetics always meditate upon such a truthful body of the lord.

## \|\| 17.69 \|\|

*yūyaṃ ca vaiṣṇavā brūta kasya vaṃśodbhavo bhavān \|*  
*śiṣyaḥ kasya munīndrasyetyevaṃ māṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. All of you are Vaishnavas and you are enquiring of me again and again, to which rays of the lord I belong and who happens to be my teacher.

## \|\| 17.70 \|\|

*yasya vaṃśodbhavo'haṃ ca yasya śiṣyaśca bālakaḥ \|*  
*tasyedaṃ vacanaṃ jñānaṃ devasaṃghā nibodhata \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. O gods, the race in which I am born and the one of whom I happen to be the child and the pupil, this entire knowledge is attributed to him, the lord. You must think over it.

## \|\| 17.71 \|\|

*śīghraṃ jīvaya gandharvaṃ deveśvara sureśvara \|*  
*vyakte vicāre mūrkhaḥ ko vāgyuddhe kiṃ prayojanam \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. О god of gods, you revive this Gandharva back to life. In case you think over deeply, you will come to know as to who is intelligent and who happens to be the fool. What is the use of raising any controversy?

## \|\| 17.72 \|\|

*ityuktvā bālakastatra viprarūpī janārdanaḥ \|*  
*virarāma sabhāmadhye prajahāsa ca śaunaka \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. О Shaunaka, the lord Janardana who had appeared in the form of a boy kept quiet after thus speaking and he laughed aloud in the assembly.

## Chapter 18

## \|\| 18.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*devāḥ sārddhaṃ brāhmaṇena mohitā viṣṇumāyayā \|*  
*prayayurmālatīmūlaṃ brahmeśānapurogamāḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said — All the gods were influenced by the illusion of Vishnu including Brahma and Shiva who reached before Malavati together with the Brahmana boy.

## \|\| 18.2 \|\|

*brahmā kamaṇḍalujalaṃ dadau gātre śavasya ca \|*  
*saṃcāraṃ manasastasya cakāra sundaraṃ vapuḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Brahma then sprinkled water out of his Kamandalu over the dead body of Upbarhana, as a result of which, life came back in his body and he became extremely active.

## \|\| 18.3 \|\|

*jñānadānaṃ dadau tasmai jñānānandaḥ śivaḥ svayam \|*  
*dharmajñānaṃ svayaṃ dharmo jīvadānaṃ ca brāhmaṇaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Shiva who is the lord of all knowledge bestowed his knowledge on him. Dharma, on the other hand, educated him in Dharma.

## \|\| 18.4 \|\|

*vahnidarśanamātreṇa babhūva jaṭharānalaḥ \|*  
*kāmadarśanamātreṇa sarvakāmaḥ suniścitam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. At the sight of Agni, the fire was kindled in his body. At the sight of the god of love, all the desires flourished in his body.

## \|\| 18.5 \|\|

*tasya vāyoradhiṣṭhānājjagatprāṇasvarūpiṇaḥ \|*  
*niśvāsasya ca sañcāraḥ prāṇānāṃ ca babhūva ha \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The wind god who happens to be the life of all, inserted wind into the body.

## \|\| 18.6-7 \|\|

*sarvvādhiṣṭhānamātreṇa dṛṣṭiśaktirbabhūva ha \|*  
*vākyaṃ vāṇīdarśanena śobhā śrīdarśanena ca \|\| 6 \|\|*

*śavastathā'pi nottasthau yathā śete jaḍastathā \|*  
*viśiṣṭabodhanaṃ prāpa cādhiṣṭhānaṃ vinā''tmanaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. With the rays of the sun falling on the body his eye sight was revived. With the graceful look of Sarasvati at the body, his strength of speech was revived and he could see the glory of the lord everywhere. Inspite of all this, that body remained lying on earth because without self-consciousness one cannot revive.

## \|\| 18.8 \|\|

*brahmaṇo vacanātsādhvī tuṣṭāva parameśvaram \|*  
*snātvā śīghraṃ sarittoye dhṛtvā dhaute ca vāsasī \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Thereafter at the instance of Brahma, the chaste lady washed the body with the water of the sacred river and made him wear the divine garments. Thereafter both of them started offering prayer to the lord.

## \|\| 18.9 \|\|

*mālāvatyuvāca \|*  
*vande taṃ paramātmānaṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam \|*  
*vinā yena śavāḥ sarve prāṇino jagatītale \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Malavati said — I bow in reverence to the great soul who happens to be the cause of all the causes. Without whom all the people of the world are lifeless.

## \|\| 18.10 \|\|

*nirliptaṃ sākṣirūpaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ sarvakarmasu \|*  
*vidyamānaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ ca sarvaiḥ sarvatra sarvadā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. He is uninvolved and he remains witness to all the deeds of all the people at the times but all the people cannot see him.

## \|\| 18.11 \|\|

*yena sṛṣṭā ca prakṛtiḥ sarvādhāro parātparā \|*  
*brahmaviṣṇuśivādīnāṃ prasūryā triguṇātmikā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The same Brahman has created Prakriti (nature), who is the base of all the creatures of the universe and also happens to be the creator of Brahma, Vishnu, Shiva and others.

## \|\| 18.12-13 \|\|

*jagatsraṣṭā svayaṃ brahmā niyato yasya sevayā \|*  
*pātā viṣṇuśca jagatāṃ saṃharttā śaṅkaraḥ svayam \|\| 12 \|\|*

*dhyāyante yaṃ surāḥ sarve munayo manavastathā \|*  
*siddhāśca yoginaḥ santaḥ santataṃ prakṛteḥ param \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. Brahma himself, who is the creator of this world always serves him with devotion. Vishnu and the lord Shiva who are the destroyers of all, also serve him. he is beyond Prakriti and the god is always adored by all the gods, ascetics, Manu, siddhas, yogis and other mendicants.

## \|\| 18.14-15 \|\|

*sākāraṃ ca nirākāraṃ paraṃ svecchāmayaṃ vibhum \|*  
*varaṃ vareṇyaṃ varadaṃ varārhaṃ varakāraṇam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*tapaḥphalaṃ tapobījaṃ tapasāṃ ca phalapradam \|*  
*svayaṃtapaḥsvarūpaṃ ca sarvarūpaṃ ca sarvataḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. Не is visible, invisible and is the best. He moves according to his own sweet-will. He pervades everywhere; he is the best of all; granter of boons; cause of boons and giver of the fruits of tap as. He is the seed of tapas, grants results of tapas and he is himself the form of tapas.

## \|\| 18.16-17 \|\|

*sarvādhāraṃ sarvabījaṃ karma tatkarmaṇāṃ phalam \|*  
*teṣāṃ ca phaladātāraṃ tadbījaṃ kṣayakāraṇam \|\| 16 \|\|*

*svayaṃtejaḥsvarūpaṃ ca bhaktānugrahavigraham \|*  
*sevādhyānaṃ na ghaṭate bhaktānāṃ vigrahaṃ vinā \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. He is the base of everyone, the seed of everyone, the reward of all the deeds of the people. He grants the fruits and is the destroyer of the seed of karma. He himself is the fonn of lustre, is compassionate to his devotees and to grace them he takes to human form. Because without the bodies how shall the devotees be able to serve him.

## \|\| 18.18 \|\|

*tattejo maṇḍalākāraṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabham \|*  
*atīva kamanīyaṃ ca rūpaṃ tatra manoharam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. He has a disk of lustre, which could be compared with crores of the Suryas. He is quite beautiful and charming to look at.

## \|\| 18.19-22 \|\|

*navīnanīradaśyāmaṃ śaratpaṅkajalocanam \|*  
*śaratpārvaṇacandrāsyamīṣaddhāsyasamanvitam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*koṭikandarpalāvaṇyaṃ līlādhāma manoharam \|*  
*candanokṣitasarvāṅgaṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*dvibhujaṃ muralīhastaṃ pītakauśeyavāsasam \|*  
*kiśoravayasaṃ śāntaṃ rādhākāntamanantakam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*gopāṅganāparivṛtaṃ kutracinnirjane vane \|*  
*kutracidrāsamadhyasthaṃ rādhayā pariṣevitam \|\| 22 \|\|*

19-22. He has the dark complexion comparable to the dark clouds; the eyes are like the lotus flowers; he wears a smile resembling the grace of the full moon and holds the beauty which could put crores of gods of love to shame. These are his normal qualities. The sandal-paste is applied over all the limbs of his body. All the limbs of the body are adorned with gems studded ornaments. He has two arms and he holds a flute in his hands. He is clad in the yellow lower garment and always appears in the tender age. He is peaceful and is the lord of Radha. He is quite blissful and is surrounded by cowherdesses in lonely forests. Sometimes he is engaged in Rasallla with Radha.

## \|\| 18.23-25 \|\|

*kutracidgopaveṣaṃ ca veṣṭitaṃ gopabālakaiḥ \|*  
*śataśṛṅgācalotkṛṣṭe ramye vṛndāvane vane \|\| 23 \|\|*

*nikaraṃ kāmadhenūnāṃ rakṣantaṃ śiśurūpiṇam \|*  
*goloke virajātīre pārijātavane vane \|\| 24 \|\|*

*veṇuṃ kvaṇantaṃ madhuraṃ gopīsammohakāraṇam \|*  
*nirāmaye ca vaikuṃṭhe kutracicca caturbhujam \|\| 25 \|\|*

23-25. Sometimes taking to the form of a cowherd, he is accompanied by cowherd boys in the Vmdavana garden having a mount Govardhan with a hundred high peaks. Sometimes he is found grazing the Kamadhenu cows. The same Krishna sometimes roams about in the Goloka accompanied by Lakshmi and entering the forest of Parijata flowers, prepares the garlands, adorning the heads of cowherdesses with them. Sometimes he appears in the Vaikuntha, having four arms.

## \|\| 18.26-30 \|\|

*lakṣmīkāntaṃ pārṣadaiśca sevitaṃ ca caturbhujaiḥ \|*  
*kutracitsvāṃśarūpeṇa jagatāṃ pālanāya ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

*śvetadvīpe viṣṇurūpaṃ padmayā pariṣevitam \|*  
*kutracitsvāṃśakalayā brahmāṇḍabrahmarūpiṇam \|\| 27 \|\|*

*śivasvarūpaṃ śivadaṃ svāṃśena śivarūpiṇam \|*  
*svātmanaḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśena sarvādhāraṃ parātparam \|\| 28 \|\|*

*svayaṃ mahadvirāḍrūpaṃ viśvaughaṃ yasya lomasu \|*  
*līlayā svāṃśakalayā jagatāṃ pālanāya ca \|\| 29 \|\|*

*nānāvatāraṃ bibhrantaṃ bījaṃ teṣāṃ sanātanam \|*  
*vasantaṃ kutracitsantaṃ yogināṃ hṛdaye satām \|\| 30 \|\|*

26-30. Sometimes taking to the fonn of the lord of Lakshmi, having four arms, is surrounded by his attendants. Sometimes in order to preserve the three lokas he appears in the Shveta-dvipa in the form of a Vishnu and is served by Kamala or Laks ml. Sometime, he roams about in the form of a Brahmana in the universe. Sometimes with the application of his sixteenth rays he influences lord Shiva and takes to his universal form. The entire universe is lodged in his body appropriately. Sometimes, displaying his illusion, he incarnates on earth, of which, he happens to be the eternal seed. Sometime, he resides in the hearts of the virtuous yogis.

## \|\| 18.31-32 \|\|

*prāṇarūpaṃ prāṇināṃ ca paramātmānamīśvaram \|*  
*taṃ ca stotumaśaktā'hamabalā nirguṇaṃ vibhum \|\| 31 \|\|*

*nirlakṣyaṃ ca nirīhaṃ ca sāraṃ vāṅmanasoḥ param \|*  
*yaṃ stotumakṣamo'nantaḥ sahasravadanena ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. He is the invisible one and how can a helpless lady like me, offer prayers to him? The limitless Sheshanaga is unable to recite his glory with thousands of his hoods.

## \|\| 18.33 \|\|

*pañcavaktraścaturvaktro gajavaktraḥ ṣaḍānanaḥ \|*  
*yaṃ stotuṃ na kṣamā māyā mohitā yasya māyayā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Influenced by the illusion of whom the five faced Shiva and the four headed Brahma and the six headed Karttikeya are unable to recite his glory.

## \|\| 18.34-35 \|\|

*yaṃ stotuṃ na kṣamā śrīśca jaḍībhūtā sarasvatī \|*  
*vedā na śaktā yaṃ stotuṃ ko vā vidvāṃśca vedavit \|\| 34 \|\|*

*kiṃ staumi tamanīhaṃ ca śokārta strī parātparam \|*  
*ityuktvā sā ca gāvī virarāma ruroda ca \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. His glory cannot be recited by Lakshmi or Sarasvati who stand quietly before him. The Vedas are unable to sing his glory. Then how could one of the intellectuals do so? How can a helpless woman like me recite the glory of lord Krishna?” Saying this the Gandharva damsel started speaking and tears started flowing from her eyes.

## \|\| 18.36-37 \|\|

*kṛpānidhiṃ praṇanāma bhayārtā ca punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*kṛṣṇaśca śaktibhiḥ sārddhamadhiṣṭhānaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 36 \|\|*

*bharturasyāntare tasyāḥ paramātmā nirākṛtiḥ \|*  
*utthāya śīghraṃ vīṇāṃ ca dhṛtvā snātvā ca vāsasī \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. In panic she bowed in reverence to her lord again. Then lord Krishna, the great soul, rejuvenated the heart of her husband. The body of the Gandharva was suddenly activated and he got up at once. Thereafter he took his lute and was clad in a couple of garments.

## \|\| 18.38 \|\|

*praṇanāma devasaṃghaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ purataḥ sthitam \|*  
*nedurdundubhayo devāḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ ca cakrire \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. He saluted the group of gods which was headed by lord Brahma. Thereafter the gods started plying on trumpets and showered flowers on him.

## \|\| 18.39-40 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā copari dampatyoḥ pradaduḥ paramāśiṣam \|*  
*gandharvo devapurato nanartta ca jagau kṣaṇam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*jīvitaṃ purataḥ prāpa devānāṃ ca vareṇa ca \|*  
*jagāma patnyā sārddhaṃ ca pitrā mātrā ca harṣitaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. The gods then blessed them variously. Gandharva then danced before the gods for a while besides singing and he got a new life. Thereafter the Gandharva left the place and went to his abode accompanied by his parents and wife.

## \|\| 18.41-43 \|\|

*upabarhaṇagandharvo gandharvanagaraṃ punaḥ \|*  
*mālāvatī ratnakoṭiṃ dhanāni vividhāni ca \|\| 41 \|\|*

*pradadau brāhmaṇebhyaśca bhojayāmāsa tānsatī \|*  
*vedāṃśca pāṭhayāmāsa kārayāmāsa maṅgalam \|\| 42 \|\|*

*mahotsavaṃ ca vividhaṃ harernāmaikamaṅgalam \|*  
*jagmurdevāśca svasthānaṃ viprarūpī hariḥ svayam \|\| 43 \|\|*

41-43. His wife Malavati gave away in charity crores of gems and riches to the Brahmanas feeding them at the same time. He also made them to recite the Vedas and performed other welfare activities; many types of festivities were organised and the reciting of the name of Hari dominated all the welfare ceremony. Thereafter the gods and the lord who had appeared in the fonn of a Brahmana went back to their own abodes.

## \|\| 18.44-47 \|\|

*etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ stavarājaṃ ca śaunaka \|*  
*idaṃ stotraṃ puṇyarūpaṃ pūjākāle tu yaḥ paṭhet \|\| 44 \|\|*

*haribhaktiṃ harerdāsyaṃ labhate vaiṣṇavojanaḥ \|*  
*varārthī yaḥ paṭhedbhaktyā cāstikaḥ paramāsthayā \|\| 45 \|\|*

*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ niścitaṃ labhate phalam \|*  
*vidyārthī labhate vidyāṃ dhanārthī labhate dhanam \|\| 46 \|\|*

*bhāryyārthī labhate bhāryyāṃ putrārthī labhate sutam \|*  
*dharmārthī labhate dharmaṃ yaśo'rthī labhate yaśaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

44-47. О Shaunaka, I have narrated this story to you with Stavaraja. Whosoever will recite this auspicious stotra at the time of puja, will achieve the grace of Vishnu, his devotion and the wish to serve him. Whosoever will recite it with a belief in the lord or the one who will recite this stotra with devotion, he will achieve Dharma, Artha, Kama and Moksa undoubtedly. Similarly a student will achieve his learnings, riches by the desirous person, wife to the wife-seeker, son to the son-seeker and dharma to the religious people.

## \|\| 18.48 \|\|

*bhraṣṭarājyo labhedrājyaṃ prajābhraṣṭaḥ prajāṃ labhet \|*  
*rogārtto mucyate rogādbaddho mucyeta bandhanāt \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The kings who are dethroned and have become wanderers will get back their kingdoms. The sick will be relieved of their sickness. Those who are in bondages will be relieved of them.

## \|\| 18.49-50 \|\|

*bhayānmucyeta bhītastu dhanaṃ naṣṭadhano labhet \|*  
*dasyugrasto mahāraṇye hiṃsrajantusamanvitaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*dāvāgnidagdho mucyeta nimagnaśca jalārṇave \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. The terrified persons would be relieved of the terror and the people who have lost their riches will get them back. When one is surrounded by wild animals or robbers in the forest, the reciting of this stotra would relieve them of the danger. If a person is surrounded by a forest fire or is about to drown in the ocean, he can be saved with the reciting of the stotra.

## Chapter 19

## \|\| 19.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*mālāvatī dhanaṃ dattvā brāhmaṇebhyaḥ praharṣitā \|*  
*cakāra vividhaṃ veśaṃ svātmanaḥ svāminaḥ kṛte \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said — Malavati got delighted and gave away in charity enormous riches to the Brahmana and thereafter she decorated herself with cosmetics to welcome her husband.

## \|\| 19.2 \|\|

*bhartuścakāra śuśrūṣāṃ pūjāṃ ca samayocitām \|*  
*tena sārddhaṃ surasikā reme sā suciraṃ mudā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Serving her husband and adoring him appropriately, that beautiful damsel enjoyed the company of her husband for a long time.

## \|\| 19.3 \|\|

*mahāpuruṣastotraṃ ca pūjāṃ ca kavacaṃ manum \|*  
*vismṛtaṃ bodhayāmāsa svayaṃ rahasi suvratā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Thereafter the chaste lady enlightened her husband surprisingly of Mahapurusa, stotra, puja, kavacha and the relevant Mantras.

## \|\| 19.4 \|\|

*purā dattaṃ vasiṣṭhena stotrapūjādikaṃ hareḥ \|*  
*gandharvāya ca mālatyai mantramekaṃ ca puṣkare \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. In the earlier times Vasistha imparted the knowledge of the stotras to Gandharva and Malavati.

## \|\| 19.5 \|\|

*vismṛtaṃ stotrakavacaṃ vasiṣṭhaśca kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*gandharvarājaṃ rahasi bodhayāmāsa śūlinaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Thereafter the compassionate Vasistha, imparted the knowledge of Shivastotra and kavacha.

## \|\| 19.6 \|\|

*evaṃ cakāra rājyaṃ ca kuberabhavanopame \|*  
*āśrame paramānando gandharvo bāndhavaiḥ saha \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Thus the Gandharva resided delightfully in the Kubera Bhavana together with his relatives, enjoying all the royal pleasures.

## \|\| 19.7 \|\|

*yathātathā gatābhiśca strībhiranyābhireva ca \|*  
*āgatya tābhiḥ svasvāmī saṃprāptaḥ parayā mudā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The other wives of Upabarhana also reached there and enjoyed the company of their husband.

## \|\| 19.8 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ stotraṃ kavacaṃ viṣṇormantrapūjāvidhiḥ purā \|*  
*datto viśiṣṭastābhyāṃ ca taṃ bhavānvaktumarhati \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Shaunaka said — In the earlier times you have told me that the sage Vasistha had imparted the knowledge of PUjanavidhi (the method of performing puja), to both of them. You kindly enlighten me about the same now.

## \|\| 19.9-10 \|\|

*dvādaśākṣaramantraṃ ca śūlinaḥ kavacādikam \|*  
*dattaṃ gandharvarājāya vasiṣṭhena ca kiṃ purā \|\| 9 \|\|*

*tadapi brūhi he saute śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ mama \|*  
*śaṅkarastotrakavacaṃ mantraṃ durgatināśanam \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. You also kindly enlighten me about the twelve letter Mantra and kavacha which Vasistha had bestowed on the Gandharva king in the earlier times. I am quite anxious to listen to the same. The kavacha of Shiva, the stotra and the Mantra remove all misfortunes.

## \|\| 19.11 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*tuṣṭāva yena stotreṇa mālatī parameśvaram \|*  
*tadeva stotraṃ dattaṃ ca mantraṃ ca kavacaṃ śṛṇu \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Sauti said — The stotra, by reciting which Malavati pleased lord Krishna was bestowed by Vasistha to the Gandharva couple. You listen to the same kavacha and the mantra.

## \|\| 19.12 \|\|

*oṃ namo bhagavate rāsamaṇḍaleśāya svāhā \|*  
*imaṃ mantraṃ kalpataruṃ pradadau ṣoḍaśākṣaram \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Salutation to the lord of the Rasmandala, this is the sixteen letter mantra which is like the kalpavrksa (wish-fulfilling tree) which was given to the couple.

## \|\| 19.13 \|\|

*purā dattaṃ kumārāya brahmaṇā puṣkare hareḥ \|*  
*purā dattaṃ ca kṛṣṇena goloke śaṅkarāya ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The same mantra in earlier times was given over by Brahma to Kumara in the Puskara region and the same was bestowed by lord Krishna on Shiva in Goloka.

## \|\| 19.14 \|\|

*dhyānaṃ ca viṣṇorvedoktaṃ śāśvataṃ sarvadurlabham \|*  
*mūlena sarvaṃ deyaṃ ca naivedyādikamuttamam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. It is even difficult to meditate upon lord Vishnu according to the Vedic rites, but I am telling you the same. With the aforesaid basic mantra lord Vishnu should be offered eatables and the best of other things.

## \|\| 19.15-16 \|\|

*atīva guptakavacaṃ piturvaktrānmayā śrutam \|*  
*pitre dattaṃ purā vipra gaṅgāyāṃ śūlinā dhruvam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*śūline brahmaṇe dattaṃ goloke rāsamaṇḍale \|*  
*dharmāya gopīkāntena kṛpayā paramādbhutam \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. О Brahmana, I listen to the secret kavacha of the lord from my father’s mouth. The same was bestowed on my father by Shiva over the bank of the Ganga and the same was bestowed on Shiva in the Goloka during the performing of Rcisamanclala by lord Krishna. The same was also given out to Brahma and Dharma. I am now telling you about the kavacha.

## \|\| 19.17 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*rādhākānta mahābhāga kavacaṃ yatprakāśitam \|*  
*brahmāṇḍapāvanaṃ nāma kṛpayā kathaya prabho \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Brahma said — О lord of Radha, О virtuous one, О lord, the kavacu about which you have narrated for the purification of the universe, you kindly tell us.

## \|\| 19.18 \|\|

*māṃ maheśaṃ ca dharmaṃ ca bhaktaṃ ca bhaktavatsala \|*  
*tvatprasādena putrebhyo dāsyāmi bhaktisaṃyutaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. О lord, О beslower of the kindness to the devotees, all the three of us namely myself Mahesa and Dharma happen to be your devotees. By knowing the secret from you, we shall pass it on to our sons.

## \|\| 19.19-20 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vakṣyāmi brahmeśa dharmedaṃ kavacaṃ param \|*  
*ahaṃ dāsyāmi yuṣmabhyaṃ gopanīyaṃ sudurlamam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*yasmai kasmai na dātavyaṃ prāṇatulyaṃ mamaiva hi \|*  
*yattejo mama dehe'sti tattejaḥ kavace'pi ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. Krishna said — О lord Brahma, О Dharma, I am revealing to you the most secret and difficult kavacha which is like my life. Therefore, the knowledge of the same should not be imparted to everyone, because, the lustre which is possessed by me in my body is also possessed by it.

## \|\| 19.21 \|\|

*kuru sṛṣṭimimaṃ dhṛtvā dhātā trijagatāṃ bhava \|*  
*saṃharttā bhava he śambho mama tulyo bhave bhava \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. О Brahman, you resort to creation after knowing it, which will establish you as the creator of the universe. О Shiva, by wearing this kavacha, after destroying the entire universe, you will possess the same prowess as I do.

## \|\| 19.22 \|\|

*he dharma tvamimaṃ dhṛtvā bhava sākṣī ca karmaṇām \|*  
*tapasāṃ phaladātāro yūyaṃ bhavata madvarāt \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. О Dharma, by following this kavacha you will witness the deeds of others and with the boon granted by me, you bestow the result of their tapas on all the people.

## \|\| 19.23-24 \|\|

*brahmāṇḍapāvanasyāsya kavacasya hariḥ svayam \|*  
*ṛṣiśchandaśca gāyatrī devo'haṃ jagadīśvaraḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu viniyogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*trilakṣavāra paṭhanātsiddhidaṃ kavacaṃ vidhe \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. This kavacha which is the purifier of the universe has Vishnu as the Rsi, Gayatri as the metre, Jagdlsvara, (lord Krishna) happens to be the god and the Viniyoga (the application) has to be performed. О Brahma by reciting it three lakhs of times, one attains success with the kavacha.

## \|\| 19.25 \|\|

*yo bhavetsiddhakavaco mama tulyo bhavecca saḥ \|*  
*tejasā siddhiyogena jñānena vikrameṇa ca \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The one who achieves success with this kavacha equates with me in glory, intelligence and prowess.

## \|\| 19.26-27 \|\|

*praṇavo me śiraḥ pātu namo rāmeśvarāya ca \|*  
*bhālaṃ pāyānnetrayugmaṃ namo rādheśvarāya ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇaḥ pāyācchrotrayugmaṃ he hare prāṇameva ca \|*  
*jihvikāṃ vahnijāyā tu kṛṣṇāyeti ca sarvataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Pranava (orhkara) should protect my head, salutation to the lord of Rasa (dancing), salutation to him. (яяжтя ЧЯ:) This mantra should protect my forehead. Salutation to the lord of Radha; (Я«ТЯЯЯ ЧЯ:) this mantra should protect both my eyes. Lord Krishna should protect both my ears. О Hari, protect my nose. Let Svaha the wife of Agni, should protect my speech and the mantra Svaha for Krishna (зйртЧТЯ ччтзт) should protect everyone.

## \|\| 19.28-29 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇāya svāheti ca kaṇṭhaṃ pātu ṣaḍakṣaraḥ \|*  
*hrīṃ kṛṣṇāya namo vaktraṃ klīṃpūrvaśca bhujadvayam \|\| 28 \|\|*

*namo gopāṅganeśāya skandhāvaṣṭākṣaro'vatu \|*  
*dantapaṅktimoṣṭhayugmaṃ namo gopīśvarāya ca \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. The mantra (f44 гяш) Svaha for Krishna, should protect my neck and (ff ЧЯ:) Hrim salutation to Krishna, should protect the face. The mantra (45ft' ^тштрт ЧЧ:) Klim salutation to Krishna should protect my arm and the mantra 414FR?T14 ЧЧ: salutation to the lord of gopis should protect both my shoulders. The mantra salutation to the lord of the gopis hthtwr ЧЧ: should protect the teeth beside both the lips.

## \|\| 19.30 \|\|

*oṃ namo bhagavate rāsamaṇḍaleśāya svāhā \|*  
*svayaṃ vakṣaḥsthalaṃ pātu mantro'yaṃ ṣoḍaśākṣaraḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The mantra of sixteen letters for salutation to the lord: "зтг ччг ЧмЧсГ ЧРЯЧ^'йда нщт” should protect the chest.

## \|\| 19.31 \|\|

*aiṃ kṛṣṇāya svāheti ca karṇayugmaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ viṣṇave svāheti ca kapolaṃ savarto'vatu \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The mantra for salutation to Krishna ft '^ЛЧ ччш should protect both the ears. The mantra зтг ччщ for the lord Vishnu, should protect the cheeks.

## \|\| 19.32 \|\|

*oṃ haraye nama iti pṛṣṭhaṃ pādaṃ sadā 'vatu \|*  
*oṃ govarddhanadhāriṇe svāhā sarvaśarīrakam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. От Salutation to Hari, з5\> Ftft ЧЧ: should protect the back and the feet while the mantra Svaha for the carrier of Govardhana mountain should protect the entire body ЧТЧ^ЧЧтЬчг 44TFT.

## \|\| 19.33 \|\|

*prācyāṃ māṃ pātu śrīkṛṣṇa āgneyyāṃ pātu mādhavaḥ \|*  
*dakṣiṇe pātu gopīśo nairṛtyāṃ nandanandanaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Lord Krishna is lodged in the eastern direction Madhava in the Agni direction, gopisa in the southern direction, Nanda-Nandana should protect the Nairrtya direction.

## \|\| 19.34 \|\|

*vāruṇyāṃ pātu govindo vāyavyāṃ rādhikeśvaraḥ \|*  
*uttare pātu rāseśa aiśānyāmacyutaḥ svayam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The western region should be protected by Govinda, the Vayavya direction should be protected by the lord of Radhika and the northern direction should be protected by the lord of Rasalila. The north-east direction should be protected by Acyuta.

## \|\| 19.35-36 \|\|

*satataṃ sarvataḥ pātu paro nārāyaṇaḥ svayam \|*  
*iti te kathitaṃ brahmankavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 35 \|\|*

*mama jīvanatulyaṃ ca yuṣmabhyaṃ dattameva ca \|*  
*aśvamedhasahasrāṇi vājapeyaśatāni ca \|*  
*kalāṃ nārhanti tānyeva kavacasyaiva dhāraṇāt \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. Lord Narayana should himself protect all the sides. О Brahmana, this is quite a surprising kavacha, the knowledge of which has been imparted by me to you. This is like my own life. The, one who practises this kavacha, earns the merit equivalent to thousands of Asvamedha sacrifices and hundreds the Vajpeya sacrifices.

## \|\| 19.37 \|\|

*gurumabhyarcya vidhivadvastrālaṅkāracandanaiḥ \|*  
*snātvā taṃ ca namaskṛtya kavacaṃ dhārayetsudhīḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The wise people after taking a bath give away in charity clothes, ornaments and adore the teachers regularly while practising on this kavacha.

## \|\| 19.38 \|\|

*kavacasya prasādena jīvanmukto bhavennaraḥ \|*  
*yadi syātsiddhakavaco viṣṇureva bhaveddvija \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The Brahmanas with the use of this kavacha achieve salvation. The success in this kavacha equates one with lord Vishnu.

## \|\| 19.39-40 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*śivasya kavacaṃ stotraṃ śrūyatāmiti śaunaka \|*  
*vaśiṣṭhena ca yaddattaṃ gandharvāya ca yo manuḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

*oṃ namo bhagavate śivāya svāheti ca manuḥ \|*  
*datto vaśiṣṭhena purā puṣkare kṛpayā vibho \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. Sauti said — О Shaunaka, now you listen to the kavacha of Shiva and the related stotra which was bestowed by Vasistha to Gandharva. In earlier times, О Brahmana, the knowledge of this kavacha was imparted by the priest Vasistha in the Puskara region which reads зтг ччг чччй f?T4T4 43T5T. This mantra was bestowed by the sage on Gandharva.

## \|\| 19.41 \|\|

*ayaṃ mantro rāvaṇāya pradatto brahmaṇā purā \|*  
*svayaṃ śambhuśca bāṇāya tathā durvāsase purā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The same mantra was bestowed by Brahma on Ravana and Shiva himself gave to Banasura and Durvasa.

## \|\| 19.42 \|\|

*mūlena sarvaṃ deyaṃ ca naivedyādikamuttamam \|*  
*dhyāyennityādikaṃ dhyānaṃ vedoktaṃ sarvasammatam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. With this basic mantra all the eatables and the best of other things should be offered. The Vedic type of meditation on this mantra is like the verse, “I adore lord Shiva always” b4l

## \|\| 19.43 \|\|

*oṃ namo mahādevāya \|*  
*bāṇāsura uvāca \|*  
*maheśvara mahābhāga kavacaṃ yatprakāśitam \|*  
*saṃsāra pāvanaṃ nāma kṛpayā kathaya prabho \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Salutation to lord Mahadeva.

## \|\| 19.44 \|\|

*maheśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vakṣyāmi he vatsa kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|*  
*ahaṃ tumyaṃ pradāsyāmi gopanīyaṃ sudurlabham \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Banasura said — О lord Maheshvara, O glorious one, О lord, you bestow the knowledge of the kavacha which purifies the universe.

## \|\| 19.45-46 \|\|

*purā durvāsase dattaṃ trailokyavijayāya ca \|\| 45 \|\|*

*mamaivedaṃ ca kavacaṃ bhaktyā yo dhārayetsudhīḥ \|*  
*jetuṃ śaknoti trailokyaṃ bhagavannavalīlayā \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. Maheshvara said — О son, “I am now speaking about that wonderful kavacha. It is difficult to get. In spite of that I bestow the knowledge of the same on you.

## \|\| 19.47 \|\|

*saṃsārapāvanasyāsya kavacasya prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*ṛṣiśchandaśca gāyatrī devo'haṃ ca maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu viniyogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. In the earlier times I had bestowed the knowledge of this kavacha on Durvasa for the conquering of the worlds. Therefore, whosoever owns this kavacha with great devotion he would be able to conquers the three worlds like the god.

## \|\| 19.48 \|\|

*paṃcalakṣajapenaiva siddhidaṃ kavacaṃ bhavet \|*  
*yo bhavetsiddhakavaco mama tulyo bhavedbhuvi \|*  
*tejasā siddhi yogena tapasā vikrameṇa ca \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. This kavacha is the purifier of the universe. Its Rsi is Prajapati, its metre is Gayatri, the god is Maheshvara; Dharma, Artha, Kama and Moksa are the applications or viniyoga for the same.

## \|\| 19.49 \|\|

*śambhurme mastakaṃ pātu mukhaṃ pātu maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*dantapaktiṃ nīlakaṇṭho'pyadharoṣṭhaṃ haraḥ svayam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. One who recites this mantra five lakhs of times achieves success for the same. The one who succeeds in this kavacha equates me with the universe in glory, accomplishment, tapas and prowess.

## \|\| 19.50 \|\|

*kaṇṭhaṃ pātu candracūḍaḥ skandhau vṛṣabhavāhanaḥ \|*  
*vakṣaḥsthalaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaḥ pātu pṛṣṭhaṃ digambaraḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Lord Sambhu should protect my forehead, Maheshvara should protect my mouth, NUakantha should protect the teeth and lord Shiva himself should protect my lips.

## \|\| 19.51 \|\|

*sarvāṅgaṃ pātu viśveśaḥ sarvadikṣu ca sarvadā \|*  
*svapne jāgaraṇe caiva sthāṇurme pātu santatam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Lord Chandracuda should protect my neck. The one whose vehicle happens to be the bull should protect my shoulders Nllkantha should protect the chest; the Digambara should protect the back.

## \|\| 19.52 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ bāṇa kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|*  
*yasmai kasmai na dātavyaṃ gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The Visvesha should protect all the parts of body and all directions, while sleeping or awake.

## \|\| 19.53 \|\|

*yatphalaṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ snānena labhate naraḥ \|*  
*tatphalaṃ labhate nūnaṃ kavacasyaiva dhāraṇāt \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. О Bana, I have imparted the knowledge of a wonderful kavacha to you. Don’t impart its knowledge to everyone because it is quite secret.

## \|\| 19.54 \|\|

*idaṃ kavacamajñātvā bhajenmāṃ yaḥ sumandadhīḥ \|*  
*śatalakṣaprajapto'pi na mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. The merit one earns by having a bath at al1 he sacred places, achieve the same by the reciting of this kavacha.

## \|\| 19.55 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*idaṃ ca kavacaṃ proktaṃ stotraṃ ca śṛṇu śaunaka \|*  
*mantrarājaḥ kalpatarurvasiṣṭho dattavānpurā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. The fool who tries to recite this kavacha without understanding it and adores me, cannot achieve success even if he recites the same a hundred lakhs of times.

## \|\| 19.56-57 \|\|

*oṃ namaḥ śivāya \|*  
*bāṇāsura uvāca \|*  
*vande surāṇāṃ sāraṃ ca sureśaṃ nīlalohitam \|*  
*yogīśvaraṃ yogabījaṃ yogināṃ ca gurorgurum \|\| 56 \|\|*

*jñānānandaṃ jñānarūpaṃ jñānabījaṃ sanātanam \|*  
*tapasāṃ phaladātāraṃ dātāraṃ sarvasampadām \|\| 57 \|\|*

56-57. Sauti said — О Shaunaka, I have narrated about this kavacha to you; now you listen to the mantra also which is like the kalpavrksa. The same was recited by the sage Vasistha in earlier times.

## \|\| 19.58-60 \|\|

*taporūpaṃ tapobījaṃ tapodhanadhanaṃ varam \|*  
*varaṃ vareṇyaṃ varadamīḍyaṃ siddhagaṇairvaraiḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

*kāraṇaṃ bhuktimuktīnāṃ narakārṇavatāraṇam \|*  
*āśutoṣaṃ prasannāsyaṃ karuṇāmayasāgaram \|\| 59 \|\|*

*himacandanakundendukumudāmbhojasannibham \|*  
*brahmajyotiḥsvarūpaṃ ca bhaktānugrahavigraham \|\| 60 \|\|*

58-60. Banasura said — I adore the best of the gods and the lord of the gods, lord Shiva who is the great yogi, the cause of the yogis and their teachers. He is the teacher of the teachers. The all-knowledgeable, the form of knowledge, the seed of knowledge, eternal, the result of the lapas he bestows all the riches.

## \|\| 19.61-62 \|\|

*viṣayāṇāṃ vibhedena bibhrataṃ bahurūpakam \|*  
*jalarūpamagnirūpamākāśarūpamīśvaram \|\| 61 \|\|*

*vāyurūpaṃ candrarūpaṃ sūryyarūpaṃ mahatprabhum \|*  
*ātmanaḥ svapadaṃ dātuṃ samarthamavalīlayā \|\| 62 \|\|*

61-62. He is the form of tapas, the seed of tapas and the treasure of the great ascetics. He is the boon, the bestower of the boons and is adorable by siddhas, the granter of salvation and adoration. He is the one who makes the people cross the ocean of the hell. He is Asutosa, who gets pleased quickly, always has a smiling face and is the ocean of compassion.

## \|\| 19.63 \|\|

*bhaktajīvanamīśaṃ ca bhaktānugrahakātaram \|*  
*vedā na śaktā yaṃ stotuṃ kimahaṃ staumi taṃ prabhum \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. He is always shining bright like the ice, sandal-paste, the moon and the lotuses. He is like the divine light and showers his grace over the devotees. He is always in compassionate form according to the exigencies of the situations and represents the water, the fire, the sky, the wind, the moon and the Sun. He is the lord and the great god and he bestows his grace suitably on all.

## \|\| 19.64-65 \|\|

*aparicchinnamīśānamaho vāṅmanasoḥ param \|*  
*vyāghracarmāmbaradharaṃ vṛṣabhasthaṃ digambaram \|*  
*triśūlapaṭṭiśadharaṃ sasmitaṃ candraśekharam \|\| 64 \|\|*

*ityuktvā stavarājena nityaṃ bāṇaḥ susaṃyataḥ \|*  
*prāṇamacchaṅkaraṃ bhaktyā durvāsāśca munīśvaraḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. He is the life of the devotees, is the great lord of the devotees and he always remains anxious to bestow his grace on the devotees. Therefore the lord whose glory even cannot be recited by the Vedas, is limitless Ishana and is beyond mind and speech; how can I praise him?

## \|\| 19.66 \|\|

*idaṃ dattaṃ vasiṣṭhena gandharvāya purā mune \|*  
*kathitaṃ ca mahāstotraṃ śūlinaḥ paramādbhutam \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. He is clad in the skin of a tiger, mounts on the bull, remains without clothes and is the holder of a trident and a Pattisha. He wears a serene smile on his face and his forehead is adorned with a crescent. I bow in reverence to this form of Shiva. The great sage Durvasa also conducted himself with devotion accordingly.

## \|\| 19.67 \|\|

*idaṃ stotraṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ paṭhedbhaktyā ca yo naraḥ \|*  
*snānasya sarvatīrthānāṃ phalamāpnoti niścitam \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. О sage in earlier times Vasistha had imparted the stotra of Shiva to the Gandharva.

## \|\| 19.68 \|\|

*aputro labhate putraṃ varṣamekaṃ śṛṇoti yaḥ \|*  
*saṃyataśca haviṣyāśī praṇamya śaṅkaraṃ gurum \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Such of the human being who recited this sacred stotra achieves the merit of having a bath in all the sacred places.

## \|\| 19.69 \|\|

*galatkuṣṭhī mahāśūlī varṣamekaṃ śṛṇoti yaḥ \|*  
*avaśyaṃ mucyate rogādvyāsavākyamiti śrutam \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. He who observes self-discipline consumes pure food, recites this stotra, bows at the feet of lord Shiva as well as his teacher, achieves a son if he is without one. He gets himself relieved of the horrible leprosy or stomach-ache. If one listens to the stotra for a full year he surely gets himself relieved of all ailments. I have heard this from the sage Vyasa.

## \|\| 19.70 \|\|

*kārāgāre'pi baddho yo naiva prāpnoti nirvṛtim \|*  
*stotraṃ śrutvā māsamekaṃ mucyate bandhanād dhruvam \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. The one who is bound in bondages and is unable to free himself, if he recites this stotra for a month or listens to it, surely is relieved of all the bondages.

## \|\| 19.71 \|\|

*bhraṣṭarājyo labhedrājyaṃ bhaktyā māsaṃ śṛṇoti yaḥ \|*  
*māsaṃ śrutvā saṃyataśca labhedbhraṣṭadhano dhanam \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Similarly anyone who listens to the stotra with devotion for a month he regains his last royal position and gets back the lost riches.

## \|\| 19.72 \|\|

*yakṣmagrasto varṣamekamāstiko yaḥ śṛṇoti cet \|*  
*niścitaṃ mucyate rogācchaṅkarasya prasādataḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. A believer who is infested with the ailment of consumption, if he recites the stotra or listens to it for a year, is relieved of the ailment with the grace of Shiva.

## \|\| 19.73 \|\|

*yaḥ śṛṇoti sadā bhaktyā stavarājamimaṃ dvija \|*  
*tasyāsādhyaṃ tribhuvane nāsti kiṃcicca śaunaka \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. О Shaunaka, О Brahmana, the one who listens to it with devotion, for him nothing remains impossible in the three worlds.

## \|\| 19.74 \|\|

*kadācidbandhuvicchedo na bhavettasya bhārate \|*  
*acalaṃ paramaiśvaryyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. In Bharata he is never separated from his relatives and he earns immense riches. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 19.75 \|\|

*susaṃyato'tibhaktyā ca māsamekaṃ śṛṇoti yaḥ \|*  
*abhāryyo labhate bhāryyāṃ suvinītāṃ satīvarām \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. One who listens to it with devotion and self-discipline for a month gets a chaste wife, if he does not have one.

## \|\| 19.76 \|\|

*mahāmūrkhaśca durmedhā māsamekaṃ śṛṇoti yaḥ \|*  
*buddhiṃ vidyāṃ ca labhate gurūpadeśamātrataḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. The one who is a great fool and is an evil minded one, if he listen to this stotra for a month, achieves intelligence and knowledge by the grace of his Guru.

## \|\| 19.77 \|\|

*karmaduḥkhī daridraśca māsaṃ bhaktyā śṛṇoti yaḥ \|*  
*dhruvaṃ vittaṃ bhavettasya śaṅkarasya prasādataḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. The one who leads a miserable life and has no riches at all, if he recites this stotra for a month lord Shiva gets pleased with him and he achieves all the riches.

## \|\| 19.78-79 \|\|

*iha loke sukhaṃ bhuktvā kṛtvā kīrtiṃ sudurlabhām \|*  
*nānāprakāradharmaṃ ca yātyante śaṅkarālayam \|\| 78 \|\|*

*pārṣadapravaro bhūtvā sevate tatra śaṅkaram \|*  
*yaḥ śṛṇoti trisandhyaṃ ca nityaṃ stotramanuttamam \|\| 79 \|\|*

78-79. The one who recites the stotra thrice in a day, achieves pleasures in the world and glory which is difficult to get. By performing many religious rites, he ultimately achieves the place of Shiva and by attaining a high position, he serves lord Shiva.

## Chapter 20

## \|\| 20.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*mudā mālāvatīsārddhaṃ gandharvaścopabarhaṇaḥ \|*  
*reme kālāvaśeṣaṃ ca tābhiśca nirjane vane \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said — The Gandharva named Upabarhana delightfully spent time in the company of Malavati and other wives in the uninhabited forest for the rest of his life.

## \|\| 20.2 \|\|

*gandharvarājo mumude putradārādibhiḥ saha \|*  
*nānāvidhaṃ kratuvaraṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. His father who was the lord of the Gandharvas also resided delightftilly with his wives. He performed many virtuous and the best of yajnas.

## \|\| 20.3 \|\|

*rājatvaṃ bubhuje rājā kuberabhavanopame \|*  
*reme suśīlayā sārddhaṃ sthirayauvanayuktayā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. His palace was like the abode of Kubera and he spent time there joyfully in the company of his chaste wife enjoying all the royal pleasures.

## \|\| 20.4 \|\|

*gandharvarājaḥ kāle ca gaṅgātīre manohare \|*  
*patnyā sārddhamasūṃstyaktvā vaikuṇṭhaṃ ca yayau mudā \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Ultimately he left for his heavenly abode in the company of the river Gangs and ultimately reached Vaikuntha.

## \|\| 20.5 \|\|

*śaivaḥ śivaprasādena putrasya viṣṇusevayā \|*  
*babhūva dāso vaikuṇṭhe viṣṇoḥ śyāmacaturbhujaḥ \|\| 5\|\|*

5\. He was a devotee of Shiva who graced him. His son, however, adored Vishnu, who also graced him. Because of all this he became four-armed attendant of lord Vishnu in Vaikuntha.

## \|\| 20.6 \|\|

*kṛtvā pitrośca satkāraṃ gandharvaścopabarhaṇaḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇebhyo dadau vipra dhanāni vividhāni ca \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. О Brahmana, thereafter the Gandharva named Upabarhana performed the last rites of his parents distributing many riches to the Brahmana.

## \|\| 20.7 \|\|

*kāle svayaṃ brahmaśāpātprāṇāṃstyaktvā vicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*sa jajñe vṛṣalīgarbhe brahmabījena śaunaka \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. О Shaunaka, at the appropriate time the intelligent Gandharva left for his heavenly abode willingly and was reborn from the womb of a Shudra lady who had a Brahmana as her husband.

## \|\| 20.8 \|\|

*mālāvatī vahnikuṇḍe puṣkare bhārate bhuvi \|*  
*kṛtvā tu vāñchitaṃ kāmaṃ prāṇāṃstatyāja sā satī \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Thereafter the chaste Malavati reaching the Puskara region performed the yajna in the fire-altar appropriately and ended her life there.

## \|\| 20.9-10 \|\|

*sṛṃjayasya tu patnyāṃ ca manuvaṃśodbhavasya ca \|*  
*jajñe nṛpasya sādhvī sā puṇyā jātismarā varā \|\| 9 \|\|*

*upabarhaṇagandharvaḥ patirme bhaviteti ca \|*  
*itikāmā kāmukī sā sundarī sundarīvarā \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. Thereafter that chaste lady was reborn as the wife of the great king Snijaya. But the memory of her earlier birth was available with her. Therefore, she always desired to have Gandharva Upabarhana as her husband.

## \|\| 20.11 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*brahmavīryyācchūdrapatnyāṃ gandharvaścopabarhaṇaḥ \|*  
*jātaḥ kena prakāreṇa tadbhavānvaktumarhati \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Shaunaka said — Gandharva Upabarhana was born with the Brahmana semen from his Shudra wife. You kindly tell me the entire story about them.

## \|\| 20.12 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*kānyakubje ca deśe ca drumilo nāma rājakaḥ \|*  
*kalāvatī tasya patnī vandhyā cāpi pativratā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Sauti said — There was a king named Drumila in the region of Kanyakubja. He had a chaste wife named Kalavati who was childless.

## \|\| 20.13 \|\|

*svāmidoṣeṇa sā vandhyā kāle ca bharturājñayā \|*  
*upatasthe vane ghore nāradaṃ kāśyapaṃ munim \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. She could not conceive because of some defect in her husband and as such during the menstrual period she with the permission of her husband went to Narada the son of sage Kashyapa in the wild forest.

## \|\| 20.14 \|\|

*dhyāyamānaṃ ca śrīkṛṣṇaṃ jvalantaṃ brahmatejasā \|*  
*tasthau suveṣaṃ kṛtvā sā dhyānāntaṃ ca muneḥ puraḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The great sage possessing divine glory was engrossed in meditation for lord Krishna. She stood before him adorning herself with all the costumes and ornaments.

## \|\| 20.15 \|\|

*grīṣmamadhyāhnamārtaṇḍaprabhātulyena tejasā \|*  
*tapantaṃ dūrato'pyevaṃ samīpaṃ gantumakṣamā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. She could not remain aloof finding the sage there, who was illumining hke the lustre of the sun. She kept on standing at a distance from him.

## \|\| 20.16-19 \|\|

*dhyānānte ca muniśreṣṭhaḥ paraḥ kṛṣṇaparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*dadarśa purato dūre sundarīṃ sthirayauvanām \|\| 16 \|\|*

*cārucampakavarṇābhāṃ śaratpaṅkajalocanām \|*  
*śaratpārvaṇacaṃdrāsyāṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitām \|\| 17 \|\|*

*bṛhannitambabhārārttāṃ pīnaśroṇipayodharām \|*  
*śobhitāṃ pītavastreṇa sasmitāṃ raktalocanām \|\| 18 \|\|*

*mohitāṃ munirūpeṇa kāmabāṇaprapīḍitām \|*  
*darśayantīṃ stanaśroṇiṃ maithunāsaktacetasā \|\| 19 \|\|*

16-19. Thereafter the sage who was deeply devoted to lord Krishna looked at the lady from a distance. She had a fair complexion like that of the campaka flower. Her eyes resembled the lotus flower, his face was like the full moon of the winter season, she was adorned with all the ornaments; she was feeling disturbed with the weight of her breasts; she had developed thighs and buttocks. Her eyes were red and she was adorned in yellow garments, wearing a smile on her face. She developed a great passion for the sage as a result of which she started displaying her body.

## \|\| 20.20-22 \|\|

*sindūrabindubhūṣāḍhyāṃ sucārukajjalojjvalām \|*  
*padālaktakaśobhāḍhyāṃ rūpeṇaiva yathorvaśīm \|\| 20 \|\|*

*muniḥ papraccha dṛṣṭvā tāṃ kā tvaṃ kāmini nirjane \|*  
*kasya patnī kathaṃ vā'tra satyaṃ brūhi ca puṃścali \|\| 21 \|\|*

*muneśca vacanaṃ śrutvā kampitā ca kalāvatī \|*  
*uvāca vinayenaiva kṛtvā ca śrīhariṃ hṛdi \|\| 22 \|\|*

20-22. The saffron, the ornaments, beautiful colliriyum were adorning her body. She had a beautiful complexion. Her feed were painted red and she looked beautiful like UrvasT. Finding her alone in the lonely forest the sage asked her. “O beautiful one, who are you? Who is your husband? What for have you come here"? О damsel, tell me truthfully. On hearing the words of the sage, Kalavati felt panicky. She then spoke with humble words with her mind devoted to lord Hari.

## \|\| 20.23 \|\|

*kalāvatyuvāca \|*  
*gopikā'haṃ dvijaśreṣṭha drumilasya ca kāminī \|*  
*putrārthinī cāgatā'haṃ tvanmūlaṃ bharturājñayā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Kalavati said: “O best of the Brahmana, I am a cowherdesses by casts and happen to be the wife of the king Drumila. I have arrived here with the desire of getting a son from you.

## \|\| 20.24 \|\|

*vīryyādhānaṃ kuru mayi strī nopekṣyā hyupasthitā \|*  
*tejīyasāṃ na doṣāya vahneḥ sarvabhujo yathā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Therefore, you kindly grant me your semen. One should not disappoint a damsel coming for a company and the glorious people do not commit a sin by that, like the god of fire who consumes everything.

## \|\| 20.25 \|\|

*vṛṣalīvacanaṃ śrutvā cukopa munisattamaḥ \|*  
*uvāca nītyaṃ satyaṃ ca kopaprasphuritādharaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. On hearing these words of the damsel the sage was immensely enraged as a result of which his lips started of fluttering. He then spoke to her.

## \|\| 20.26 \|\|

*kāśyapa uvāca \|*  
*yaḥ svalakṣmīṃ ca bhogārhāṃ parāya dātumicchati \|*  
*taṃ sā tyajati mūḍhaṃ ca vedavāda iti dhruvam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Kashyapa said: “O lady, if a woman intends to bestow her favour on an outsider, it amounts to disowning her husband. This is ordained in the Vedas.

## \|\| 20.27 \|\|

*na tvaṃ drumilabhogārhā punareva bhaviṣyasi \|*  
*viraktena svayaṃ tyaktā na gṛhṇāti ca tvāṃ punaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Therefore, you will not hereafter be able to be of any use to your husband, the king Drumila. If by getting disinterested in you, he has himself disowned you, then how can he own you back?

## \|\| 20.28 \|\|

*yaḥ śūdrapatnīṃ gṛhṇāti brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ \|*  
*sa caṇḍālo bhavetsatyaṃ na karmārho dvijātiṣu \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. A Brahmana getting devoid of wisdom, when he enjoys the wife of a Shudra, he achieves Chandala-hood and is deprived of the privilege of performing any good deeds.

## \|\| 20.29 \|\|

*pitṛśrāddhe ca yajñe ca śilāsparśe surārcane \|*  
*adhikāraśca tasyaivamityāha kamalodbhavaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Не is unable to performed sraddhas, yajnas, adoration of the images and the gods. This is the command of Brahma, that such a person has no right in performing any religious deeds.

## \|\| 20.30 \|\|

*kumbhīpākaṃ svayaṃ yāti pātayitvā ca pūruṣān \|*  
*mātāmahānsvātmanaśca daśa pūrvāndaśāparān \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Ultimately, he finds himself thrown in the Kumbhlpaka hell and he destroys the ancestors of his own race besides the race of his maternal parents. He also destroys the coming ten generations as well.

## \|\| 20.31-32 \|\|

*tattarpaṇaṃ mūtrameva piṇḍaṃ sadyaḥ purīṣakam \|*  
*śālagrāmasya tatsparśe copavāsastrirātrakam \|\| 31 \|\|*

*tadiṣṭadevo gṛhṇāti na naivedyaṃ na tajjalam \|*  
*saṃnyāsināṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ tadannaṃ ca purīṣavat \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. The tarpana perfonned by him is taken as urine and the Pindadana offered by him is turned into refuse. With the mere touch of Shalagrama he has to observe for three nights and his family gods refuse to accept the food and water offered by him. For the recluses and the Brahmanas, the food offered by him is considered like a refuse.

## \|\| 20.33 \|\|

*kumbhīpāke pacyate sa śakrāntaṃ yāvadeva hi \|*  
*ekaviṃśatipuruṣaiḥ sārddhaṃ satyaṃ ca puṃścali \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. О damsel, he is thrown in the terrific hell for twenty one generations. This is the truth.

## \|\| 20.34 \|\|

*patrocchiṣṭaṃ ca yo bhuṅkte śūdrāṇāṃ brāhmaṇādhamaḥ \|*  
*tattulyo'dharabhojī caivetyāṅgiresabhāṣitam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Such of the Brahmana as consume the leftover of the Shudras become degraded like them. These are the words of the sage Angirasa.

## \|\| 20.35 \|\|

*śūdro vā yadi gṛhṇāti brāhmaṇīṃ jñānadurbalaḥ \|*  
*sa pacyate kālasūtre yāvadindrāścaturdaśa \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. In case a Shudra accepts a Brahmana girl as wife, he is lodged in the kalasQtra-hell for fourteen generations.

## \|\| 20.36 \|\|

*aṣṭādaśendrāvacchinnaṃ kālaṃ ca kālasūtrake \|*  
*brāhmaṇī pacyate tatra bhakṣitā krimibhirdhruvam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. And if a Brahmana girl is thrown in the same hell for the duration of the period of eighteen Indras she is bitten by insects in the hell.

## \|\| 20.37 \|\|

*tataścaṇḍālayonau ca labdhvā janma ca brāhmaṇī \|*  
*śūdraśca kuṣṭhī bhavati jñātibhiḥ parivarjitaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Thereafter the Brahmana girl is reborn in the family of Chandalas and the Shudra attracts leprosy and is discarded by his relatives.

## \|\| 20.38-40 \|\|

*ityuktvā ca muniśreṣṭho virarāma ca śaunaka \|*  
*vṛṣalī tatpurastasthau śuṣkakaṇṭhauṣṭhatālukā \|\| 38 \|\|*

*etasminnantare tena pathā yāti ca menakā \|*  
*tasyā ūruṃ stanaṃ dṛṣṭvā munervīryyaṃ papāta ha \|\| 39 \|\|*

*ṛtusnātā ca vṛṣalī pītvā tatra kṣaṇaṃ mudā \|*  
*muniṃ praṇamya prahṛṣṭā prayayau bharturantikam \|\| 40 \|\|*

38-40. О Shaunaka, thus speaking the best of the sages kept quiet. But the Shudra girl kept on standing before him. Her lips, throat and palate were dried up. By about the same time, Menaka passed by that way. Looking at the breasts and thighs of whom the semen of sage fell but the Shudra damsel consumed it delightfully and thereafter bowing in reverence to the sage delightfully went to her husband.

## \|\| 20.41 \|\|

*gatvā praṇamya drumilaṃ kāntā kāntaṃ manoharam \|*  
*sarvaṃ nivedayāmāsa vṛttāṃttaṃ garbhahetukam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Reaching there, she bowed in reverence of glorious Drumila and narrated to him the story of her conception.

## \|\| 20.42 \|\|

*kalāvatīvacaḥ śrutvā prahṛṣṭavadanekṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*uvāca kāntāṃ madhuraṃ pariṇāmasukhāvaham \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. On hearing the words of Kalavati, Drumila was delighted and his face and eyes expressed pleasure. Thereafter he spoke pleasing and sweet words to his wife, thus.

## \|\| 20.43 \|\|

*drumila uvāca \|*  
*viprasya vīryaṃ tvadgarbhe vaiṣṇavasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*vaiṣṇavo bhavitā bālastvaṃ ca bhāgyavatī satī \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Drumila said — The semen of a great Brahmana ascetic is enshrined in your womb and as such a Vaishnava child would be born to you. You are quite fortunate and a chaste lady as well.

## \|\| 20.44 \|\|

*yadgarbhe vaiṣṇavo jāto yasya vīryeṇa vā sati \|*  
*tayoryāti ca vaikuṇṭhaṃ puruṣāṇāṃ śataṃ śatam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Anyone by his semen, from whose womb a Viasnava child is born, tlieir hundred generations achieve Vaikuntha.

## \|\| 20.45 \|\|

*tau ca viṣṇuvimānena sadratnanirmitena ca \|*  
*yātau vaikuṇṭhanagaraṃ janmamṛtyujarāharam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Both of them then mounted on the divine plane of Vishnu, studded with gems and arrived in the Vaikuntha where birth, death and old age disappear.

## \|\| 20.46 \|\|

*kasyacidbrāhmaṇasyaiva gehaṃ gaccha śubhānane \|*  
*paścānmamāntikaṃ bhadre yāsyasīti hareḥ puram \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О beautiful one, "you now go to the use of a Brahmana and thereafter you reach me in the abode of the gods.”

## \|\| 20.47-49 \|\|

*ityuktvā goparājaśca snātvā kṛtvā tu tarpaṇam \|*  
*saṃpūjyābhīṣṭadevaṃ ca brāhmaṇebhyo dhanaṃ dadau \|\| 47 \|\|*

*aśvānāṃ ca caturlakṣaṃ gajānāṃ lakṣameva ca \|*  
*śataṃ mattagajendrāṇāṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 48 \|\|*

*uccaiḥśravaḥ pañcalakṣaṃ rathānāṃ ca sahasrakam \|*  
*śakaṭānāṃ trilakṣaṃ ca brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. Thus speaking the king of Gopas, took a bath, performed tarpana and adored his family gods and he distributed riches among the Brahmana. He gave away in charity four lakhs of horses, a lakh of ordinary elephants, a hundred intoxicated elephants to the Brahmanas. Besides this, he gave away five lakhs of horses born in the race of Uccaishrava, a thousand chariots, three lakhs of bullock-carts.

## \|\| 20.50 \|\|

*gavāṃ dvādaśalakṣaṃ ca mahiṣāṇāṃ trilakṣakam \|*  
*trilakṣaṃ rājahaṃsānāṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. He further gave away twelve lakhs of buffaloes, three lakh of geese to the Brahmana.

## \|\| 20.51-52 \|\|

*pārāvatānāṃ lakṣaṃ ca śukānāṃ ca śataṃ mune \|*  
*lakṣaṃ ca dāsadāsīnāṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 51 \|\|*

*grāmāṇāṃ ca sahasraṃ ca nagarāṇāṃ śataṃ śatam \|*  
*dhānyataṇḍulaśailaṃ ca brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. О sage, he also gave away a lakh of slaves. He further gave away a thousand villages, two hundred cities and mountain — like heaps of rice and other cereal to the Brahmana.

## \|\| 20.53 \|\|

*śatakoṭiṃ suvarṇānāṃ ratnānāṃ ca sahasrakam \|*  
*mudrāṇāṃ koṭikalaśaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. He further gave away to them a hundred crores of gold coins, a thousand gems and pitchers filled with coins numbering in crores.

## \|\| 20.54-55 \|\|

*dadau taijasapātrāṇāṃ bhūṣaṇānāmasaṃkhyakam \|*  
*tāṃ striyaṃ ratnabhūṣāḍhyāṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 54 \|\|*

*rājyaṃ dattvā mahārājo'pyantarbāhye hariṃ smaran \|*  
*jagāma badarīṃ gopo manogāmī mudā'nvitaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. He further gave away innumerable shining vases and ornaments besides gems studded ornaments, adorning ladies. Thereafter he gave his own kingdom in charity and while reciting the name of Hari, the king Gopa reached the place of Badrikasrama with the speed of the mind.

## \|\| 20.56 \|\|

*tatra māsaṃ tapaḥ kṛtvā gaṅgātīre manohare \|*  
*prāṇāṃstatyāja yogena sadyo dṛṣṭo maharṣibhiḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. There, he performed tapas seated on the bank of the river Ganga for a month and then left for his heavenly abode by yogic practices. This was spotted by the great sages.

## \|\| 20.57-60 \|\|

*sa ca viṣṇuvimānena ratnendranirmitena ca \|*  
*saṃyukto viṣṇudūtaiśca vaikuṇṭhaṃ ca jagāma ha \|\| 57 \|\|*

*tatra prāpa harerdāsyaṃ haridāso babhūva saḥ \|*  
*vṛttāntaṃ ca kalāvatyā śrūyatāmiti śaunaka \|\| 58 \|\|*

*gate kalāvatī nāthe uccaiśca praruroda ha \|*  
*vahnau prāṇāṃstyaktukāmā brāhmaṇenaiva rakṣitā \|\| 59 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇo mātarityuktvā tāṃ gṛhītvā mudā'nvitaḥ \|*  
*jagāma ratnapūrṇaṃ ca svagehaṃ ca kṣaṇena ca \|\| 60 \|\|*

57-60. Thereafter mounting the best of the planes studded with gems which belonged to lord Vis nu, he, accompanied with the messengers of the lord, arrived in Vaikuntha. There devoting his mind to the lord, he became the servant of the lord there. О Shaunaka now you listen to the story of Kalavati after the departure of her husband. Kalavati started crying aloud and got ready to end her life by entering into fire but the Brahmana saved her.

## \|\| 20.61 \|\|

*sā vipragehe sādhvī ca suṣāva tanayaṃ varam \|*  
*taptakāñcanavarṇābhaṃ jvalantaṃ brahmatejasā \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. There, a Brahmana calling her to be his mother took her with him and in a movement he reached his abode filled with gems. In the house of that Brahmana, she gave birth to a son who had a lustre comparable to that of molten gold and the glory of Brahma.

## \|\| 20.62 \|\|

*tatrasthā yoṣitaḥ sarvā dadṛśurbālakaṃ śubham \|*  
*grīṣmamadhyāhnamārtaṇḍajitaṃ taṃ brahmatejasā \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. All the ladies of the place had a look at the child and the divine glory of the face of the child resembled that of the lustre of the sun at noon during the summer season.

## \|\| 20.63-64 \|\|

*kāmadevādhikaṃ rūpe candrādhikaśubhānanam \|*  
*śaratpārvaṇacandrāsyaṃ śaratpaṅkajalocanam \|\| 63 \|\|*

*hastapādādivalitaṃ sukapolaṃ manoharam \|*  
*padmacakrāṅkitaṃ pādapadmaṃ vā'tulamujjvalam \|\| 64 \|\|*

63-64. His beauty was far greater than that of the god of love. His face was more shining than the moon and his face emitted a lustre which could be compared with that of the full moon of the winter season. His hands, feet and cheeks, were quite beautiful and charming. His feet were embossed with the lotus and cakra symbols and were very bright.

## \|\| 20.65-67 \|\|

*karayugmaṃ vā'tulaṃ ca rudantaṃ ca stanārthinam \|*  
*yoṣito bālakaṃ dṛṣṭvā prayayuḥ svāśramaṃ mudā \|\| 65 \|\|*

*putradārayuto vipraḥ prahṛṣṭaśca nanarta ha \|*  
*sa bālo vavṛdhe tatra śuklapakṣe yathā śaśī \|\| 66 \|\|*

*pupoṣa brāhmaṇastāṃ ca saputrāṃ ca yathā sutām \|\| 67\|\|*

65-67. Both his hands were extremely beautiful and were desirous of sucking the milk. He started crying and delighted all the ladies present there, who then dispersed to their respective homes gleefully. The Brahmana also started dancing in pleasure with his wife and sons. The child started growing in that place like the moon of the bright fortnight and the Brahmana started serving the lady like his own daughter taking good care of both of them.

## Chapter 21

## \|\| 21.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*babhūva kāle bālaśca krameṇa pañcahāyanaḥ \|*  
*jātismaro jñānayuktaḥ pūrvamantrasmṛtaḥ sadā \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said — In due course of time the child became five years old and he always remembered the happenings of the past births and started the reciting of the mantras.

## \|\| 21.2 \|\|

*gīyate satataṃ kṛṣṇayaśonāmaguṇādikam \|*  
*kṣaṇaṃ roditi nṛtyena pulakāñcitavigrahaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. He continued reciting the glory of the lord Krishna. Sometimes, he cried and sometimes he became emotional.

## \|\| 21.3 \|\|

*kṛṣṇasambandhinīṃ gāthāṃ śṛṇoti yatra tatra vai \|*  
*tatsambandhi purāṇaṃ ca tatra tiṣṭhati bālakaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. He used to stop at any place wherever there was the recitation of the glory of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 21.4 \|\|

*dhūlidhūsarasarvāṅgo dhūlinaivedyamīpsitam \|*  
*dhūliṣu pratimāṃ kṛtvā dhūlinā pūjayeddharim \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. With his entire body covered with dust he used to make the images of the lord in mud and offered him the Naivedya also of the dust performing his pitja.

## \|\| 21.5 \|\|

*putramāhvayate mātā prātarāśāya cenmune \|*  
*hariṃ saṃpūjayāmīti mātaraṃ saṃvadetpunaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. О sage, when the mother called him in the morning for eating, he used to reply to her that he was busy with the adoration of the lord.

## \|\| 21.6 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ nāma bālakasyāsya janmanyatra babhūva ha \|*  
*vyutpattyā saṃjñayā vā'pi tadbhavānvaktumarhati \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Shaunaka said — What was the name given to the child in his new birth? You kindly tell us all that in detail.

## \|\| 21.7 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*anāvṛṣṭyavaśeṣe ca kāle bālo babhūva ha \|*  
*nāraṃ dadau janmakāle tenāyaṃ nāradābhidhaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Sauti said — It was a time of draught when this boy was born. With his birth the rain fell on earth and because of the rains he was given the name of Narada.

## \|\| 21.8-9 \|\|

*dadāti nāraṃ jñānaṃ ca bālakebhyaśca bālakaḥ \|*  
*jātismaro mahājñānī tenāyaṃ nāradābhidhaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*vīryeṇa nāradasyaiva babhūva bālako mune \|*  
*munīndrasya vareṇaiva tenāyaṃ nāradābhidhaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. That child was illustrious, intellectual and used to impart knowledge to the other boys of the place; therefore he was given the name of Narada. О sage, he was also given the name of Narada because he was born from the semen of the great sage.

## \|\| 21.10 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*śiśunāma ca vijñātaṃ vyutpattyā ca yathocitam \|*  
*munīndrasya kathaṃ nāma nāradaśceti maṃgalam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Shaunaka said — I have come to know of the appropriate name of the boy but how did his father also came to be known by the name of Narada?

## \|\| 21.11 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*aputrakāya viprāya dharmaputro naro muniḥ \|*  
*dadau putra kaśyapāya tenāyaṃ nāradābhidhaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Sauti said — Sage Nara who was the son of Dharma gave to Kashyapa a son who had no son and was childless. Therefore, because a son was given to him by Nara, he was also known by the name of Narada.

## \|\| 21.12 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*adhunā nāmavyutpattiḥ śrutā saute śiśorapi \|*  
*śūdrayonau brahmaputraḥ kathaṃ sa nāradābhidhaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Shaunaka said — О son of Suta, I have now come to know the genesis of the name of that child. Now you tell me how he was known as Narada when he was the combination of a Brahmana and a Stidra.

## \|\| 21.13 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*kalpāntare brahmakaṇṭhādbabhūvurbahavo narāḥ \|*  
*narāndadau tatkaṇṭhaṃ ca tena tannāradaṃ smṛtam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Sauti said — At the end of kalpa many Naras were born out of the neck of Brahma; his neck gave charity to Narayana and that is why he was called Narada.

## \|\| 21.14 \|\|

*tato babhūva bālaśca nāradātkaṇṭhadeśataḥ \|*  
*ato brahmā nāma cakre nāradaśceti maṅgalam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The same child was born out of the neck and as such Brahma gave him the name of Narada.

## \|\| 21.15 \|\|

*sāmprataṃ śiśuvṛttāntaṃ sāvadhānaṃ niśāmaya \|*  
*upālambharahasyena viśiṣṭaṃ kiṃ prayojanam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. I am now speaking about the story of that child, you listen to me attentively. By knowing the secret about the birth of Narada what purpose is going to be served, you listen to me”?

## \|\| 21.16 \|\|

*vavṛdhe gopikābālo vipragehe dinedine \|*  
*saputrāṃ pālitāṃ cakre brāhmaṇaḥ svasutāṃ yathā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The son of the Gopika went on growing in the house of a Brahmana, who took good care of him together with his daughter and the Gopika.

## \|\| 21.17 \|\|

*etasminnantare viprā āyayurvipramandiram \|*  
*śiśavaḥ pañcavarṣīyā mahātejasvino yathā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. In the meantime some glorious Brahmanas arrived in the house of that Brahmana who looked like children of five years age.

## \|\| 21.18 \|\|

*pracchannaṃ hṛtavantaśca grīṣmamadhyāhnabhāskaram \|*  
*madhuparkādikaṃ dattvā tānnanāma gṛhī dvija \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. They wore a lustre on their body which excelled the lustre of the sun of the summer season at noon. The house-holder Brahmana welcomed them offering curd with honey. Thereafter all the four Brahmana boys accepted the offering of fruits, roots and other eatables by the house holder. The left-overs were consumed by that child.

## \|\| 21.19-20 \|\|

*phalamūlādikaṃ kāle catvāro munipuṅgavāḥ \|*  
*vipradattaṃ bubhujire taccheśaṃ bubhuje śiśuḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*caturthako munistasmai kṛṣṇamantraṃ dadau mudā \|*  
*teṣāṃ babhūva dāsaḥ sa dvijasya māturājñayā \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. The fourth Brahmana was pleased with the boy and gave him the mantra of Krishna. With the permission of his mother, they took up the boy to serve them as an attendant.

## \|\| 21.21-22 \|\|

*ekadā śiśumātā ca gacchantī niśi vartmani \|*  
*mamāra sarpadaṣṭā ca tatkṣaṇaṃ smaratī harim \|\| 21 \|\|*

*sadyo jagāma vaikuṇṭhaṃ viṣṇuyānena sā satī \|*  
*viṣṇupārṣadasaṃyuktā sadratnanirmitena ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. Once the mother of that boy was leaguing the house at midnight and was bitten by a snake on the way, as a result of which she left for heavenly abode, reciting the name of the lord. The chaste cowherdesses mounted on the divine chariot of lord Vishnu, reached his place called Vaikuntha in an instant.

## \|\| 21.23 \|\|

*prātarbālo dvijaiḥ sārdhaṃ prayayau vipramandirāt \|*  
*tattvajñānaṃ dadustasmai brāhmaṇāśca kṛpālavaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. In the morning the child came out of the house of Brahmana and accompanied the Brahmana ascetics. The compassionate Brahmana imparted the knowledge of tattvas to the child.

## \|\| 21.24 \|\|

*brahmaputrāḥ śiśuṃ tyaktvā svasthānaṃ prayayuḥ kila \|*  
*mahājñānī śiśustasthau gaṅgātīre manohare \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Thereafter those Brahmana-ascetics left the child and went for a bath and the intelligent child started residing at the bank of the Ganga.

## \|\| 21.25 \|\|

*tatra snātvā vipradattaṃ viṣṇumantraṃ jajāpa saḥ \|*  
*kṣutpipāsārogaśokaharaṃ vedeṣu durlabham \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Taking a bath there, he started reciting the mantra given to him by the Brahmana ascetics, which relieves one of hunger thirst, ailments and grief and was difficult to get pronounced in the Vedas.

## \|\| 21.26 \|\|

*mahāraṇye ca ghore ca aśvatthamūlasannidhau \|*  
*kṛtvā yogāsanaṃ tasthau suciraṃ tatra bālakaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. In the thick and dangerous forest the boy sat in meditation under a Plpala tree for a long time.

## \|\| 21.27 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*kaṃ mantraṃ bālakaḥ prāpa kumāreṇa ca dhīmatā \|*  
*dattaṃ paraṃ śrīṃhareśca tadbhavānvaktu marhati \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Shaunaka said — What was the mantra given to the boy by the intelligent Sanat Kumaras, you kindly tell us?

## \|\| 21.28-29 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*kṛṣṇena datto goloke kṛpayā brahmaṇe purā \|*  
*dvāviṃśatyakṣaro mantro vedeṣu ca sudurlabhaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*taṃ ca brahmā dadau bhaktyā kumārāya ca dhīmate \|*  
*kumāreṇa sa dattaśca mantraśca śiśave dvija \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. Sauti said — In the ancient times, lord Krishna had given to Brahma in the Goloka a mantra of twenty two letters which is difficult to get even in the Vedas. The same mantra was given by Brahma to the intelligent Sanat Kumaras taking into consideration their devotion. О Brahmana Sanat-kumaras gave away the mantra to a Brahmana boy.

## \|\| 21.30-31 \|\|

*oṃ śrīṃ namo bhagavate rāsa maṇḍaleśvarāya \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇāya svāheti ca mantro'yaṃ kalpapādapaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*mahāpuruṣastotraṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ kavacaṃ ca yat \|*  
*asyaupayaugikaṃ dhyānaṃ sāmavedoktameva ca \|\| 31 \|\|*

30-31. The mantra goes like this: — atf ят 44tHWl ^1ЖЙто ylfwirq ^rwr. This is the wish-fulfilling-tree and should be accompanied by the kavacha and the stotra of Mahapurusa, which would be quite useful.

## \|\| 21.32-36 \|\|

*tejomaṇḍalarūpe ca sūryyakoṭisamaprabhe \|*  
*yogibhirvāñchitaṃ dhyāne yogaiḥ siddhagaṇaiḥ suraiḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*dhyāyante vaiṣṇavā rūpaṃ tadabhyantarasannidhau \|*  
*atīva kamanīyānirvacanīyaṃ manoharam \|\| 33 \|\|*

*navīnajaladaśyāmaṃ śaratpaṅkajalocanam \|*  
*śaratpārvaṇacandrāsyaṃ pakvabimbādhikādharam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*muktāpaṅktivinindaikadantapaṃktimanoharam \|*  
*sasmitaṃ muralīnyastahastāvalambanena ca \|\| 35\|\|*

*koṭikandarpalāvaṇyaṃ līlādhāma manoharam \|*  
*candralakṣaprabhājuṣṭaṃ puṣṭaśrīyuktavigraham \|\| 36 \|\|*

32-36. The yogis meditate upon him who is having the glory of crores of Suryas and is illustrious in outlook who is beyond description having the eternal shine. The siddhas and the gods adore him who could get their desire fulfilled. The Vais nava people adore him in their heart with great devotion. He is extremely beautiful, beyond description and quite charming. He has the complexion of the new clouds, his eyes are like the lotuses of winter season. His face bears the lustre of the full moon of the winter season. The lips resembles the ripe bimba fruit; he has beautifully shining teeth which look like lines of gems. He wears a serene smile on his face and holds a flute in his hands. His beauty excels crores of gods of love he has a fine glory and his lustre exceeds lakhs of moons. He has a well-developed body.

## \|\| 21.37-39 \|\|

*tribhaṅgasaṅgi vā yuktaṃ dvibhujaṃ pītavāsasam \|*  
*ratnakeyūravalayaratnanūpurabhūṣi tam \|\| 37 \|\|*

*ratnakuṇḍalayugmena gaṇḍasthalavirājitam \|*  
*mayūrapucchacūḍaṃ ca ratnamālāvibhūṣitam \|\| 38 \|\|*

*śobhitaṃ jānuparyyantaṃ mālatīvanamālayā \|*  
*candanokṣitasarvāṅgaṃ bhaktānugrahakārakam \|\| 39 \|\|*

37-39. He appears in tribhanga pose, he has two arms. His arms are adorned with armlets. He is clad in yellow garments and also wears wristlets and anklets studded with gems. He has gems-studded Kundalas adorning the ears, while a feather of the peacock serves as a mukufa. The gems-studded necklaces are worn around the neck. He wears a long garland of forest flowers; his entire body is plastered with sandal-wood paste. He is the one who is compassionate to his devotees.

## \|\| 21.40 \|\|

*maṇinā kaustubhendreṇa vakṣasthalasamujjvalam \|*  
*vīkṣitaṃ gopikābhiśca śaśvadvrīḍitalocanaiḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The beautiful kaustubha gem appear on his chest. The cowherdesses look at him in shyness continuously.

## \|\| 21.41 \|\|

*sthirayauvanayuktābhirveṣṭitābhiśca santatam \|*  
*bhūṣaṇairbhūṣitābhiśca rādhāvakṣaḥsthalasthitam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The youthful cowherdesses adorned with all the ornaments surround him always. He is enshrined in the heart of Radha.

## \|\| 21.42-43 \|\|

*brahmaviṣṇuśivādyaiśca pūjitaṃ vanditaṃ stutam \|*  
*kiśoraṃ rādhikākāntaṃ śāntarūpaṃ parātparam \|\| 42 \|\|*

*nirliptaṃ sākṣirūpaṃ ca nirguṇaṃ prakṛteḥ param \|*  
*dhyāyetsarveśvaraṃ taṃ ca paramātmānamīśvaram \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. The gods like Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva adore him, offer prayers to him, praising him at the same time. He is of tender age and is the lord of Radha, is quite peaceful and is beyond the reach of all. He remains uninvolved and has a definite form, he is beyond the invisible nature. One should always adore the great soul who is the lord of all.

## \|\| 21.44 \|\|

*idaṃ te kathitaṃ dhyānaṃ stotraṃ ca kavacaṃ mune \|*  
*mantropayaugikaṃ satyaṃ mantraśca kalpapādapaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. О sage, I have explained to you the Dhyanam, stotra, kavacha and the mantra which is like the wish-fulfilling-tree.

## \|\| 21.45-50 \|\|

*sāmprataṃ bālakastasthau dhyānasthastatra śaunaka \|*  
*divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ ca nirāhāraḥ kṛśodaraḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*śaktimānparipuṣṭaśca siddhamantraprabhāvata \|*  
*dadarśa bālako dhyāne divyaṃ lokaṃ ca bālakam \|\| 46 \|\|*

*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|*  
*kiśoravayasaṃ śyāmaṃ gopaveṣaṃ ca sasmitam \|\| 47 \|\|*

*gopairgopāṅganābhiśca veṣṭitaṃ pītavāsasam \|*  
*dvibhujaṃ muralīhastaṃ candanena vicarcitam \|\| 48 \|\|*

*brahmaviṣṇuśivādyaiśca stūyamānaṃ parātparam \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā ca suciraṃ śāntaṃ śāntaśca gopikāsutaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*virarāma ca śokārto yadā taddraṣṭumakṣamaḥ \|*  
*rurodāśvatthamūle ca na dṛṣṭvā bālakaṃ śiśuḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

45-50. О Shaunaka, that child continued his meditation for a thousand divine years while tasting. He became quite lean and thin in meditation. Inspite of that, but because of the influence of the said mantra, he remain quite hail and hearty. While in meditation, the boy visualised a small child seated over a gems-studded lion-throne. He was adorned with all the ornaments studded with gems and was of tender age, dark complexioned and was clad in the garments of cowherd. He had a smiling face. He was surrounded by the cowherds and cowherdesses and was wearing a yellow lower garment, having two hands and a flute held in both of them. All his limbs were plastered with the sandal-paste; he was being adored by the gods like Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva. Looking at such a cowherd the peaceful child kept on meditating. After the meditation was over when he could not find him there, he was upset with grief. When he could not find the boy after finishing his meditation, the child started crying under the shade of the tree.

## \|\| 21.51-52 \|\|

*babhūvākāśavāṇīti rudantaṃ bālakaṃ prati \|*  
*satyaṃ prabodhayuktaṃ ca hitameva mitākṣaram \|\| 51 \|\|*

*sakṛdyaddarśitaṃ rūpaṃ tadeva nādhunā punaḥ \|*  
*api pakvakaṣāyāṇāṃ durdarśaṃ ca kuyoginām \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. Finding him constantly weeping, there was a divine voice from the sky addressed to him. The beneficial truth and knowledge was enshrined in these words. “The form which appeared before you just now would never appear before you again, because it is difficult for the sinful people to have an audience with the divine person.

## \|\| 21.53 \|\|

*etasminvigrahe'tīte saṃprāpte divyavigrahe \|*  
*punardrakṣyasi govindaṃ janmamṛtyuharaṃ harim \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. After you leave this body you will achieve divine form and you will achieve the place of lord Govinda which is free from birth and death. You will find that form there again.

## \|\| 21.54-55 \|\|

*iti śrutvā bālakaśca virarāma mudā'nvitaḥ \|*  
*kāle tatyāja tīrthe ca tanuṃ kṛṣṇaṃ hṛdi smaran \|\| 54 \|\|*

*nedurdundubhayaḥ svarge puṣpavṛṣṭirbabhūva ha \|*  
*babhūva śāpamuktaśca nāradaśca mahāmuniḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. On hearing this the child stopped making efforts for search for the divine form. After sometime reciting the name of Krishna in his mind, he went to a holy place and ended his life. At that point of time, the drums were beaten in the heaven and there was a showering of flowers from the sky. This way the great sage Narada was freed from the curse.

## \|\| 21.56-57 \|\|

*tanuṃ tvakvā sa jīvaśca vilīno brahmavigrahe \|*  
*babhūva prāktanānnityaḥ kālabhede tirohitaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

*āvirbhāvastirobhāvaḥ svecchayā nityadehinām \|*  
*janmamṛtyujarāvyādhirbhaktānāṃ nāsti śaunaka \|\| 57 \|\|*

56-57. After leaving his human body, the soul entered the divine body of the Brahman. He became eternal as compared to his earlier life and ultimately he vanished. О Shaunaka, the devotees are born on the earth at their own sweet-will and also disappear in the same way. They are not affected by birth, death, old age and illness.

## Chapter 22

## \|\| 22.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*kati kalpāntare'tīte sraṣṭuḥ sṛṣṭi vidhau punaḥ \|*  
*marīcimiśraimunibhiḥ sārddhaṃ kaṇṭhādbabhūva saḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said — After many kalpas, Brahma was again engaged in the creation work. Out of the neck of Brahma several sages were born who were free from any curse.

## \|\| 22.2 \|\|

*vidhernāradanāmnaśca kaṇṭhadeśādbabhūva saḥ \|*  
*nāradaśceti vikhyāto munīndastena hetunā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Because of that, out of those sages, one of them was named as Narada.

## \|\| 22.3-4 \|\|

*yaḥ putraścetaso dhāturbabhūva munipuṃgavaḥ \|*  
*tena pracetā iti ca nāma cakre pitāmahaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*babhūva dhāturyaḥ putraḥ sahasā dakṣapārśvataḥ \|*  
*sarvakarmāṇi dakṣaśca tena dakṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. The one who was born out of the mind of Brahma was given the name of Praceta, the one who emerged from the-right side of Brahma’s back, he was given the name of Daksha.

## \|\| 22.5 \|\|

*vedeṣu kardamaḥ śabdaśchāyāyāṃ varttate sphuṭaḥ \|*  
*babhūva kardamādvālaḥ kardamastena kīrttitaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. In the Vedas the word Kardama has been used for shadow; therefore from the shadow of Brahma a sage emerged who was given the name of Kardama.

## \|\| 22.6-7 \|\|

*tejobhede marīciśca vedeṣu varttate sphuṭam \|*  
*jātaḥ sadyo'titejasvī marīcistena kīrtitaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*kratusaṃghaśca bālena kṛto janmāntare'dhunā \|*  
*brahmaputre'pi tannāma kraturityabhidhīyate \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. The word Marlca is used in the Vedas for the purpose of glamour; therefore the sage who emerged out of the glamour of Brahma was given the name of Marlca. The child who had performed many yajnas in the earlier birth was given the name of Rtu when he was born of Brahma.

## \|\| 22.8-9 \|\|

*pradhānāṅgamukhaṃ dhātustato jātaśca bālakaḥ \|*  
*irastejasvivacano'pyaṅgirāstena kīrttitaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*atitejasvini bhṛgurvarttate nāgni śaunaka \|*  
*jātaḥ sadyo'titejasvī bhṛgustena prakīrttitaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. The son who emerged out of the face of Brahma was quite glorious and therefore he was given the name of Arigira. О Shaunaka, for the lustre the word Bhrigu has been used, therefore the child who was born with lustre was given the name of Bhrigu:

## \|\| 22.10 \|\|

*bālo'pyaruṇavarṇaśca jātaḥ sadyo'titejasā \|*  
*prajvalannūrdhvatapasā cāruṇistena kīrttitaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Inspite of being a child the one who was quite glorious and looked red like Aruna and because of his high tapas he started shining; he was therefore given the name of Aruni.

## \|\| 22.11 \|\|

*haṃsā ātmavaśā yasya yogena yogino dhuvam \|*  
*bālaḥ paramayogīndrastena haṃsī prakīrttitaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*
