11\. The ascetic because of whose tapas the geese became subordinate to him, that illustrious boy was given the name of Hansl.

## \|\| 22.12 \|\|

*vaśībhūtaśca śiṣyaśca jātaḥ sadyo hi bālakaḥ \|*  
*atipriyaśca dhātuśca vaśiṣṭhastena kīrttitaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The child which having emerged out of Brahma and became his pupil was known by the name of Vasistha.

## \|\| 22.13-15 \|\|

*santataṃ yasya yatnaṃ ca tapaḥsu bālakasya ca \|*  
*prakīrttito yatistena saṃyataḥ sarvakarmasu \|\| 13 \|\|*

*pulastapaḥsu vedeṣu varttate'haḥ sphuṭe'pi ca \|*  
*sphuṭastapaḥsamūhaśca pulahastena bālakaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*pulastapaḥ samūhaśca yasyāsti pūrvajanmanām \|*  
*tapaḥsaṃghasvarūpaśca pulastyastena bālakaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

13-15. After being born the child who was always engaged in tapas and who was involved in many actions was given the name of Yati, because of that. In the Vedas the word Pula has been used for performing tapas, therefore he was given the name of Pulaha. Pulaha is also known for a combination therefore the one who was combined in the earlier birth the child came to be known as Pulastya.

## \|\| 22.16 \|\|

*triguṇāyāṃ prakṛtyāṃ trirviṣṇāvaśca pravarttate \|*  
*tayobhaktiḥ samā yasya tena bālo'trirucyate \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The word Tri is used for all the three Bhuvanas of the world and the word is representative of Vishnu; therefore the one who was equally sacred to both, was given the name of Atri.

## \|\| 22.17-18 \|\|

*jaṭā vahniśikhārūpāḥ pañca santi ca mastake \|*  
*tapastejobhavā yasya sa ca pañcaśikhaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*apāntaratame deśe tapastepe'nyajanmani \|*  
*apāntaratamā nāma śiśostena prakīrtitam \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. Because of the eternal glory the one who had five flames of fire over his head, came to be known as Pancasikha. The one who performed tapas in the region where there was no darkness, was given the name of Apantaratma.

## \|\| 22.19 \|\|

*svayaṃ tapaḥ samāpnoti vāhayetprāpayetparān \|*  
*voḍhuṃ samarthastapasi voḍhustena prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The one who himself became recluse and caused others to be like him and carried the land of tapas on his head was given the name of Vodhu.

## \|\| 22.20-21 \|\|

*tapasastejasā bālo dīptimānsatataṃ mune \|*  
*tapaḥsu rocate cittaṃ rucistena prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*kopakāle babhūvurye sraṣṭurekādaśa smṛtāḥ \|*  
*rodanādeva rudrāśca kopitāstena hetunā \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. О sage, the child who was always engrossed in tapas and was always interested in the same, was given the name of Ruci. Those eleven sages, who appeared from the anger of Brahma and started crying, were known by the name of Rudras.1

## \|\| 22.22 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*rudreṣvekatamo bālo maheśa iti me bhramaḥ \|*  
*bhavānpurāṇatattvajñaḥ sandehaṃ chettumarhati \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Shaunaka said — One of the eleven Rudras was given the name of Mahesa. Therefore you tell me whether it is the same Mahesa or it represents some one else. I am in doubt; you therefore kindly remove my doubt.

## \|\| 22.23 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*viṣṇuḥ sattvaguṇaḥ pātā brahmā sraṣṭā rajoguṇaḥ \|*  
*tamoguṇāste rudrāśca durnivārā bhayaṃkarāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Sauti said — Because of the influence of Sattvaguna, Vishnu has been declared as the protector of the worlds. Because of the influence of Rajoguna, Brahma has been declared as the creator. Because of the influence of Tamoguna Rudra has been conceived to be the terrific one and the destroyer of the universe.

## \|\| 22.24 \|\|

*kālāgnirudraḥ saṃharttā teṣvekaḥ śaṃkarāṃśakaḥ \|*  
*śuddhasattvasvarūpaśca śivaśca śivadaḥ satām \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. One of the Rudras is known by the name of Kalagni-Rudra (Rudra of the time of dissolution). The one who is destroyer is the part of Sankara. Shiva has the pure Sattvaguna in him and he also bestows welfare on the people.

## \|\| 22.25 \|\|

*anye kṛṣṇasya ca kalāstāvaṃśau viṣṇuśaṃkarau \|*  
*samau sattvasvarūpau dvau paripūrṇatamasya ca \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The rest of the Rudras are merely of the race of lord Krishna he also bestows welfare on the people.

## \|\| 22.26 \|\|

*uktaṃ rudrodbhave kāle kathaṃ vismarati dvija \|*  
*māyayā mohitāḥ sarve munīnāṃ ca matibhramaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. The rest of the Rudras are merely of the race of lord Krishna, only Vishnu and Shiva are the complete parts of lord Krishna. Both of them are equally of the form of sattva.

## \|\| 22.27 \|\|

*sanakaśca sanaṃdaśca tṛtīyaśca sanātanaḥ \|*  
*sanatkumāro bhagavāṃścaturtho brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. О Brahmana, I have narrated to you the story of the creation of Rudras earlier. Have you forgotten it? All are moving under the influence of the illusion of the lord; therefore even the sages also developed doubts.

## \|\| 22.28 \|\|

*brahmā sraṣṭuṃ pūrvaputrānuvāca te na sehire \|*  
*tena prakopito dhātā rudrāḥ kopodbhavā mune \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Out of the sons of Brahma, Shaunaka happens to the first, Sananda the second, Sanatana the third and the fourth one is the lord the Sanat-kumaras. О sage, Brahma had initially created them and asked them to engage themselves in the creation of the universe but they refused to do so which enraged Brahma and out of rage, the Rudras were born.

## \|\| 22.29 \|\|

*sanakaśca sanandaśca tau dvāvānandavācakau \|*  
*ānanditau ca bālau dvau bhaktipūrṇatamau sadā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Both the boys Sanaka and Sananda represents the welfare; therefore both of them always remain filled with devotion and spend their time joyfully. Therefore they are known by the name of Sanaka and Sananda.

## \|\| 22.30 \|\|

*sanātanaśca śrīkṛṣṇo nityaḥ pūrṇatamaḥ svayam \|*  
*tadbhaktastatsamaḥ satyaṃ tena bālaḥ sanātanaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Lord Krishna himself has been eternal and complete in all respects, his devotee also becomes like him. Therefore the third child was given the name of Sanatana.

## \|\| 22.31 \|\|

*sanattu nityavacanaḥ kumāraḥ śiśuvācakaḥ \|*  
*sanatkumāraṃ tenemamuvāca kamalodbhavaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The word Sanatana represents eternity and the word Kumara stands for a child. Therefore Brahma gave the child, the name of Sanat-kumara.

## \|\| 22.32 \|\|

*brahmaṇo bālakānāṃ ca vyutpattiḥ kathitā mune \|*  
*sāmprataṃ nāradākhyānaṃ śrūyatāṃ ca yathākramam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. О sage, thus have I narrated the names of all the sons of Brahma; now you listen to the story about Narada.

## Chapter 23

## \|\| 23.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*sraṣṭā sṛṣṭividhānena niyojya sarvabālakān \|*  
*nāradaṃ prerayāmāsa sṛṣṭiṃ karttuṃ ca śaunaka \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said — О Shaunaka, Brahma asked all his son to get engaged in the work of the creation of the universe and he also asked Narada to follow suit.

## \|\| 23.2 \|\|

*hitaṃ satyaṃ vedasāraṃ pariṇāmasukhāvaham \|*  
*uvāca nāradaṃ brahmā vedavedāṅgapāragam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Brahma had already imparted the knowledge of Vedas and later Vedic literature to Narada. He, therefore, asked them to perform the job which was strictly according to the Vedas and could bestow welfare in the long run.

## \|\| 23.3 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*ehi vatsa kulaśreṣṭha nārada prāṇavallabha \|*  
*jñānadīpaśikhājñānatimirakṣayakāraka \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Brahma said — О son, you come here; you are the best of all my sons and are dearer to me than my life. You are the flame of intelligence and are the destroyer of a darkness of ignorance.

## \|\| 23.4-5 \|\|

*pūrveṣāmapi vandyānāṃ janakaḥ paramo guruḥ \|*  
*vidyādātā mantradātā dvau samau ca pituḥ parau \|\| 4 \|\|*

*tavāhaṃ janakaḥ putra vidyādātā ca pālakaḥ \|*  
*mamājñayā ca matprītyā kuru dāraparigraham \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. The father happens to be the great teacher; he is respectable of all the people. He is the one who bestows the knowledge and the mantras are both equal and are superior to the father. О son, I am your father, the bestower of knowledge and have brought you up. Therefore at my command and for my pleasure, you marry yourself.

## \|\| 23.6-8 \|\|

*sa ca śiṣyaḥ so'pi putro yaścājñāṃ pālayedguroḥ \|*  
*na kṣemaṃ tasya mūḍhasya yo guroravacaskaraḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*sa paṇḍitaḥ sa ca jñānī sa kṣemī sa ca puṇyavān \|*  
*gurovarcaskaro yo hi kṣemaṃ tasya pade pade \|\| 7 \|\|*

*sarveṣāmāśramāṇāṃ ca pradhānaḥ puṇyavāngṛhī \|*  
*strīputrapautrayuktaṃ ca mandiraṃ tapasaḥ phalam \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. He alone could be called as true sons and the true pupils who obeys to the command of his teacher; the one who disregards the teacher, cannot earn any welfare. Such of the learned people, intellectuals could be sharer of welfare and virtues who obey the command of the teacher. He meets with welfare at every step in all the Ashramas the Grhastha-Asrama happens to be the best, because with the glory of his tapas he maintenance his wife and sons, beside the grandsons joyfully.

## \|\| 23.9 \|\|

*pitaraḥ pūrvakāle ca tithikāle ca devatāḥ \|*  
*sarve gṛhasthamāyānti nipānamiva dhenavaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. As the cows go to a tank for drinking water, similarly the gods and the manes go to the house-holder in the morning and at noon.

## \|\| 23.10 \|\|

*nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ kurvanti gṛhiṇaḥ sadā \|*  
*iha etatsukhaṃ puṇyaṃ svargabhogaḥ paratra ca \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The house-holders regularly perform the essential rites and many of the sacrifices. By thus conducting himself the house-holders enjoys the sacred life on earth and achieves the heavenly abode after death.

## \|\| 23.11 \|\|

*jīvanmukto gṛhasthaśca svadharmaparipālakaḥ \|*  
*yaśasvī puṇyavāṃścaiva kīrtimāndhanavānsukhī \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. He follows his dharma truthfully; he becomes illustrious, meritorious, glorious, rich and enjoys all the pleasures.

## \|\| 23.12 \|\|

*yaśasvī kīrtimānyo hi mṛto jīvati santatam \|*  
*yaśaḥkīrtivihīno hi jīvannapi mṛto hi saḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The one who is glorious and enjoys popularity, he remain alike even after his death and the people devoid of glory and popularity are considered to be dead even during their life time.

## \|\| 23.13 \|\|

*brahmaṇo vacanaṃ śrutvā nārado munisattamaḥ \|*  
*uvāca vinayaṃ bhītaḥ śuṣkaṇṭhauṣṭhatālukaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. At these words of Brahma the lips throat and mouth of Narada were dried up. He get terrified he spoke meekly.

## \|\| 23.14 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*ekadā vāgvirodhena cobhayostātaputrayoḥ \|*  
*hānirbabhūva daivena mahatī vā'yaśaskarī \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Narada said — Once both father and son had to suffer gravely censurable because of their mutual disagreement by opposing dialogue.

## \|\| 23.15-16 \|\|

*mayā prāptaṃ ca tvacchāpādgāndharvaṃ śaudrameva ca \|*  
*janma karma ca macchāpāttvamapūjyo bhave bhava \|\| 15 \|\|*

*babhūva śāpo mukto me kāle te bhavitā vidhe \|*  
*doṣāya kalpate śaśvadvirodho na guṇāya ca \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. Because of your curse I was born in the race of Gandharvas as well as the SOdras. You became unadorable in the universe in the universe because of my curse. I could rid myself of your curse after a long time. Therefore, it is said that internal disagreement always leads to harmful situation and can earn no merit.

## \|\| 23.17 \|\|

*sa pitā sa gururbandhuḥ sa putraḥ samadhīśvaraḥ \|*  
*yaḥ śrīkṛṣṇapādapadme dṛḍhāṃ bhaktiṃ ca kārayet \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The one who makes his sons devoted to lord Krishna, he alone could be called a father or a teacher or as a true son of the lord.

## \|\| 23.18 \|\|

*asadvartmani cājñānādgacchanti yadi bālakāḥ \|*  
*nivarttayati tāneva sa pitā karuṇānidhiḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. If a child moves on the wrong path, then the compassionate father takes him back from that path.

## \|\| 23.19-20 \|\|

*kārayitvā kṛṣṇapāde bhaktityāgaṃ ca yaḥ pitā \|*  
*anyasminviṣaye putraṃ sa kiṃ hantuṃ pravarttayet \|\| 19 \|\|*

*dāragraho hi duḥkhāya kevalaṃ na sukhāya ca \|*  
*tapaḥsvargabhaktimuktikarmaṇāṃ vyavadhāyakaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. Lord Krishna’s lotus-like feet are adorable and discarding his devotion, if one leads his sons to the worldly pleasures, he cannot be called a true father. It is troublesome to have a wife and one get on pleasures out of it. She is an obstruction in the performance of tapas, achieving of heavens and salvation.

## \|\| 23.21 \|\|

*yoṣitastrividhā brahmangṛhiṇāṃ mūḍhacetasām \|*  
*sādhvī bhogyā ca kulaṭāstāḥ sarvāḥ svārthatatparāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The foolish house-holders have three types of ladies who are virtuous, enjoyable and wicked. All of them are selfish.

## \|\| 23.22 \|\|

*paralokabhiyā sādhvī tatheha yaśasā''tmanaḥ \|*  
*kāmasnehācca kurute bhartuḥ sevāṃ ca santatam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The virtuous lady conducts herself with the fear of her glory and also serves her husband for conjugal pleasures.

## \|\| 23.23 \|\|

*bhogyā bhogārthinī śaśvatkāmasnehena kevalam \|*  
*kurute kāntasevā ca na ca bhogādṛte kṣaṇam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The enjoyable lady lives only for enjoying the worldly pleasures serving her husband. In fact she does not serve her husband even for a moment.

## \|\| 23.24 \|\|

*vastrālaṃkārasambhogasusnigdhāhāramuttamam \|*  
*yāvatprāpnoti sā bhogyā tāvacca vaśagā priyā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Till such time she gets the clothes, ornaments, conjugal pleasures, she remains attached to her husband enjoying all the luxury. She remains attached to her husband, till a such time all these pleasures are available to her.

## \|\| 23.25-26 \|\|

*kulāṅgārasamā nārī kulaṭā kulanāśinī \|*  
*kapaṭātkurute sevāṃ svāmino na ca bhaktitaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*sadā puṃyogamāśaṃsurmanasā madanāturā \|*  
*āhārādadhikaṃ jāraṃ prārthayantī navaṃ navam \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. The wicked woman are like the fire in the family and are responsible for the destruction of the race. If such a woman serves her husband, she does so only deceitfully, devoid of any devotion. She always remains passionate and is always desirous of the company of others. Besides good food, she always remains anxious to have more and more men to enjoy their company.

## \|\| 23.27 \|\|

*jārārthe svapatiṃ tāta hantumicchati puṃścalī \|*  
*tasyāṃ yo viśvasenmūḍho jīvanaṃ tasya niṣphalam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O father, for the sake of her lover, such a degraded women even kills her husband. Therefore, the fools who believe in her their lives remains of no consequence.

## \|\| 23.28 \|\|

*kathitā yoṣitaḥ sarvā uttamādhamamadhyamāḥ \|*  
*svātmārāmā vijānanti manastāsāṃ na paṇḍitāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. I have thus explained the conduct of the virtuous and the ordinary types of women. Even the great yogis cannot read the mind of such women.

## \|\| 23.29 \|\|

*hṛdayaṃ kṣuradhārābhaṃ śaratpadmotsavaṃ mukham \|*  
*sudhāsamaṃ sumadhuraṃ vacanaṃ svārthasiddhaye \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Their hearts are quite sharp and their faces retain the beauty of the lotus flower. They speak in sweet tone for the accomplishment of their tasks.

## \|\| 23.30 \|\|

*prakope viṣatulyaṃ ca viśvāse sarvanāśanam \|*  
*durjñeyaṃ tadabhiprāyaṃ nigūḍhaṃ karma kevalam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. When enraged they vomit words which are like poison, the one who believes in them is sure to ruin himself. It is very difficult to understanding their mind; all their actions are deep rooted.

## \|\| 23.31 \|\|

*sadā tāsāmavinayaḥ prabalaṃ sāhasaṃ param \|*  
*doṣotkarṣaśchalotkarṣaḥ śaśvanmāyā duratyayā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Such women are of extremist nature and possess great courage. They are filled with deceitfully actions creating illusions everywhere.

## \|\| 23.32-34 \|\|

*puṃsaścāṣṭaguṇaḥ kāmaḥ śaśvatkāmo jagadguro \|*  
*āhāro dviguṇo nityaṃ naiṣṭhuryyaṃ ca caturguṇam \|\| 32 \|\|*

*kopaḥ puṃsaḥ ṣaḍguṇaśca vyavasāyaśca niścitam \|*  
*yatreme doṣanivahāḥ kā''sthā tatra pitāmaha \|\| 33 \|\|*

*kā krīḍā kiṃ sukhaṃ puṃso viṇmūtramalaveśmani \|*  
*tejaḥ pranaṣṭaṃ sambhoge divālāpe yaśaḥkṣayaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

32-34. О teacher, of the universe, they possess the patience eight times more then the men, they eat double the food and are four times more cruel then the men. How can one believe in them? What type of pleasures can a man derive from a woman and what type of pleasure can he get? By indulging in intercourse with them, there is loss of strength and by talking to them one loses the glory.

## \|\| 23.35 \|\|

*dhanakṣayo'tiprītau cātyāsaktau ca vapuḥkṣayaḥ \|*  
*sāhitye pauruṣaṃ naṣṭaṃ kalahe mānanāśanam \|*  
*sarvanāśaśca viśvāse brahmannārīṣu kiṃ sukham \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. When one is immensely attached to them, there is a loss of riches. By getting immensely attracted towards them, one loses the strength of the body; by indulging in conjugal pleasures there is a loss of wisdom, by quarrelling with them there is a loss of honour and everything. Therefore О Brahman what type of pleasure can we get from a woman?

## \|\| 23.36 \|\|

*yāvaddhanī ca tejasvī saśrīko yogyatāparaḥ \|*  
*pumānnārīṃ vaśīkartuṃ samarthastāvadeva hi \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The man is virtuous, rich, full of lustre and quite competent. He can control the ladies only up to the time, he has all the riches available with him.

## \|\| 23.37 \|\|

*rogiṇaṃ nirddhanaṃ vṛddhaṃ yoṣidvai prekṣate'priyam \|*  
*lokācārabhayāttasmai dadātyāhāramalpakam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The ladies do not love the husbands who are pauper and are old. They give them little food because of adverse reaction in the society.

## \|\| 23.38-39 \|\|

*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ brahmannātmāgamo yathā \|*  
*sarvaṃ jānāsi sarvajña svātmārāmeśvaro bhavān \|\| 38 \|\|*

*anugrahaṃ kuru vibho vidāyaṃ dehi sāmpratam \|*  
*kṛṣṇabhaktiṃ prārthayāmi tvayi kalpataroḥ pare \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. О Brahman, I have spoken all these on the basis of my knowledge, you know everything because you are the lord of the soul of a human being. Therefore you kindly permit me to go. О lord you be compassionate on me, you are greater than the kalpavrksa I am desirous of the devotion of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 23.40 \|\|

*ityuktvā nāradastatra dhṛtvā tātapadāmbujam \|*  
*ājñāṃ yayāce pitaraṃ gantu tapasi maṅgale \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Thus speaking Narada bowed down at the feet of Brahma and seeking his permission for leaving to perform tapas.

## \|\| 23.41 \|\|

*kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā bhaktinamrātmakandharaḥ \|*  
*kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ natvā brahmāṇaṃ gantumudyataḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Thereafter, he circumambulated Brahma with folded hands and bowing in reverence he was about to leave.

## \|\| 23.42-44 \|\|

*gacchantaṃ tanayaṃ dṛṣṭvā vidhātā jagatāṃ mune \|*  
*rurodoccairmuktakaṇṭhaṃ mahāsāṃsāriko yathā \|\| 42 \|\|*

*kare dhṛtvā samāliṅgya cucumba ca punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*ciraṃ vakṣasi kṛtvā ca vāsayāmāsa jānuni \|\| 43 \|\|*

*svātmārāmeśvaro brahmā yogīndrāṇāṃ gurorguruḥ \|*  
*bheda soḍhuṃ na śakto'bhūdvicchedo duḥsaho nṛṇām \|\| 44 \|\|*

42-44. О sage, Brahma the creator of the universe finding his son leaving for penance was over-whelmed and becoming emotional he started weeping bitterly. Thereafter holding his hand and embracing Narada, Brahma kissed his face many times. Lord Brahma inspite of being so knowledgeable could not bear the separation of Narada, because separation is intolerable.

## \|\| 23.45 \|\|

*kātaraḥ putrabhedena mohito viṣcumāyayā \|*  
*śokārtto vaktumārebhe sutaṃ sambodhya śaunaka \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. О Shaunaka, infested with the illusion of lord Vishnu, Brahma was infested with the grief of separation from his son and said to him.

## Chapter 24

## \|\| 24.1 \|\|

*śrībrahmovāca \|*  
*tvaṃ gaccha tapase vatsa kiṃ me saṃsārakarmaṇi \|*  
*ahaṃ yāsyāmi golokaṃ vijñātuṃ kṛṣṇamīśvaram \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Brahma said — О son, you can move along for performing tapas. This creation of the universe is of no consequence for me. I shall go to Goloka to know about lord Krishna.

## \|\| 24.2-5 \|\|

*sanakaśca sanandaśca tṛtīyaśca sanātanaḥ \|*  
*sanatkumāro nīrāgaścaturthaḥ putra eva ca \|\| 2 \|\|*

*yatī haṃsī cāruṇiśca voḍhuḥ pañcaśikhastathā \|*  
*putrāstapasvinaḥ sarve kiṃ me saṃsārakarmaṇi \|\| 3 \|\|*

*vacaskaro marīcirme aṅgirāśca bhṛgustathā \|*  
*ruciratriḥ kardamaśca pracetāśca kraturmanuḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*vasiṣṭho vaśagaḥ śaśvatsarveṣu ca suteṣu ca \|*  
*anye 'vivekino'sādhyāḥ kiṃ me saṃsārakarmaṇi \|\| 5 \|\|*

2-5. Sanaka, Sanandana, Sanatana and the fourth son Sanatkumara have already become recluses. The son named Yati, HansI, ArunI, Vodhu, Pancas'ikha and other sons also have also become mendicants. Then how my I concerned with the creation of the universe. Marichi, Angira, Bhrigu, Ruci, Atri, Kardama, Praceta, Kratu and Manu these are the sons who are quite obedient to me. Out of these sons Vasistha is extremely obedient, who always moves according to my wishes. The rest of the sons are thoughtless and do not care for my command. In such circumstances why should I brother about the creation of the universe.

## \|\| 24.6 \|\|

*nibodha vatsa vakṣyāmi vedoktaṃ vacanaṃ śubham \|*  
*pāramparyyakramaparaṃ caturvargaphalapradam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. О son, listen, I speak to you the welfare words which are contained in the Vedas. These words are being followed with great effort and bestow Dharma, Artha, Kama and Moksa.

## \|\| 24.7-9 \|\|

*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṃśca sarve vāñchanti paṇḍitāḥ \|*  
*vedapraṇihitānetānsabhāsu muniśaṃsitān \|\| 7 \|\|*

*vedapraṇihito dharmo hyadharmastadviparyyayaḥ \|*  
*ādau vipro yajñasūtraṃ paridhāya sukhaṃ sukhī \|\| 8 \|\|*

*samadhītya tato vedāndadāti gurudakṣiṇām \|*  
*tataḥ prakṛṣṭakulajāṃ suvinītāṃ samudvahet \|\| 9 \|\|*

7-9. In the assembly of the sages mentioned in the Vedas, Dharma, Artha, Kdma and Moksa are praised and desired by all the intellectuals. The Dharma is found established in the Vedas and the actions which are denounced in the Vedas are called adharma. The Brahmana first of all should to be adorned with yajnopavlta and should study the Vedas. Thereafter we should offer daksina to his teacher. Then he should marry a spotless girl of noble race.

## \|\| 24.10 \|\|

*sā sādhvī kulajā yā ca patisevāsu tatparā \|*  
*sadvaro durvinītā ca saṃbhavenna kadācana \|*  
*ākare padmarāgāṇāṃ janma kācamaṇeḥ kutaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The woman born in high family remains attached to her husband and lives the life of chastity; the woman of the high race is also well disciplined. How could a glass-gem emerge out of the mine of pure gems?

## \|\| 24.11 \|\|

*asadvaṃśaprasūtā yā pitrordoṣeṇa nārada \|*  
*durvinītā ca sā duṣṭā svatantrā sarvakarmasu \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. О Narada, only a girl born in some low family becomes harsh and wicked and acts according to her own will.

## \|\| 24.12 \|\|

*na vatsa duṣṭāḥ sarvāśca yoṣitaḥ kamalākalāḥ \|*  
*svarveśyāṃśāśca kulaṭā asadvaṃśa samudbhavāḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. О son, all the ladies cannot be wicked because they happen to be the rays of Lakshmi. Such of the ladies who are born of the race of Apsaras and low family. Only they become wicked.

## \|\| 24.13 \|\|

*nirguṇaṃ svāminaṃ sādhvī sevate ca praśaṃsati \|*  
*na sevate ca kulaṭā priyaṃ nindati sadguṇam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. A chaste woman serves even a husband who is devoid of any good qualities. But a wicked woman disregards even a virtuous husband and turns her good qualities as the vices.

## \|\| 24.14 \|\|

*sādhuḥ sadvaṃśajāṃ kanyāṃ prayatnena parigrahet \|*  
*tasyāṃ putrānsamutpādya vṛddhastu tapase vrajet \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Therefore the men of noble nature marry their daughters with great efforts and produce children from them. After attaining the old age they leave the house for performing tapas.

## \|\| 24.15 \|\|

*varaṃ hutavahe vāsaḥ sarpavaktre ca kaṇṭake \|*  
*etebhyo duḥkhado vāsaḥ striyā durmukhayā saha \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. It is better to live in the fire, over the serpent hood or over the thorn, but it is difficult to manage with a wife who is ill-tongued, because she is more troublesome even than the fire, the snakes, of the thorns.

## \|\| 24.16-17 \|\|

*matto'dhītastvayā vedo mahyaṃ ca gurudakṣiṇām \|*  
*putra dehīdameveha kuru dāraparigraham \|\| 16 \|\|*

*vatsa tvaṃ kulajātāṃ ca pūrvapatnī ca mālatīm \|*  
*vivāhaṃ kuru kalyāṇa kalyāṇe ca dine 'nagha \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. О son, you have learnt Vedas from me and I want only this much daksina in return that you marry yourself. О son, your earlier spotless wife named Malatl has been reborn, she is sinless. You marry her on some auspicious day.

## \|\| 24.18 \|\|

*manuvaṃśodbhavasyeha sṛṃjayasya gṛhe satī \|*  
*tvatkṛte janma labdhvā ca kurute bhārate tapaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Having been born in the race of Manu and the house of king Srnjaya, she is performing tapas in the land of Bharatavarsa to achieve you.

## \|\| 24.19 \|\|

*gṛhṇīṣva paramāṃ ratnamālāṃ ca kamalākalām \|*  
*bhārate na bhavedvyarthaṃ janānāṃ tapasaḥ phalam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Her present name is Ratnamala, she is the ray of Lakshmi and therefore you go and accept her as your wife, because in Bharatavarsa the tapas performed by someone never becomes infructuous.

## \|\| 24.20 \|\|

*ādau bhavedgṛhī loko vānaprasthastataḥ param \|*  
*tatasta pasvī mokṣāya krama eṣa śrutau śrutaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. A man should first of all conduct himself as a house-holder. Thereafter as a Vanaprastha. At the third stage he should aspire for salvation turning himself to the form of an ascetic and performing tapas. This sequence has been defined in the Vedas.

## \|\| 24.21-22 \|\|

*vaiṣṇavānāṃ harerarcā tapasyā ca śrutau śrutā \|\| 21 \|\|*

*vaiṣṇava tvaṃ gṛhe tiṣṭha kuru kṛṣṇapadārcanam \|*  
*antarbāhye hariryasya tasya kiṃ tapasā suta \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. It has been described in the Vedas. the adoration of the lord is tapas for a Vaishnava who should conduct himself as a house-holder and adore lord Krishna. О son, a person in whom lord Vishnu resides and even surrounds him, what is the use of tapas for him?

## \|\| 24.23 \|\|

*nāntarbāhye hariryasya tasya kiṃ tapasā'nagha \|*  
*tapasā harirārādhyo nānyaḥ kaścana vidyate \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. О sinless one, who is not surrounded by Hari, the performing of tapas is of no use for me because by performing tapas, one adores lord Vishnu only and none else.

## \|\| 24.24 \|\|

*yatra tatra kṛtaṃ kṛṣṇasevanaṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ \|*  
*vatsa madvacanenaiva gṛhe sthitvā hariṃ bhaja \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. О son wherever one renders services to lord Krishna it amounts to the best of tapas. Therefore listening to my words, you stay at home and recite the name of the lord.

## \|\| 24.25 \|\|

*gṛhī bhava muniśreṣṭha gṛhiṇāṃ sarvadā sukham \|*  
*kāminyāṃ sukhasambhogaḥ svargabhogasamo mataḥ \|\| 25\|\|*

25\. О best of the sages, you become the best of the house-holder which will bestow immense pleasure on you. The pleasure one derives from the company of his damsel is equivalent to all the pleasures of the world.

## \|\| 24.26 \|\|

*taddarśanamupasparśaṃ vāñchantyeva mumukṣavaḥ \|*  
*sarvasparśasukhātstrīṇāmupasparśasukhaṃ param \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Even the truth seekers are desirous of having an audience with her and to touch her. The touch of a lady, is considered to be the best of the touch of all others.

## \|\| 24.27 \|\|

*tataḥ sukhatamaṃ putradarśanaṃ sparśanaṃ mune \|*  
*sarvebhyaḥ preyasī kāntā priyā tena prakīrttitā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. О sage looking to the face of a son and touching him also gives immense pleasures. The wife is considered to be the dearest of all and that is why she is called the beloved.

## \|\| 24.28-29 \|\|

*putraprayojanā kāntā śatakāntāpriyaḥ sutaḥ \|*  
*nāsti putrātparo bandhurnāsti putrātparaḥ priyaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*sarvebhyo jayamanvicchetputrādekātparājayam \|*  
*na cātmani priyo'rthaśca tasmādapi sutaḥ priyaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. You need a wife for producing a son, who happens to be dearer than hundreds of wives. No other relation becomes dearer to anyone then his own son. You try to overcome all the desires and think of a defeat from your son only. No good things are kept by anyone for himself and are given away to the son. Therefore the son happens to be the dearest of all.

## \|\| 24.30 \|\|

*ataḥ priyatame putre nyasedātmaparaṃ dhanam \|*  
*ityevamuktvā sa brahmā virarāma ca śaunaka \|*  
*uvāca vacanaṃ tātaṃ nārado jñānināṃ varaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Therefore one should give away all his riches to his dearest son. О Shaunaka, thus speaking Brahma kept quiet; thereafter Narada the best of the intellectual spoke to him.

## \|\| 24.31 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*svayaṃ vijñāya sarvārthaṃ svaputraṃ vedadarśane \|*  
*pravarttayatyasanmārge sa dayāluḥ kathaṃ pitā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Narada said — How can the father who leads a son to the wrong path, be considered as the compassionate one.

## \|\| 24.32 \|\|

*jalabudbudavatsarvaṃ saṃsāramatinaśvaram \|*  
*jalarekhā yathā mithyā tathā brahmañjagattrayam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. О Brahman the entire universe is like the water bubble and vanishes in no time. It is like the line drawn over the water.

## \|\| 24.33 \|\|

*vihāya haridāsyaṃ ca viṣaye yanmanaścalam \|*  
*durlabhaṃ mānavaṃ janma mā bhūttanniṣphalaṃ kvacit \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The mind should not be allowed to be influenced by the worldly pleasures and passions and should be devoted to lord Hari. Because of this, one should make it successful.

## \|\| 24.34-35 \|\|

*kā vā kasya priyā putro bandhuḥ ko vā bhavārṇave \|*  
*karmormibhiryojanā ca tadapāyo viyojanā \|\| 34 \|\|*

*sukarma kārayedyo hi tanmitraṃ sa pitā guruḥ \|*  
*durbuddhiṃ janayedyo hi sa ripuḥ syātkathaṃ pitā \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. Who is the beloved of whom in this ocean of universe and who is the wife, the son and the brother. Because of the movement of waves they come together and after the decline of the deeds they again get separated. The one who performs noble deeds or inspires to do so can be called a true friend, a father and the teacher and one who inculcates evil thoughts, he cannot be considered as a father but is an enemy.

## \|\| 24.36 \|\|

*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ tāta vedabījaṃ yathāgamam \|*  
*dhruvaṃ tathā'pi karttavyaṃ tavājñāparipālanam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. О father in this way I have spoken out the essence of the Vedas and the Shastras. Though it is absolutely true, inspite of that I should obey your command.

## \|\| 24.37 \|\|

*ādau yāsyāmi bhagavannaranārāyaṇāśramam \|*  
*nārāyaṇakathāṃ śrutvā kariṣye dārasaṃgraham \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. О lord first of all I shall visit the abode of Naranarayana. Thereafter listening to the advised of lord Vishnu I shall marry myself.

## \|\| 24.38 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā sa munirvirarāma pituḥ puraḥ \|*  
*puṣpavṛṣṭistadupari tatkṣaṇena babhūva ha \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Thus speaking Narada kept quiet. At that very movement there was a showering of flowers.

## \|\| 24.39 \|\|

*kṣaṇaṃ pituḥ puraḥ sthitvā nārado munisattamaḥ \|*  
*uvāca ca punarvedaṃ vacanaṃ maṅgalapradam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Thereafter Nerada, the best of the sages, stood before his fathem (Brahma), and further spoke pleasant words.

## \|\| 24.40 \|\|

*śrīnārada uvāca \|*  
*dehi me kṛṣṇamantraṃ ca yanmanovāñchitaṃ mama \|*  
*tatsambandhi ca yajjñānaṃ yatra tadguṇavarṇanam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Narada said — О lord, you bestow the mantra of Krishna on me, which is desired by me in my life. Whatever knowledge is associated with the mantra of Krishna, you better bestow the same on me. You tell me all about it.

## \|\| 24.41 \|\|

*tataḥ paścātkariṣyāmi tvatprītyā dārasaṃgraham \|*  
*mānase paripūrṇe ca kāryyaṃ kartuṃ pumānsukhī \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Thereafter I shall marry himself in order to please you, because a person feels happy after his desired tasks is accomplished and then he attends to the other jobs.

## \|\| 24.42 \|\|

*nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā prahṛṣṭaḥ kamalodbhavaḥ \|*  
*uvāca punarevedaṃ putraṃ jñānavidāṃ varaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The all knowledgeable Brahma felt delighted at the words of Narada and said to him.

## \|\| 24.43-44 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*patyurmantraṃ piturmantraṃ na gṛhṇīyādvicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*viviktāśramiṇāṃ caiva na mantraḥ sukhadāyakaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*niṣekāllabhyate mantro gururbharttā ca kāminī \|*  
*vidyā sukhaṃ bhayaṃ duḥkhaṃ puruṣaiḥ svecchayā na ca \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. Brahma said — the intelligent person should not receive mantra of his father or the husband. Even to receive a mantra from a female ascetic does not bestow welfare on anyone. Therefore a person should receive mantra from a teacher, a lord and not from a woman, otherwise one cannot get pleasure and has to face misery. He cannot achieve them by his own efforts.

## \|\| 24.45 \|\|

*maheśvarastava guruḥ prāktano naḥ purātanaḥ \|*  
*gaccha vatsa śivaṃ śāntaṃ śivadaṃ jñānināṃ gurum \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Therefore lord Shiva happens to be your old teacher besides mine. Therefore you go to lord Shiva who is peaceful, bestower of welfare ancLis the teacher of all the intellectuals.

## \|\| 24.46 \|\|

*tata eva bhavānmantraṃ jñānaṃ labdhvā purātanāt \|*  
*nārāyaṇakathāṃ śrutvā śīghramāgaccha madgṛham \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. You go to him, have a mantra from him besides listening to the stories of Narayana and return to me at once.

## \|\| 24.47 \|\|

*ityuktvā jagatāṃ dhātā virarāma ca śaunaka \|*  
*praṇamya pitaraṃ bhaktyā śivalokaṃ yayau muniḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. О Shaunaka, Brahma the creator of the universe kept quiet after speaking all this and the sage Narada also went to Shiva bowing in reverence to Brahma.

## Chapter 25

## \|\| 25.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*kṣaṇena viprapravaro mudā'nvito jagāma śambhoḥ sadanaṃ manoharam \|*  
*ūrdhvaṃ dhruvādyojanalakṣamīpsitaṃ mahārharatnaughavinirmitaṃ mahat \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. SaUti said — Narad a the best of the Brahmartas felt delighted and in an instant he reached the pleasant abode of lord Shiva located at a distance of a lakh of yojanas above the pole-star (dhruva) and was built with precious stones.

## \|\| 25.2 \|\|

*nirāśraye yogabalena śambhunā dhṛtaṃ vicitraṃ vividhālayānvitam \|*  
*dṛṣṭaṃ supuṇyāśayasādhakairvarairmunīndravaryaiḥ paripūritaṃ śubham \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Shiva with the influence of his yogic powers had created in the sky having no base a beautiful loka. It is inhabited by the glorious sages having spotless minds besides the best of ascetic.

## \|\| 25.3 \|\|

*mayūkhaśūnyaṃ ravicandrayormune hutāśanairveṣṭitameva kevalam \|*  
*prākārarūpairatiriktavarddhitairuccairasaṃkhyāpramitaiḥ śikhojjvalaiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The rays oY the sun and the moon do not reach there; that loka is surrounded by high flames of fire which serve as the boundary wall.

## \|\| 25.4 \|\|

*puraṃ varaṃ yojanalakṣavistṛtaṃ trikoṭiratnendragṛhānvitaṃ sadā \|*  
*virājitaṃ hīrakasāranirmitaiścitrairvicitrairvividhairmanoharaiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. That beautiful city is spread over an area of a lakh of yojanas which has three crores of houses studded with gems. The gems have also been used in the inner apartments which are of different shades and looked quite charming.

## \|\| 25.5-6 \|\|

*māṇikyamuktāmaṇidarpaṇairyutaṃ nasvapnadṛṣṭaṃ dvija viśvakarmaṇaḥ \|*  
*ākalpamekaiḥ śivasevitairjanairniṣevitaṃ santatameva śaunaka \|\| 5\|\|*

*siddhairniyuktaṃ śatakoṭilakṣaikaistrikoṭilakṣaiśca yutaṃ svapārṣadaiḥ \|*  
*yuktaṃ trilakṣairvikaṭaiśca bhairavaiḥ kṣetraiścaturlakṣaśataiśca veṣṭitam \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. О Brahmana named Shaunaka, the city has mirrors made of the pearls and gems which are invisible to Vishvakarma even in dreams. In such palaces only the devotees of lord Shiva are allowed to reside. The Shivaloka has a hundred crores lakhs of the devotees of Shiva. Three lakhs of deformed Bhairavas also reside there, hundred of lakhs of regions surround it.

## \|\| 25.7 \|\|

*suradrumairveṣṭitameva santataṃ mandāravṛkṣapravaraiḥ supuṣpitaiḥ \|*  
*virājitaṃ sundarakāmadhenubhiryathā balākāśatakairnabhastalam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. There is the beautiful Mandara mountain with the divine trees laden with beautiful flowers. There are beautiful Kamadhenu (wish fulfilling) cows which add, grandeur of the place. A hundreds of Apsaras add to the beauty of the heaven.

## \|\| 25.8 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā munirvismayamāpa mānase kiṃ nātra citraṃ surayogināṃ gurau \|*  
*lokaṃ trilokācca vilakṣaṇaṃ paraṃ bhīmṛtyurogārttijarāharaṃ varam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The sage Narada was surprised by looking at them. He thought, “The place is inhabited by Shiva who happens to be the teacher all of all the yogis and intellectual. It is really surprising to find such astonishing things here. This loka is of astonishing nature as compared to the other lokas and is indeed the best. The fear, ailments, old age are removed from this place.

## \|\| 25.9 \|\|

*dūre sabhāmaṇḍalamadhyagaṃ śivaṃ dadarśa śāntaṃ śivadaṃ manoharam \|*  
*padmatrinetraṃ vidhupañcavaktraṃ gaṅgādharaṃ nirmalacandraśekharam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Narada found that Shiva was lodged in the Sabhamandapa. In the centre of the Sabhamandapa he was seated having five faces, which were quite peaceful. He looked as the bestower of welfare, quite charming and was having three eyes resembling the lotus flowers. The river Ganga was emerging from his head and the crescent was adorning his forehead.

## \|\| 25.10 \|\|

*prataptahemābhajaṭādharaṃ vibhuṃ digambaraṃ śubhramanantamakṣaram \|*  
*mandākinīpuṣkarabījamālayā kṛṣṇeti nāmaiva mudā japantam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Не was wearing matted locks of hair over the heads, which were issuing lustre of the molten gold. He was wearing no clothes and looked eternal. He was wearing a garland of lotus flowers emerging from heavenly Ganga. Filled with ecstasy he was reciting the name of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 25.11-12 \|\|

*sunīlakaṇṭhaṃ bhujagendramaṇḍitaṃ yogīndrasiddhendra munīndravanditam \|*  
*siddheśvaraṃ siddhavidhānakāraṇaṃ mṛtyuṃjayaṃ kālayamāntakārakam \|\| 11 \|\|*

*prasannahāsyāsyamanoharaṃ varaṃ viśvāśrayāṇāṃ śivadaṃ varapradam \|*  
*sadā''śutoṣaṃ bhavarogavarjitaṃ bhaktapriyaṃ bhaktajanaikabandhum \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. A beautiful blue sign was adoring his neck. He was adorned with the ornaments of snakes and the great sages, yogis and siddhas bowed in reverence at his feet. He is the lord of the siddhas and has overcome the death because of the attainment of the siddhas. He is the one, who can put an end to Yama and the time. He wears a serene smile over his face. He provides complete welfare to all who take refuge in him and grant the desired wishes. He get pleased in no time, he is free from all the worldly ailments, is loved by his devotees and is the only relative of his devotees.

## \|\| 25.13 \|\|

*gatvā samīpaṃ munireṣa śūlinaṃ nanāma mūrdhnā pulakāṅkavigrahaḥ \|*  
*vīṇāṃ tritantrīṃ kvaṇayanpunarjagau kṛṣṇaṃ sa tuṣṭāva kalaṃ sukaṇṭhaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Reaching before such a type of Shiva the sage Narada became emotional and he bowed in reverence to him. Thereafter creating sound from the lute of three cords, he started reciting the glory of lord Krishna before Shiva.

## \|\| 25.14 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā munīndrapravaraṃ ca sasmitaṃ vidheḥ sutaṃ vedavidāṃ variṣṭham \|*  
*yogīndrasiddhendramaharṣibhiḥ samaṃ harṣeṇa pīṭhādudapaśyadīśvaraḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Finding Narada before him, who is the best of those well-versed in the Vedas and is the son of Brahma, Shiva looked at him smilingly and got up from his seat with the siddhas and great ascetics to welcome Narada.

## \|\| 25.15 \|\|

*dadau ca tasmai munaye sa saṃbhramādāliṅganaṃ cāśiṣamāsanādikam \|*  
*papraccha bhadrāgamanaprayojanaṃ tapodhanaṃ taṃ tapasā ca śaunaka \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O Shaunaka, Shiva then embraced Narada without any hesitation and blessing him he offered him a seat asking him at the same time the reason for his arrival.

## \|\| 25.16 \|\|

*sadratnasiṃhāsanasundare paraścovāsa śambhurvarapārṣadaiḥ saha \|*  
*novāsa dhātustanayaḥ kṛtāñjalistuṣṭāva bhaktyā praṇataḥ prabhuṃ dvija \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. О Brahmana, lord Shiva then again took his seat over the gem-studded lion-throne with the courtiers but the sage Narada instead of seating himself continued to stand with devotion. Thereafter with folded hands, he started offering prayer to Shiva.

## \|\| 25.17 \|\|

*gandharvarājena kṛtena nāradaḥ stotreṇa ramyeṇa śubhapradena ca \|*  
*stutvā praṇāmaṃ punareva kṛtvā bhavājñayovāsa bhavasya vāmataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Thereafter he recited the stolra which was bestower of the welfare and composed by the king of Gandharvas according to the Vedic texts. Thereafter Narada again offered his salutation to him and taking permission from him, he took a seat to the left of Shiva.

## \|\| 25.18 \|\|

*cakāra tatraiva nivedanaṃ śive mano'bhilāṣaṃ nijakāmapūrake \|*  
*śrutvā munestadvacanaṃ kṛpānidhirdrutaṃ pratijñāya cakāra comiti \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Thereafter he expressed the cause of his arrival before Shiva. On listening to the words of the sage, the compassionate Shiva said: “Alright.”

## Chapter 26

## \|\| 26.1 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*haristotraṃ ca kavacaṃ mantraṃ pūjāvidhiṃ param \|*  
*haraṃ yayāce devarṣirdhyānaṃ ca jñānameva ca \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Sauti said — The sage Narada then prayed to Shiva to enlighten him on the stotra of lord Hari, his kavacha, his mantra and the best of the method of his adoration, including meditation and the knowledge of tattva.

## \|\| 26.2-3 \|\|

*stotraṃ ca kavacaṃ mantraṃ dhyānaṃ pūjāvidhiṃ tathā \|*  
*tatprāktanīyajñānaṃ ca dadau tasmai maheśvaraḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*sarvaṃ prāpya muniśreṣṭhaḥ paripūrṇamanorathaḥ \|*  
*uvāca praṇato bhaktyā guruṃ praṇatavatsalam \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. Maheshvara on his part bestowed on him the knowledge of stotra, kavacha, mantra meditation and method of adoration. Narada the best of the sages felt that all his wishes have been fulfilled. Thereafter he bowing at his feet and his mind filled with devotion, spoke to his Gum, Who was always kind to the devotees.

## \|\| 26.4 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*āhnikaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca vada vedavidāṃ vara \|*  
*svadharmapālanaṃ nityaṃ yato bhavati nityaśaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Narada said — О lord you are best of the gods and therefore you kindly enlighten me on the method for the performing of daily routine of a Brahmana and other daily prayers.

## \|\| 26.5-9 \|\|

*śrīmaheśvara uvāca \|*  
*brāhme muhūrte cotthāya bahmarandhasthapaṅkaje \|*  
*sūkṣme sahasrapatre sve nirmale glānivarjite \|\| 5 \|\|*

*rātrivāsaṃ parityajya guruṃ tatraiva cintayet \|*  
*vyākhyāmudrākaraṃ prītaṃ sasmitaṃ śiṣyavatsalam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*prasannavadanaṃ śāntaṃ parituṣṭaṃ nirantaram \|*  
*sākṣādbrahmasvarūpaṃ ca paramaṃ cintayetsadā \|\| 7 \|\|*

*dhyātvaivaṃ gurumārādhya hṛtpadme nirmale site \|*  
*sahasrapatre vistīrṇe devamiṣṭaṃ vicintayet \|\| 8 \|\|*

*yasya devasya yaddhyānaṃ yadrūpaṃ tadvicintayet \|*  
*gṛhītvā tadanujñāṃ ca karttavyaṃ samayocitam \|\| 9 \|\|*

5-9. Lord Maheshvara said — One should get up early in the morning (at four o’clock) from the bed and changing his clothes he should meditate with his spiritual, unblemished, spotless mind, seated over the lotus of thousand petals and meditate upon his teacher, he should conceive that his teacher as seated over the thousand petaled lotus in Brahmarandhra. He is wearing a serene smile and is in Vyakhyana-mudra; his hand is raised. He is quite compassionate for his pupil, he had a smiling face, he always remains peaceful and satisfied and happens to be the form of the lord eternal. One should adore the lord in this manner. Thus meditating in the mind, one should adore his teacher. Thereafter he should meditate upon his family-god, always seated, being blissful, spotless clean, adorned with a thousand petals and having a heart like lotus. Which ever of gods has been described in a particular manner, one should adore him smilingly.

## \|\| 26.10-11 \|\|

*ādau dhyātvā guruṃ natvā saṃpūjya vidhipūrvakam \|*  
*paścāttadājñāmādāya dhyāyediṣṭaṃ prapūjayet \|\| 10 \|\|*

*gurupradarśito devo mantrapūjāvidhirjapaḥ \|*  
*na devena gururdṛṣṭastasmāddevādguruḥ paraḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. According to the routine, one should first bow before his teacher, offering him his salutation. Then he should be adored in a proper manner. And he should then start adoring the family-god with the permission of the teacher. The teacher only makes the correct form of the god to appear before you. He also reveals the mantras of the family god and also describes the form of adoration besides other details. Guru is well aware of the family god but the family god is not aware of the teacher and as such the teacher happens to be superior than the family god. чЭ

## \|\| 26.12 \|\|

*gururbrahmā gururviṣṇurgururdevo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*guruḥ prakṛtirīśādyā guruścandro'nalo raviḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. That is why it is stated that Brahma is the teacher, Vishnu is the teacher, lord Shiva is the teacher. Teacher has a divine nature beside the sun and the moon.

## \|\| 26.13 \|\|

*gururvāyuśca varuṇo gururmātā pitā suhṛt \|*  
*gurureva paraṃ brahma nāsti pūjyo guroḥ paraḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Teacher happens to be the wind-god, Varuna, the mother and the father, a friend as well the eternal Brahman. Therefore no one else is more adorable then the teacher.

## \|\| 26.14-16 \|\|

*abhīṣṭadeve ruṣṭe ca samartho rakṣaṇe guruḥ \|*  
*na samarthā gurau ruṣṭe rakṣaṇe sarvadevatāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*yasya tuṣṭo guruḥ śaśvajjayastasya pade pade \|*  
*yasya ruṣṭo gurustasya sarvanāśaśca sarvadā \|\| 15 \|\|*

*na saṃpūjya guruṃ devaṃ yo mūḍhaḥ pūjayed bhramāt \|*  
*brahmahatyāśataṃ pāpī labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

14-16. The teacher is in a ppsition to save one from the wrath of the enraged gods but when the teacher gets enraged all the gods combined cannot save him. The one who keeps the teacher pleased he meets with the success at every step and the one on whom the teacher get annoyed he has to face total destruction always. A food who adorned other gods in preference to the teachers that sinful person attains the sin of Brahmahatya. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 26.17 \|\|

*sāmavede ca bhagavānityuvāca hariḥ svayam \|*  
*tasmādabhīṣṭadevācca guruḥ pūjyatamaḥ paraḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. It has been recorded in the Samaveda by lord Vishnu himself. Similarly the teacher is more adorable than the gods.

## \|\| 26.18 \|\|

*gurumiṣṭaṃ svayaṃ dhyātvā stutvā vai sādhako mune \|*  
*nirmalaṃ sthalamāsādya viṇmūtraṃ hyutsṛjenmudā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Thus first adoring the Guru and then the family god, one should leave the place and free himself from the daily routine.

## \|\| 26.19-24 \|\|

*jalaṃ jalasamīpaṃ ca sarandhaṃ prāṇisannidhim \|*  
*devālayasamīpaṃ ca vṛkṣamūlaṃ ca vartma ca \|\| 19 \|\|*

*halotkarṣasthalaṃ caiva sasyakṣetraṃ ca goṣṭhakam \|*  
*nadīkandaragarbhaṃ ca puṣpodyānaṃ ca paṃkilam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*grāmādyabhyantaraṃ caiva nṛṇāṃ gṛhasamīpakam \|*  
*śaṅkuṃ setuṃ śaravaṇaṃ śmaśānaṃ vahnisannidhim \|\| 21 \|\|*

*krīḍāsthalaṃ mahāraṇyaṃ maṃcakādhaḥsthalaṃ tathā \|*  
*vṛkṣacchāyāyutaṃ sthānamantaḥprāṇyavaparṇakam \|\| 22 \|\|*

*dūrvāsthānaṃ kuśasthānaṃ valmīkasthānameva ca \|*  
*vṛkṣāropaṇabhūbhiṃ ca kāryārthaṃ ca pariṣkṛtam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*etatsarvaṃ parityajya sūryyatāpavivarjitam \|*  
*kṛtvā garttaṃ purīṣaṃ ca mūtraṃ ca parivarjayet \|\| 24 \|\|*

19-24. One should drop refuse or urine away from the abodes of the people, the land near the water or near the temple of the god or the root of a tree or the road, ploughed field, the land over which seeds are sown, the cow shed, the river, inside of a mountain cave, orchards, swamps, the land inside the villages the places surrounding the human habitation or those surrounding pillars or the poles, the bridges, forest of reeds, the cremation ground, the ground near fire, the thick forest, the hunter’s platform shed of a tree, the place under which the humans live or the place over which heaps of leaves are collected, abode of snakes, the ground over which the trees are planted or the place which has made neat and clean for a particular purpose should not be used for dropping the refuse or formation. Leaving all this one should use the place which is free from heat of the sun. A pit should be dug and used for dropping the refuse.

## \|\| 26.25 \|\|

*purīṣamūtrotsargaṃ ca divā kuryādudaṅmukhaḥ \|*  
*paścimābhimukho rātrau sandhyāyāṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. During the day time, one should free himself from refuse and urination facing north and during the night one should do so facing the west. During the evenings one should do so facing the south.

## \|\| 26.26-27 \|\|

*maunī bhūtvā ca niśvāsaṃ yathā gandho na sañcaret \|*  
*tyaktvā mṛdā samācchādya śaucaṃ kuryyādvicakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*kṛtvā tu loṣṭaśaucaṃ ca jalaśaucaṃ tataḥ param \|*  
*mṛdyuktaṃ tajjalaṃ caiva tatpramāṇaṃ niśāmaya \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. One should keep quiet while doing so and should not even breath heavily. First of all the spectrum should be cleaned with the peace of a brick or stone and thereafter it should be washed with water. The water stored in an earthen pitcher is used for washing after dropping the refuse. You listen to it attentively.

## \|\| 26.28 \|\|

*ekāṃ liṅge mṛdaṃ dadyādvāmahaste catuṣṭayam \|*  
*ubhayorhastayordve tu mūtraśaucaṃ prakīrtitam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. After easing one-self, the organ should be washed four times and the hand should be washed by applying the dust twice.

## \|\| 26.29-30 \|\|

*mūtraśaucaṃ dviguṇitaṃ maidhunānantaraṃ yadi \|*  
*maithunānantaraṃ yadvā mūtraśaucaṃ caturguṇam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*ekā liṅge gude tisrastathā vāmakare daśa \|*  
*ubhayoḥ sapta dātavyāḥ pādaḥ ṣaṣṭhena śudhyati \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. Similarly after co-habitation one should use double the amount of water and four times the dirt for purification. After easing, one should worth the lingas thrice with the left hands. He should then wash the hands. Both the hand should be washed seven times, and the feet should be washed applying the dust half a dozen time for purification. This is the method prescribed for the Brahmanas house-holder for purification, after relieving himself of the refuse.

## \|\| 26.31 \|\|

*purīṣaśaucaṃ viprāṇāṃ gṛhiṇāmidameva ca \|*  
*vidhavānāṃ dviguṇitaṃ śaucameva prakīrttitam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The method prescribed for the house-hold Brahmana, should be adopted by the widows and double the standards are provided for purification.

## \|\| 26.32-34 \|\|

*yatīnāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca brahmarṣerbrahmacāriṇām \|*  
*caturguṇaṃ ca gṛhiṇāṃ teṣāṃ śaucaṃ prakīrttitam \|\| 32 \|\|*

*no yāvadupanīyeta dvijaḥ śūdrastathā'ṅganā \|*  
*gandhalepakṣayakaraṃ teṣāṃ śaucaṃ prakīrttitam \|\| 33 \|\|*

*śaucaṃ kṣatraviśoścaiva dvijānāṃ gṛhiṇāṃ samam \|*  
*dviguṇaṃ vaiṣṇavādīnāṃ munīnāṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 34 \|\|*

32-34. The Yatis, the Vaishnavas, Brahmarsis and Brahmacarins can be purified by adopting the method four times prescribed for a house holder. Such of the Brahmanas who have not been initiated for the yajnopavlta, ceremony, the Stidras and the ladies can be purified with the quantity of water which the particular place should get clean. The self purification of Ks atriyas and Vaishyas can be achieved in the same way as for the house-holder Brahmana. The Vais navas and sages have to purify themselves by applying double the standard prescribed for the Brahmana.

## \|\| 26.35 \|\|

*nyūnādhikaṃ na kartavyaṃ śaucaṃ śuddhimabhīpsatā \|*  
*prāyaścittaṃ prayujyeta vihitātikrame kṛte \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The one who is purifying himself should strictly follow the standards because by violating the prescribed rites, one attracts the repentance.

## \|\| 26.36 \|\|

*śaucaṃ tanniyamaṃ mattaḥ sāvadhānaṃ niśāmaya \|*  
*mṛcchauce ca śucirvipro'pyaśuciśca vyatikrame \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. I have described the method of purification after dropping the refuse or urination. You listen to it attentively, because purifying himself with the dust Brahmana is purified and by violating the nonns, he remains impure.

## \|\| 26.37-40 \|\|

*valmīkamūṣikotkhātāṃ mṛdamantarjalāṃ tathā \|*  
*śaucāvaśiṣṭāṃ gehācca na dadyātkleśasambhavām \|\| 37 \|\|*

*antaḥprāṇyavaparṇāṃ ca halotkhātāṃ viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*kuśamūlotthitāṃ caiva dūrvāmūlotthitāṃ tathā \|\| 38 \|\|*

*aśvatthamūlānnītāṃ ca tathaiva śayanotthitām \|*  
*catuṣpathācca goṣṭhānāṃ goṣpadānāṃ tathaiva ca \|\| 39 \|\|*

*śasyasthalānāṃ kṣetrāṇāmudyānānāṃ mṛdaṃ tyajet \|*  
*snāto vā'pyathavā'snāto vipraḥ śaucena śudhyati \|\| 40 \|\|*

37-40. The earth of the ant-hill, the earth dug by the mice, the earth from the water, the earth which remains after purification and the earth of the wall of the house should not be used Tor purification. Besides the earth which is used for plastering is prohibited from being used for plastering is prohibited from being used for purification. The place were the people live, the place over which leaves of trees have fallen in heaps, the ploughed lands should not be used for a purification, the earth which is removed with the roots of Kusa and Durva grass, the roots of Plpala tree, the earth taken out of the pedestal used for sleeping, is not be used for cleaning after shedding refuse. The earth of crossings, the cowshed, earth of the field and the earth of the orchard is prohibited from use for purification. Whether the Brahmana has taken his bath or not by application of the above norms purification he gets purified.

## \|\| 26.41-42 \|\|

*śaucahīno'śucirnityamanarhaḥ sarvakarmasu \|*  
*kṛtvā śaucamidaṃ vipro sukhaṃ prakṣālayetsudhīḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*ādau ṣoḍaśagaṇḍūṣairmukhaśuddhiṃ vidhāya ca \|*  
*dantakāṣṭhena dantaṃ ca tatpaścātparimārjayet \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. The person who does not purify himself, remain impure always therefore he remains incapable of performing any religious deeds. The, intelligent Brahmana does purify himself with the washing of his face. He should rinse his mouth sixteen times. Thereafter he should rub his teach sixteen time with the brush of tree branch.

## \|\| 26.43-48 \|\|

*punaḥ ṣoḍaśagaṇḍūṣairmukhaśuddhiṃ samācaret \|*  
*dantamārjanakāṣṭhānāṃ niyamaṃ śṛṇu nārada \|\| 43 \|\|*

*nirūpitaṃ sāmavede hariṇā cāhnikakrame \|*  
*apāmārgaṃ sindhuvāramāmraṃ ca karavīrakam \|\| 44 \|\|*

*khadiraṃ ca śirīṣaṃ ca jātipunnāgaśālakam \|*  
*aśokamarjunaṃ caiva kṣīrivṛkṣaṃ kadambakam \|\| 45 \|\|*

*jambūkaṃ bakulaṃ tokmaṃ palāśaṃ ca praśastakam \|*  
*badarīṃ pāribhadraṃ ca mandāraṃ śālmaliṃ tathā \|\| 46 \|\|*

*vṛkṣaṃ kaṇṭakayuktaṃ ca latādi parivarjayet \|*  
*pippalaṃ ca priyālaṃ ca tinti ḍīkaṃ ca tālakam \|\| 47 \|\|*

*kharjūraṃ nārikelaṃ ca tālaṃ ca parivarjayet \|*  
*dantaśaucavihīnaśca sarvaśaucavihīnakaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

43-48. Thereafter he should rinse his mouth sixteen times. О Narada you listen to the method for brushing the mouth which has been prescribed by lord Vishnu in the Samaveda. The branches of apamarga (cicira), myodl, mango, karavira, (kanera), khaira, sirasa, jayaphala, nagakesara, sdklui (teak tree), asoka tree, arjuna (tenninelia arguna), gulara (ficus glomerata), kadamba (anthocephalus indicuo), jamuna (syoygium cumani), maulasirl (mimusops elengi), tokma (green hair of j'au) and palasa (butea frondasa) should not be used to brush the teeth. Similarly the branches of Ziziphus, Cadrus deodara, Asclepias gigcintea, cotton tree, trees with thorns, creepers, Ficus religiosa, Buchana nialatifolia Tamorind, Borassus jlabellifer (fan palin) grapes and coconaut should not be used for brushing the teeth. One who does not brush his teeth, is always considered to be impure.

## \|\| 26.49-50 \|\|

*śaucahīno'śuci rnityamanarhaḥ sarvakarmasu \|*  
*kṛtvā śaucaṃ śucirvipro dhṛtvā dhaute ca vāsasī \|\| 49 \|\|*

*prakṣālya pādāvācamya prātaḥsandhyāṃ samācaret \|*  
*evaṃ trisaṃdhyaṃ saṃdhyāṃ ca kurute kulajo dvijaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. A person who is devoid of purity, is incompetent to perform any job being impure. That is why a Brahmana after purifying himself would take his bath and should clad himself in washed clean garments. He should then perform Acamana (sipping of water) and should perform his adoration or sandhya. In this way the spotless Brahmana who performs his adoration thrice a day, he earns the merit of having a bath at all the sacred places.

## \|\| 26.51-58 \|\|

*sa snātaḥ sarvatīrtheṣu trisandhyaṃ yaḥ samācaret \|*  
*sandhyātritayahīnassyādanarhaḥ sarvakarmasu \|\| 51 \|\|*

*yadahnā kurute karma na tasya phalabhāgbhavet \|*  
*nopatiṣṭhati yaḥ pūrvāṃ nopāste yastu paścimām \|\| 52 \|\|*

*sa śūdravadbahiṣkāryyaḥ sarvasmāddvijakarmaṇaḥ \|*  
*pūrvāṃ sandhyāṃ parityajya madhyamāṃ paścimāṃ tathā \|\| 53\|\|*

*brahmahatyāmātmahatyāṃ pratyahaṃ labhate dvijaḥ \|*  
*ekadaśīvihīno yaḥ sandhyāhīnaśca yo dvijaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

*kalpaṃ vrajetkālasūtraṃ yathā hi vṛṣalīpatiḥ \|*  
*prātaḥsandhyāṃ vidhāyaivaṃ gurumiṣṭaṃ suraṃ ravim \|\| 55 \|\|*

*brahmāṇamīśaṃ viṣṇuṃ ca māyāṃ padmāṃ sarasvatīm \|*  
*praṇamya gurumājyaṃ ca darpaṇaṃ madhu kāñcanam \|\| 56 \|\|*

*spṛṣṭvā snānādikaṃ kāle kuryātsādhakasattamaḥ \|*  
*puṣkariṇyāṃ tu vāpyāṃ tu yadā snānaṃ samācaret \|\| 57 \|\|*

*samuddhṛtya pañcapiṇḍānādau dharmī vicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*nadyāṃ nade kandare vā tīrthe vā snānamācaret \|\| 58 \|\|*

51-58. Because a person who is deprived of the performing of the sandhya thrice cannot earn the merit of the good deed performed during the day. A person who does not perform the sandhya, thrice should be segregated from all and be treated like a Siidra. Because by not performing the sandhya during the morning, noon and evening one earns the sin of Brahmahatya and self-killing. Thus a person who does not perform the vow of Ekadashi is treated like the one who cohabited with a sinful Siidras woman and is thrown into the kalasutra hell for a kalpa. Thus performing the sandhya in the morning one should bow in reverence to his teacher and the family god, besides Surya, Brahma, Shiva, Vishnu, Goddess Lakshmi and Sarasvati. Thereafter touching his teacher, ghee, the mirror, honey and gold, he should take a bath appropriately. When he takes a bath in a step well or pond, he should take out five handfuls of the earth from the water and throw it away outside the water. One should take a bath in a river, stream, mountain-cave or the sacred place.

## \|\| 26.59 \|\|

*kuryyātsnātvā tu saṅkalpaṃ tataḥ snānaṃ punarmune \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇaprītikāmaśca vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahātmanām \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. О sage, after bath, one should first take a Samkalpa. Then he should take bath again. The Sarhkalpa of the Vaishnava ascetics is meant for lord Krishna.

## \|\| 26.60-63 \|\|

*saṅkalpo gṛhiṇāṃ caiva kṛtapātakanāśakaḥ \|*  
*vipraḥ kṛtvā tu saṅkalpaṃ mṛdaṃ gātre pralepayet \|\| 60 \|\|*

*vedoktamantreṇānena dehaśuddhikṛte naraḥ \|*  
*aśvakrānte rathakrāṃte viṣṇukrānte vasundhare \|\| 61 \|\|*

*mṛttike hara me pāpaṃ yanmayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam \|*  
*uddhṛtāsi varāheṇa kṛṣṇena śatabāhunā \|\| 62 \|\|*

*āruhya mama gātrāṇi sarvaṃ pāpaṃ pramocaya \|*  
*puṇyaṃ dehi mahābhāge snānānujñāṃ kuruṣva mām \|\| 63 \|\|*

60-63. And the Samkalpa of the house-holder is taken by them with the purpose of the destructions of the sin. The Brahmana after taking the Samkalpa should use the dust as prescribed in the Vedas and rub it over his body saying, О earth, goddess, you are trampled by the horses and the chariots. Lord Vsnu also trampled you with his feet. О dust, whatever sin have been committed by me, you relieve me of them all. Lord Krishna in the form of a boar, having hundreds of anns, rescued you from the deep waters, I apply you over my limbs and you should therefore relieve me of all the sings. You grant me all the merit and allow me to take a bath.”

## \|\| 26.64-65 \|\|

*ityuktvā ca jale nābhipramāṇe mantrapūrvakam \|*  
*caturhastapramāṇāṃ ca kṛtvā maṇḍalikā śubhām \|\| 64 \|\|*

*tīrthānyāvāhayettatra hastaṃ dattvā tapodhana \|*  
*yāni yāni ca tīrthāni sarvāṇi kathayāmi te \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. O sage, thus speaking he should enter the waters as deep as navel and the reciting the mantra he should create a circle four feet in width. He should then invoke the sacred places at that place. “I am now narrating the names of the sacred places.

## \|\| 26.66-71 \|\|

*gaṅge ca yamune caiva godāvari sarasvati \|*  
*narmade sindhu kāveri jale'sminsannidhiṃ kuru \|\| 66 \|\|*

*nalinī nandinī sītā mālinī ca mahāpagā \|*  
*viṣṇupādābjasambhūtā gaṅgā tripathagāminī \|\| 67 \|\|*

*padmāvatī bhogavatī svarṇarekhā ca kauśikī \|*  
*dakṣā pṛthvī ca subhagā viśvakāyā śivāmṛtā \|\| 68 \|\|*

*vidyādharī suprasannā tathā lokaprasādhinī \|*  
*kṣemā ca vaiṣṇavī śāntā śāntidā gomatī satī \|\| 69 \|\|*

*sāvitrī tulasī durgā mahālakṣmīḥ sarasvatī \|*  
*kṛṣṇaprāṇādhikā rādhā lopāmudrā ditīratiḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*ahalyā cāditiḥ saṃjñā svadhā svāhā'pyarundhatī \|*  
*śatarūpā devahūtirityādyāḥ saṃsmaretsudhīḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

66-71. All the rivers including Ganga, the Yamuna, Godavari, Sarasvati, Narmada, Sindhu, Kaveri, should come and reside at this place.” Thereafter he should recite the names of Nalini, Nandini, Sita, MahanadI, Malini and the Ganga which emerged from the lotus-like feet of Vishnu, Padmavati, Bhogavati, Svamarekha, KausikI, Daksha, PrthivI, Subhaga, Vishvakaya, Shivamrta, Suprasanna, Vidyadharl, Lokaprasadhini, Ks ema, Vaishnavl, Santa, Santida, Gomatl, Sati, Savitri, Tulasi, Durga, Maha-Lakshmi, Sarasvati, Radhika the beloved of Krishna, Lopamudra, Diti, Rati, Ahalya, Aditi, Saiijna, Svaha, Svadha, Arundhatl, Satarupa and DevadQti and all other gods.

## \|\| 26.72 \|\|

*snātvā snātvā mahāpūtaḥ kuryyāttu tilaka budhaḥ \|*  
*bāhvormūle lalāṭe ca kaṇṭhadeśe ca vakṣasi \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Getting purified, by taking a bath, the intellectual should plaster his arms, the forehead, the neck and the chest with sandal-paste.

## \|\| 26.73 \|\|

*snānaṃ dānaṃ tapo homo devatāpitṛkarma ca \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ yāti lalā ṭe tilakaṃ vinā \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. Because without applying the sandal-paste after taking bath the charity perfonned beside the tap as, homa, adoration of gods and the manes becomes in fructuous.

## \|\| 26.74 \|\|

*brāhmaṇastilakaṃ kṛtvā kuryyātsandhyāṃ ca tarpaṇam \|*  
*namaskṛtya surānbhaktyā gṛhaṃ gacchenmudā'nvitaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Applying tilakam over the forehead, Brahmana should perfonn sandhya and tarpana. Therefore he should adore the gods with utmost devotion with pleasures.

## \|\| 26.75 \|\|

*prakṣālya pādau yatnena dhṛtvā dhaute ca vāsasī \|*  
*mandiraṃ praviśetprājña ityāha harireva ca \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. He should wash his feet pretty well and should clad himself in washed and neat clothes. Thereafter the wise person should visit the temples. This has been ordained by Hari.

## \|\| 26.76 \|\|

*vinā pādakṣālanaṃ yaḥ snātvā viśati mandiram \|*  
*tasya snānādikaṃ naṣṭaṃ japahomādipañcakam \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. The one who, after taking his bath enters the temple without washing his feet, all his actions in taking bath, tapas, performing of homa become infructuous.

## \|\| 26.77 \|\|

*paridhāya snigdhavastraṃ gṛhaṃ ca praviśedgṛhī \|*  
*ruṣṭā lakṣmīrgṛhā dyāti śāpaṃ dattvā sudāruṇam \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. The one who enters the temple with wet clothes or with the clothed soaked in oil enters the house, the goddess Lakshmi gets annoyed pronouncing a terrific curse on him from his mouth.

## \|\| 26.78 \|\|

*jaṅghordhvataśca yo vipraḥ pādau prakṣālayedyadā \|*  
*tāvadbhavati cāṇḍālo yāvadgaṅgāṃ na paśyati \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. The Brahmana who while washing his feet also washes thighs he become a Chandala. And remains in that position till he sights the Ganga.

## \|\| 26.79 \|\|

*upaviśyāsane brahmañchucirācamya sādhakaḥ \|*  
*pūjāṃ kuryyāttu vedoktā bhaktiyukto hi saṃyataḥ \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. О Brahmana the purified truth seeker should sip Acamana seated over the Asana. Thereafter he should adore his family god according to the Vedic rites with devotion.

## \|\| 26.80 \|\|

*śālagrāme maṇau mantre pratimāyāṃ jale sthale \|*  
*gopṛṣṭhe vā gurau vipre praśastamarcanaṃ hareḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. The adoration of Shalagrama, the gem, a mantra, the image, water, the earth, the back of the cows, the teacher and the Brahmanas are considered to be quite adorable.

## \|\| 26.81 \|\|

*sarveṣu śastā pūjā ca śālagrāme ca nārada \|*  
*surāṇāmeva sarveṣāṃ yatrādhiṣṭhānameva ca \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. But, О Narada, the best type of adoration of the lord is to adore him in the form of Shalagrama because all the gods stand enshrined in the same.

## \|\| 26.82 \|\|

*sa snātaḥ sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ \|*  
*śālagrāmodakenaiva yo'bhiṣekaṃ samācaret \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. Therefore the one who adores Shalagrama with water, he achieves the merit of having a bath in all the sacred places and performing of all the yajna.

## \|\| 26.83 \|\|

*śālagrāmajalaṃ bhaktyā nityamaśnāti yo naraḥ \|*  
*jīvanmuktaḥ sa ca bhavedyātyante kṛṣṇamandiram \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. The one who sips the water with which Shalagrama is washed, he is liberated from the human bondage and he ultimately reaches the abode of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 26.84 \|\|

*śālagrāmaśilācakraṃ yatra tiṣṭhati nārada \|*  
*sacakro bhagavāṃstatra sarvatīrthāni niścitam \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. O Narada wherever the stone of Shalagrama is kept, the place is occupied by the lord holding a cakra in his hand.

## \|\| 26.85 \|\|

*tatra yo hi mṛto dehī jñānājñānena daivataḥ \|*  
*ratnanirmitayānena sa yāti śrīhareḥ padam \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Therefore the person who meets the end of his life at that particular place knowingly and unknowingly, he straight away proceeds to the abode of lord Vishnu.

## \|\| 26.86 \|\|

*śālagrāmaṃ vinā'nyatra kaḥ sādhuḥ pūjayeddharim \|*  
*kṛtvā tatra hareḥ pūjāṃ paripūrṇaṃ phalaṃ labhet \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. Which one of the noble ascetics, would prefer to adore any other god discarding Shalagramal Because by adoring the gods one gets full satisfaction.

## \|\| 26.87 \|\|

*pūjādhāraśca kathitaḥ śrūyatāṃ pūjanakramaḥ \|*  
*hareḥ pūjāṃ bahumatāṃ kathayāmi yathāgamam \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. I have thus explained the dimensions of the pdjd of the lord, now you listen from me about the perfonning of other deeds concerning the adoration of the lord.

## \|\| 26.88 \|\|

*kaściddadāti haraye copacārāṃśca ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*sundarāṇi pavitrāṇi nityaṃ bhaktyā ca vaiṣṇavaḥ \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. Some of the Vaishnava devotees offers sixteen beautiful presents to the lord.

## \|\| 26.89 \|\|

*kaściddvādaśa vastūni pañca vastūni kaścana \|*  
*yeṣāmeva yathā śaktirbhaktirmūlaṃ ca pūjane \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. Similarly some of the people offer twelve things as presents while others offer only five. But the people should adore the lord according to their competence. The utmost devotion towards the lord is the basis of adoration.

## \|\| 26.90 \|\|

*āsanaṃ vasanaṃ pādyamarghyamācamanīyakam \|*  
*puṣpaṃ candanadhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ naivedyamuttamam \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. The sixteen types of presents can be made to the lord which include a seat, the clothes, the padya, arghya sipping of water, flowers, sandal-wood paste, burning of lamps, the best of food, fragrance, flower garland, beautiful bed, water and betels.

## \|\| 26.91-95 \|\|

*gandhaṃ mālyaṃ ca śayyāṃ ca lalitāṃ suvilakṣaṇām \|*  
*jalamannaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ sādhāraṃ deyameva ca \|\| 91 \|\|*

*gandhānnatalpatāmbūlaṃ vinā dravyāṇi dvādaśa \|*  
*pādyārghyajalanaivedyapuṣpāṇyetāni paṃca ca \|\| 92 \|\|*

*sarvāṇyetāni mūlena dadyātsādhakasattamaḥ \|*  
*gurūpadiṣṭaṃ mūlaṃ ca praśastaṃ sarvakarmasu \|\| 93 \|\|*

*ādau kṛtvā bhūtaśuddhiṃ prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ param \|*  
*aṅgapratyaṅgayornyāsaṃ mantranyāsaṃ tataḥ param \|\| 94 \|\|*

*varṇanyāsaṃ vinirvartya cārghyapātraṃ vinirdiśet \|*  
*trikoṇamaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā tatra kūrmaṃ prapūjayet \|\| 95 \|\|*

91-95. Accept the fragrance, the food, the bed and the betel, the rest of the articles are considered as of formal usages. The padhya, arghya, water, food and the flower are the five types of offerings. The best of the truth seeker should offer all these things reciting the mantras. The mantra given by the teacher is considered to be the best of all. After purification one should performed Pranayama first of all. Thereafter, he should perform the rites of touching the limbs and other routines. First of all, one should prepare a triangular mandala, establishing a tortoise in it.

## \|\| 26.96 \|\|

*jalenāpūryya śaṅkhaṃ ca tatra saṃsthāpayeddvijaḥ \|*  
*jalaṃ saṃpūjya vidhivattīrthānyāvāhayettattaḥ \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. Thereafter a conch filled with water could be placed there and the water of all sacred places should be invoked.

## \|\| 26.97-98 \|\|

*pūjopakaraṇaṃ tena jalena kṣālayetpunaḥ \|*  
*tato gṛhītvā puṣpaṃ ca kṛtvā yogāsanaṃ śuciḥ \|\| 97 \|\|*

*dhyānena gurudattena dhyāyetkṛṣṇamananyadhīḥ \|*  
*dhyātvā pādyādikaṃ sarvaṃ dadyānmūlena sādhakaḥ \|\| 98 \|\|*

97-98. He should thereafter wash all the articles of the рща with the same water. Thereafter, the Sadhaka should be seated in Yogasana holding the flowers. He should then meditate upon lord Krishna according to the guide-lines provided to him by his teacher. Thereafter the Sadhaka, reciting the basic mantra should give his offerings.

## \|\| 26.99 \|\|

*aṅga pratyaṅgadevaṃ ca tantroktaṃ pūjayeddharim \|*  
*mūlaṃ japtvā yathāśakti devamantraṃ visarjayet \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. Thus according to the tantras and their appendices, one should adore lord Vishnu. The basic mantra should be recited according to the strength of a person offering the mantra to his family god.

## \|\| 26.100 \|\|

*dattvopahāraṃ vividhaṃ stutvā ca kavacaṃ paṭhet \|*  
*tataḥ kṛtvā parīhāraṃ mūrdhnā ca praṇamedbhuvi \|\| 100 \|\|*

100\. Thereafter he should make several types of offerings reciting the prayers and kavachas. He should then send them off after bowing before them.

## \|\| 26.101 \|\|

*kṛtvā vai devapūjāṃ ca yajñaṃ kuryyādvicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*śrautasmārttāgniyuktaṃ ca baliṃ dadyāttato mune \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. After perfonning worship of gods, a wise person should perfonn yajna appropriately. О sage, after the yajna the offering should be made to the Dikpalas.

## \|\| 26.102 \|\|

*nityaśrāddhaṃ yathāśakti dānaṃ vittānurūpakam \|*  
*kṛtvā kṛtī sa viharetkrama eṣa śrutau śrutaḥ \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. Thereafter one should regularly perfonn srciddha and gift away in charity, the riches according to his capacity; after doing all this, one should get himself engaged into his daily business.

## \|\| 26.103 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ vedoktaṃ sūtramuttamam \|*  
*āhnikasya ca viprāṇāṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi \|\| 103 \|\|*

103\. Thus I have spoken to you about the daily routine of the Brahmanas. What more do you want to listen from me.

## Chapter 27

## \|\| 27.1-2 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*bhakṣyaṃ kiṃ vā'pyabhakṣyaṃ ca dvijānāṃ gṛhiṇāṃ prabho \|*  
*yatīnāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca vidhavābrahmacāriṇām \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kiṃ karttavyamakarttavyamabhogyaṃ bhogyameva vā \|*  
*sarvaṃ kathaya sarvajña sarveśaḥ sarvakāraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Narada said — О lord tell me what is eatable and what is non-eatable for Brahmanas house-holders, Yati, Vaishnava, widows and a Brahmacarl. You also tell me all their duties and the deeds from which they should reframe. The things which they should enjoy and should avoid, because you,, are well aware of all these details being lord of the universe.

## \|\| 27.3 \|\|

*maheśvara uvāca \|*  
*kaścittapasvī vipraśca nirāhārī ciraṃ muniḥ \|*  
*kaścitsamīraṇāhārī phalāhārī ca kaścana \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Maheshvara said — Some of the ascetic Brahmanas remain without food, some of them live only by in-hailing the air, some of them live on the fruits and roots.

## \|\| 27.4 \|\|

*annāhārī yathā kāle gṛhī ca gṛhiṇīyutaḥ \|*  
*yeṣāmicchā ca yā brahmanrucīnāṃ vividhā gatiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О Brahmana the house-holders appropriately consumes foods with their wives. Similarly everyone does according to his liking because everyone has different taste and different liking.

## \|\| 27.5 \|\|

*haviṣyānnaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ praśastaṃ gṛhiṇāṃ sadā \|*  
*nārāyaṇocchiṣṭamiṣṭamabhakṣyamaniveditam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. But for a Brahmana house-holder the taking of the food available after the performing of homa is prescribed, is the best. The left over of Narayana are consumed by them as a gifts. Only that food can be described as non-eatables which was not been initially offered to lord.

## \|\| 27.6 \|\|

*annaṃ viṣṭhā jalaṃ mūtraṃ yadviṣṇoraniveditam \|*  
*viṇmūtraṃ sarvathā proktamannaṃ ca harivāsare \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Because the food which is not offered to lord Vishnu can be treated as refuse and the urine. Thus on the eleventh day of the fort-night, the food and the water is discarded.

## \|\| 27.7 \|\|

*brāhmaṇaḥ kāmato'nnaṃ ca yo bhuṅkte harivāsare \|*  
*trailokyajanitaṃ pāpaṃ so'pi bhuṅkte na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Therefore a Brahmana who willingly or at the instance of someone else takes food actually consumes the sins. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 27.8 \|\|

*na bhoktavyaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ ca nārada \|*  
*gṛhibhirbrāhmaṇairannaṃ saṃprāpte harivāsare \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. О Narada that is why, the house-holder Brahmanas are prohibited from taking any food on the eleventh day of the fortnight.

## \|\| 27.9 \|\|

*gṛhī śaivaśca śāktaśca brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ \|*  
*prayāti kālasūtraṃ ca bhuktvā ca harivāsare \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On the day of Hari, the house-holder Brahmana whether he believes in Shiva are sakti, if he eats the food he falls in the hell called kalasiWa.

## \|\| 27.10-11 \|\|

*kṛmibhiḥ śālimānaiśca bhakṣitastatra tiṣṭhati \|*  
*viṇmūtrabhojanaṃ kṛtvā yāvadindrāścaturdaśa \|\| 10 \|\|*

*janmāṣṭamīdine rāmanavamīdivase hareḥ \|*  
*śivarātrau ca yo bhuṅkte so'pi dviguṇapātakī \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. The same food is turned into the insects and they eat up his body bit by bit. Therefore that person consuming refuse and urine spends the time in hell for the longevity of the age of fourteen Indras. Similarly the person who consumes food on Janmastml, RamanavamT and Shivaratrl, meets with the same fate.

## \|\| 27.12 \|\|

*upavāsāsamarthaśca phalaṃ mūlaṃ jalaṃ pibet \|*  
*naṣṭe śarīre sa bhavedanyathā cātmaghātakaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. If one is unable to maintain the fast he should consume fruits and roots besides the water; otherwise if the body is destroyed the human invites the sin of self-killing.

## \|\| 27.13 \|\|

*sakṛdbhuṅkte haviṣyānnaṃ viṣṇornaivedyameva ca \|*  
*na bhavetpratyavāyī sa copavāsaphalaṃ labhet \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The one who consumes the food of the homa, offering the same to lord Vishnu, he does not attract any sin and on the other hand, he earns the merit of performing the fast.

## \|\| 27.14 \|\|

*ekādaśyāmanāhāraṃ gṛhī vipraśca bhārate \|*  
*sa ca tiṣṭhati vaikuṇṭhe yāvadvai brahmaṇo vayaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Therefore, the house-holder Brahmanas perform the Ekadashi-vrata while fasting in the Bharatvarsa, as a result of which they enjoy the Vaikuntha up to the life of Brahma.

## \|\| 27.15 \|\|

*gṛhiṇāṃ śaivaśāktānāmidamuktaṃ ca nārada \|*  
*viśeṣato vaiṣṇavānāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The same process has been prescribed for the house-holder, whether he believes in Shiva, sakti or is a Vaishnava or is a Yati and Brahmacarl.

## \|\| 27.16 \|\|

*nityaṃ naivedyabhojo yaḥ śrīviṣṇossa hi vaiṣṇavaḥ \|*  
*nityaṃ śatopavāsānāṃ jīvanmuktaphalaṃ bhavet \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The one who consumes the food after offering it to Vishnu he is considered to be a Vaishnava and earns the merit of performing hundred fasts and achieves ultimately the salvation.

## \|\| 27.17 \|\|

*vāñchanti tasya saṃsparśaṃ tīrthānyakhiladevatāḥ \|*  
*ālāpaṃ darśanaṃ caiva sarvapāpapraṇāśanam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. All the gods and the sacred places get desirous of meeting, touching and talking with him.

## \|\| 27.18 \|\|

*dviḥ svinnamannaṃ pṛthukaṃ śuddhaṃ deśaviśeṣake \|*  
*nātyantaśastaṃ viprāṇāṃ bhakṣaṇe na nivedane \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The food which is cooked twice or the Ciura (half fried rice) which is considered to be pure in some regions are not considered quite appropriate for offering to the lord and for the consumption of the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 27.19 \|\|

*abhakṣyaṃ vai yatīnāṃ ca vidhavābrahmacāriṇām \|*  
*tāmbūlaṃ ca yathā brahmaṃstathaitadvastu na dhruvam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. О Brahmana, a recluse, widow and Brahmacarls are prohibited from consuming the betels.

## \|\| 27.20 \|\|

*tāmbūlaṃ vidhavāstrīṇāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām \|*  
*tapasvināṃ ca viprendra gomāṃsasadṛśaṃ dhruvam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. О best of the Brahmanas, the widow, the Yati, Brahmacarls and Tapasvls are strictly prohibiting from consuming the betels.

## \|\| 27.21 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ yadabhakṣyaṃ śṛṇu nārada \|*  
*yaduktaṃ sāmavede ca hariṇā cāhnikakrame \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. О Narada, the things which are prohibited for consumption of the Brahmanas as ordained in the Samaveda by lord Hari, are now going to be described by me, you therefore listen to it.

## \|\| 27.22 \|\|

*tāmrapātre payaḥpānamucchiṣṭe ghṛtabhojanam \|*  
*dugdhaṃ lavaṇasārddhaṃ ca sadyo gomāṃsabhakṣaṇam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. One should not consume milk in a copper vase, one should not consume ghee which is left over and the milk with salt. All these tantamount to consuming the flesh of the cow.

## \|\| 27.23-24 \|\|

*nārikelodakaṃ kāṃsye tāmrapātre sthitaṃ laghu \|*  
*aikṣavaṃ tāmrapātrasthaṃ surātulyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*utthāya vāmahastena yastoyaṃ pibati dvijaḥ \|*  
*surāpī ca sa vijñeyaḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. The coconut water should not be taken in the copper vase or the vase of a copper alloy bronze. If one takes honey or the juice of sugarcane in a copper vase, it turns into wine, there is no doubt about it. A Brahmana, who drinks water with his left hand, is treated like a drunkard and he should be discarded for all the religious rites.

## \|\| 27.25 \|\|

*anivedyaṃ harerannaṃ bhuktaśeṣaṃ ca nityaśaḥ \|*  
*pītaśeṣajalaṃ caiva gomāṃsasadṛśaṃ mune \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. О sage, if one takes food without first offering to lord Vishnu and the food or water that is left over, such food or water Is prohibited from consumption.

## \|\| 27.26-27 \|\|

*vāniṅgaṇaphalaṃ caiva gomāṃsaṃ kārttike smṛtam \|*  
*māghe ca mūlakaṃ caiva kalambī śayane tathā \|\| 26 \|\|*

*śvetavarṇaṃ ca tālaṃ ca masūraṃ matsyameva ca \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca tyājyaṃ sarvatra deśake \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Similarly in the month of Kartika, the fruit of egg-plant, the radish in the month of Magha and in the four rainy months, the cabbage1 should not be taken. The white palm tree, the small grained pulses, the fish of any country is prohibited and should not be taken by a Brahmana. If a Brahmana willingly consumes the fish, he will have to fast for three days and thereafter perform the repentance. Only after that, he gets purified.

## \|\| 27.28 \|\|

*matsyāṃśca kāmato bhuktvā sopavāsastryahaṃ vaset \|*  
*prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śuddhimāpnoti vāḍavaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The consumption of Kusamanda is prohibited on the first day of moon, because it results into the loss of riches. On the second day of moon, the forest fruit is prohibited and if one does so, he should recite the name of Vishnu. On the third day of the moon, cucumber should not be taken and in case one does so, his so, the enemies are increased. Therefore it should not be eaten on that particular day. On the fourth day of the moon the radish should not be taken which results into the lose of money. By consuming the wood-apples on the fifth day of the moon one earns blemish. If one consumes Nun a on the sixth day of the moon, he is reborn as a bird.

## \|\| 27.29-31 \|\|

*pratipatsu ca kūṣmāṇḍamabhakṣyaṃ hyarthanāśanam \|*  
*dvitīyāyāṃ ca bṛhatībhojanena smareddharim \|\| 29 \|\|*

*abhakṣyaṃ ca paṭolaṃ ca śatruvṛddhikaraṃ param \|*  
*tṛtīyāyāṃ caturthyāṃ ca mūlakaṃ dhananāśanam \|\| 30 \|\|*

*kalaṅkakāraṇaṃ caiva paṃcamyāṃ bilvabhakṣaṇam \|*  
*tiryyagyoniṃ prāpayettu ṣaṣṭhyāṃ vai nimbabhakṣaṇam \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. On the seventh day of the moon if one consumes date-palm he attracts ailments, it also is injurious for the body.

## \|\| 27.32 \|\|

*rogavṛddhikaraṃ caiva narāṇāṃ tālabhakṣaṇam \|*  
*saptamyāṃ ca tathā tālaṃ śarīrasya ca nāśakam \|\| 32 \|\|*
