32\. 1. Costus speciosus.

## \|\| 27.33 \|\|

*nārikelaphalaṃ bhakṣyamaṣṭamyāṃ buddhināśanam \|*  
*tumbī navamyāṃ gomāṃsaṃ daśamyāṃ ca kalambikā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. On the eighth day of the moon the wisdom is lost by consuming the coconut. If the guard is consumed on the ninth day of fortnight it is sinful and the vegetable of Kalambl prohibited to be taken on the tenth day of the fortnight.

## \|\| 27.34 \|\|

*ekādaśyāṃ tathā śimbī dvādaśyāṃ pūtikā tathā \|*  
*trayodaśyāṃ ca vārttākī na bhakṣyā putranāśanam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The beans are prohibited to be taken on the eleventh day of the fortnight and the fried rice on the twelve day of the fortnight. The fruit of egg-plant is prohibited for the thirteenth day of the fortnight which might result in the loss of the sons.

## \|\| 27.35 \|\|

*caturdaśyāṃ māṣabhakṣyaṃ mahāpāpakaraṃ param \|*  
*pañcadaśyāṃ tathā māṃsamabhakṣyaṃ gṛhiṇāṃ mune \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. О sage the black-grams are prohibited to be taken for fourteenth day of the fortnight. On the moonless dark night the consumption of meat is prohibited for the house-holder.

## \|\| 27.36-38 \|\|

*gṛhiṇāṃ prokṣitaṃ māṃsaṃ bhakṣyamanyadineṣu ca \|*  
*prātaḥ snāne tathā śrāddhe pārvaṇe vratavāsare \|\| 36 \|\|*

*praśastaṃ sārṣapaṃ tailaṃ pakvatailaṃ ca nārada \|*  
*kuhūpūrṇendusaṃkrāṃticaturdaśyaṣṭamīṣu ca \|\| 37 \|\|*

*ravau śrāddhe vratāhe ca duṣṭaṃ strītilatailakam \|*  
*māṃsaṃ ca raktaśākaṃ ca kāṃsyapātre ca bhojanam \|\| 38 \|\|*

36-38. For the rest of the days a house-holder is allowed to take meat available from the yajha. О Narada one should get up early in the morning, take a bath and on the day of performing the vrata and srnddha the use of sea-same oil or fried oil has been considered to be useful. On the moonless day, full moon day, first day of the month, fourteen day of the fortnight, eighth day of the fortnight Sundays and the days on which sraddha are performed, the co-habitation with the wife is strictly prohibited. Similarly on these days the consumption of meat, vegetables of red colour and the consumption of food in the copper alloy or bronze is strictly prohibited.

## \|\| 27.39-40 \|\|

*niṣiddhaṃ śayanaṃ caiva kūrmamāṃsaṃ ca mantritam \|*  
*niṣiddhaṃ sarvavarṇānāṃ divā svastrīniṣevaṇam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*rātrau ca dadhi bhakṣyaṃ ca śayanaṃ sandhyayordine \|*  
*rajasvalāstrīgamanametannarakakāraṇam \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. Sleeping in the day is prohibited for all the people of the four varnas. Besides by eating of the meat of the tortoise and enjoying of the conjugal pleasures with wife, one vies hell. By consuming curd during the night, sleeping in the morning and evening and enjoying the company of a lady in the period one attracts sin.

## \|\| 27.41-43 \|\|

*udakyavīrayorannaṃ puṃścalyannamabhakṣakam \|*  
*śūdrānnaṃ yājakānnaṃ ca śūdraśrāddhānnameva ca \|\| 41 \|\|*

*abhakṣyānnaṃ ca viprarṣe yadannaṃ vṛṣalīpateḥ \|*  
*brahmanvārdhuṣikānnaṃ ca gaṇakānnamabhakṣakam \|\| 42 \|\|*

*agradānidvijānnaṃ ca cikitsākārakasya ca \|*  
*hastacitrāharau tailamagrāhyaṃ cāpyabhakṣakam \|\| 43 \|\|*

41-43. The food cooked by a woman in period, a wicked woman, a Siidras woman and the food offered by a priest and the food offered by a Shudra is strictly prohibited from consumption. О best of the Brahmana, the food of the Shudra is prohibited from consumption in the Hasta and Chitra constellations. Oil should not be applied on the body or consumed as such days. The food of the Brahmana and of Vaishya should not be taken.

## \|\| 27.44-47 \|\|

*mūle mṛge bhādrapade māṃsaṃ gomāṃsatulyakam \|*  
*maghāyāṃ kṛttikāyāṃ vai cottarāsu ca nārad \|\| 44 \|\|*

*karoti maithunaṃ yo hi kumbhīpākaṃ sa ca vrajet \|*  
*rohiṇyāṃ ca viśākhāyāṃ yaitra caivoktarāsu ca \|*  
*amāyāṃ kṛttikāyāṃ ca dvijaiḥ kṣauraṃ vivarjitam \|\| 45 \|\|*

*kṛtvā tu maithunaṃ kṣauraṃ yo devāṃstarpayetpitṝn \|*  
*rudhiraṃ tadbhavettoyaṃ dātā ca narakaṃ vrajet \|\| 46 \|\|*

*yatkarttavyamakarttavyaṃ yadbhojyaṃ yadabhojyakam \|*  
*sarvaṃ tubhyaṃ nigaditaṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi \|\| 47 \|\|*

44-47. In the Mula and Mrgasira constellations, in the month of Bhadrapada, the consumption of rneat is strictly prohibited. О Narada the one who соhabits in the Krttika and Uttara constellations, he falls in the terrific hell. The shaving of the head in the Rohini, Vishakha, Anuradha, Uttratrya and Krttika constellations besides on the moonless day, is strictly prohibited. The one who performs tarpana for the gods and the manes, the water so offered by him turns like blood and he ultimately achieves the hell; О Narada I have explained to you the deeds which are to be performed and those which are prohibited, for a householder. I have also told you the eatables and those which are not to be eaten. “Now what more do you want listen from me”?

## Chapter 28

## \|\| 28.1-5 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*śrutaṃ sarvaṃ jagannātha tvatprasādājjagadguro \|*  
*bhavānbrahmasvarūpaṃ ca vada brahmanirūpaṇam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*prabho kiṃ brahma sākāraṃ kiṃ nirākāramīśvara \|*  
*kiṃ tadviśeṣaṇaṃ kiṃ vā'pyaviśeṣaṇameva ca \|\| 2 \|\|*

*kiṃ vā dṛśyamadṛśyaṃ vā liptaṃ dehiṣu kiṃ na vā \|*  
*kiṃ vā tallakṣaṇaṃ śāstre vede vā kiṃ nirūpitam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*brahmātiriktā prakṛtiḥ kiṃ vā brahmasvarūpiṇī \|*  
*prakṛterlakṣaṇaṃ kiṃ vā sārabhūtaṃ śrutau śrutam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*prādhānyaṃ tasya sṛṣṭau ca dvayormadhye varaṃ param \|*  
*vicāryya manasā sarvaṃ sarvajña vada māṃ dhruvam \|\| 5 \|\|*

1-5. Narada said — О lord of the universe and teacher of the universe, by your grace I have been enlightened about everything. Now you kindly enlighten me on the fonn of Brahman and Brahmatattva. “O lord, tell me, whether Brahman is visible or invisible”? Are there any adjectives for him? Or is he without adjective? Can be remain invisible or out of sight? Does he reside in the human bodies or not? What are his features described in the Vedas and other scriptures? Does nature form part of Brahman or it has a separate from? What is the essence of the nature in the Vedas? Who dominates in the universe? Who is the better of the two? You are all knowledgeable and as such you kindly give a deep thought in your mind over the subject and enlighten me about the truth.”

## \|\| 28.6 \|\|

*nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā pañcavaktraḥ prahasya ca \|*  
*bhagavānkartumārebhe parabrahmanirūpaṇam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. On hearing the words of Narada the five faced Shiva smiled for a movement and then started narrating the form of Brahman to Narada.

## \|\| 28.7 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*yadyatpṛṣṭaṃ tvayā vatsa nigūḍhaṃ jñānamuttamam \|*  
*sudurlabhaṃ ca vedeṣu purāṇeṣu ca nārada \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Mahadeva said — О son Narada, you have enquired from me about the best of the knowledge which is difficult to get even from the Vedas and the Puranas.

## \|\| 28.8-9 \|\|

*ahaṃ brahmā ca viṣṇuśca śeṣo dharmo mahānvirāṭ \|*  
*sarvaṃ nirūpitaṃ brahmannasmābhiḥ śrutibhirmune \|\| 8 \|\|*

*yadviśeṣaṇayuktaṃ ca dṛśyaṃ pratyakṣameva ca \|*  
*tannirūpitamasmābhirvede vedavidāṃvara \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. O sage, the shrutis and myself, have describe about Shiva, Brahma, Vishnu, Shesha, Dharma and the great universal form. О Narada you are the best of those who are well-versed in the Vedas, the element which is apparently visible has been described in the Vedas.

## \|\| 28.10-11 \|\|

*vaikuṇṭhe ca purā pṛṣṭe dharmeṇa brahmaṇā tadā \|*  
*yaduvāca hariḥ kiñcinnibodha kathayāmi te \|\| 10 \|\|*

*sārabhūtaṃ ca tattvānāmajñānāndhakalocanam \|*  
*dvaidhabhramatamodhvaṃsa suprakṛṣṭapradīpakam \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. I am going to tell you now, whatever was spoken to us by lord Vishnu in Vaikuntha, when myself, Brahma and Dharma questioned him similarly on the subject. You listen to me. The gist of those tattvas is like the eyes for blind and remover of the darkness of illusion and jealously because it illumines.

## \|\| 28.12 \|\|

*paramātmasvarūpaṃ ca paraṃ brahma sanātanam \|*  
*sarvadehasthitaṃ sākṣisvarūpaṃ dehikarmaṇām \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The eternal Brahman is like the form of lord which resides in all the bodies and remains witness to the deeds of all.

## \|\| 28.13 \|\|

*prāṇāḥ pañca svayaṃ viṣṇurmano brahmā prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*sarvajñānasvarūpo'haṃ śaktiḥ prakṛtirīśvarī \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The five Pranas of the universe are Vishnu, the mind is Prajapati Brahma and the nature is the salcti for all of us.

## \|\| 28.14 \|\|

*ātmādhīnā vayaṃ sarve sthite tasminvayaṃ sthitāḥ \|*  
*gate gatāśca parame naradevamivānugāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Like the courtiers of the king, we are all subordinate to the soul till such time he resides in our body we remain active and the moment the great Brahman leaves our body, the life also disappears.

## \|\| 28.15-16 \|\|

*jīvastatpratibimbaṃ ca sarvabhogī hi karmaṇām \|*  
*yathā'rkacandrayo rbimbaṃ jalapūrṇaghaṭeṣu ca \|\| 15 \|\|*

*bimbaṃ ghaṭeṣu bhagneṣu pralīnaṃ candrasūryyayoḥ \|*  
*tathā layaprasaṃge sa jīvo brahmaṇi līyate \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. The soul happens to be the reflection of the eternal soul and has to reap the harvest of its deeds. As the reflection of sun and moon can be seen in a pitcher filled with water and after the pitcher is broken the reflection of sun and moon disappears. Similarly at the time of dissolution, the lord enters the great soul.

## \|\| 28.17 \|\|

*ekameva paraṃ brahma śeṣe vatsa bhavakṣaye \|*  
*vayaṃ pralīnāstatraiva jagadetaccarācaram \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. О son, at the time of dissolution and the destruction of the universe, only the Brahman remains and the entire universe beside the moveable and immovable elements of the universe also merge in it.

## \|\| 28.18-19 \|\|

*tacca jyotiḥsvarūpaṃ ca maṇḍalākārameva ca \|*  
*grīṣmamadhyāhnamārttaṇḍakoṭikoṭisamaprabham \|\| 18 \|\|*

*ākāśamiva vistīrṇaṃ sarvavyāpakamavyayam \|*  
*sukhadṛśyaṃ yathā candrabimbaṃ yogibhireva ca \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. The same eternal Brahman appears in the form of lustre resembling the crores of Sflryas at noon in the summer season. He is wide-spread like the sky, indestructible and appears to the yogis like the reflection of moon which is quite pleasant.

## \|\| 28.20 \|\|

*vadanti yoginastatra paraṃ brahma sanātanam \|*  
*divāniśaṃ ca dhyāyante satyaṃ tatsarvamaṅgalam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The yogis and ascetics describe him as the eternal Brahman and meditate upon on him through out the day and night as the bestower of welfare and truthful.

## \|\| 28.21 \|\|

*nirīhaṃ ca nirākāraṃ paramātmānamīśvaram \|*  
*svecchāmayaṃ svatantraṃ ca sarvakāraṇakāraṇam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. He is without any desire, invisible the supreme soul, the lord who moves according to his own sweet-will to be the cause of all the causes.

## \|\| 28.22-24 \|\|

*paramānandarūpaṃ ca paramānandakāraṇam \|*  
*paraṃ pradhānaṃ puruṣaṃ nirguṇaṃ prakṛteḥ param \|*  
*tatraiva līnā prakṛtiḥ sarvabījasvarūpiṇī \|\| 22 \|\|*

*yathā'gnau dāhikā śaktiḥ prabhā sūryye yathā mune \|*  
*yathā dugdhe ca dhāvalyaṃ jale śaityaṃ yathaiva ca \|\| 23 \|\|*

*yathā śabdaśca gagane yathā gandhaḥ kṣitau sadā \|*  
*tathā hi nirguṇaṃ brahma nirguṇā prakṛtistathā \|\| 24 \|\|*

22-24. He is blissful, cause of the bliss and best of the Purusas, virtuous, all virtuous, free from sattva, rajas and tamas, besides being beyond the nature. The nature containing the seeds of everything merges in him at the time of dissolution. In the same way, as the rays in the sun, the flames in the fire, the whiteness in milk and coolness in the water, О sage as the sound always echoes in the sky and the fragrance on earth similarly the Nirguna Brahman always remains united with Nirguna Prakriti.

## \|\| 28.25-26 \|\|

*sṛṣṭyunmukhena tad brahma cāṃśena puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*sa eva saguṇo vatsa prākṛto viṣayī smṛtaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*triguṇā sā hi tatraiva parā cchāyāmayī smṛtā \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. The same Brahman turns to the form of a Purusa at the time of creation and he is called as possessing the gunas and the associate of Prakriti (nature). The eternal Prakriti having three fold form, reside in him as a shadow.

## \|\| 28.27 \|\|

*yathā mṛdā kulālaśca ghaṭaṃ kartuṃ kṣamaḥ sadā \|*  
*tathā prakṛtyā tadbrahma sṛṣṭiṃ sraṣṭuṃ kṣamo mune \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. As a potter is competent to create any number of pitcher with earth, similarly the Brahman creates the universe with the help of Prakriti (nature).

## \|\| 28.28 \|\|

*svarṇena kuṇḍalaṃ karttuṃ svarṇakāraḥ kṣamo yathā \|*  
*tathā brahma tayā sārddha sṛṣṭiṃ kartumiheśvaraḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. As a gold-smith can prepare any number of ornaments with gold, similarly the Brahman can create the universe with the help of Prakriti.

## \|\| 28.29 \|\|

*kulālasṛṣṭā na ca mṛnnityā caiva sanātanī \|*  
*na svarṇakārasṛṣṭaṃ tatsvarṇaṃ vā nityameva ca \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The earth which is used by the potter for the creation of the vases is neither eternal nor everlasting. Similarly gold of which the ornaments are made by the gold-smith can never be eternal or everlasting.

## \|\| 28.30-31 \|\|

*nityaṃ tatparamaṃ brahma nityā ca prakṛtiḥ smṛtā \|*  
*dvayoḥ samaṃ ca prādhānyamiti kecidvadanti hi \|\| 30 \|\|*

*mṛdaṃ svarṇaṃ samāhartuṃ kulālasvarṇakārakau \|*  
*na samarthau ca mṛtsvarṇaṃ tayorāharaṇe kṣamam \|\| 31 \|\|*

30-31. But the eternal Brahman and the Prakriti are everlasting because both of them are equally important according to the opinion of certain people. The potter and the gold-smith cannot themselves produce the earth or the gold. On the other hand the earth and the gold are not competent to bring out the potter and the pitcher beside the gold. The gold-smith and the ornaments are equally important.

## \|\| 28.32 \|\|

*tasmāttatprakṛterbrahma parameva ca nārada \|*  
*iti kecidvadantyevaṃ dvayorvai nityatā dhruvam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. О Narada, Brahma is better than Prakriti. Thus some of the people describe both of them as eternal.

## \|\| 28.33 \|\|

*kecidvadanti tadbrahma svayaṃ ca prakṛtiḥ pumān \|*  
*brahmātiriktā prakṛtirvadantīti ca kecana \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Some people (philosophers) say that the said Brahman represent Prakriti and Purusa both. Some people consider Prakriti separate from the Brahman.

## \|\| 28.34 \|\|

*tadbrahma paramaṃ dhāma sarvakāraṇakāraṇam \|*  
*tadbrahmalakṣaṇaṃ brahmannidaṃ kiṃcicchrutau śrutam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The cause of all the causes. О Narada, the personality of that Brahman has been variously described by the people.

## \|\| 28.35 \|\|

*brahma cātmā ca sarveṣāṃ nirliptaṃ sākṣirūpi ca \|*  
*sarvavyāpi ca sarvādi lakṣaṇaṃ ca śrutau śrutam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The same Brahman happens to be the soul of all uninvolved, witness, omnipresent and the bestower of all. This is what has been described in the Vedas.

## \|\| 28.36 \|\|

*tadbrahmaśaktiḥ prakṛtiḥ sarvabījasvarūpiṇī \|*  
*yatastacchaktimadbrahma cedaṃ prakṛtilakṣaṇam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. He represents in the form of seeds of all and Prakriti happens to be the force of the Brahman. It is also conceived, if Prakriti is taken to be the force, Brahman in that case has to be the possessor of the force.1

## \|\| 28.37 \|\|

*tejorūpaṃ ca tadbrahma dhyāyante yoginaḥ sadā \|*  
*vaiṣṇavāstanna manyante madbhaktāḥ sūkṣmabuddhayaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. All the yogis meditate upon the illustrious Brahman but those who are highly intelligent my devotees Vaishnavas do not agree with this.

## \|\| 28.38 \|\|

*tattejaḥ kasya nāścaryyaṃ dhyāyante puruṣaṃ vinā \|*  
*kāraṇena vinā kārya kuto vā prabhavedbhuvi \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Who will not get surprised on the lustre of that Brahman without existence of Pumsa? On the earth, there is no object without any causative form.

## \|\| 28.39 \|\|

*dhyāyante vaiṣṇavāstasmāttatra rūpaṃ manoharam \|*  
*svecchāmayasya puṃsaśca sākārasyātmanaḥ sadā \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Therefore the Vaishnavas meditate upon the eternal Purusa, who moves at will, has a beautiful form and is the visible form of the great lord.

## \|\| 28.40 \|\|

*tattejomaṇḍalākāre sūryyakoṭisamaprabhe \|*  
*nityaṃ sthalaṃ ca pracchannaṃ golokābhidhameva ca \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. He represents the accumulation of lustre of crores of suns and resides iira circular form with an eternal abode inside the same known as Goloka.

## \|\| 28.41 \|\|

*lakṣakoṭyā yojanānāṃ caturasraṃ manoharam \|*  
*ratnendrasāranirmāṇairgopībhiścāvṛtaṃ sadā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. That divine loka is spread over to lakhs and crore of yojanas. It has been constructed with the use of the best of the divine gems. That loka is filled with beautiful houses and the cowherdessess.

## \|\| 28.42 \|\|

*sudṛśyaṃ vartulākāraṃ yathā candrasya maṇḍalam \|*  
*nānāratnaiśca khacitaṃ nirādhāraṃ tadicchayā \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. It can be seen pleasantly. It is circular like the moon. Built with the use of gems, that place stayed in the sky without any support with the desire Krishna.

## \|\| 28.43-44 \|\|

*ūrdhvaṃ ca nityavaikuṇṭhātpañcāśatkoṭiyojanam \|*  
*gogopagopīsaṃyuktaṃ kalpavṛkṣasamanvitam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*kāmadhenubhirākīrṇaṃ rāsamaṇḍalamaṇḍitam \|*  
*vṛndāvanavanācchatraṃ virajāveṣṭitaṃ mune \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. O sage, this Goloka is situated fifty crores of yojanas beyond Vaikuntha. It is filled with cows, cowherds, cowherdesses, Kalpvrksa (wish fulfilling trees), Kamadhenus (wish fulfilling cows), dancing halls an orchard named of Vrindavana, beside a river named Viraja.

## \|\| 28.45-51 \|\|

*śataśṛṅgaṃ śātakumbhaiḥ sudīptaṃ śrīmadīpsitam \|*  
*lakṣakoṭyā parimitairāśramaiḥ sumanoharaiḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*śatamaddirasaṃyuktamāśramaṃ sumanoharam ।*  
*ralaprākāraparikhāvicivreṇa virājitam \|\| 46 \|\|*

*apūlyaralanirpāṇaṃ lakṣamandirasundaram ।*  
*āśramaṃ caturasraṃ ca canravimbākṛte varam \|\| 47 \|\|*

*golokamadhyadeśasyamatīva sumanoharam ।*  
*prākāraparikhāyuktaṃ pārijātavanāvvitam \|\| 48 \|\|*

*kausubheddheṇa maṇinā rājitaṃ paramojjvalam ।*  
*hīrasārasusaṃklṛṣsopānaiścātisundaraiḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*maṇīdrasāraracitaiḥ kapaṣadarpaṇānvitaiḥ।*  
*nānācitravicitrāḍhyairāśrama ca susaṃ skṛtam \|\| 50 \|\|*

*poḍaśadārasaṃyuktaṃ sudīpaṃ raladīpakaiḥ।*  
*salasihāsane ramye mahārthamaṇinirmite \|\| 51 \|\|*

45-51. Himalaya the king of mountains resides there with hundreds of its peaks. There are lakhs and crores of Ashramas built in gold. They all live enjoying all the riches, in the centre of the loka, there is a beautifi.il Asrama having a hundred buildings. It is surrounded by the boundary walls and moats studded with gems, it has been built with precious gems. It looks beautiful like lakhs of the houses. Its size is like that of the reflection of moon. It is beautifully located in the centre of Goloka. It is surrounded by the boundary walls and moats beside the forests of Parijata trees. The pitchers installed in that Asrama were made of precious Kustubha-gem. Because of this, the shining lustre emerges from that place. The steps have been built with the dust of the gems.

## \|\| 28.52-54 \|\|

*nānācitravicitrāḍhye vasantaṃ varamīśvaram ।*  
*navīnanīradaśyāmaṃ kiśoravayasaṃ śiśum \|\| 52 \|\|*

*śaranmadhyāhnamārttaṇḍaprabhāmocakalocanam \|*  
*śaratpārvaṇapūrṇenduśubhadīptimadānanam \|\| 53 \|\|*

*koṭikandarpalāvaṇyalīlāninditamanmatham \|*  
*koṭicandraprabhājuṣṭaṃ puṣṭaṃ śrīyuktavigraham \|\| 54 \|\|*

52-54. The doors are made of gems and have mirrors fitted in them. The Asrama is decorated with various types of instruments, it has sixteen doors and it is illuminated with the burning lamps made of gems. There is a lion throne in that Asrama which is made of precious gems over which Shri Krishna the lord of universe, is seated. He has a complexion of the fresh clouds. He is represented in a child form.

## \|\| 28.55-57 \|\|

*sasmitaṃ muralīhastaṃ supraśastaṃ sumaṅgalam \|*  
*paramottamapītāṃśukayugena samujjvalam \|\| 55 \|\|*

*candanokṣitasarvāṃgaṃ kaustubhena virājitam \|*  
*ājānu mālatīmālāvanamālāvibhūṣitam \|\| 56 \|\|*

*tribhaṅgabhaṅgyā saṃyuktaṃ maṇimāṇikyabhūṣitam \|*  
*mayūrapucchacūḍaṃ ca sadratnamukuṭojjvalam \|\| 57 \|\|*

55-57. His eyes emit lustres like the rays of the sun during the summer season and his face reflects the charming glory of the full moon of Sarada-purnima. His beauty puts crores of the gods of love to shame. His developed body emitting rays like crores of moon. He wear a serene smile over his face and holds a flute in his hand. His beautiful appearance is commendable and he bestowed welfare. He wears two lower garments of the colour of molten gold, his body always remain shining.

## \|\| 28.58 \|\|

*ratnakeyūravalayaratnamañjīrarañjitam \|*  
*ratnakuṇḍalayugmena gaṇḍasthalasuśobhitam \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. All his limbs are plastered with sandal-paste. His chest is adorned with the kaustubha gem. He wears a long garland of forest flowers, which falls up to the knees.

## \|\| 28.59-60 \|\|

*muktāpaṅktisadṛkṣābhadaśanaṃ sumanoharam \|*  
*pakvabimbādharoṣṭhaṃ ca nāsikonnatiśobhanam \|\| 59 \|\|*

*vīkṣitaṃ gopikābhiśca veṣṭitābhissamantataḥ \|*  
*sthirayauvanayuktābhiḥ sasmitābhiśca sādaram \|\| 60 \|\|*

59-60. His teeth resemble those of the lustre of gems and his lips resembled the ripe bimba fruit. His developed nose looks beautiful. He is surrounded by the cowherdesses from all the sides, who always look on him. These cowherdesses are always youthful, wear serene smile over their faces and are adorned with all the ornaments. The sages, Indra, ascetics, humans, Brahma, Vis nu, Shiva and other gods adore him with devotion. He is loved by his devotees, he happens to be their lord and is always compassionate to them. О sage, all the Vaishnavas adore the lord who is beloved of Radha in whose heart, he always resides.

## \|\| 28.61-63 \|\|

*bhūṣitābhiśca sadratnanirmitairbhūṣaṇaiḥ param \|*  
*surendraiśca munīndraiśca munibhirmānavendrakaiḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

*brahmaviṣṇuśivānantadharmādyairvanditaṃ mudā \|*  
*bhaktapriyaṃ bhaktanāthaṃ bhaktānugrahakārakam \|\| 62 \|\|*

*rāseśvaraṃ surasikaṃ rādhāvakṣasthalasthitam \|*  
*evaṃrūpamarūpaṃ taṃ mune dhyāyanti vaiṣṇavāḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

61-63. Such a lord is adorable for us. He is indestructible the eternal Brahman and everlasting lord.

## \|\| 28.64 \|\|

*satataṃ dhyeyamasmākaṃ paramātmānamīśvaram \|*  
*akṣaraṃ paramaṃ brahma bhagavantaṃ sanātanam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. He stands in tribhanga posture and is adorned with all the ornaments and gems, he wears a crown of peacock feathers, besides a crown studded with gems, he wears the gems-studded armlets, wristlets and the anklets, the gems studded Kundalas appear in his ears.

## \|\| 28.65-68 \|\|

*svecchāmayaṃ nirguṇaṃ ca nirīhaṃ prakṛteḥ param \|*  
*sarvādhāraṃ sarvabījaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvameva ca \|\| 65 \|\|*

*sarveśvaraṃ sarvapūjyaṃ sarvasiddhikaraṃ pradam \|*  
*sa eva bhagavānādirgoloke dvibhujaḥ svayam \|\| 66 \|\|*

*gopaveṣaśca gopālaiḥ pārṣadaiḥ pariveṣṭitaḥ \|*  
*paripūrṇatamaḥ śrīmān śrīkṛṣṇo rādhikeśvaraḥ \|\| 67\|\|*

*sarvāntarātmā sarvatra pratyakṣaḥ sarvagaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*kṛṣiśca sarvavacano nakāraścātmavācakaḥ \|*  
*sarvātmā ca paraṃ brahma tena kṛṣṇaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

65-68. He moves according to his wishes, is formless, unattached, beyond Prakriti, the base of all, the seed of all, all knowledgeable lord of everyone, adorable by all and bestowers of all the success, he is the only lord who reside in the Goloka in the form of a cowherd having two arms only. He is surrounded by the cowherd courtiers. He is known as lord Krishna, the lord of Radha, the soul of all and is present everywhere. (In Krishna word) The word b/s is derived from the root which means all and n stands for the soul. Therefore Krishna stands for the soul of all.

## \|\| 28.69-73 \|\|

*kṛṣiśca sarvavacano nakāraścādivācakaḥ \|*  
*sarvādipuruṣo vyāpī tena kṛṣṇaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

*sa evāṃśena bhagavānvaikuṇṭhe ca caturbhujaḥ \|*  
*caturbhujaiḥ pārṣadaistairāvṛtaḥ kamalāpatiḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*sa eva kalayā viṣṇuḥ pātā ca jagatāṃ prabhuḥ \|*  
*śvetadvīpe sindhukanyāpatireva caturbhujaḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

*etatte kathitaṃ sarvaṃ parabrahmasvarūpakam \|*  
*asmākaṃ cintanīyaṃ ca sevyaṃ vanditamīpsitam \|\| 72 \|\|*

*ityuktvā śaṅkarastatra virarāma ca śaunaka \|*  
*gandharvarājastotreṇa tuṣṭuve taṃ ca nāradaḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

69-73. It has been explained in another form according to which krs stands for the eternal and n stands for the soul. Therefore Krishna is conceived to be the soul eternal. The same lord appears in the Vaikuntha from his Amsas having four arms and is the lord of Lakshmi. He appears from his Amsa as Vishnu, protects the universe and residing in the Shveta-dvipa, he resides as the husband of the daughter of Sindhu, having four arms. Thus I have explained to you the form of Parabrahman to you, who is adored by everyone. О Shaunaka, thus speaking lord Shiva kept quiet.

## \|\| 28.74-75 \|\|

*munistotreṇa santuṣṭo bhagavānādiracyutaḥ \|*  
*jñānaṃ mṛtyuñjayastasmai pradadau varamīpsitam \|\| 74 \|\|*

*munīndrastaṃ saṃpraṇamya prahṛṣṭavadanekṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*tadājñayā puṇyarūpaṃ yayau nārāyaṇāśramam \|\| 75 \|\|*

74-75. Thereafter, Narada offered prayer to the lord, with the prayer composed by the king of the Gandharvas; thereafter lord Shiva getting pleased with his adoration bestowed the desired knowledge on him. The sage Narada then delightfully bowed in reverence before Shiva. With his permission Narada there went to Narayanasrama.

## Chapter 29

## \|\| 29.1-2 \|\|

*sautiruvāca \|*  
*dadarśāśramamāścaryyaṃ devarṣirnāradastathā \|*  
*ṛṣirnārāyaṇasyaiva badarīvanasaṃyutam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*nānāvṛkṣaphalākīrṇaṃ puṃskokilarutaśrutam \|*  
*śarabhendraiḥ kesarīndrairvyāghraughaiḥ pariveṣṭitam \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Sauti said — Narada the divine sage, went to the abode of Narayana and had a look at his astonishing Asrama. It had a large number of Jugube trees, besides various other types of trees, in which the cuckoos, were issuing sweet notes. It had a number of stages, lions and flocks of tiger.

## \|\| 29.3 \|\|

*ṛṣīndrasya prabhāveṇa hiṃsābhayavivarjitam \|*  
*mahāraṇyamagamyaṃ ca svargādapi manoharam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. But with the influence of Narayana, the place was free from danger and violence. Thus the inaccessible forest was looking more charming then the heaven.

## \|\| 29.4-6 \|\|

*siddhendrāṇāṃ munīndrāṇāmāśramāṇāṃ trikoṭibhiḥ \|*  
*āvṛtaṃ candanāraṇyaiḥ pārijātavanānvitam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*dadarśa tamṛṣīndraṃ ca sabhāmadhye manoharam \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca vasantaṃ yogināṃ gurum \|\| 5 \|\|*

*japantaṃ paramaṃ brahma kṛṣṇamātmānamīśvaram \|*  
*praṇanāma ca taṃ dṛṣṭvā brahmaputraśca śaunaka \|\| 6 \|\|*

4-6. Thirty three crores of siddhas and fifty crores of ascetics dwelt there. О Brahmana, he found Narayana as there the head of all the Rsis observing the dance of Vidyadharas, wearing a smile over his face. The sound of music and songs relating to Krishna was heard there. There were three crores of siddhas and sages living in Asramas which was surrounded by the forest of Parijata and sandal-wood trees. Thus the sage Narada found a gem-studded lion throne in the centre of the assembly hall, having a beautiful path. He happen to be the teacher of the yogis. О Shaunaka, the sage Narada reciting the name of Shri Krishna bowed at the feet of the lord.

## \|\| 29.7 \|\|

*utthāya sahasā''liṅgya yuyuje paramāśiṣam \|*  
*papraccha kuśalaṃ snehāccakārātithipūjanam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Thereafter sage got up and embraced him showering blessings on him. He then enquired about his welfare and welcomed him.

## \|\| 29.8 \|\|

*ratnasiṃhāsane ramye vāsayāmāsa nāradam \|*  
*nivasannāsane ramye vartmaśramavivarjitaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Не made Narada to sit over the beautiful lion throne by which the fatigue of journey of Narada was removed.

## \|\| 29.9-10 \|\|

*uvāca tamṛṣiśreṣṭhaṃ bhagavantaṃ sanātanam \|*  
*adhītya vedānsarvāṃśca pituḥ sthāne sudurgamān \|\| 9 \|\|*

*jñānaṃ samprāpya yogīndrānmantraṃ vai śaṅkarādvibho \|*  
*mano me na hi tṛpnoti durnivāraṃ ca caṃcalam \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. Thereafter Narada spoke to the eternal lord, “O lord my mind is not satisfied after getting the knowledge of Vedas from my father Brahma and the divine knowledge from lord Shiva. My mind is still unstable.

## \|\| 29.11-12 \|\|

*dṛṣṭaṃ mayā tvatpadābjaṃ manasā preritena ca \|*  
*kiṃcijjñānaviśeṣaṃ ca labdhumicchāmi sāmpratam \|\| 11 \|\|*

*yatra kṛṣṇaguṇākhyānaṃ janmamṛtyujarāpaham \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. Therefore, inspite by my own umogiit I have come to have a look at your lotus-like teci. You therefore, impart the divine knowledge to me in order to rid myself of the birth, death and old age and to recite always he glory of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 29.13 \|\|

*brahmaviṣṇuśivādyāśca surendraśca surā vibho \|*  
*kaṃ cintayanti munayo manavaśca vicakṣaṇāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. О lord he is the ore who is adored by Brahma, Vishnu, Shiva, Indra a no other gods besides the intelligent sages.

## \|\| 29.14-15 \|\|

*kasmātsṛṣṭiśca bhavati kutra vā saṃpralīyate \|*  
*ko vā sarveśvaro viṣṇuḥ sarvakāraṇakārakaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*tasyeśvarasya kiṃ rūpaṃ karma vā kiṃ jagatpate \|*  
*vicāryya manasā sarvaṃ tadbhavānvaktumarhati \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. You kindly tell me where from the universe emerges and in whom does it merge ultimately? Who happens to be the lord of all including Vishnu and happens to be the cause of all the causes? О lord of the universe, you kindly enlighten me about the great lord.”

## \|\| 29.16 \|\|

*nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā prahasya bhagavānṛṣiḥ \|*  
*kathāṃ kathitumārebhe puṇyāṃ bhuvanapāvanīm \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. On hearing the words of Narada the lord smiled and started narrating the story which purifies all the three worlds.

## Chapter 30

## \|\| 30.1 \|\|

*śrīnārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*lambodaro harirumāpatirīśaśeṣā brahmādayaḥ suragaṇā manavo munīndrāḥ \|*  
*vāṇīśivātripathagā kamalādikāśca saṃcintayedbhagavataścaraṇāravindam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shri Narayana said — The lord whose feet are I adored by Ganesha, Vishnu, Shiva, Shesha, Brahma I and other gods besides Manu, the sages, i Sarasvati, Gauri, Ganga, Kamala and other goddesses; all should adore him.

## \|\| 30.2 \|\|

*saṃsārasāgaramatīva gabhīraghoraṃ dāvāgnisarpapariveṣṭitaceṣṭitāṅgam \|*  
*saṃlaṃghya gantumabhivāñchati yo hi dāsyaṃ saṃcintayedbhagavataścaraṇāravindam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. This universe is like the deep ocean which is surrounded by the forest fire and the same has to oe crossed by everyone. One who intends cross the same, should adore the lotus-like feet of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 30.3-4 \|\|

*govarddhanoddharaṇakīrttiratīva khinnā bhūrdhāritā ca daśanāgrata eva cārdrā \|*  
*viśvāni lomavivareṣu bibharturādeḥ saṃcintayedbhagavataścaraṇāravindam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*vedāṅgavedamukhaniḥsṛtakīrttiraṃśairvedāṅgavedajanakasya harervidhātuḥ \|*  
*janmāntakādibhayaśokavidīrṇadehaḥ saṃcintayedbhagavataścaraṇāravindam \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. The lord lifted Govardhana and rescued the earth over the tip of his tooth and is the one who preserve everybody. All the lokas are enshrined in his hair pits, one should always adore the god like him. All the six parts of the Vedas recite his glory throughout the day and night and he is the one who is the creator of the Vedas with their parts. Such a type of lord Hari should be adored to rid himself from the cycle of birth and death.

## \|\| 30.5-6 \|\|

*gopāṅganāvadanapaṅkajaṣaṭpadasya rāseśvarasya rasikāramaṇasya puṃsaḥ \|*  
*vṛndāvane viharato vrajaveṣaviṣṇoḥ saṃcintayedbhagavataścaraṇāravindam \|\| 5 \|\|*

*cakṣurnimeṣapatito jagatāṃ vidhātā tatkarma vatsa kathituṃ bhuvi kaḥ samarthaḥ \|*  
*tvaṃ cāpi nārada mune paramādareṇa saṃcintanaṃ kuru hareścaraṇāravindam \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. The lord who is the like black-wasps of the lotus-like faces of the cowherdesses and roams about in Vrindavana, the same lord Vishnu appears in the form of people of Vraja who is the lord of Rasa and should be adored by the people. The one with the fall of whose eyelids the age of the Brahma comes to an end. Who else is there to describe about his glory on earth? Therefore, О sage Narada, you also adore at the lotus-like feet the same lord.

## \|\| 30.7 \|\|

*yūyaṃ vayaṃ tasya kalākalāṃśāḥ kalākalāṃśā manavo munīndrāḥ \|*  
*kalāviśeṣā bhavapāramukhyā mahānvirāḍ yasya kalāviśeṣaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Both you and me are mere small particles of the great lord. Similarly all the sages and the Manu also happen to be the particles of the same grand personality. Brahma and Shiva have special qualities. The universal form of the lord is also a particle of the same lord.

## \|\| 30.8 \|\|

*sahasraśīrṣā śirasaḥ pradeśe bibhartti siddhārthasamaṃ ca viśvam \|*  
*kūrme ca śeṣo maśako gaje yathā kūrmaśca kṛṣṇasya kalākalāṃśaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The serpent Shesha having a thousand hoods, who carries the entire universe over his hoods like a small seed but when he is seated over the back of the tortoise, he appears like elephant. The same lord Kurma (tortoise) is just a particle of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 30.9 \|\|

*golokanāthasya vibhoryaśo'malaṃ śrutau purāṇe nahi kiṃcana sphuṭam \|*  
*na pādmamukhyāḥ kathituṃ samarthāḥ sarveśvaraṃ taṃ bhaja pādmamukhyam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Thus the immense glory of the lord of the Goloka could not be revealed in the Vedas of the Puranas fully. The chief gods like Brahma and others are unable to recite his glory. Therefore you should adore the same god who is the best of all.

## \|\| 30.10 \|\|

*viśveṣu sarveṣu ca viśvadhāpnaḥ \|*  
*santyeva śaśacadavidhiviṣṇurudarāḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ ca saṃkhyāḥ śrutayaśca devāḥ \|*  
*paraṃ na jānanti tamīśvaraṃ bhaja \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The all-pervading lord establishes Brahma, Vishnu and Mahesa in the universe and their number cannot be known even by the Vedas and the gods. Therefore one should serve the lord whole-heatedly.

## \|\| 30.11 \|\|

*karoti sṛṣṭiṃ sa vidhervidhātā vidhāya nityāṃ prakṛtiṃ jagatprasūm \|*  
*brahmādayaḥ prākṛtikāśca sarve bhaktipradāṃ śrīprakṛtiṃ bhajanti \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The same lord creates Brahma and Brahma in turn creates the universe and the Prakriti. Therefore Brahma and other gods and the humans, adores him alone with devotion.

## \|\| 30.12 \|\|

*brahmasvarūpā prakṛtirna bhinnā yayā ca sṛṣṭiṃ kurute sanātanaḥ \|*  
*śriyaśca sarvāḥ kalayā jagatsu māyā ca sarve ca tayā vimohitāḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The Prakriti cannot be separate from Brahman. The eternal lord creates the universe with help of Prakriti. Form the ray of the same Prakriti all the ladies of the world have appeared. Prakriti is illusion. All are influenced by it.

## \|\| 30.13 \|\|

*nārāyaṇī sā paramā sanātanī śaktiśca puṃsaḥ paramātmanaśca \|*  
*ātmeśvaraścāpi yayā ca śaktimāṃstayā vinā sraṣṭumaśakta eva \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. She is eternal Narayani, the great force behind the eternal Purusa, as a result of which he is called to be the possessor of the force. He feels himself inactive in the absence of the same force and is unable to resort to creation.

## \|\| 30.14 \|\|

*gatvā vivāhaṃ kuru vatsa sāmprataṃ kartuṃ prayuktaśca piturnideśaḥ \|*  
*gurornideśapratipālako bhaveḥ sarvatra pūjyo vijayī ca santatam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. О son, you must marry at present, obeying to the command of your father, because by obeying the command of the teacher you will surely be adorable by all, beside being victorious.

## \|\| 30.15 \|\|

*svapatnīṃ pūjayedyo hi vastrālaṅkāracandanaiḥ \|*  
*prakṛtistasya santuṣṭā yathā kṛṣṇo dvijārccane \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. One who appeases his wife with the smearing of sandal-paste and providing with garments and ornaments, the nature (wife) is fully pacified by him as lord Krishna is pleased when Brahmanas are honoured.

## \|\| 30.16 \|\|

*sā ca yoṣitsvarūpā ca prativiśveṣu māyayā \|*  
*yoṣitāmapamānena parābhūtā ca sā bhavet \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Thus in every age the Prakriti remains in the form of Maya. Therefore with the denouncing of a lady the nature itself feels humiliated.

## \|\| 30.17 \|\|

*divyā strī pūjitā yena patiputravatī satī \|*  
*prakṛtiḥ pūjitā tena sarvamaṅgaladāyinī \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Therefore, the one who honours a lady having a husband and a son, he actually adores the Prakriti who is the bestower of all the welfare.

## \|\| 30.18 \|\|

*mūlaprakṛtirekā sā pūrṇabrahmasvarūpiṇī \|*  
*sṛṣṭau pañcavidhā sā ca viṣṇumāyā sanātanī \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The Mula Prakriti which is fonn of eternal Brahman is one, but was divided into five parts at the time of illusory creation of lord Vishnu.

## \|\| 30.19-21 \|\|

*prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*sarvāsāṃ preyasī kāntā sā rādhā parikīrtitā \|\| 19 \|\|*

*nārāyaṇapriyā lakṣmīḥ sarvasampatsvarūpiṇī \|*  
*rāgādhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā sā ca pūjyā sarasvatī \|\| 20 \|\|*

*sāvitrī vedamātā ca pūjyarūpā vidheḥ priyā \|*  
*śaṅkarasya priyā durgā yasyāḥ putro gaṇeśvaraḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. In this way lord Krishna adores Radha better than all other goddesses. She takes to the form of all the riches besides Lakshmi who is the beloved of Narayana. The second Prakriti happens to be the goddess of speech, is quite adorable and is known by the name of Sarasvati, Savitri the beloved of Brahma who happens to be the mother of the Vedas is the fourth Prakriti. Durga the beloved of Shiva, the mother of Ganesha happens to be the fifth Prakriti.
